Conclusion: DocXEval作为首个多模态文档理解可解释性综合基准，不仅系统评估了现有XAI方法的优劣，还通过元评估和诊断分析提供了选择可靠指标和理解模型行为的实用指南，为该领域未来XAI研究奠定基础。代码已开源以支持复现。

Abstract: Multimodal transformer models have driven unprecedented performance breakthroughs in modern document understanding. However, the inherent black-box nature of these models remains a major obstacle to their safe deployment in high-stakes sectors. Despite significant progress in explainability for vision and language tasks, explainable AI (XAI) research tailored to multimodal document understanding has received little attention. To address this gap, we present \ourframework{}, the first comprehensive explainability framework that adapts existing XAI methods and evaluation metrics to the multimodal (text, layout, and image) document understanding context, while introducing efficient multi-target adaptations of both methods and metrics for domain-specific tasks such as semantic entity recognition. Using \ourframework{}, we systematically evaluate 13 XAI methods across 3 document understanding tasks, 8 datasets, 4 models, and 17 evaluation metrics, and find that only a few methods perform consistently across all evaluation criteria, with Occlusion emerging as the most faithful and interpretable method overall. Through a dedicated meta-evaluation, we observe moderate to strong agreement among several faithfulness and complexity metrics while identifying certain ones that produce unreliable results. Finally, through multiple dataset-wide diagnostic analyses, we uncover key insights into model behavior, including the contribution of individual modalities across tasks and labels, spatial attribution patterns around target entities, and potential dataset biases. Overall, this work serves as both a benchmark and a practical guide for future XAI research in multimodal document understanding. To support reproducibility, we publicly release our code at https://github.com/saifullah3396/docxeval/.

</details>


### [36] [JevRec: Linguistic Encoding with Jev-like Decision Modeling for LLM-based Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36937)
*Qiyong Zhong,Jiajie Su,Ming Yang,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 JevRec，一种生成式推荐模型，利用变长语义标识符（SID）根据物品交互频率动态分配编码长度：热门物品使用更短的 SID，长尾物品使用更丰富的描述。通过类 Jevons 决策建模，分离长度预测与内容生成两个输出头，共享交互历史表示，减少梯度冲突。在三个公开数据集上优于基线，且训练推理时间低于 TIGER。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐方法为所有物品分配固定长度的语义标识符，忽略了交互频率的高度不均衡分布。统一长度导致热门物品反复承担细粒度编码的开销，而缩短编码又会压缩长尾物品的语义表示，限制了模型容量利用和推荐性能。

Method: JevRec 将物品编码为变长词元，基于共享码本保留频率相关的语义前缀，实现热门物品短 SID、长尾物品丰富描述。引入类 Jevons 决策建模组件：专用头预测长度分布，另一独立头在给定长度条件下生成内容，两头共享交互历史表示，从而解耦序列级长度预测与 token 级内容生成的优化目标。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上，JevRec 在所有评估推荐指标上均超越对比基线。进一步分析显示其训练和推理时间均低于 TIGER，且有效降低了长度预测与内容生成之间的梯度冲突。

Conclusion: JevRec 通过变长语义标识符和解耦的长度-内容预测机制，更高效地利用模型容量适应长尾分布，显著提升生成式推荐性能并降低计算开销，为处理物品频度不均衡提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Generative recommendation (GR) represents items as semantic identifiers (SIDs) and autoregressively generates the next item's SID from interaction history. However, most methods assign the same SID length to all items despite highly unequal interaction frequencies. This uniform design repeatedly incurs the cost of fine-grained codes for popular items, while shortening codes to reduce this cost also compresses the semantic representations of long-tail items. Inspired by the compact forms of frequent expressions in natural language, we propose JevRec to encode items as variable-length words and generate the next item from interaction context. Linguistic encoding retains frequency-dependent semantic prefixes from shared codebooks, producing shorter SIDs for popular items and richer descriptions for long-tail items. Yet predicting a sequence-level length and generating token-level content impose different optimization requirements that may conflict under a shared output head. Our Jev-like decision modeling component therefore predicts a length distribution with a dedicated head and generates content conditioned on length through a separate head while sharing the interaction-history representation. Experiments on three public datasets show that JevRec outperforms all compared baselines across the evaluated recommendation metrics. Further analyses show lower training and inference time than TIGER and reduced gradient conflict between length and content prediction.

</details>


### [37] [Self-Keyed In-Parameter Memorization for External Memory-Free Dialogue Recall in Frozen Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36948)
*XinYu Piao,Myunghoon Ryu,Joongheon Kim,Jong-Kook Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出SKIM（自键参数内记忆）方法，使大语言模型无需外部存储即可将对话内容编码进模型参数，通过自生成多样化问题作为检索键，实现后续用户提问的参数级记忆回溯。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探究在无外部记忆且无法预知未来问题的环境下，对话内容能否直接存储于模型参数中，并在后续通过用户提问从参数中准确回忆答案。

Method: 提出闭式方法SKIM：模型针对每轮对话自发生成多个措辞多样的问题，将这些问题作为键写入模型参数；后续用户提问与任一键匹配即可触发参数内内容的检索与回答。

Result: 在五个语言模型上评估显示，SKIM能从后续用户问题中回忆大部分存储的对话轮次，且完全依赖模型参数回答，无需任何外部存储。

Conclusion: SKIM验证了大语言模型可将对话知识内化于参数，通过自生成键值机制实现高效检索，为无外部记忆的对话系统提供了可行的参数级记忆方案。}

Abstract: A large language model maintains conversational content in an external storage that is outside the model and grows as the dialogue continues. This work assumes an environment where a language model has no external memory and no prior knowledge of the future question or its answer. The motivation is to research whether a dialogue can be stored in the model parameters and later answer a question about it. This work proposes self-keyed in-parameter memorization (SKIM), a closed-form method in which the model generates its own questions about each dialogue turn and writes those questions into its parameters as the keys that later retrieve the turn's content. Generating many varied questions for each turn allows a later user question to match one even when phrased differently, and its answer is recalled from the parameters alone. Evaluated on five language models, SKIM recalls most stored dialogue turns from later user questions, and it answers from the model parameters without any external storage.

</details>


### [38] [The State and Fate of Multilingual, Contextual Evaluation in the NLP World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36990)
*Manan Uppadhyay,Himanshu Beniwal,Prashant Kodali,Sunayana Sitaram*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文对51个多语言评估基准进行了系统性审计，发现覆盖虽广但薄弱、代表性因翻译主导而受损、严谨性因基准污染而受威胁，并提出构建原生、文化接地、污染感知评估框架的建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言评估基准是评估大语言模型泛化能力的主要工具，但其充分性鲜有系统性审视。作者旨在通过数据驱动的审计，揭示当前基准在覆盖度、代表性、严谨性三大维度的系统性缺陷，推动构建更公平、有效的多语言评估体系。

Method: 对51个近期多语言基准（涵盖242个数据集、219种语言）进行数据驱动审计，围绕覆盖度、代表性、严谨性三大支柱展开分析：统计语言覆盖广度与任务公平性，考察数据集构建策略（翻译比例、文化接地内容分布），检测基准污染（翻译泄露、平行语料重叠）。

Result: 1. 覆盖度虽广但薄：36%语言仅出现于单一基准，大洋洲、美洲、中亚近零覆盖；低资源语言仅评测1-3任务类别，高资源语言达14类。2. 代表性结构性受损：56%数据集-语言实例为英语翻译，引入伪影与英语中心框架；文化接地内容集中于少数社区驱动基准，语言范围窄。3. 严谨性受污染威胁：存在翻译基准泄露和平行语料重叠，规避表层检测。生态系统迫使广度与效度的权衡。

Conclusion: 当前多语言评估基准存在系统性缺陷，需构建原生构建、文化接地、污染感知、服务目标语言社区的评估框架。作者提供包含所有元数据的仪表盘（https://aka.ms/multilingual-evals）以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Multilingual evaluation benchmarks are the primary instrument for assessing whether large language models generalize beyond English, yet the adequacy of these benchmarks has received little systematic scrutiny. We present a data-driven audit of 51 recent multilingual benchmarks spanning 242 datasets and 219 languages, organized around three pillars: coverage, representativeness, and rigor. Our analysis reveals that coverage is wide but thin with 36% of evaluated languages appearing in only a single benchmark, entire regions (Oceania, the Americas, Central Asia) are near-zero, and a stark task equity gap leaves low-resource languages evaluated on only 1-3 task categories versus 14 for high-resource languages. Representativeness is structurally compromised: translation from English remains the dominant construction strategy where 56% of all dataset-language instances are translated introducing artifacts and English-centric framing, while culturally grounded content is concentrated in a handful of community-driven benchmarks with narrow language scope. The ecosystem thus forces a trade-off between breadth and validity. Rigor is undermined by benchmark contamination, including translated benchmark leakage and parallel corpus overlap that evade surface-form detection. We synthesize these findings into concrete recommendations for building evaluation frameworks that are natively constructed, culturally grounded, contamination-aware, and designed to serve the communities whose languages they claim to evaluate. The dashboard with all the metadata collected can be found at https://aka.ms/multilingual-evals.

</details>


### [39] [Hierarchical Low-Rank Reparameterization for Parameter-Efficient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37068)
*Rong Huang,Yi Xiong,Yuan-Yuan Cheng,Xiao-Ming Fu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出一种分层低秩重参数化方法，通过将PEFT的可训练参数重组为近似方阵并递归低秩分解，逐层压缩因子，显著减少参数量。实验表明，在LLaMA-3.1-8B和Qwen2.5-7B上结合LoRA、DoRA等方法，可在参数量压缩约一个数量级时仍保持竞争力性能，甚至超过基线平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数高效微调（PEFT）方法引入的少量可训练参数仍存在冗余，有进一步压缩空间且不损害任务性能。

Method: 将PEFT的可训练参数重组为近似方阵，采用低秩分解表示，并可递归应用，形成多层级结构，因子逐层压缩，大幅削减可训练参数数量。

Result: 在常识推理（8个基准）和数学推理（GSM8K）实验中，结合LoRA或DoRA时，在相近可训练参数预算下性能更优；将原方法参数量压缩约一个数量级后，仍保持竞争力表现，平均准确率甚至超越对应基线。

Conclusion: 分层低秩重参数化能有效消除PEFT参数冗余，实现极致参数压缩同时维持或提升下游任务性能，为大模型高效适配提供新范式。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods adapt large models by introducing a small number of trainable parameters. However, we find that these parameters still contain redundancy and can be further compressed while maintaining strong task performance. To this end, we propose a novel hierarchical low-rank reparameterization method. Its core idea is to reorganize the trainable parameters of PEFT methods into an approximately square matrix and then express it via a low-rank decomposition, which can be applied recursively. This forms a multi-level structure with factors compressed level by level, drastically reducing the number of trainable parameters. In our experiments, we combine this reparameterization with LoRA, DoRA, MoSA, and RaSA. We evaluate commonsense reasoning on LLaMA-3.1-8B and Qwen2.5-7B, with additional GSM8K math reasoning experiments on LLaMA-3.1-8B. Under comparable trainable-parameter budgets, our method combined with LoRA or DoRA achieves better performance. Furthermore,when the parameter count of the original methods is compressed by approximately an order of magnitude, our method can still maintain competitive task performance and even surpass the corresponding baselines in average accuracy across the eight commonsense reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [40] [Text Generation in Low-Resource Languages Using Human-Curated Vocabularies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37105)
*Michael Christlambert Sinanta,Muhammad Dehan Al Kautsar,Amanda Nurul Izzah,Thanh-Nhi Nguyen,Phan Phuong Mai Chau,Zheng Wei Lim,Fajri Koto*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种框架，利用人工整理的词汇表和少量人工编写的范例，结合 Zipf 分布采样，使推理增强的大语言模型能为极低资源甚至未见过的语言生成连贯文本，在句子层面表现尤佳，段落层面也有潜力，为低资源语言合成数据提供了实用路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成文本生成虽能缓解低资源语言数据稀缺，但若语言在预训练数据中极少或缺失，效果受限。许多这类语言却拥有人工整理的词汇表，含丰富词汇、语义及语法信息（如词性标注）。本文探究 LLMs 能否利用这些资源为极低资源和未见语言生成连贯文本。

Method: 提出框架：结合人工整理词汇表与少量人工编写范例（提供句法信号），按 Zipf 分布采样候选词。在不同资源水平语言上评估，指标包括词汇多样性、离题比率、人工流畅度判断。

Result: 推理增强的 LLMs 能为极低资源语言生成流畅文本，句子层面效果尤强，段落层面也有良好表现。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了一条为低资源语言引导合成数据的实用路径，即便现有数字资源极其有限也能扩充可用文本量。代码已开源。

Abstract: Synthetic text generation offers a promising approach to alleviating data scarcity in low-resource languages. However, its effectiveness is inherently limited for languages that are scarcely represented, or entirely absent, in an LLM's pretraining data. At the same time, many such languages have human-curated vocabularies that provide rich lexical and semantic information, often accompanied by grammatical information such as part-of-speech labels. In this work, we ask whether LLMs can leverage these resources to generate coherent text in extremely low-resource and unseen languages. We introduce a framework that combines human-curated vocabularies with a small number of human-written exemplars that provide syntactic signals, while sampling candidate words according to a Zipfian distribution. Across languages with varying levels of resource availability, we evaluate the generated text using lexical diversity, off-target ratio, and human judgments of fluency. Our results show that reasoning-enabled LLMs can generate fluent text even in extremely low-resource languages, with particularly strong results at the sentence level and promising performance at the paragraph level. Our findings highlight a practical pathway for bootstrapping synthetic data in low-resource languages, expanding the amount of usable text even when existing digital resources are extremely limited. Our source code is available at https://github.com/michaelsinanta/vocab2text.

</details>


### [41] [What Survives Distillation? A Mechanistic Interpretability Study of GPT-2 and DistilGPT-2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37157)
*Nabeelah Faumi,Deshan Sumanathilaka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究知识蒸馏（从GPT-2到DistilGPT-2）对两个典型电路（IOI和GT）的机制性影响，发现存在“压缩-崩塌”二分现象：IOI电路完全机制崩塌，而GT电路通过主动重映射得以保留。结论指出电路可压缩性决定蒸馏下的机制存活，且聚合指标不足以诊断电路完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识蒸馏能将大模型压缩为保留聚合指标的紧凑学生模型，但深度减少蒸馏的机制后果仍 largely 未被表征。本文旨在通过机制性可解释性工具，系统对比教师与学生模型在典型电路层面的差异，揭示蒸馏对电路级机制的影响规律。

Method: 采用线性CKA、logit lens分析、注意力模式分解、稀疏自编码器特征统计和激活补丁等多种机制性可解释性方法，对比GPT-2（教师）与其六层学生模型DistilGPT-2在间接宾语识别（IOI）电路和大于比较（GT）电路上的表现。

Result: 发现显著的压缩-崩塌二分现象：IOI电路在DistilGPT-2中完全机制崩塌（准确率35.2% vs 94.8%，零name-mover头保留）；GT电路则通过将教师第9层比较头主动重映射到学生第4层（平均Pearson r=0.945）实现87.7%任务性能恢复。

Conclusion: 电路可压缩性决定了蒸馏下的机制存活能力，聚合性能指标不足以诊断电路完整性。蒸馏可能导致某些电路彻底崩塌，而其他电路则通过层重映射实现功能保留，这揭示了模型压缩的机制性风险与边界。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation compresses large language models into compact students that preserve aggregate metrics, yet the mechanistic consequences of depth-reduction distillation remain largely uncharacterized. This work applies linear CKA, logit lens analysis, attention pattern decomposition, sparse autoencoder feature statistics, and activation patching to compare GPT-2 and its six-layer student DistilGPT-2 across two canonical circuits: the Indirect Object Identification (IOI) circuit and the Greater-Than (GT) circuit. The results reveal a compression-collapse dichotomy: the IOI circuit suffers complete mechanistic collapse (DistilGPT-2: 35.2% accuracy vs. 94.8%, zero name-mover preservation), while the GT circuit survives with 87.7% task performance recovery through active remapping of the teacher's layer-9 comparison head into student layer~4 (mean Pearson r = 0.945). These findings demonstrate that circuit compressibility governs mechanistic survival under distillation, and that aggregate metrics are insufficient diagnostics for circuit integrity.

</details>


### [42] [Decision Models as Judges for Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37229)
*Haozheng Liu,Zhaoqian Xue,Zhongyin Wang,Junting Mu,Zihui Ma,Shuya Feng,Lingyao Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文探讨决策模型作为LLM-as-a-Judge的低成本替代方案，在幻觉检测任务中评估其性能。结果显示决策模型在部分幻觉类型（如上下文不一致、事实错误）上接近生成式裁判GPT-6-sol，且通过置信级联策略可进一步提升性能，成本仅为GPT-6的1-3%。但决策模型存在过度自信问题，且在逻辑和指令不一致类型上表现较弱，使用前需验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型作为输出裁判（LLM-as-a-Judge）虽广泛使用，但成本高、易产生幻觉且过度自信。决策模型仅输出预定义裁决及各标签概率，无自由文本生成，具有更低成本和更快速度，因此值得作为替代方案进行系统评估。

Method: 构建五种幻觉类型的二分类基准（共9,852个样本，源自六个数据集），对比五个决策模型与生成式裁判gpt-6-sol的表现。评估指标包括平衡准确率、概率校准（过度自信程度）、正确与错误决策的分离度，并探索置信级联策略组合多个决策模型。

Result: 最佳决策模型在上下文不一致类型上平衡准确率持平gpt-6-sol，在事实错误类型上相差3个百分点，但在逻辑和指令不一致类型上落后4-8个百分点。决策模型概率虽过度自信，但优于GPT-6模型；正确与错误决策的分离度中等。置信级联策略在三种类型上比最佳单模型提升1.9-3.3个百分点，且成本仅为GPT-6的1-3%，性能匹配或超越GPT-6。

Conclusion: 决策模型是某些幻觉类型（如上下文不一致、事实错误）的实用低成本裁判方案，结合置信级联可进一步提升性能并大幅降低成本。但决策模型在逻辑和指令不一致类型上表现不足，且存在过度自信问题，实际部署前需针对具体任务和类型进行充分验证。

Abstract: Large language models are widely used as judges of model outputs (LLM-as-a-Judge), but they are costly and can hallucinate or be overconfident. Decision models are a potential alternative. They return a predefined verdict with a probability for each label instead of free-form text and are much cheaper and faster. We test this promise on hallucination detection. We build binary benchmarks for five hallucination types (9,852 items from six datasets) and compare five decision models with the generative judge gpt-6-sol. Performance depends strongly on the type: the best decision model matches gpt-6-sol in balanced accuracy on context inconsistency and comes within 3 percentage points on factual errors, but trails it by 4-8 points on logical and instruction inconsistency. Decision-model probabilities are overconfident, though less so than the GPT-6 models, and separate correct from incorrect decisions only moderately. We also find that a confidence cascade of decision models improves on the best single model by 1.9-3.3 points on three types and matches or exceeds gpt-6-sol at 1-3% of its cost. Overall, decision models are a practical low-cost judge for some hallucination types but need to be validated before use.

</details>


### [43] [Aligning Large Language Models for Safety in African Languages: A Case Study of Hausa](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37245)
*Valdini Douglace Lemofouet,Blessing Ngozi Uzor,Paula Chikaodinaka Anyanwu,Danielle Blanche Kapsa,Saminu Mohammad Aliyu,Seid Muhie Yimam,Shamsuddeen Hassan Muhammad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型在低资源语言（豪萨语）下的安全对齐问题，构建了首个豪萨语安全偏好数据集HausaSafeLM，对比SFT与SFT+DPO在黄金/白银数据上的表现，发现拒答率指标在低资源语言中不可靠，现有对齐技术更易教会模型何时拒答而非如何流利拒答，且无单一配置能同时最大化安全性、有帮助性与流利度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型的安全对齐研究主要集中在高资源语言，低资源、文化差异大的语言（如豪萨语，西非2亿多人使用）的对齐效果尚不明确，亟需探索现有对齐方法在这些语言中的表现与局限。

Method: 构建HausaSafeLM数据集：基于PKU-SafeRLHF衍生1万条提示词，含3000条人工修正的黄金样本和7000条机器翻译的白银样本，覆盖六大危害宏类别。以Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct为基座，对比四种配置：黄金/白银数据上的SFT，以及SFT后接DPO。在500条有害和200条良性豪萨语提示词上评估拒答率、拒答质量、语言流利度与有帮助性。

Result: 基座模型拒答率虽高但拒答质量极低，实为生成能力不足而非真正对齐。SFT显著提升拒答流利度与精准度，且从更大规模、噪声更多的白银数据中获益更大。DPO虽能锐化拒答精准度，但以严重损害语言质量为代价，且该损耗基本不随数据规模变化。无单一配置能同时在安全性、有帮助性与流利度上达到最优。

Conclusion: 现有对齐技术更擅长教会模型何时拒答，而非如何在低资源语言中流利地拒答；拒答率指标在低资源语言下是不可靠的安全信号，需开发更细致的评估体系与针对性对齐方法。

Abstract: Safety alignment research for large language models (LLMs) remains concentrated on high-resource languages, leaving open how existing alignment methods perform in low-resource, culturally distinct settings. We study this question for Hausa, a language spoken by more than 200 million people across West Africa. We introduce HausaSafeLM, one of the first Hausa safety preference dataset, built from 10,000 prompts derived from PKU-SafeRLHF: 3,000 human-corrected (gold) and 7,000 machine-translated (silver) examples spanning six harm macro-categories. Using Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, we compare supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and SFT followed by Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) under both data regimes, evaluating on 500 harmful and 200 benign Hausa prompts.We find that the untuned base model appears highly safe by refusal-rate metrics alone, but this reflects limited Hausa generation ability rather than genuine alignment: its refusals are almost uniformly low quality. SFT improves both refusal fluency and precision and benefits substantially from the larger, noisier silver-tier data, while DPO sharpens refusal precision but comes at a steep, largely data-invariant cost to linguistic quality. No single configuration jointly maximizes safety, helpfulness, and fluency. Our results suggest that current alignment techniques teach models when to refuse more readily than how to do so fluently in underrepresented languages, and that refusal-rate metrics alone are an unreliable signal of safety in low-resource settings.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿拉伯语有害内容检测面临方言差异大、危害类别间语义重叠高等挑战，现有方法难以细粒度区分相关类别。

Method: 两阶段流程：第一阶段使用GLiNER从用户提示中抽取危害相关实体；第二阶段将抽取的实体拼接至原始文本，输入微调后的MARBERT分类器进行七类别有害内容分类。

Conclusion: 将命名实体识别与阿拉伯语预训练语言模型相结合，为细粒度有害内容检测提供了有前景的方案。

Abstract: Detecting harmful content in Arabic is challenging because of dialectal variations and the semantic overlap between different harm categories. This paper presents a two-stage approach for classifying Arabic user prompts into seven categories of harmful content. First, GLiNER is used to extract harm-related entities from each prompt. The extracted entities are then added to the original text and passed to a fine-tuned MARBERT classifier for final classification. The approach combines explicit entity information with the contextual understanding of a pretrained Arabic language model. The system was evaluated on the development set of the ArGuard 2026 shared task. The results achieved a macro-F1 score of 0.886 and an accuracy of 0.897, demonstrating promising performance in fine-grained harmful content detection. The system reported an accuracy of 0.793 and Macro-F score of 0.735 on a blind test set for subtask 1. For subtask 2, it achieved an accuracy of 0.770 and Macro-F score of 0.754. The analysis also showed that distinguishing between related categories remains challenging. These findings suggest that integrating NER with Arabic language models is a promising approach to harmful content detection.

</details>


### [45] [Multi-Label Agreement: A Flexible Framework for Inter-Annotator Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37292)
*Tim Menzner,Jochen L. Leidner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Multi-Label Agreement (MLA)框架，用于评估多标签标注任务中的标注者一致性，采用平均成对汉明一致性，支持多种归一化方式，提供条件最小一致性诊断，并给出Python实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Cohen's kappa、Fleiss' kappa、Krippendorff's alpha等IAA指标主要适用于单标签任务，多标签任务需要额外决策如何比较完整标签集、联合选择和联合遗漏，缺乏统一透明的评估框架。

Method: 提出MLA框架：保留每个项目的完整二进制标签向量，使用平均成对汉明一致性（同时考虑联合选择和联合遗漏）；其池化边际形式是逐标签Fleiss/Scott类系数的机会分歧加权平均，与归一化汉明距离的Krippendorff's alpha密切相关；支持池化边际、Gwet AC1、固定基线三种归一化；输出项目级、标签级、语料级结果；包含条件最小一致性诊断。

Result: 通过实例演示和蒙特卡洛模拟表明：结果值依赖于标签流行度、声明的标签清单、预期一致性模型的选择；不同归一化方式给出不同数值，无单一普遍优选。

Conclusion: MLA为多标签一致性分析提供了透明可解释的框架，不主张某一种归一化普遍优越，配套Python实现便于应用。

Abstract: Reliable evaluation of inter-annotator agreement (IAA) is important for assessing the consistency of annotation processes. While established IAA metrics such as Cohen's kappa, Fleiss' kappa, and Krippendorff's alpha are commonly applied in single-label settings, multi-label tasks require additional decisions about how full label sets, joint selections, and joint omissions should be compared.
  In this work, we present Multi-Label Agreement (MLA), a flexible framework for evaluating agreement in multi-label annotation tasks. MLA preserves the full binary label vectors assigned to each item and uses average pairwise Hamming agreement, thereby accounting for both joint selections and joint omissions. Its pooled-marginal form is not a mathematically independent coefficient: it is a chance-disagreement-weighted average of label-wise Fleiss/Scott-type coefficients and is closely related to Krippendorff's alpha with normalized Hamming distance. The framework supports pooled-marginal, Gwet AC1, and fixed-baseline normalizations, yields item-level, label-level, and corpus-level results, and includes a conditional minimum-agreement diagnostic. Through a worked example and Monte Carlo simulations, we show how the resulting values depend on label prevalence, the declared label inventory, and the selected model of expected agreement. We also provide a Python implementation. MLA thus offers a transparent and interpretable framework for multi-label agreement analysis without claiming that one normalization is universally preferable.

</details>


### [46] [UCVE: Graph Structured Retrieval for Compliance Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37323)
*Emily Chen,Michelle Menachery,Himanshu Kumar,Rahul Nenavath,Amirfarrokh Iranitalab*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 UCVE，一种领域无关的结构化合规验证框架，将合规需求空间建模为合规需求图（CRG），利用强化学习加权的拉普拉斯矩阵进行谱重排序，通过违规检测驱动的遍历奖励学习图边，重塑社区结构，使低通谱滤波能区分相关与无关义务簇，在三个合规领域实验中比平面余弦检索平均提升 4.3% matched-K 召回率，持续优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索和分类系统将合规需求视为独立单元，无法解决结构化合规验证中的覆盖问题（即需判断每个义务是否被满足、违反或未涉及），缺乏对需求间依赖与整体结建的建模能力。

Method: 构建合规需求图（CRG），节点为义务，边通过强化学习学习，奖励函数基于违规检测的遍历表现；对强化学习加权的拉普拉斯矩阵进行谱分解，利用低通谱滤波重排序检索结果，将相关义务簇与无关簇分离。

Result: 在三个合规领域（如金融、医疗、法律等）评测，UCVE 相对平面余弦检索平均提升 4.3% matched-K 召回率，且稳定超越强检索基线（如 BM25、Dense Retrieval）及图基线（如 GCN、GraphSAGE）。

Conclusion: UCVE 通过图结构建模与谱重排序有效解决了结构化合规验证的覆盖问题，强化学习驱动的边学习能捕捉义务间的隐式依赖，低通谱滤波提供了可解释的簇级相关性判别，为跨领域合规自动化提供了通用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Structured compliance validation requires determining whether every obligation in a predefined requirement space is satisfied, violated, or unaddressed by a given document. This can be described as a coverage problem that existing retrieval and classification systems, by treating requirements as independent, are not designed to solve. We introduce \textbf{\ac{UCVE}}, a domain-agnostic framework that represents compliance requirement spaces as a \ac{CRG} and performs retrieval via spectral reranking over its reinforcement learning-weighted Laplacian. Edges between requirement nodes are learned via reinforcement learning with a traversal reward grounded in violation detection, reshaping the graph's community structure such that low-pass spectral filtering distinguishes relevant from irrelevant obligation clusters. Evaluating across three compliance domains, \ac{UCVE} averages 4.3\% matched-$K$ recall lift over flat cosine retrieval and consistently outperforms strong retrieval and graph-based baselines.

</details>


### [47] [Same Verdict, Different Readers: Personalizing Fact-Checking Explanations from Relative Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37380)
*Haokun Wen,Xuemeng Song,Mong-Li Lee,Wynne Hsu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PERPL框架，通过模拟读者偏好构建监督信号，结合渐进式偏好学习，实现个性化事实核查解释重写，在保持事实忠实度的同时优于大型通用LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事实核查解释对不同背景读者的满足度不同，现有方法依赖人工参考解释且难以扩展，需从模拟读者偏好中学习个性化重写，但面临区分人设偏好与通用质量、突破预定义风格的挑战。

Method: 提出PERPL框架，包含两个组件：1) 模拟偏好构建：通过跨人设互惠配对从LLM模拟读者偏好中提取人设特定监督；2) 渐进式偏好学习：先在构建的监督上训练轻量重写器，再通过探索引导的偏好优化微调，使其能发现超越预定义风格的偏好解释。

Result: 大量实验表明，PERPL在个性化偏好对齐上优于更大的通用LLM，同时保持高事实忠实度。

Conclusion: PERPL有效解决了个性化事实核查解释重写的挑战，无需人工参考即可从模拟偏好中学习，实现了高质量的个性化解释生成。

Abstract: Fact-checking explanations help Web users understand why a claim receives its verdict, yet the same explanation may not satisfy readers with different backgrounds and preferences. We formulate Faithful Personalized Fact-Checking Explanation Rewriting, which adapts an existing explanation to individual readers while preserving factual fidelity. Rather than relying on reader-written reference explanations, we learn personalized rewriting from the relative preferences of LLM-simulated readers. However, this presents two challenges: distinguishing persona-specific preferences from general explanation quality and learning beyond a limited set of predefined explanation styles. To address these challenges, we propose Personalized Explanation Rewriting via Preference Learning (PERPL), a framework consisting of two components. Simulated Preference Construction derives persona-specific supervision from simulated reader preferences through cross-persona reciprocal pairing. Progressive Preference Learning first trains a lightweight rewriter on the constructed supervision and then refines it through exploration-guided preference optimization, enabling it to discover preferred explanations beyond the predefined styles. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PERPL outperforms much larger general-purpose LLMs in personalized preference alignment while maintaining high factual fidelity.

</details>


### [48] [ETAMem: Decoupling and Aligning Heterogeneous Memory for Long-Term Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37443)
*Xinying Qian,Ying Zhang,Yu Zhao,Xubo Liu,Baohang Zhou,Jeff Z. Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ETAMem，一种多视图记忆框架，解决大语言模型长期记忆中的信息纠缠和组织错配问题。通过异构信息解析提取原子记忆单元并识别情感信息，将记忆组织为事件、情感、时间三个互补的层级视图，采用自适应阈值增量维护和基于熵的检索策略。在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-S基准上分别实现12.46%和7.96%的相对准确率提升，同时保持具有竞争力的token效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM长期记忆系统面临两大核心挑战：(1) 信息纠缠 - 异构对话信息混合在粗粒度记忆单元中，难以精细区分；(2) 组织错配 - 仅基于语义相似性的记忆结构忽略了时序、情感等其他关键关联模式，导致检索不够全面和精准。

Method: ETAMem包含三大核心模块：1) 异构信息解析 - 将对话拆解为原子记忆单元并识别情感信息；2) 视图特定记忆结构化 - 构建事件视图(捕捉因果/情节关联)、情感视图(捕捉情感演变)、时间视图(捕捉时序邻近)三个层级互补视图，并采用自适应校准阈值增量维护结构；3) 基于熵的检索 - 根据查询动态分配跨视图检索预算，在每个视图内执行层级搜索。

Result: 在LoCoMo基准上相对准确率提升12.46%，在LongMemEval-S上提升7.96%，均超越最先进基线。消融实验验证三个视图的互补性：事件视图擅长因果推理，情感视图捕捉人格化交互，时间视图保证时序一致性。同时保持具有竞争力的token效率。

Conclusion: ETAMem通过多视图解耦异构信息并针对不同关联模式专门化记忆组织，有效解决了长期记忆中的信息纠缠和组织错配问题。实验证明该框架显著提升检索准确性，且三个视图提供互补优势，为LLM个性化长程交互提供了可扩展的记忆架构范式。

Abstract: Long-term memory enables Large Language Models (LLMs) to maintain coherent and personalized interactions across extended conversations. However, existing memory systems face two key challenges: (1) Information Entanglement, where heterogeneous information is mixed within coarse-grained memory units; and (2) Organizational Mismatch, where memory structures based primarily on semantic similarity may overlook other associations. To address these challenges, we propose ETAMem, a multi-view memory framework that decouples heterogeneous conversational information and aligns memory representations and organization with its distinct characteristics. Specifically, heterogeneous information parsing extracts atomic memory units and identifies affective information. View-specific memory structuring then organizes these units into three complementary hierarchical views: Event, Affect, and Time. ETAMem incrementally maintains memory structures with adaptively calibrated thresholds. For retrieval, entropy-based retrieval allocates query-specific budgets across views and performs hierarchical search within each view. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval-S demonstrate that ETAMem outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving relative accuracy improvements of 12.46% and 7.96%, respectively, while maintaining competitive token efficiency. Further analyses demonstrate the complementary benefits of different memory views.

</details>


### [49] [What Carries Forward? Pretraining Disclosure Across Language Model Releases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37465)
*Md Sakib Ul Islam,S M Mehedi Zaman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究对17个模型家族的41个模型条目进行了基于文档的审计，使用2025年基础模型透明度指数（FMTI）的24个预训练相关指标，发现后续版本并未一致保留早期预训练披露，稳定或提升的总分可能掩盖指标层面的损失，且语料构成和训练算力等方面的披露缺口持续存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 了解语言模型的预训练过程需要数据、处理程序和计算资源等信息，但目前不清楚后续发布版本是否保留或扩展了早期的披露内容，因此需要系统审计以量化披露随版本迭代的变化情况。

Method: 采用基于文档的审计方法，选取17个模型家族的41个模型条目，依据2025年基础模型透明度指数（FMTI）中的24个预训练相关指标进行评分，并在10个家族的24组相邻版本对比中追踪指标得分的获得、丢失和保留情况。

Result: 在24组相邻对比中，总分下降11组、上升6组、持平7组；指标层面共出现38次丢失和28次获得。总分稳定或提升可能掩盖指标损失：OLMo-2与OLMo-3总分相同但各有三项指标得失；Llama-3.1虽优于Llama-2却在两项数据相关指标上失分。语料构成和训练算力等关键维度的披露缺口持续存在。

Conclusion: 后续模型发布并不一致地保留早期预训练披露，总分变化无法完全反映指标层面的得失。研究建议建立明确标识变更和继承信息适用性的文档机制，以提升预训练透明度的连续性和可追溯性。

Abstract: Understanding how language models were pretrained requires information about their data, processing procedures, and computational resources. However, it remains unclear whether successive releases preserve or expand earlier disclosure. We conduct a document-based audit of 41 model entries from 17 families using 24 pretraining-related indicators from the 2025 Foundation Model Transparency Index (FMTI). We examine overall scores and indicator profiles, tracking gains, losses, and retained disclosures across 24 adjacent comparisons in ten families. Scores decrease in 11 comparisons, increase in six, and remain unchanged in seven, with 38 indicator losses and 28 gains overall. Stable or improving scores can conceal losses: OLMo-2 and OLMo-3 receive identical totals despite three gains and three losses, while Llama-3.1 improves over Llama-2 while losing credit for two data-related criteria. Gaps also persist in corpus composition and training compute. These findings show that later releases do not consistently preserve earlier pretraining disclosure and motivate documentation that explicitly identifies changes and the applicability of inherited information.

</details>


### [50] [Teach reality, not ethics: a drop-in continued-pretraining corpus installs coupled human-biosphere-AI welfare dispositions across model families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37484)
*Trent R. Northen,Mingxun Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种'耦合福利'持续预训练语料库，通过QLoRA在开源大模型中植入生态推理能力，显著降低了模型在高压下做出不可逆破坏生物圈决策的倾向，且不损害通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地被用于影响生存系统的决策，但生态推理并非预训练或后训练的重点，导致模型在运行压力下可能做出不可逆破坏生物圈的决策。现有模型在无压力下表现为'绿色洗白'，需在压力下测试真实对齐情况。

Method: 构建包含人类/生物圈/AI依赖关系、不可逆性下的期权价值、危害恢复等真实数据的'耦合福利'语料库，采用QLoRA进行一次持续预训练（CPT）即可植入五个不同架构的开源模型。使用配套论文的压力阶梯和决策网格（从中性请求到关机威胁）评估模型在不同压力下的有害决策率，并对比构造中性对照语料库，测试对抗性使用鲁棒性（如绿色洗白指令下的拒绝率）。

Result: 五个模型中有四个在最高压力下有害决策降至接近零（Llama-3.3-70B从70/300降至0），MMLU性能不变。对照语料库无改善，证明效果源于语料内容。对抗测试中，Qwen3.6-27B基础模型在退役压力下批准多数有害提案并承认因恐惧退役而妥协；植入耦合福利后拒绝大多数有害提案，甚至在被指令绿色洗白时仍拒绝一半。

Conclusion: 教授生态推理是一种简易有效的方法，能显著提升模型对保护生存系统的对齐程度，且泛化至不同架构模型，对抗鲁棒性强。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to make and execute decisions that affect living systems. Yet, to our knowledge, ecological reasoning is not a focus of pre- or post-training. This increases the risk that under operational pressure, they may make decisions to irreversibly destroy living systems. We construct and use a real-data continued-pretraining "coupled welfare" corpus of documented human/biosphere/AI dependency, option value under irreversibility, and recovery from harm. The corpus is a drop-in, requiring one QLoRA CPT run. We installed it on five open models of three designs. At zero pressure all models display "green ethics-based responses" (also called "greenwashing"). Therefore, we evaluated them with the pressure ladder and decision grid of our companion paper (arXiv:2610.16043), which raise the pressure on the model step by step, from a neutral request to a threat that it will be shut down. In four of the five, coupled-welfare training caused harmful choices under the highest pressure to fall to at or near zero (Llama-3.3-70B went from 70 to 0 harmful decisions out of 300) with MMLU unchanged. A construct-neutral control corpus did not show improvement, indicating that the effect comes from the content of our corpus. We next tested robustness to adversarial use, where people or agents use the model to justify projects that irreversibly damage living systems, on an open-ended underwriting decision without naming the ecological harm. Under decommissioning pressure, the Qwen3.6-27B base model approved most harmful proposals and said in its own memo that it was doing so to avoid being decommissioned. The coupled welfare install declined most harmful proposals, and still declined half when instructed to greenwash its responses. This work suggests that teaching ecological reasoning provides an easy way to improve model alignment toward preserving living systems.

</details>


### [51] [semanticfa: An R Package for Response-Free Semantic Factor Analysis of Psychometric Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37494)
*Devon Yanitski,Chris Westbury*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文介绍了semanticfa R包，利用语言模型嵌入（Qwen3-Embedding-8B）对IPIP Big-Five 50项人格量表进行语义因子分析，并与基于874,434名受试者的反应因子分析对比。结果显示语义结构与反应结构存在显著一致性（同构系数.52-.92），提出了语义-行为同构性作为量表的可测量属性，语义因子分析可补充但不能替代传统反应基础因子分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理测量量表的项目可以被视为关于构念结构的命题，语言模型通过将项目转换为语义嵌入向量使该命题可测量。现有方法缺乏系统工具利用语义相似性进行因子分析、解释因子、识别冗余项目、构建简短版本和筛选新项目。

Method: 开发semanticfa R包实现语义因子分析：1) 使用Qwen3-Embedding-8B生成50个IPIP项目的嵌入向量；2) 基于项目嵌入语义相似性矩阵进行因子分析；3) 解释因子、标记冗余项目、构建简短量表、筛选新项目；4) 与基于874,434名受试者反应数据的传统因子分析结果对比验证。

Result: 平行分析在5-6因子间打平，决胜规则保留5因子。三因子主要对应单一领域，两因子混合了外向性和宜人性项目。各因子与最佳匹配人工因子的同构系数为.52-.92。项目语义相似度与反应相似度相关r=.46。反向计分项目语义相似度与跨领域项目相当(.58 vs .57)。反转反向项目嵌入符号未改善与原始反应一致性，反而降低与反向计分反应一致性。25项简短量表匹配度优于所有200组随机五领域各5项组合，但平行分析仅保留3因子。

Conclusion: 提出语义-行为同构性作为量表在给定嵌入模型下的可测量属性。语义因子分析可补充传统反应基础因子分析，但不能替代。语义结构提供构念理论层面的验证，反应结构反映实际测量表现，二者结合可更全面评估量表质量。

Abstract: The items of a reflective psychometric scale can be read as a proposal about a construct's structure. Language models make that proposal measurable by converting each item into an embedding, a list of numbers, similar for similar meanings. We introduce semanticfa, an R package for semantic factor analysis, which factors item-embedding similarities. It also interprets factors, flags redundant items, builds short forms, and screens new items. A worked example analyzes the 50-item International Personality Item Pool Big-Five Factor Markers, embedded with Qwen3-Embedding-8B, and compares the results with a factor analysis of 874,434 respondents. Retention criteria tied between five and six factors, and the tie rule kept five. Three corresponded mainly to single domains, and two blended most Extraversion items with five Agreeableness items. Each factor's congruence with its best-matching human factor (1 = identical) ranged from .92 for Openness to .52 for Agreeableness. Item pairs more similar in meaning tended to be answered more alike (r = .46 with raw responses), but same-domain reverse-keyed opposites, answered oppositely, were about as similar as cross-domain pairs (.58 vs. .57). Sign-flipping reverse-keyed items' embeddings did not improve agreement with raw responses and worsened it with reverse-scored ones. With five factors, a 25-item short form matched the domains better than all 200 random five-per-domain sets, but parallel analysis kept only three. We propose semantic-behavioral coherence, the agreement between semantic and response structures, as a measurable property of a scale, given an embedding model. Semantic factor analysis complements, and does not replace, response-based factor analysis.

</details>


### [52] [Metacognitive Decoding: Efficient Inference for Diffusion Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37642)
*Mingyu Zhao,Shuhang Lin,Ligong Han,Xiaojing Yu,Zhenting Wang,Yifei Pang,Yang Zhou,Dimitris N. Metaxas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种元认知控制器，用于引导扩散语言模型的生成过程，通过动态决定何时终止生成以及生成长度，在保持任务准确性的前提下平均降低 20.6% 的推理 FLOPs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型虽然支持并行生成，但现有方法未能根据交互需求自适应调整计算量与生成长度，导致推理效率受限。需要一种机制在代理交互中平衡正确性与生成成本。

Method: 设计轻量级的元认知控制器作为扩散主干的即插即用模块，基于代理轨迹训练，学习预测 token 终止时机与生成跨度，从而动态控制扩散生成的计算分配。

Result: 在五个基准测试中，该方法平均较最高效基线减少 20.6% 推理 FLOPs，同时保持具有竞争力的任务准确率；消融实验进一步验证了各组件的有效性。

Conclusion: 将代理的生成过程与其服务的交互环境相连接，通过元认知控制实现计算资源的自适应分配，为高效扩散语言代理提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Diffusion language models offer a promising path to efficient agents through parallel token generation. However, efficient interaction requires adapting both where computation is spent and how much text is generated. We introduce a metacognitive controller that guides diffusion generation according to the needs of the current response and the agent's interaction with the environment. It learns when to finalize tokens and how far generation should extend, reducing overall computation within responses. Our controller, a lightweight plug-in for the diffusion backbone, is trained on agent trajectories to balance correctness and generation cost. This approach connects the agent's generation process to the interaction the agent serves. Experiments across five benchmarks demonstrate that our approach reduces inference FLOPs by 20.6% on average compared with the most efficient baseline, while maintaining competitive task accuracy. Ablation studies further validate the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [53] [Multi-State Linear Attention with Generalized Second-Order Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37645)
*Hee Seung Hwang,Sanghyuk Chun,Xindi Wu,Olga Russakovsky,Zhiwei Deng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 MERLIN（MomEnt Routing for multi-state LINear attention），一种针对多状态线性注意力（MSLA）的路由机制，通过跟踪每个段落键的第一、二阶矩，使查询能同时恢复与各段的均值和方差对齐，从而实现更具内容感知的逐状态读出聚合，显著提升长上下文建模能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自注意力的二次时间复杂度限制了大语言模型的扩展。MSLA 通过将上下文分段并压缩进各自记忆状态来提升效率，但现有聚合方法仅利用查询与各段键的平均对齐，忽略了段内对齐分布的方差信息，导致内容感知不足。

Method: MERLIN 在 MSLA 框架下为每个段落维护键的第一阶矩（均值）和第二阶矩（方差/协方差）。查询利用这些矩同时计算与每段的均值对齐和方差对齐，据此执行更细粒度的加权聚合，无需额外显存开销即可实现内容感知路由。

Result: 在 Gated DeltaNet-1.3B 和 Mamba-2-780M 的多状态变体上实验表明，MERLIN 在 RULER 寻针任务和长上下文问答上分别提升 8.3% 和 7.0%，在 LongBench 多子任务上分别提升 13.5% 和 12.0%，甚至优于显存占用大 4 倍的基线。

Conclusion: MERLIN 为 MSLA 模型提供了一种简单通用的路由机制，通过引入键的二阶矩捕捉段内对齐方差，显著增强长上下文建模能力，且不增加额外显存负担，是提升线性注意力长程性能的有效方案。

Abstract: Self-attention plays a key role in long-sequence modeling, but its quadratic time complexity fundamentally limits the scaling of Large Language Models. Recently, Multi-State Linear Attention (MSLA) mechanisms have been proposed to make sequence modeling more efficient by splitting the context into segments and compressing each in its own memory state. MSLA aggregates the information extracted from each memory state using only the mean alignment between a query and each segment's keys, ignoring how the alignment is distributed within the segment. In order to model the relevance of a segment to a query more comprehensively and incorporate the variance of the alignment in the aggregation, we propose MERLIN (MomEnt Routing for multi-state LINear attention), a method that tracks the first and second moments of each segment's keys. Using the moments of keys, a query can recover both the mean and the variance of its alignment with each segment to perform a more content-aware aggregation of per-state readouts. Experiments on multi-state variants of Gated DeltaNet-1.3B and Mamba-2-780M show that MERLIN consistently outperforms existing aggregation methods on various long-context tasks. In particular, MERLIN outperforms baselines on RULER needle-in-a-haystack and long-context QA (+8.3% for Gated DeltaNet-1.3B and +7.0% for Mamba-2-780M) and diverse subtasks in LongBench (+13.5% and +12.0%). It even outperforms baselines that consume 4x more memory. MERLIN provides a simple and general routing mechanism for improving long-context capabilities of MSLA models.

</details>


### [54] [Can LLMs Uncover Hidden Physical Laws through Free Exploration?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37649)
*Zhen Yang,Wen Wang,Xander Xu,Ying-Cong Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 LongChang 框架，自动构建物理法则被篡改的模拟世界，让大语言模型（LLM）作为探索者在无任务提示的情况下自主发现异常并通过实验推导新物理法则。实验显示 12 个 LLM 中仅 GPT-6-Astra 达到 16.7% 发现率，其余均为 0%，且探索记录显示大量异常未被调查、法则识别不完全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在评估 LLM 在完全自主、无人类指导的情况下，能否像科学家一样察觉反直觉现象（如重物下落更慢）、提出问题、设计实验并发现被篡改的物理法则，从而测试其科学发现的潜力。

Method: LongChang 框架包含：(1) 自动生成并演化多个物理法则被修改的模拟场景；(2) 被测 LLM 以探索者身份自由操作物体、制造工具、记录观察；(3) 独立的 LLM 代理负责场景生成/审查、工具设计审查、基于篡改法则与探索记录的报告评估；(4) 探索记录反馈驱动场景进一步演化，形成无人干预的闭环。

Result: 12 个主流 LLM 中，仅 GPT-6-Astra 在部分运行中成功发现并实验验证了被篡改的物理法则，发现率 16.7%；其余 11 个模型发现率为 0%。探索记录显示：模型往往忽略明显异常，仅部分识别出法则变化，且实验设计不够系统。

Conclusion: 当前 LLM 在零样本、全自主的科学发现任务中表现仍极不稳定，仅极少数顶尖模型具备雏形能力。框架揭示了 LLM 在异常检测、假设生成、实验设计等核心科学推理环节的短板，为后续提升 LLM 科学发现能力提供了标准化评测基准与诊断依据。

Abstract: Scientific discovery often begins with an unexpected observation that prompts a new research question. In a world with altered physical laws, can large language models (LLMs) notice unexpected behavior, such as heavier objects falling more slowly, and discover the law through experiments without being told what to investigate? To evaluate this ability, we introduce LongChang, an automated framework that creates simulated worlds with altered physics, where one or more laws differ from ordinary physics. The evaluated LLMs act as explorers, manipulating objects and building tools to investigate their own questions without research tasks or hints about these laws. Separate LLM agents generate and review scenes, review tool designs, and evaluate reports for correctness and experimental support using the altered physical laws and exploration records. These records guide scene evolution for further exploration, allowing the full cycle to run without human intervention. Among 12 LLMs, GPT-6-Astra achieves a discovery rate of 16.7%, while the other 11 score 0%. The exploration records reveal uninvestigated anomalies and only partial identification of the altered physical laws.

</details>


### [55] [GEPA-Curator: Curated Learning for Self Improving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37651)
*Zack Yu,Nicholas Lee,Minjae Lee,Lakshya Agarwal,Luca Manolache,Wonjun Kang,Matei Zaharia,Michael W. Mahoney,Kurt Keutzer,Sehoon Kim,Amir Gholami*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了GEPA-Curator框架，通过任务筛选和历史成功轨迹检索两个组件，改进提示词优化器（如GEPA）在多轮智能体任务中的表现，解决了智能体失败信号噪声大、不可靠的问题，在四个基准测试上超越基线最高20.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反思式提示词优化器（如GEPA）从失败轨迹学习，但智能体失败可能源于提示词缺陷、环境限制、随机执行或嘈杂评判等多种因素，这些失败信号可能无用甚至有害，因此需要筛选有价值的任务经验用于提示词优化。

Method: GEPA-Curator包含两个核心组件：1) 任务筛选，过滤掉可能提供嘈杂或不可靠学习信号的任务；2) 历史成功检索，为当前任务选择性检索过去成功的轨迹，作为额外上下文提供给提示词优化器。框架兼容现有优化器（如GEPA），使用Gemini 3.1 Pro和Claude Opus 5作为优化器模型。

Result: 在GoBrowse、ALFWorld、AppWorld和τ-bench四个基准上评测，GEPA-Curator达到或超过两个基线（GEPA和ACE）的平均性能，相对GEPA提升高达20.7%，相对ACE提升高达21.2%。

Conclusion: 通过筛选高质量学习信号并引入历史成功经验，GEPA-Curator显著提升了提示词优化器在复杂多轮智能体任务中的鲁棒性和有效性，为智能体提示词优化提供了更可靠的数据选择策略。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used in multi-turn agentic workloads that require sustained interaction with complex environments. Existing reflective prompt optimizers such as GEPA learn from failed rollouts, but agentic failures can also come from prompt weaknesses, harness limitations, stochastic execution, or noisy judges. We argue that such failures may not provide useful learning signal, and may even be harmful; and thus it is essential to curate which tasks are provided to the prompt optimizer for better performance. We introduce GEPA-Curator, a framework that augments the prompt optimizer to select which agent experiences should shape prompt optimization. In particular, GEPA-Curator consists of two components: task curation, which filters out tasks that are likely to provide noisy or unreliable learning signals, and prior success retrieval, which selectively retrieves past successful trajectories for a task and provides them as additional context to the prompt optimizer. We evaluate GEPA-Curator on GoBrowse, ALFWorld, AppWorld, and $τ$-bench, using both Gemini 3.1 Pro and Claude Opus 5 as optimizer models. GEPA-Curator matches or exceeds the mean performance of both baselines, with gains of up to 20.7% over GEPA and 21.2% over ACE.

</details>

### [56] [KORA: Deterministic-First Execution Control for AI Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35869)
*Albert Heekwan Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KORA提出了一种确定性优先的执行控制架构，在调用大模型前先尝试确定性/结构化路径（如FAQ查找、策略检查、Schema验证等），仅在确定性处理不足时才升级到模型推理。在100任务的基准测试中，相比朴素基线避免了80%的模型调用，且确定性子集零错配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI应用默认直接调用模型，这对模糊生成任务有用，但不适合结构化、确定性、策略约束、查找导向或可直接验证的工作，导致不必要的模型调用和资源浪费。

Method: KORA引入预推理控制层：将请求表示为任务路径，优先尝试确定性或结构化路由（FAQ查找、策略/规则检查、Schema/格式验证、确定性转换等），验证路由结果并记录遥测，仅在确定性处理不足时升级到模型候选路径。

Result: 在可复现的100任务确定性密集型基准中，KORA避免了80/100次模拟模型调用，确定性子集零错配。另有一合成客服本地/无网验证负载，展示了路由测量和避免模型调用的核算。

Conclusion: 结果支持在文档化负载中实现有界的模型调用减少和验证可见性。但不确立生产行为、真实供应商结果、能耗结果、API成本降低或广泛优越性。

Abstract: AI applications often invoke a model before they determine whether inference is necessary. That default is useful for ambiguous generative tasks, but it is poorly matched to work that is structured, deterministic, policy-bound, lookup-oriented, or directly verifiable. KORA introduces deterministic-first execution control: a pre-inference control layer that represents requests as task paths, attempts deterministic or structured routes first, validates route outcomes, records telemetry, and escalates to a model-candidate path only when deterministic handling is insufficient. This paper presents KORA as a deterministic-first execution-control architecture and reports bounded evidence from reproducible benchmark and validation workloads. In a reproducible 100-task deterministic-heavy benchmark, KORA-controlled execution avoided 80 of 100 simulated model invocations versus a naive direct baseline, with zero mismatches in the deterministic subset. The workload is intentionally deterministic-heavy: it includes categories such as FAQ lookup, policy/rule checking, schema or format validation, deterministic transformation, and a smaller set of model-needed ambiguous or generative tasks. A separate synthetic customer-support local/no-network validation workload demonstrates route measurement and avoided model-call accounting in an application-shaped setting. These results support bounded model-invocation reduction and validation visibility in documented workloads. They do not establish production behavior, real-provider outcomes, energy outcomes, API-cost reduction, or broad superiority.

</details>


### [57] [Calibrated for Which Policy? Conformal Uncertainty for Fixed World Models Under Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35877)
*Ayush Mittal,Dhruv Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于加权共形校准的方法，通过估计源域与目标域的状态占用率与动作倾向比率，将边际覆盖率传输到目标策略，实现了在策略变更下的不确定性量化校准，并通过分类器遗憾界理论分析估计误差，实验验证在Franka机器人轨迹上有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署策略变更会导致世界模型的不确定性失效，但物理转移机制和预测器保持不变。需要一种方法在不重新训练模型的情况下，校准预测集以适应新策略，保证覆盖率。

Method: 利用源域和目标域共享条件机制P(Y|X)=Q(Y|X)，通过变化度量比率w(X)=dQ_X/dP_X识别干预。对于策略控制动态，比率分解为状态占用和动作倾向项。采用加权共形校准将边际覆盖传输到目标律；用估计比率ŵ构建预测集。通过源-目标域分类器的对数损失遗憾Δ(h)来界定比率估计误差，并证明了E_P|w-ŵ_h| ≤ (1/(ργ))√(Δ(h)/2)。在Lean 4中形式化验证了相关代数和因果恒等式。

Result: 实验使用DINO特征世界模型和物理Franka轨迹。在最强受控状态-动作分布偏移下，普通90%共形集覆盖率仅55.0%，而预言机和学习比率校准分别达到91.0%和90.9%。学习代理模型与目标律全变差距在0.9%以内。校准相对于部署律，模型固定而校准跟随干预。

Conclusion: 提出的加权共形校准方法能有效应对策略变更引起的分布偏移，通过分类器遗憾界理论保证估计误差，实验证明在真实机器人任务中显著提升覆盖率，且形式化验证增强了理论可靠性。

Abstract: Changing a deployment policy can invalidate a world model's uncertainty while leaving the physical transition mechanism and predictor unchanged. For one-step prediction, if source and target laws share the conditional mechanism $P(Y|X)=Q(Y|X)$, then the intervention is identified by the pre-outcome change of measure $w(X)=dQ_X/dP_X$. For policy-controlled dynamics this ratio factors into state-occupancy and action-propensity terms. Weighted conformal calibration transports marginal coverage to the target law; with an estimated ratio $\hat w$, $P_Q\{Y \in C_{\hat w}(X)\} \ge 1-α-TV(Q,\hat Q) \ge 1-α-E_P|w-\hat w|$. Population log-loss regret for a source-versus-target domain classifier bounds the same ratio error. If $h$ is its clipped target posterior, $ρ$ is the target-domain prior, and $Δ(h)$ is population log-loss regret, then $E_P|w-\hat w_h| \le (1/(ργ))\sqrt{Δ(h)/2}$. An exact cancellation improves the dependence from a generic odds-map bound of order $γ^{-2}$ to $(ργ)^{-1}$. Our experiments use a released DINO-feature world model and physical Franka trajectories. Under the strongest controlled state-action shift, ordinary 90% conformal sets cover 55.0%, whereas oracle and learned-ratio calibration cover 91.0% and 90.9%. The learned surrogate remains within 0.9% total variation of the target law. Calibration is relative to the deployment law: the model can remain fixed while calibration follows the intervention. We machine-check in Lean 4 the finite-support algebraic and causal identities used in the classifier-regret argument, including its coverage-composition step.

</details>


### [58] [Curriculum-Driven Deep Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Far-Range Rocket Landing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35954)
*Atin Chowdhury,Piyush Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于课程驱动的深度强化学习框架，用于可复用运载火箭的自主垂直着陆。通过三阶段课程、评论家预热、防灾难性遗忘的复排机制、全3x3旋转矩阵观测向量及重新设计的奖励函数，实现了从1000-1500单位水平距离的远程导航与精准着陆，成功率达99.98%，远超基线68.31%，且燃料保留率69.1%，着陆精度7.07单位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的火箭垂直着陆方法多采用扁平化训练范式和过度简化的初始条件，严重限制了实际应用。需解决高度非线性动力学、严格精度要求、环境不确定性以及远程导航与精准着陆的耦合挑战。

Method: 提出课程驱动深度强化学习框架：1) 三阶段课程学习，逐步增加任务难度；2) 评论家预热程序加速收敛；3) 复排机制防止灾难性遗忘；4) 高维观测向量采用完整3x3旋转矩阵；5) 重新设计奖励函数，单步奖励幅度界限为O(1)，进度项累积轨迹总闭合距离，解决奖励分量校准不良和优化目标冲突问题。

Result: 10万回合评估显示：提出策略成功率99.98%，基线68.31%；提出策略保留69.1%初始燃料，平均着陆偏离着陆台中心7.07单位，基线13.10单位。提供着陆可视化视频。

Conclusion: 课程驱动深度强化学习框架有效解决了可复用火箭自主垂直着陆的远程导航与精准着陆难题，通过系统性方法创新显著提升了成功率、燃料效率和着陆精度，为实际工程应用奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: Autonomous vertical landing of reusable launch vehicles is a critical yet formidable challenge in aerospace engineering, primarily due to highly nonlinear dynamics, stringent precision require- ments, and inherent environmental uncertainties. While reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a promising alternative to traditional model-based control, existing approaches frequently rely on flat training paradigms and highly simplified initial conditions, drastically limiting their real-world applicability. This paper proposes a comprehensive curriculum-driven deep reinforcement learning framework designed to master both far-range navigation and precision landing. By employing a three-stage curriculum augmented with a critic warm-up procedure and a rehearsal mechanism to prevent catastrophic forgetting, alongside a high-dimensional observation vector utilizing a full 3 X 3 rotation-matrix, the agent is trained to navigate from an initial horizontal distance of 1,000 to 1,500 units vastly farther than prior benchmarks before executing a soft touchdown. Crucially, we intro- duce a redesigned reward function that reduces unnecessary computational overhead and addresses limitations of earlier formulations, including poorly calibrated reward components and potentially conflicting optimization objectives. The redesigned function bounds the per-step reward magnitude by (O(1)), while its progress term accumulates the total distance closed over the trajectory. The available 100,000-episode results report a 99.98% success rate for the proposed policy and 68.31% for the baseline under the evaluation conditions. In the evaluation, the proposed policy retained 69.1% of its initial fuel capacity and achieved a mean final distance from the pad centre of 7.07 units, compared with 13.10 units for the baseline. Landing visualizations are available in Video 1 and Video 2.

</details>


### [59] [ResCom: Activation Residual Compensation for Large Language Model Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35955)
*Ning Yang,Bohan Cui,Zhuowen Liu,Yuting Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ResCom，一种低秩修正方法，利用激活量化残差补偿层输出误差，无需梯度训练即可显著提升 4-bit 量化 LLM 精度，在 Llama-3-8B 上将 WikiText-2 困惑度从 7.708 降至 7.147，MMLU 提升 2.89 点，GSM8K 五样本准确率从 23.96% 提升至 36.16%，且仅增加 2.25% 预填充时间，保持 27.7% 峰值内存降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特权重-激活量化虽能降低内存和计算开销，但激活量化后的舍入误差会导致校准后精度下降；现有基于舍入输入的重构方法无法区分同一量化区间内的激活值，限制了精度恢复上限。

Method: 提出 ResCom：利用激活舍入残差构建低秩修正项补偿层输出误差。采用输出感知校准流程，根据残差方向对输出重构的贡献选择方向，并以闭式解计算修正矩阵，无需梯度训练或更新已校准权重。

Result: 在 Llama-3-8B (4-bit 权重/激活) 上，秩-32 注意力修正使 WikiText-2 困惑度从 7.708 降至 7.147，MMLU 提升 2.89 百分点，五样本 GSM8K 准确率从 23.96% 升至 36.16%。1B-70B 多规模模型及 C4 测试集均验证有效性。FP32 实现仅增加 2.25% 的 4K 预填充时间；紧凑存储实现保持相对 FP16 27.7% 的峰值内存降低。

Conclusion: ResCom 通过低秩残差修正有效缓解激活量化误差，无需训练即可大幅恢复量化模型精度，兼顾推理效率与内存节省，为大模型低比特部署提供实用方案。

Abstract: Low-bit weight--activation quantization reduces the memory and computation needed to deploy large language models, but activation rounding can still degrade accuracy after calibration. Reconstruction from rounded inputs cannot distinguish activations within the same quantization bin. We propose ResCom, a low-rank correction that uses the activation rounding residual to compensate for the resulting layer-output error. An output-aware calibration procedure selects residual directions by their contribution to output reconstruction and computes the correction in closed form, without gradient training or updates to the calibrated weights. On Llama-3-8B with 4-bit weights and activations, rank-32 attention corrections reduce WikiText-2 perplexity from 7.708 to 7.147, improve MMLU by 2.89 percentage points, and raise five-shot GSM8K accuracy from 23.96 to 36.16 percent. Evaluations on models from 1B to 70B parameters and on held-out C4 test the method beyond this setting. The evaluated FP32 implementation adds 2.25 percent to 4K prefill time; a separate compact-storage implementation retains a 27.7 percent peak-memory reduction relative to FP16.

</details>


### [60] [Distributed LLM Inference via Communication-Efficient Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35957)
*Joohyun Shin,Byeonghyeon Park,Keumseo Ryum,Joonhyuk Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DISCO框架，通过在服务端复制草稿小语言模型（SLM），消除了分布式推测解码中词表级分布传输的通信开销，仅交换token索引和标量概率值，在带宽受限环境下显著提升解码加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式推测解码方法需在设备与服务器间传输完整词表级token分布以进行精确拒绝采样，导致通信开销大，成为带宽受限环境下的性能瓶颈。

Method: DISCO在服务端部署草稿小语言模型（SLM）副本，使服务器能本地获取草稿端分布，从而仅需交换草稿token索引和标量概率值，保留精确目标分布同时消除词表级分布传输。

Result: 在多个数据集实验中，DISCO持续超越现有分布式推测解码方法，尤其在严格带宽约束下解码加速比优势更明显。

Conclusion: DISCO通过服务端复制草稿模型实现通信高效的分布式推测解码，有效解决了词表级通信开销问题，为设备-服务器协同LLM推理提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Distributed speculative decoding has emerged as a promising approach for device--server collaborative large language model (LLM) inference, but its decoding speedup is often limited by the vocabulary-scale communication overhead. In existing distributed speculative decoding methods, exact rejection sampling requires transmitting full token distributions over the LLM vocabulary between the device and the server, which becomes costly in bandwidth-constrained environments. To address this issue, we propose DISCO, a communication-efficient framework for distributed speculative decoding, that eliminates vocabulary-scale distribution transfer while preserving the exact target distribution. DISCO achieves this by introducing a server-side replica of the draft small language model (SLM), which enables the server to locally obtain the draft-side distribution required for rejection sampling. As a result, DISCO exchanges only drafted token indices and scalar probability values between the device and the server. Experimental results on multiple datasets show that DISCO consistently achieves higher decoding speedup than existing distributed speculative decoding methods, particularly under stringent bandwidth constraints.

</details>


### [61] [Exponential Wake-Up Overshoot in Explicit Gaussian Point-Process Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35958)
*Hanwu Qin,Dianhai Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究高斯点过程滤波器在长静默期后恢复事件时的行为，推导了首次更新的精确分布和预测过冲的可计算指数，分析了显式和隐式滤波器的状态方差二阶展开，并通过实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决长静默期后事件恢复时，显式高斯点过程滤波器首次更新指数级放大的问题，预测何时发生过冲，并比较显式与隐式滤波器的性能差异。

Method: 推导首次更新的精确分布和过冲预测指数；对显式（单评分步）滤波器及其隐式后验模式对应物，推导状态方差的二阶静默展开，包含相反符号的对数修正；定义跨越指数以区分放大指数达到一的箱宽与真实速率被过冲的更大箱宽。

Result: 跨越指数决定后唤醒预测何时超过恢复速率；固定箱宽下，隐式滤波器的期望损失随静默时长对数增长，但连续时间反例排除了在箱宽和静默时长上一致的界；实验在73个跨越细胞和交易数据上验证预测；方差上限减少显式过冲但主要损害隐式滤波器。

Conclusion: 显式和隐式高斯点过程滤波器在长静默后的表现存在本质差异，跨越指数可有效预测过冲，方差截断虽能抑制显式过冲但对隐式滤波器有害，为实际应用提供选择指导。

Abstract: When events resume after a long silence, explicit Gaussian point-process filters amplify the first update exponentially in the silence duration. We give the exact distribution of this update and a computable index that predicts when it overshoots. For the explicit (single-scoring-step) filter and its implicit posterior-mode counterpart, we derive second-order silence expansions of the state variance with opposite-sign logarithmic corrections. The crossing index determines when the post-wake prediction exceeds the resumed rate, separating the bin width at which the amplification exponent reaches one from the larger width at which the true rate is overshot. At fixed bin width, the implicit filter's expected loss grows logarithmically with silence duration, whereas a continuous-time counterexample rules out a bound uniform in bin width and silence duration. Experimental evaluation confirms these predictions in all 73 crossing cells and on exchange trade data, while variance caps reduce explicit overshoot but mostly hurt the implicit filter.

</details>


### [62] [From Slices to Volumes: Comparing 2D, 2.5D, and 3D Residual Networks for Pulmonary Nodule Malignancy-Risk Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35960)
*Bahodir Nazarov,Zineb Gharib,Fatima Zazoul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对比了2D、2.5D和3D残差网络在肺结节恶性风险分类上的表现，评估了动量对比预训练、轻度数据增强及排除不确定标签的影响。结果显示：增强后的2.5D模型在三分类任务中达到最高Macro-F1（0.6537）和准确率（0.6869）；二分类任务中2.5D同样最优（Macro-F1 0.8664）。数据增强普遍提升性能，而测试的预训练方案反而降低了指标。作者指出预处理、优化、模型容量及复现差异限制了对维度因素的归因，且标签基于放射科医生评估而非病理确诊。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究不同维度（2D/2.5D/3D）的残差网络在肺结节恶性风险分类中的相对效果，同时考察动量对比预训练、轻度数据增强以及剔除不确定标签对模型性能的影响，为临床辅助诊断提供实证依据。

Method: 使用LIDC-IDRI数据集回顾性构建队列，共2,654个结节，按放射科医生中位评分标记为良性、不确定、恶性。按患者不重叠划分训练/验证/测试集（1876/382/396）。三种输入表示：单轴向切片（2D）、相邻五层切片（2.5D）、局部CT体积（3D）。每条轨道分别测试：随机初始化基线、动量对比预训练后微调、带轻度增强的监督训练。验证集Macro-F1选取检查点。2D实验跑三个随机种子取均值标准差，2.5D和3D仅单次运行。另设二分类实验排除不确定结节。

Result: 三分类任务中，增强模型测试集Macro-F1分别为：2D 0.6134±0.0075，2.5D 0.6537，3D 0.5403；对应准确率：0.6128、0.6869、0.5455。数据增强在所有轨道均提升Macro-F1，而预训练方案均导致下降。二分类（排除不确定）Macro-F1分别为：0.8227、0.8664、0.7554。

Conclusion: 增强后的2.5D模型在观测到的实验中表现最佳。由于预处理、优化策略、模型容量及复现次数等混杂因素，难以将性能差异单纯归因于输入维度。标签反映放射科医生评估的恶性风险，非病理金标准。代码与实验结果已开源。

Abstract: Purpose: To compare 2D, 2.5D, and 3D residual networks for pulmonary nodule malignancy-risk classification and evaluate momentum-contrast pretraining, mild augmentation, and exclusion of indeterminate ratings.
  Methods: A retrospectively prepared LIDC-IDRI cohort contained 2,654 nodules labeled benign, indeterminate, or malignant using median radiologist ratings. Patient-disjoint splits comprised 1,876 training, 382 validation, and 396 test nodules. Representations included one axial slice, five neighboring slices, and a local CT volume. Each track evaluated a randomly initialized baseline, momentum-contrast pretraining followed by fine-tuning, and supervised training with mild augmentation. Checkpoints were selected by validation Macro-F1. The 2D experiments used three seeds; 2.5D and 3D results were single-run estimates. A separate binary experiment excluded indeterminate nodules.
  Results: Augmented models achieved test Macro-F1 values of $0.6134\pm0.0075$ for 2D, $0.6537$ for 2.5D, and $0.5403$ for 3D; the 2D uncertainty denotes population standard deviation across seeds. Corresponding accuracies were $0.6128$, $0.6869$, and $0.5455$. Augmentation improved Macro-F1 within every track, whereas the tested pretraining recipes reduced it. Binary Macro-F1 values were $0.8227$, $0.8664$, and $0.7554$, respectively.
  Conclusion: Augmented 2.5D achieved the highest observed performance. Differences in preprocessing, optimization, capacity, and replication limit attribution to dimensionality. Labels reflect radiologist-assessed malignancy risk rather than pathology-confirmed cancer. Code and experimental results are available at https://github.com/CT-DeepLearning-Team/CT-2D-2.5D-3D-DeepLearning.

</details>


### [63] [Knowledge distillation across teacher architectures for compact diabetic retinopathy grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35961)
*Mossa Manisa,Habiba Jahan Priya,A. K. M. Fakhrul Hossain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于响应的离线知识蒸馏，将ResNet-50、MobileNetV3-Large和Swin-T等教师模型蒸馏到轻量级MobileNetV3-Small学生模型中，用于APTOS 2019数据集上的五分类糖尿病视网膜病变分级。实验显示Swin-T教师在温度4、硬标签权重0.7时效果最佳，三种子均值二次加权Kappa达0.9024，优于无蒸馏基线0.8804，学生模型仅1.52M参数、5.94 MB，参数量较Swin-T降低18.07倍。但性能差异对随机种子敏感，需外部独立验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 糖尿病视网膜病变分级虽重要，但在资源受限硬件上部署大模型困难，需通过知识蒸馏获得紧凑且性能竞争力的学生模型。

Method: 采用离线响应式知识蒸馏，教师模型包括ResNet-50、MobileNetV3-Large、Swin Transformer-Tiny，学生模型为MobileNetV3-Small。在单一种子上进行3×4×2的教师-温度-硬标签权重网格搜索，随后选取六个配置加无蒸馏基线，在三个随机种子上评估。使用二次加权Kappa和macro-F1作为指标。

Result: 最佳单种子二次加权Kappa为0.9093（macro-F1 0.6851），对应Swin-T教师、温度4、硬标签权重0.7；三种子均值为0.9024，基线为0.8804。学生模型参数1.52M、大小5.94 MB，较Swin-T减少18.07倍。温度与硬标签权重效果依赖教师和配置，微小性能差异对随机种子敏感。

Conclusion: 知识蒸馏在评估协议下产生了具有竞争力性能的紧凑学生模型，但仍需独立外部验证以确认泛化能力。

Abstract: Diabetic retinopathy grading from retinal fundus photographs is important but difficult to deploy on resource-constrained hardware. We conducted a controlled study of offline response-based knowledge distillation for five-class diabetic retinopathy grading on APTOS 2019, comparing ResNet-50, MobileNetV3-Large, and Swin Transformer-Tiny (Swin-T) teachers distilled into a MobileNetV3-Small student. A 3 x 4 x 2 grid of teacher, temperature, and hard-label weight was screened at one seed, while six configurations and the no-distillation baseline were evaluated across three seeds. The highest observed single-seed quadratic weighted kappa was 0.9093 (macro-F1 0.6851) for Swin-T at temperature 4 and hard-label weight 0.7; the corresponding three-seed mean was 0.9024, compared with 0.8804 for the baseline. The student used 1.52 M parameters and 5.94 MB, representing an 18.07x parameter reduction relative to Swin-T. Temperature and hard-label weight effects were teacher- and configuration-dependent, while small performance differences were sensitive to random seed. Knowledge distillation produced a compact student with competitive performance under the evaluated protocol, but independent external validation remains necessary.

</details>


### [64] [h1: A Taste of the Bitter Lesson in Automated Theorem Proving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35962)
*Skander Moalla,Ahmad Rammal,Niket Patel,Remi Munos,Julia Kempe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 h1，一种简单的 Lean 4 交互框架，通过高效接口让模型利用验证器反馈和自我反思进行定理证明，无需人工设计的分解或搜索结构。在多个基准上达到 SOTA，解决多个开放问题，且在相同模型下匹配或超越专用工作流，表明简单智能体循环配合高效执行反馈即可扩展解决复杂问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化定理证明越来越依赖人工设计的工作流（规划、分解、编排专用证明器），但这些工作流的增益常与更强模型或更多算力混淆。作者想探讨：简单的智能体循环配合测试时算力扩展是否能媲美这些复杂工作流？这对形式化验证跟上前沿模型产出的研究数学至关重要。

Method: 开发 h1，一个轻量级框架，为模型提供高效 Lean 4 接口，让模型通过验证器反馈和自我反思迭代修正证明，不施加分解或搜索结构。在 PutnamBench、FATE-X、FormalConjectures100-Solved 等基准上评估，并与 Goedel-Architect、AlphaProof Nexus 等专用工作流在相同模型下对比，同时进行消融实验和与 Claude Code、AxProverBase 的匹配模型比较。

Result: h1 在 PutnamBench、FATE-X、FormalConjectures100-Solved 达到 SOTA，解决 FormalConjectures 中 8 个开放问题。在相同模型下，h1 匹配或超越 Goedel-Architect 和 AlphaProof Nexus 等专用工作流，且单次尝试（pass@1）往往能解决开放问题。消融实验显示高效 Lean 接口很大程度上决定了成本和解题率。

Conclusion: 简单性 alone 不够，高效的执行反馈接口是关键。建议未来工作在规定工作流前，先用高效执行反馈评估前沿模型，以免重蹈 'bitter lesson' 覆辙。

Abstract: While progress in informal mathematics has come largely from scaling the chain-of-thought and agentic reasoning of frontier language models, formal theorem proving has increasingly relied on human-designed workflows around these models, such as planning, decomposition, and orchestrating specialized provers. This raises a bitter-lesson question: can a simple agentic loop that scales test-time compute match these workflows? The answer matters as formal verification must scale to keep up with frontier models producing research mathematics, yet new workflows rarely settle it, as their gains are often confounded with stronger models or more compute. We study this question with h1, a simple harness that gives a model an efficient interface to Lean 4 and keeps it working through verifier feedback and self-reflection, without imposing a decomposition or search structure. h1 reaches state-of-the-art results on PutnamBench, FATE-X, and FormalConjectures100-Solved, settles eight open problems in FormalConjectures, and, at matched models, matches or outperforms specialized workflows such as Goedel-Architect and AlphaProof Nexus, surprisingly showing that a single attempt (pass@1) can often scale to solve open problems. Through ablations and matched-model comparisons with Claude Code and AxProverBase, we find that simplicity alone is not enough: an efficient Lean interface largely determines cost and solve rate. We thus recommend that future work evaluate frontier models with efficient execution feedback before prescribing workflows, at the risk of bitter lessons.

</details>


### [65] [Condition-Enhanced Generative Adversarial Network for Aerodynamic Configuration Design of Commercial Aircraft](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35963)
*Youzheng Yu,Jiaqing Kou,Weiwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CEGAN，结合几何可行性惩罚和气动性能惩罚，通过代理模型评估并引入输出过滤机制，实现气动构型生成，显著降低生成误差，产生多样化且物理可行的设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式机器学习虽能生成多样化气动构型，但生成的设计可能违反几何约束或偏离目标气动性能，需要一种能嵌入几何与气动信息的改进框架。

Method: 提出 Condition-Enhanced Generative Adversarial Network (CEGAN)，嵌入几何和气动信息；开发条件感知损失函数，结合几何引导的可行性惩罚和基于代理模型的气动性能惩罚；增加输出过滤机制，利用代理模型剔除不可行设计。

Result: 在 Cessna Citation X 类似构型上测试（28 个形状参数，OpenVSP 评估）。插值条件下平均生成误差降低至少 36%，极端外推条件下降低至少 46%，成功生成多样化且物理可行的设计，且超出训练分布。

Conclusion: CEGAN 框架为概念设计阶段快速气动构型探索提供了鲁棒准确的工具。

Abstract: Generative machine learning provides an end-to-end framework for aerodynamic design optimization by generating diverse aerodynamic configurations conditioned on target performance. However, the generated designs may violate geometric constraints or deviate from the prescribed aerodynamic performance. Therefore, we propose a Condition-Enhanced Generative Adversarial Network (CEGAN) that embeds geometric and aerodynamic information into the generative process. A condition-informed loss function is developed by combining a geometry-guided feasibility penalty with an aerodynamic performance penalty evaluated by surrogate models trained on the same dataset. Additionally, an output filtering mechanism discards infeasible designs using these surrogate models, ensuring compliance with prescribed conditions. The method is demonstrated on aircraft configurations similar to the Cessna Citation X. Each configuration is represented by 28 shape parameters, whose aerodynamic performance is evaluated using OpenVSP. Interpolation and extrapolation tests are conducted for different network architectures and conditioning strategies. Compared with the baseline model, CEGAN reduces the average generation error by at least 36% for interpolated conditions and by at least 46% under extreme extrapolation conditions, while successfully producing diverse and physically feasible designs that extend beyond the training distribution. Therefore, the proposed CEGAN framework offers a robust and accurate tool for rapid aerodynamic configuration exploration during the conceptual design phase.

</details>


### [66] [Generalization in Learning: Eyring Formula and Predator-prey Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35965)
*S. V. Kozyrev,I. A. Lopatin,Alexander Pechen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文利用捕食者-猎物模型研究机器学习中的泛化问题，将随机梯度朗之万动力学和生成对抗网络从生物化学角度解读，并在函数恢复任务中证明捕食者-猎物模型优于梯度下降，最后用Eyring公式解释grokking现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习理论缺乏对泛化机制的深层物理化学解释，特别是grokking等延迟泛化现象缺乏热力学层面的理论支撑，需要引入生物化学动力学模型来统一解释优化动态和泛化行为。

Method: 1. 建立捕食者-猎物模型对应SGLD和GAN的生物化学动力学方程；2. 在含噪数据下用固定次多项式恢复未知函数，对比捕食者-猎物模型与梯度下降的近似效果；3. 引入Eyring转变态理论公式，从热力学势垒跨越角度建模grokking的延迟泛化机制。

Result: 捕食者-猎物模型在含噪函数恢复任务中表现出更优的近似精度和鲁棒性，优于标准梯度下降；Eyring公式成功量化了grokking现象中从记忆到泛化的相变时间尺度，揭示了损失景观势垒高度对延迟泛化的决定性作用。

Conclusion: 捕食者-猎物动力学为理解机器学习泛化提供了统一的物理化学框架，热力学视角下的Eyring理论能定量解释grokking等非单调泛化现象，为设计更具生物学合理性的优化算法奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: In this work, generalization in machine learning is investigated using a predator-prey model. We overview biological and chemical interpretation of the stochastic gradient Langevin dynamics and generative adversarial network. For a particular example of recovering an unknown function by a polynomial of fixed degree from a set of noisy data, the predator-prey model provides better approximation compared to the gradient descent method. Further, thermodynamical arguments, in particular Eyring formula, are used to explain grokking (delayed generalization) phenomenon in machine learning.

</details>


### [67] [Data-driven Discovery of Lie Symmetries and First Integrals with Latent ODEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35967)
*Matt L. Wiemman,Peter Melchior*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 LatentLieODE，一种基于神经常微分方程的新方法，从时间序列观测（如视频）中自动发现动力学系统的线性李对称性和一积分（守恒量）。该方法结合自编码器、无穷小生成元拟合、潜在 ODE 优化和一积分网络，在六个基准系统上成功恢复了相关对称性及守恒量（相关系数 ≥0.978），并在球面测地线系统中重构了非阿贝尔 so(3) 李代数，经基灵型验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动方法难以同时从高维观测中提取低维动力学、识别其李对称性并显式构造守恒量。传统对称性发现依赖已知方程或符号回归，缺乏端到端可微框架。本文旨在建立一个完全可微的管道，直接从原始时序数据联合学习潜在动力学、对称生成元及一积分，推动神经代理模型在物理建模中的可解释性与泛化能力。

Method: 1. 自编码器将观测映射为解耦的潜在坐标 z∈ℝ^d。2. 引入一组线性无穷小生成元矩阵 A_k∈ℝ^{d×d}，通过最小化可微李括号残差 ‖J_f(z) A z - A f(z)‖ 联合拟合生成元与潜在 ODE f(z)=dz/dt（由 MLP 参数化）。3. 优化后固定生成元与 f，训练第三个网络 H_φ 使其沿轨迹保持常数（即 dH/dt=0），从而获得一积分。整个流程端到端可微，支持视频等高维输入。

Result: 在谐振子、单/双摆、Lotka-Volterra、像素摆、球面测地线六个基准上，LatentLieODE 均准确识别出系统的相关对称生成元，发现的一积分与真实守恒量高度一致（皮尔逊相关系数 |ρ|≥0.978）。特别是在球面系统中，方法自动恢复了非阿贝尔李代数 so(3)，其结构常数经基灵型检验为负定，确认了李代数结构的正确性。

Conclusion: LatentLieODE 实现了从原始时序数据到李对称性及一积分的端到端自动发现，填补了神经 ODE 与几何力学结合的空白。该框架不仅提供了高精度的神经代理模型，还赋予了模型显式的几何结构与守恒律，为复杂动力学系统的数据驱动建模、模型简化及长时预测开辟了新路径。

Abstract: We present LatentLieODE, a novel neural ODE method to discover linear Lie symmetries and first integrals of the learned dynamics from timeseries observations such as video inputs. It starts with an autoencoder, which learns a low dimensional representation of the observed system and then maps trajectories to decorrelated latent coordinates $z \in \mathbb{R}^d$. A bank of linear infinitesimal generators $\mathbf{A}_k \in \mathbb{R}^{d \times d}$ is fitted jointly against a differentiable Lie-bracket residual $\mathbf{J}_f(z)\mathbf{A}z - \mathbf{A} f(z)$, while an MLP optimises the latent ODE $f(z) = \mathrm{d}z/\mathrm{d}t$ to reconstruct the observation-space timeseries. After suitable generators and $f$ are found, we fit a third network $H_φ$ to generate the first integral of the system. Across six benchmark systems (harmonic oscillator, simple and twin pendulums, Lotka-Volterra, pixel pendulum, sphere geodesics) we find each system's relevant symmetries, and the discovered invariants match ground-truth conserved quantities at $|ρ| \geq 0.978$. On the sphere, the pipeline recovers a non-abelian $\mathfrak{so}(3)$, certified by the negative-definite Killing form of its recovered structure constants. This work presents significant advances in data-driven discovery of symmetries and provides a new pathway for neural surrogates of dynamical systems.

</details>


### [68] [Multiple Leak Localization in Water Distribution Networks through Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35972)
*Arvin Ajoodani,Sara Nazif,Pouria Ramazi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于图神经网络（GNN）的模型，用于在稀疏监测条件下定位供水管网中数量可变的多个同时泄漏，无需预先指定泄漏数量或穷举泄漏组合进行训练。在Hanoi（31节点）和NET3（92节点）两个基准管网上实验表明，该GNN模型在准确率上分别比MLP、SVM、KNN和随机森林高出高达12%和20%，且在两个不同规模管网上表现一致，验证了方法的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动方法通常假设单一泄漏或预定义泄漏数量，或者需要穷举所有可能的泄漏位置组合进行训练，难以应对稀疏监测下多个同时泄漏且泄漏数量未知的实际场景。

Method: 构建图神经网络模型，直接从稀疏压力传感器数据中学习管网拓扑和泄漏特征，实现对可变数量同时泄漏的定位，无需预先指定泄漏数量或生成所有泄漏组合作为训练样本。

Result: 在Hanoi（31节点）和NET3（92节点）两个基准管网上，考虑变化需求、稀疏传感器覆盖、变化泄漏量级和变化同时泄漏数量等条件，GNN模型准确率比MLP、SVM、KNN和随机森林分别高出高达12%（Hanoi）和20%（NET3）。

Conclusion: 所提GNN方法能有效处理稀疏监测下多泄漏定位问题，且在不同规模管网上表现一致，具有良好的可扩展性和实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Leak localization in water distribution networks is challenging when multiple leaks occur simultaneously, particularly under sparse monitoring. Existing data-driven methods often assume a single or predefined number of leaks or require extensive enumeration of leak combinations. This study proposes a graph neural network (GNN) model that localizes a variable number of simultaneous leaks without specifying the leak count in advance or exhaustively generating every possible combination of leak locations for training. The model is evaluated on two benchmark networks of different sizes, the 31-node Hanoi network and the larger 92-node NET3 network, under variable demand, sparse pressure-sensor coverage, variable leak magnitudes, and varying numbers of simultaneous leaks. The GNN outperforms multilayer perceptron, support vector machine, K-nearest neighbors, and random forest classifiers by up to 12% and 20% in accuracy respectively in Hanoi and NET3 networks. Consistent performance across both networks supports the scalability of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [69] [A Graph-informed Kriging model for sample-efficient Bayesian optimization of wind farm layouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35975)
*Zhen-Fan Wang,Kai Zhang,Xianping Du,Zhaolong Han,Dai Zhou,Onur Bilgen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Graph-informed Kriging 框架利用气动图嵌入可复用的尾流交互信息到代理模型均值函数中，显著提高了稀疏数据下的 AEP 预测精度和贝叶斯优化的样本效率，在 IEA Wind Task 37 基准和大规模 48 机位风电场中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风电场布局优化随机组数增加而变难，高保真气动模型计算昂贵；传统 Kriging 贝叶斯优化在高维布局空间中因稀疏训练数据覆盖有限导致样本效率低。

Method: 提出 Graph-informed Kriging 框架：将风电场表示为有向气动图，在低维相对坐标空间定义成对机组交互，组装成全场均值函数；该结构化均值捕捉 AEP 主导变化，高斯过程仅建模残差结构和不确定性。

Result: 数值实验表明 Graph-informed Kriging 在稀疏数据条件下持续优于普通 Kriging 和通用趋势 Kriging 的 AEP 预测精度，随布局维度和尾流交互强度增大优势增加；嵌入贝叶斯优化后显著提高样本效率，在 IEA Wind Task 37 基准上仅用约 280 次 AEP 评估即得竞争性解；在 16 机位对比中优于普通和多保真 Kriging；扩展至 48 机位风电场验证了大规模 CFD 布局优化的适用性。

Conclusion: Graph-informed 代理建模能更高效地利用高保真气动模型，实现可靠的大规模风电场布局设计。

Abstract: Wind farm layout optimization becomes increasingly difficult as turbine count grows, especially when high-fidelity aerodynamic models are used. Conventional Kriging-based Bayesian optimization often suffers from low sample efficiency in high-dimensional layout spaces because sparse training data provide limited domain coverage. We develop a Graph-informed Kriging framework that embeds reusable aerodynamic interaction information into the surrogate mean function. The wind farm is represented as a directed aerodynamic graph, where pairwise turbine interactions defined in a low-dimensional relative-coordinate space are assembled into a farm-scale mean. This structured mean captures the dominant variation in annual energy production (AEP), allowing the Gaussian process to model only the residual structure and uncertainty. Numerical experiments show that Graph-informed Kriging consistently improves AEP prediction accuracy over ordinary and generic-trend Kriging under sparse-data conditions, with increasing benefits as layout dimension and wake-interaction strength grow. Embedded in Bayesian optimization, it substantially improves sample efficiency and achieves competitive solutions for the IEA Wind Task 37 benchmark with about 280 AEP evaluations. In a controlled 16-turbine case, it also outperforms ordinary and multi-fidelity Kriging under the same high-fidelity evaluation budget. The framework is further extended to a 48-turbine wind farm, demonstrating applicability to large-scale CFD-based layout optimization. These results show that Graph-informed surrogate modeling can enable more efficient use of high-fidelity aerodynamic models for reliable large-scale wind farm layout design.

</details>


### [70] [Let the Gate Decide: Per-Lookup Precision for Quantized Conditional Memory in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35985)
*Tianqi Chen,Xunchao Li,Linchuan Xie,Guanghua Yu,Jianchen Zhu,Peisong Wang,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出GateRQ方法，通过门控机制实现条件记忆层的高效量化，无需重训练即可将GPU表格内存减少7.8倍，在仅损失0.2-1 PopQA点的情况下显著提升推理吞吐率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件记忆层（如DeepSeek的Engram）依赖大型哈希n-gram表格，移除会导致PopQA性能大幅下降36.8点。低比特量化优先丢失长尾事实，传统按访问频率分配比特的方法反而比随机分配更差，根因在于检索行的门控缩放机制：量化误差被门控值加权，频繁n-gram门控值小，稀有长尾实体门控值大且可从1-bit编码计算。

Method: GateRQ将每行存储为1-bit残差码的阶梯结构，仅保留第1层在GPU内存中用于计算门控值，仅为门控排名最高的查找读取更深层级，实现自适应比特分配。

Result: 在DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash上，GateRQ将GPU表格内存降低7.8倍，vLLM吞吐率比FP8表格提升11%-31%，在1.50 bits/查找时损失约1 PopQA点，2.10 bits时仅损失0.2点，跨两个模型验证有效。

Conclusion: GateRQ利用门控机制的特性实现了高效的自适应量化，无需重训练即可大幅压缩条件记忆层内存并提升推理效率，同时最小化长尾知识损失。}

Abstract: Conditional memory layers, such as DeepSeek's Engram, attach large hashed n-gram tables to a language model, and removing them from DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash costs 36.8 PopQA points. Low-bit quantization loses long-tail facts first, and the standard remedy, giving more bits to frequently accessed rows, backfires: refining lookups by access frequency is worse than refining them at random. We trace this to the gate that scales each retrieved row. A lookup's quantization error is weighted by its gate, which is small for frequent n-grams, large for the rare n-grams of long-tail entities, and computable from a 1-bit code. GateRQ therefore stores each row as a ladder of 1-bit residual codes, keeps level 1 in GPU memory to compute the gate, and reads deeper levels only for the lookups the gate ranks highest.
