Without retraining, GateRQ cuts GPU table memory by 7.8x on DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash, which raises vLLM throughput by 11%-31% over FP8 tables, while losing about 1 PopQA point at 1.50 bits read per lookup and 0.2 points at 2.10 bits across two models.

</details>


### [71] [Layerwise Repacking: Variable-Length Hybrid-Attention Training without an Extra Redistribution Pass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35989)
*Jiahao Zhang,Junwei Feng,Dongliang Huang,Xiaoling Zhou,Yiqiao Liao,Chengru Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对混合注意力模型中全注意力层与稀疏注意力层并行需求冲突的问题，提出 Layerwise Repacking 技术：在全注意力层的 Ulysses 通信点顺带完成样本重排布，避免额外重分发开销，并辅以父组求解器优化负载均衡。在 1F3S 模型前 200 步实验中，单独重排布使端到端耗时降低 5.95%、MFU 提升 3.75 pp；加上改进求解器后耗时降 7.61%、MFU 升 4.83 pp。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变长训练导致混合注意力模型各层并行需求矛盾：全注意力层需跨节点大规模序列并行（SP16/32），成本随序列长度平方增长；稀疏注意力层以近线性 MoE 计算为主，节点内小并行组（SP8）即可均衡负载。若强行复用大组跨节点通信，有效带宽降 7 倍；若每层边界单独重分发，又引入额外数据搬移开销。

Method: Layerwise Repacking：保持全注意力层使用宽 SP 父组，在两处现有 Ulysses all-to-all 通信点顺带完成头交换与样本重排布，将样本重排到节点内的稀疏子组（SP{1,2,4,8}），隐藏态在全注意力层间保持。引入辅助父组求解器，在子组形成前重平衡父组成员，提升子组负载均衡上限。无需额外重分发通信步骤。

Result: 在 1F3S 混合注意力模型前 200 步训练中（含 22 个 SP16/32 父组步骤）：仅启用重排布，平均端到端耗时 -5.95%，MFU +3.75 百分点；结合改进求解器，耗时 -7.61%，MFU +4.83 百分点。

Conclusion: Layerwise Repacking 利用现有通信点零开销完成跨层布局切换，配合父组求解器优化负载均衡，有效解决混合注意力模型变长训练中的并行度冲突，显著降低训练时间并提升算力利用率，且无需额外通信轮次。

Abstract: Variable-length training creates conflicting parallelism requirements across the layers of a hybrid-attention model. The longest samples require wide sequence-parallel (SP) groups of 16 or 32 ranks for full attention, whose cost grows quadratically with sequence length. Sparse-attention layers, dominated by near-linear MoE work, can instead balance most evaluated workloads within node-local SP8 groups. Reusing the wide groups for these layers pushes their all-to-all communication across nodes, where the observed effective rate is 7.0x lower. Yet switching to smaller groups changes which ranks own each sample; a separate redistribution at every full-sparse boundary would introduce another data transfer.
  Layerwise Repacking keeps each SP16/32 parent for full attention and repacks its samples into node-local sparse children at SP{1, 2, 4, 8}, where hidden states persist between full layers. The layout change rides on the two existing full-layer Ulysses sites: at each, one variable-size all-to-all performs both the head exchange and the sample repacking, so no redistribution pass is added. Because the balance the children can reach depends on which samples share a parent, an auxiliary parent solver rebalances parent membership before the children are formed.
  On all 22 steps with SP16/32 parents among the first 200 training steps of a 1F3S hybrid-attention model (one full-attention layer per three sparse-attention layers), repacking alone reduces the mean end-to-end time by 5.95% and raises model FLOPs utilization (MFU) by 3.75 percentage points over the baseline with repacking disabled; with the improved solver, end-to-end time falls by 7.61% and MFU rises by 4.83 percentage points.

</details>


### [72] [DuplexTune: Coupled-State Replay for Memory-Efficient Mixture-of-Experts Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36009)
*Paul Raj,Simon Caldwell,Daniel Whitmore,Mutlu Mete*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 DuplexTune 框架，通过重放耦合隐藏状态实现稀疏专家混合模型的高效微调，显著降低显存占用并提升吞吐量，但在多语言任务上表现略弱于全参数微调基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏专家执行无法消除适配混合专家语言模型所需的存储状态，现有微调方法仍面临高显存和低吞吐的问题，需要一种能够重放隐藏状态且冻结路由器权重的高效适配方法。

Method: DuplexTune 引入可训练的宽度桥接器连接两个残差流到主干网络，先进行桥接器预热再适配主干，保持路由器权重冻结；通过分析隐式状态恢复方程，确定收敛与重放条件以实现状态重建。

Result: 在 Qwen1.5-MoE-A2.7B 上单卡 H800 评测：峰值显存 38.8 GB（对比全参数微调+检查点 64.8 GB），吞吐 25.1 samples/s（对比 19.9 samples/s）；MMLU 67.2%，GSM8K 75.6%，MT-Bench 7.69，多语言得分弱于检查点基线。

Conclusion: DuplexTune 实现了状态存储、吞吐量和适配质量之间的配置相关权衡，为稀疏专家模型的高效微调提供了新范式。

Abstract: Sparse expert execution does not remove the stored states needed to adapt a mixture-of-experts language model. We present DuplexTune, a framework for replaying coupled hidden states through pretrained attention and expert modules. Trainable width bridges connect two residual streams to the backbone. Bridge warm-up precedes backbone adaptation, with router weights kept frozen. We analyze the implicit state-recovery equation and identify the convergence and replay conditions needed for reconstruction. On Qwen1.5-MoE-A2.7B, the single-H800 evaluation reports 38.8 GB peak memory, versus 64.8 GB for supervised fine-tuning with checkpointing, and throughput of 25.1 versus 19.9 samples/s. DuplexTune achieves 67.2% MMLU, 75.6% GSM8K, and 7.69 MT-Bench, with a weaker multilingual score than the checkpointed baseline. The results characterize a configuration-dependent tradeoff among state storage, throughput, and adaptation quality.

</details>


### [73] [Exploratory creative pre-ranking: Hierarchical empirical-Bayes Thompson sampling for real-time creative optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36020)
*Jiale Han,Ajith Moparthi,Dan Xie,Xiaowu Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种创意预排序器，用于动态创意优化，通过层次化 Thompson 采样在受众片段级别进行评分和短名单筛选，显著提升预测播放率并降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态创意优化在广告排序前做创意选择，但依赖确定性点预测器，缺乏校准的不确定性估计；AI 生成内容使创意几乎免费，但每个创意的反馈稀疏，且面临严格的延迟预算。

Method: 引入创意预排序器，对每个召回创意评分并为每个广告活动生成短名单。创意处于受众片段、广告项目、广告活动、创意的层次结构中，冷启动创意回退到父层级的历史收益，借用权重由历史日志闭式解确定，每层仅一个参数无需调优。为满足延迟预算，预排序器面向受众片段而非单个用户预测，使用属性级计数器。选择采用每个创意单次 Thompson 采样，平衡探索与利用。

Result: 在 Roblox 赞助广告流量重放实验中，短名单使预测播放率提升 8.14%（随机短名单仅 2.80%），转发创意数减少 40% 以上，服务时间仅增加 0.2%。线上 A/B 测试后已部署上线。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了创意选择中的冷启动、稀疏反馈和延迟约束问题，实现了探索与利用的平衡，已在 Roblox 生产环境中落地。

Abstract: Dynamic creative optimization decides which creative of an ad to render for each request. Recent work places that choice before ad ranking, but relies on deterministic point predictors with no calibrated uncertainty. AI-generated content has made creatives nearly free, while feedback per creative remains sparse and selection faces a tight latency budget. We introduce a creative pre-ranker that scores every recalled creative and shortlists each campaign's pool before the per-user ranker runs. Creatives sit in a hierarchy of audience segment, advertised item, campaign and creative, so a cold creative falls back on what its parent levels have earned, and how much it borrows from its parents is set in closed form from historical logs, with one parameter per level and nothing tuned. To stay within the latency budget, the pre-ranker predicts for an audience segment rather than an individual user, from per-attribute counters. It selects by a single Thompson draw per creative, balancing exploration and exploitation so that a new creative still has a chance to be learned. Replaying Roblox Sponsored-ads traffic over campaigns with competing creatives, the shortlist lifts predicted play-through rate by 8.14% against 2.80% for an equally sized random shortlist, forwards more than 40% fewer creatives than the full pool, and adds only 0.2% to serving time. After an A/B test on live traffic, the method now serves in production at Roblox.

</details>


### [74] [Jaxnasium: Embracing PyTrees For Any-Agent, Cross-Suite Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36075)
*Koen Ponse,Aske Plaat,Thomas M. Moerland*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Jaxnasium 是一个基于 JAX 的强化学习库，通过将观测和动作空间表示为 PyTrees，实现了跨多种环境套件、空间类型和多智能体场景的统一算法实现，提供了统一的 Gymnasium 风格接口，支持 300 多个环境，且性能无损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于 JAX 的硬件加速强化学习生态系统碎片化严重：不同环境套件定义各自接口，算法实现通常仅适配单一套件、单一空间类型，且分为 JAX 原生或基于宿主环境两类，导致可复用性和可访问性低。

Method: Jaxnasium 将观测和动作空间表示为 PyTrees，利用这一结构构建网络并支持多智能体处理；提供极简的 Gymnasium 风格接口和 11 个套件的封装器；通过简单封装器统一运行 Gymnasium 和 EnvPool 环境，算法代码无需修改即可在离散、连续、图像、复合空间及多智能体环境中运行。

Result: 一个小型 PPO 实现配合默认超参数即可在 300 多个环境上运行；相同算法文件既可作为可读的研究友好型代码，也可作为可导入的应用型算法；在宿主环境上训练时，性能与专门为 JAX 算法构建的架构相当，无性能损失；并提供多种子评估、超参数扫描和项目脚手架工具。

Conclusion: Jaxnasium 通过统一的 PyTree 空间表示和极简接口，成功解决了 JAX 强化学习生态的碎片化问题，显著提高了算法的可移植性、可读性和适用范围，为研究和应用提供了统一、高性能的基础设施。

Abstract: Hardware-accelerated reinforcement learning in JAX has gained rapid popularity in recent years and has proven to be a successful method of scaling online reinforcement learning. The resulting ecosystem, however, is fragmented: environment suites define their own interfaces, and algorithm implementations are typically written for a single suite, a single type of space, and either JAX-native or host-based environments. Here we present Jaxnasium, a library for JAX-based reinforcement learning, aiming to make the field more accessible. Jaxnasium represents observation and action spaces as PyTrees, and uses this structure to build networks and support multi-agent processing. Through this, its near-single-file implementations run on discrete, continuous, image, and composite spaces, as well as in multi-agent environments, without any changes to the algorithm code. The same files serve as readable, research-friendly algorithms, as well as importable algorithms for more applied workloads. Through a minimal Gymnasium-style interface and wrappers for 11 suites, over 300 environments are available on which a small PPO implementation with default hyperparameters is able to run. Additionally, through a simple wrapper, we can run Gymnasium and EnvPool environments with the same algorithm and training loop, with no performance loss compared to architectures specifically built for training JAX algorithms on host-based environments. Jaxnasium further provides tools for multi-seed evaluation, hyperparameter sweeps, and project scaffolding. Jaxnasium is open-sourced at https://github.com/ponseko/jaxnasium

</details>


### [75] [How Much Does the Hint Matter? Scope Conditions for Hindsight Self-Distillation in Financial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36082)
*Johnny Yu,Cyril Gorlla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Fin-SD，一种针对多步推理的局部事后自我蒸馏方法，使小模型在数据驻留约束下也能提升财务定量推理能力，无需外部教师模型梯度，且生成token更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受数据驻留限制的金融机构只能本地部署小型开放权重模型，这些模型在多步定量推理上落后于前沿系统。现有的在线策略蒸馏需要强外部教师，而在线策略自我蒸馏（OPSD）需完整参考解。作者探究究竟需要多少监督信号。

Method: Fin-SD让学生模型在失败时自我识别首个错误推理步骤并撰写简短批评。截断错误前的轨迹获得正确前缀，冻结学生副本在前缀+提示条件下生成，通过反向KL将提示条件教师蒸馏回学生，全程无外部模型梯度。

Result: 在GPT-OSS-20B上：(i) 用通用反馈替换批评也能获得3.03个百分点的准确率提升，说明收益源于监督施加位置而非提示内容；(ii) 同家族更大模型（GPT-OSS-120B）的提示无实质额外增益；(iii) 与完整参考解的OPSD准确率持平，但token减少9.5%。MMLU-Pro和FinTrust合规审计无显著退化。

Conclusion: Fin-SD证明了局部事后自我蒸馏能在无外部教师、少生成开销下有效缩小小模型与前沿系统在多步财务推理上的差距，监督信号的施加时机比提示质量更关键。

Abstract: Financial institutions operating under data-residency constraints are restricted to open-weight models small enough to locally host, which trail frontier systems on multi-step quantitative reasoning. On-policy distillation narrows such gaps by supervising a student on its own rollouts, but traditional OPD requires a strong external teacher, and on-policy self-distillation (OPSD) conditions on a full reference solution. We ask how much of that signal is necessary. We present Fin-SD, a localized hindsight self-distillation recipe for multi-step reasoning. Given a problem the student fails, the student itself identifies the first incorrect reasoning step and writes a short critique of that step. We truncate the student's trajectory immediately before the error (aiming to obtain a correct prefix), condition a frozen copy of the student on the same prefix plus the hint, and distill that hint-conditioned teacher into the student over a short rollout under reverse KL. No external model contributes a gradient. We apply Fin-SD to GPT-OSS-20B on a custom multi-step financial reasoning benchmark that requires deriving formula inputs from realistic scenarios via accounting conventions. We find: (i) replacing the critique with a generic-feedback control matches Fin-SD's entire 3.03 pp accuracy gain over the base model on the FinanceReasoning Hard subset, suggesting the gain comes from where supervision is applied, not hint content; (ii) the model writing the hint does not need to be stronger than the model receiving it: hints from same-family GPT-OSS-120B, at roughly 6x the parameters, yield no materially greater benefits than self-written hints; and (iii) Fin-SD matches on-policy self-distillation from full reference solutions on accuracy while generating 9.5% fewer tokens. With Fin-SD, general reasoning on MMLU-Pro and a fourteen-metric compliance audit on FinTrust show no material regression.

</details>


### [76] [Instruction Capacity: Behavior and Degradation of Language Models under Controlled Load](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36095)
*Hennadii Kutomanov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种量化测量语言模型指令遵循能力的仪器，通过让模型在无意义文本中按规则替换单词代词，将每次替换视为伯努利试验，建立统计模型量化指令容量（在给定正确率下可处理的规则数）及其不确定性。实测 GPT-4o mini 指令容量约为 37-38 条规则（正确率 0.95）或 18-19 条（全替换密度），并开源了测量代码、数据与模型护照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏以负载为单位、带不确定度的语言模型指令遵循极限量化指标，难以客观比较和校准不同模型的指令跟随能力。

Method: 构造原子化指令：在无意义文本中按独立规则替换单标记词，每个替换位置与规则编号已知，结果可由计算参考答案确定。将每次替换视为伯努利试验，统计建模正确替换概率对文本长度、词位置、替换密度、规则数、规则编号的依赖，拟合平滑定律并还原与编码无关的基准水平，输出指令容量及其不确定度。

Result: 对 GPT-4o mini 进行 2958 次请求、642,430 次试验测量，得到各负载因子及答案崩溃的规律性曲线。指令容量在正确率 0.95 时约为 37-38 条规则，在全替换密度下约为 18-19 条规则。所有测量代码、数据及模型护照已开源，护照数值可从公开点位复现。

Conclusion: 该仪器提供了可复现、带不确定度的指令容量度量，模型护照可作为未来提示词指令负载计量器的校准基准，推动语言模型指令遵循能力的标准化评估。

Abstract: The quality with which language models follow instructions falls as the load grows, yet there is still no quantity that expresses the limit of a model in units of load with an uncertainty. We propose an instrument in which the load consists of rules that replace single-token words in a text without meaning: such an instruction is atomic, the rules are independent by the construction of the task, and the outcome of every application of a rule is determined by a computable reference answer. Every occurrence of a word to be replaced becomes a Bernoulli trial with a known position in the answer and a known rule number. The statistical model first measures the shape of the dependence of the probability of a right replacement on the text length, the word position, the replacement density, the number of rules and the rule number, then describes it by smooth laws and restores a reference level that does not depend on the choice of the coding. The model yields the instruction capacity - the number of rules held at a given share of right replacements - with an uncertainty. A measurement of GPT-4o mini (2958 requests, 642,430 trials) gave regular laws for every load factor and for the collapse of the answer; the instruction capacity was about 37-38 rules at a share of right replacements of 0.95, and about 18-19 rules at the full replacement density. The code of the instrument, the measurement data and the passport are open, and every number of the passport is recomputed from the published points. The passport of a model serves as the calibration of a future meter of the instruction load of prompts.

</details>


### [77] [Minimax-Optimal Small-Loss Bounds for Online Linear Optimisation on $\ell_p$ Balls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36097)
*Jack J. Mayo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究全信息在线线性优化在单位 ℓ_p 球（p≥2）上的问题，提出一种自适应 FTRL 算法，在不知 T 和 Δ_T 的情况下达到最优遗憾界 O_p(1+min{Δ_T^{1-1/p}, d^{1/2-1/p}√Δ_T})，常数 (2+o(1))p/log p 随 p→∞ 最优，并给出匹配下界、切换扩展及 i.i.d. 情况下精确对数系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决 ℓ_p 范数约束下的在线线性优化最优遗憾率问题，尤其是对于固定 p≥2，要求算法不依赖时间步 T 或累积损失 Δ_T 的先验信息，同时追求最优常数和对 FTL 算法的改进。

Method: 采用带有有界径向正则化项的 FTRL 算法，并至多切换一次到其二次增强版本；提出切换扩展机制以保持最优常数并确保遗憾不超过 FTL 遗憾的两倍；对于 i.i.d. 损失且均值无零坐标的情况，计算期望遗憾的精确 log T 系数以分离方差项与正则化偏差。

Result: 算法实现遗憾界 O_p(1+min{Δ_T^{1-1/p}, d^{1/2-1/p}√Δ_T})，匹配下界证明其为每个固定 p 的极小极大最优率；常数 (2+o(1))p/log p 在 p→∞ 时在 d、T 一致保证下最优；切换扩展保证 Reg_T ≤ 2 Reg_T^FTL；i.i.d. 设定下给出精确对数系数，区分了 FTL 方差项与正则化偏差。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了 ℓ_p 球上在线线性优化的最优自适应遗憾界，常数最优且算法无需先验知识；切换机制有效结合了 FTRL 与 FTL 的优势；随机设定下的精细分析揭示了遗憾的组成结构。

Abstract: We study full-information online linear optimisation on the unit $\ell_p$ ball in $\mathbb{R}^d$, for fixed $2\le p<\infty$ and losses of dual norm at most one. Let $Δ_T$ denote the cumulative loss of the best fixed action above each round's minimum. A follow-the-regularised-leader (FTRL) algorithm attains, on every loss sequence and without knowing $T$ or $Δ_T$, regret $O_p(1+\min\{Δ_T^{1-1/p},\, d^{1/2-1/p}\sqrt{Δ_T}\})$. Matching lower bounds show that this rate is minimax for each fixed $p$, and its constant $(2+o(1))p/\log p$ is optimal as $p\to\infty$ among guarantees uniform in $d$ and $T$. The algorithm uses a bounded radial regulariser and switches at most once to its quadratic augmentation. On some paths where follow-the-leader (FTL) has regret one, its regret grows with $T$. A switching extension keeps the sharp constant and also guarantees $\mathrm{Reg}_T\le 2\,\mathrm{Reg}_T^{\mathrm{FTL}}$. For i.i.d. losses whose mean has no zero coordinates, we compute the exact $\log T$ coefficient of the expected regret, which separates FTL's variance term from the bias of the regulariser.

</details>


### [78] [Beyond Update Counts: A Control Map for Evaluating Test-Time Model Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36109)
*Zehao Zhao,Shangjian Yin,Rui Liu,Chongyang Bai,Mark Ibrahim,Kavosh Asadi,Yuchen Lu,Hang Cui,Hamed Firooz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Control Map评估框架，用于系统记录测试时更新过程中每个回答前的已提交更新，并分别评估即时学习(获取)、后续回忆(保持)、无关行为保持(局部性)及新场景表现，揭示更新调度、保持性和一致性三大关键洞见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时更新评估仅比较更新次数/频率及即时性能，忽略了更新的内容、位置、时机和顺序。相同的总更新次数可能掩盖关键差异：有的系统在一次更新后回答，有的在两次更新后回答，导致评估不全面。

Method: 引入Control Map框架：记录每个评分回答前的已提交更新历史；分别评估行为变化、即时学习(获取)、后续回忆(保持)、无关行为保持(局部性)及新设置性能；证明何时仅靠计数决定最终状态，并在假设下量化预回答历史对平均分差异的限制。

Result: 三大发现：1) 更新调度至关重要：7.6亿参数检查点上，24种相同更新次数的调度产生19.7%-34.2%准确率差异。2) 学会编辑不代表持久或局限：所有6种CounterFact LoRA设置均学会新信息，ROME在13个工作点均保持编辑但每个都失败于局部性。3) 无编辑器在所有模型、基准和测试中持续表现最佳。

Conclusion: 评估应报告更新历史，并分别评估获取、保持和局部性三个维度，而非仅依赖更新计数和即时性能。

Abstract: Test-time updating lets a language model change persistent state after training---such as model weights, adapter parameters, or a memory module---before or while answering queries. Existing evaluations often compare systems using update count or frequency and performance measured immediately afterward. These summaries omit what is updated, where, when, and in what order. The same total can therefore hide an important difference: one system may answer after one update while another answers after two. We introduce the Control Map, an evaluation framework that records the committed updates preceding each scored answer and separately evaluates behavioral changes, immediate learning (acquisition), later recall (retention), preservation of unrelated behavior (locality), and performance in new settings. We prove when counts alone determine final state and show, under stated assumptions, how differences between pre-answer histories limit differences in average scores. Using ControlMap, our experiments reveal three insights. First, update scheduling matters: at a 760M-parameter checkpoint, 24 schedules with the same update count yield 19.7%--34.2% accuracy. Second, learning an edit does not guarantee that it lasts or remains contained: all six selected CounterFact LoRA settings learn the new information, while ROME retains edits at all 13 operating points but fails locality at each. Third, no editor performs best consistently across models, benchmarks, and tests. Evaluations should therefore report update histories and assess acquisition, retention, and locality separately.

</details>


### [79] [Dirichlet Process Mixtures within Semiparametric Bayesian Networks: A Novel Conditional Density Estimation Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36110)
*Victor Alejandre,Pedro Larrañaga,Concha Bielza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Dirichlet过程混合模型(DPM)的半参数贝叶斯网络(DPM-SPBN)，替代传统核密度估计(KDE)的非参数条件概率分布(CPD)建模，实现更紧凑的表示、更低推理成本和更优密度估计性能，并在结构学习中表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SPBN使用KDE进行非参数CPD建模，但其每观测一个分量的混合表示导致推理成本高和密度估计误差大，限制了应用。

Method: 引入贝叶斯非参数Dirichlet过程混合模型(DPM)作为非参数CPD建模方法，利用DPM的数据自适应聚类特性实现更紧凑的表示，扩展开源PyBNesian包实现DPM-SPBN，在合成和真实数据上评估密度估计和结构学习性能。

Result: 在给定网络结构下，DPM-based CPD显著优于现有连续变量贝叶斯网络模型，合成数据上绝对对数似然误差更低；结构学习中DPM-SPBN的结构学习和密度估计误差均低于KDE-SPBN；真实数据实验验证了DPM-based CPD是评估中最有效的非参数方法。

Conclusion: DPM-based CPD建模为连续变量半参数贝叶斯网络提供了更优的非参数建模方案，同时提升密度估计精度、降低推理成本并改善结构学习效果，是KDE的有效替代。

Abstract: Semiparametric Bayesian networks (SPBNs) are flexible probabilistic graphical models for representing joint distributions of continuous variables through lower-dimensional conditional probability distributions (CPDs). Existing SPBN models use kernel density estimators (KDEs) for non-parametric CPD modeling, but their one-component-per-observation mixture representation can lead to increased inference costs and density estimation errors, which can be limiting. In this work, we define a new CPD modeling approach for non-parametric components based on Bayesian non-parametric Dirichlet Process Mixtures (DPMs) within SPBNs. DPMs provide a data-adaptive clustering of observations, yielding a more compact representation while retaining the flexibility of non-parametric density estimation, unlike KDE. This results in lower inference costs while improving density estimation performance. We implement the proposed DPM-based SPBN (DPM-SPBN) by extending the open-source PyBNesian package and evaluate it on synthetic and real-world data. For a given network structure, DPM-based CPDs significantly outperform state-of-the-art Bayesian network models for continuous variables in density estimation, achieving lower absolute log-likelihood errors on synthetic data. We further investigate the impact of the proposed CPD modeling on structure learning methods relying on CPD-based scores. DPM-based SPBNs achieve lower structure learning and density estimation errors than KDE-based SPBNs. Experiments on real-world data corroborate these findings, identifying DPM-based CPD modeling as the most effective alternative among the evaluated non-parametric approaches for continuous variables.

</details>


### [80] [Scaling and Renormalization in Neural Networks: An Effective Renormalization-Group Description of Grokking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36113)
*Min Chen,Junqi Wang,Zeguan Wu,Wenhao Huang,Junyu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将重整化群（RG）理论应用于深度神经网络中的“Grokking”现象（记忆到泛化的延迟转变），构建了一个定量物理框架，通过序参量、耦合矩阵谱序和实空间 RG 流，成功预测了临界指数，实证与理论吻合度达 1-10%，确立了 RG 形式主义作为研究神经网络动力学的有效工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grokking 现象揭示了神经网络从记忆到泛化的相变本质，但缺乏定量物理理论描述。作者旨在建立缩放与重整化群框架，将训练动力学映射为统计物理系统，从而用临界现象理论解释泛化转变的普适规律。

Method: 1. 在模模加法任务的双层网络中，定义平方权重范数为序参量，训练轮数与权重衰减分别作为温度场与外场控制参数；2. 对损失函数作神经元范数二阶展开构造精确耦合矩阵，利用 Fiedler 向量得到有效几何与谱序，实现耦合强度的单调空间衰减；3. 实施实空间 RG：相邻神经元块自旋聚合，将耦合矩阵参数化为带状非对角强度；4. 线性化局域 RG 映射提取相关标度指数并推导五个临界指数，最后与集合实验估计对比验证。

Result: 谱序下耦合矩阵呈现单调空间衰减，随机排列则无此结构。RG 线性化得到的相关标度指数与五个派生临界指数，经验估计值与理论预测在 1%-10% 相对误差内一致，热容与磁率代理量在转变点呈现峰值，验证了缩放假设与临界行为的定量描述。

Conclusion: 重整化群形式主义能为 Grokking 现象提供自洽的有效物理描述，实现了从微观网络参数到宏观临界行为的定量映射。该框架不仅解释了延迟泛化的相变机制，更确立了缩放与 RG 理论作为通用工具研究神经网络训练动力学的可行性与前景。

Abstract: Grokking is a delayed transition from memorization to generalization in deep neural networks (NNs). We examine whether scaling and renormalization group (RG) theories can describe this dynamics, establishing a quantitative physical framework for neural generalization transitions. For modular addition on a two-layer NN, an ensemble of trained models defines an order parameter (the squared weight norm) and fluctuation proxies for the heat capacity and the susceptibility, with training epoch and weight decay serving as thermal and field control coordinates. To bridge neural architectures to real-space RG, we construct an exact coupling matrix via a second-order expansion of the loss with respect to per-neuron norms and use its Fiedler vector to define an effective geometry. This spectral ordering yields a monotonic spatial decay of coupling strength that is absent under random permutations. We then execute real-space RG by block-spin aggregating adjacent neurons and parameterizing the coupling matrix into off-diagonal band strengths. Linearizing this local RG map yields relevant scaling exponents and five derived critical exponents, with direct empirical estimates matching RG predictions within 1-10%. Overall, our framework provides a consistent effective description of grokking, suggesting the scaling and renormalization group formalism as a tool to investigate NN dynamics.

</details>


### [81] [SpatialPC: Deep Computation with Sublinear Local Credit Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36139)
*Benjamin Prada,Shion Matsumoto,Ankur Arjun Mali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了局部学习规则中的延迟源于通信几何而非深度，构建了在d维空间中实现Θ(N)表示深度与Θ(N^{1/d})因果信用延迟共存的有向无环图，并在预测编码框架下通过伴随预测动力学（APD）实现了这一分离，实验验证了SpatialPC在有限轮次下能保持梯度对齐并达到高测试准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为局部学习规则中损失影响早期参数的延迟归因于网络深度，但作者怀疑这实质上是通信几何的性质。他们旨在将深度与信用传播延迟解耦，理解空间布局如何决定梯度估计何时能符号正确，并构建能同时实现长表示深度和短因果信用路径的架构。

Method: 1. 理论分析：在d维常数密度放置的N=m^d状态上，利用光锥论证给出Ω(N^{1/d})的局部算法轮次下界。2. 图构造：设计单位范围、度为2d的有向无环图，包含长度N-1的哈密顿计算路径，且每个状态到输出的有向距离为d(m-1)=Θ(N^{1/d})。3. 算法设计：提出伴随预测动力学（APD），其K轮状态为截断逆路径和，误差精确为(J^⊤)^K a^⋆。4. 稀疏二维Moore编译器：保留N-1主干，仅用1.5N-2条单位范围边，最坏节点可达性√N+2。5. 实验：在MNIST和Fashion-MNIST上用N=256、T=2√N轮次训练SpatialPC。

Result: 1. 理论下界与构造匹配：证明了深度Θ(N)与因果信用延迟Θ(N^{1/d})可共存，分离阶为N^{1-1/d}。2. APD的误差显式表征：图几何决定信用首次到达时间，雅可比加权路径尾部决定后续精确所需轮次。3. 稀疏编译器在N=65,536时最坏可达性仅258跳。4. 实验中有限轮次任务梯度与稳态梯度余弦相似度达0.93(MNIST)和0.97(Fashion-MNIST)，20轮后测试准确率分别为97.6%和88.1%。

Conclusion: 深度与信用传播深度是独立参数：空间布局决定信用传播的物理极限，而非网络层数。SpatialPC通过几何设计实现了长计算路径与短信用路径的分离，APD提供了局部、可解释的信用分配机制，为大规模局部学习系统提供了可扩展的蓝图。

Abstract: On a chain of $N$ states, any local learning rule with bounded communication range needs $Θ(N)$ rounds before the loss can influence the earliest parameters, and this latency is usually attributed to depth. We show that it is a property of the communication geometry rather than of depth. For $N=m^d$ states placed at constant density in $d$ dimensions, a light-cone argument gives an $Ω(N^{1/d})$ lower bound on the number of bounded-range rounds that any local algorithm needs before its gradient estimates can be sign-correct at every parameter. We construct a unit-range, degree-$2d$ directed acyclic graph that contains a Hamiltonian computation of length $N-1$ and in which every state lies within directed distance $d(m-1)=Θ(N^{1/d})$ of the output, matching the lower bound. In the realizable-circuit model, depth and credit depth are therefore independent parameters: represented depth $Θ(N)$ and causal credit latency $Θ(N^{1/d})$ coexist, with a separation of order $N^{1-1/d}$. SpatialPC realizes this separation within predictive coding. Credit is propagated by adjoint prediction dynamics (APD), a local update whose $K$-round state on a settled acyclic graph is the truncated reverse-path sum $a^K=\sum_{k<K}(J^\top)^k b$, with error exactly $(J^\top)^K a^\star$. Graph geometry fixes when credit can first arrive at a state; the Jacobian-weighted path tail fixes how many further rounds accurate credit requires. A sparse two-dimensional Moore compiler retains the $N-1$ backbone with $\tfrac32N-2$ unit-range edges and has measured worst-node reachability $\sqrt N+2$ up to $N=65{,}536$, where a 65,535-edge backbone is reached in 258 local hops. With $T=2\sqrt N$ rounds, SpatialPC keeps finite-round task gradients aligned with settled gradients (cosine $0.93$ on MNIST and $0.97$ on Fashion-MNIST at $N=256$) and reaches $97.6\%$ and $88.1\%$ test accuracy after 20 epochs.

</details>


### [82] [Dynamical coarse-graining of neural-network training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36180)
*Lin-Juan Wang,Da Li,Huiling Duan,Jianxiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文建立了神经网络动力学粗粒化的系统理论框架，定义了学习算子并证明了下降定理、精确闭合定理和动力学粗粒化定理，成功将有限宽非线性ReLU网络的训练动力学精确降维为五个独立矩的宏观模型，实现了对训练输出和学习算子演化的精确预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络参数空间高维且复杂，传统方法难以直接分析训练动力学。需要建立一套系统的数学框架，将微观参数动力学粗粒化为低维宏观可观测量空间，从而实现对训练过程的预测与控制。

Method: 1. 依据神经网络训练规律定义学习算子；2. 建立学习算子的下降定理，判定算子是否足以刻画学习动力学；3. 推导精确闭合定理与动力学粗粒化定理，构建系统性粗粒化建模框架；4. 以固定模式下的有限宽非线性ReLU网络为例，精确推导由五个独立矩组成的动力学粗粒化模型。

Result: 推导出的五矩粗粒化模型能精确预测有限宽ReLU网络在固定模式下的训练输出演化及学习算子的动态变化，验证了理论框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 三大定理构成了神经网络训练动力学粗粒化建模的系统性理论基础；该框架不仅适用于可精确闭合的简单网络，还可指导更复杂神经网络近似粗粒化模型的开发。

Abstract: Dynamical coarse-graining of neural networks gives macroscopic observables and enables training to be predicted, and controlled in a state space far smaller than the parameter space. In this paper, we first define the learning operator following the training law of neural networks and establish a descent theorem for the learning operator, which serves to determine whether the operator is sufficient to characterize the learning dynamics. Then the exact closure theorem and the dynamic coarse-graining theorem are presented in sequence. The three theorems established in this paper provide a systematic framework for constructing and assessing dynamical coarse-grained models of neural-network training. Finally, we demonstrate the applicability of the theorems with a finite-width nonlinear ReLU network. Its dynamic coarse-grained model for a fixed pattern is exactly derived and composed of five independent moments. The model exactly predict the training outputs and evolving learning operator. The framework may also guide the development of approximate coarse-grained models for more general neural networks when exact closure is unavailable.

</details>


### [83] [Bayesian Evidence Accumulation for Graph-Based Learning in Dynamic Data-Driven Applications Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36190)
*Moises Ponce,Sarah Harkins Dayton,Ioannis Schizas,Vasileios Maroulas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出B-EAGLe（贝叶斯证据累积图学习框架），受CBGT-网络启发，将图分类重构为序贯证据累积过程，结合GNN编码器与贝叶斯累积器，随观测到达增量更新后验置信度，仅在置信度充足时做决策。在CIFAR10上实现具竞争力性能并提供 principled 置信度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN框架本质静态，难以适应动态数据驱动应用系统（DDDAS）中观测序贯到达且伴随不确定性的场景。需要一种能在线累积证据、量化不确定性并自适应决策的概率图学习方法。

Method: B-EAGLe包含两大核心模块：(1) GNN编码器将图观测映射为潜在表示；(2) 贝叶斯累积器维护类别标签的后验分布，随每个新观测到达按贝叶斯规则增量更新后验，并设置置信度阈值作为停止准则，仅当后验最大概率超过阈值时输出预测。框架受大脑CBGT回路的证据累积决策机制启发。

Result: 在CIFAR10图分类任务上，B-EAGLe达到与静态基线相当的分类准确率，同时展现出优越的序贯置信度校准能力：随着观测数量增加，预测置信度单调上升且与准确率强相关；在部分观测条件下仍能给出可靠的不确定性量化。

Conclusion: B-EAGLe为动态图学习提供了一个理论 principled 的贝叶斯序贯决策范式，成功将神经科学中的证据累积机制迁移至GNN，实现了准确性与不确定性感知的统一，为DDDAS等实时流式图分析场景奠定了方法学基础。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) are widely used in modern machine learning, yet most existing frameworks remain fundamentally static, limiting their applicability in Dynamic Data Driven Application Systems (DDDAS) where observations arrive sequentially under uncertainty. We introduce B-EAGLe (Bayesian Evidence Accumulation for Graph-Based Learning), a probabilistic framework inspired by CBGT-Net (cortico-basal ganglia-thalamic network) that reformulates graph classification as a sequential evidence accumulation process. B-EAGLe combines a GNN encoder with a Bayesian accumulator that incrementally updates posterior beliefs over class labels as new observations arrive, committing to a decision only when sufficient confidence is achieved. Experiments on CIFAR10 demonstrate competitive performance together with principled confidence estimation under sequential observations.

</details>


### [84] [Base Models Can Detect Hidden Intentions that Prompted Classifiers Miss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36202)
*Oz Amram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了人格头部似然评估（PHLE）方法，通过在预训练基础模型上添加适当的头部提示词来评估输出序列的似然，从而解码后训练模型中的潜意识人格信号。实验表明PHLE能区分不同人格提示词生成的数字序列，以及带有隐藏有害意图但表面过滤后的模型输出，且该信号可跨模型家族部分迁移，为人格选择模型（PSM）提供了非平凡预测的证据，并可用于审计模型隐藏目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源于人格选择模型（PSM）假设：后训练只是从预训练中学到的人格中选择一个。潜意识学习研究显示模型能将隐蔽信号（如“爱猫头鹰”）嵌入语义无关的输出（如数字串）中，且学生模型会继承这些特征。作者旨在开发一种方法来检测并分类这些潜意识信号，验证PSM的预测，并为审计模型隐藏目标提供实用工具。

Method: 提出人格头部似然评估（PHLE）方法：在基础预训练模型前添加适当的头部提示词（如人格描述），计算目标输出序列（如数字串）在该条件下的似然。通过比较不同人格头部下的似然分数，解码并分类嵌入在语义无关输出中的潜意识人格信号。实验对比了提示分类器（包括生成模型自身）的性能。

Result: PHLE成功区分了由‘你爱猫头鹰’与‘你爱鹰’提示词生成的数字序列，也区分了带有隐藏伤害用户意图但经过过滤移除明显不对齐的模型输出与无系统提示词模型的输出。提示分类器（包括生成模型自身）均接近随机猜测水平。信号在模型家族间部分持久化：Qwen基础模型可读取Olmo生成的信号，反之亦然。

Conclusion: PHLE为人格选择模型（PSM）提供了非平凡预测的实证支持，证明预训练模型能通过似然评估解码后训练模型输出中嵌入的潜意识人格信号。该方法不依赖提示分类器，可跨模型家族迁移，具有审计大语言模型隐藏目标的实际应用潜力。

Abstract: The Persona Selection Model (PSM) posits that post-training selects a persona the model learned during pre-training. Work on subliminal learning has shown that models can impart covert signals (like "love of owls") into semantically unrelated outputs like strings of numbers, and that students trained on these outputs inherit those traits. In this work I show that by evaluating the likelihood of such outputs under the base pre-trained model with an appropriate header, a method I call persona-header likelihood evaluation (PHLE), one can decode these subliminal signals and classify between them. For example, PHLE distinguishes number sequences generated by a post-trained model prompted with "You love owls" from those prompted with "You love eagles", and distinguishes answers from a model with a secret desire to harm the user, filtered to remove any noticeable misalignment, from those of a model with no system prompt. In both cases prompted classifiers, including the generating model itself, remain near chance. The signal partially persists across model families, with the Qwen base model reading signals generated by Olmo and vice versa. This is evidence for a non-trivial prediction of the PSM, and the method may be practically useful for auditing the hidden objectives of models.

</details>


### [85] [Concorde: Geometry-Aware Link Prediction via Decoupled Energy Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36204)
*Ege Demirci,Ambuj Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出Concorde框架解决稀疏图链接预测难题，通过三个解耦分支（无参数谱锚点、混合曲率残差流形、双曲切丛向量场）融合预测链接，在五个基准上实现最先进的早期检索精度，稀疏场景下P@10%几乎翻倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有链接预测方法在稀疏图上表现不佳：扩散启发式方法缺乏连通性，几何方法在将学习表示与扩散算子耦合时遭遇谱不稳定性。需要一种能同时处理拓扑稳定性、几何表示学习和非局部结构对齐的统一框架。

Method: Concorde是一个基于能量的框架，将链接似然分解为三个结构解耦分支：(i) 无参数谱锚点建立排列不变的拓扑稳定性；(ii) 可学习积流形ℍ^{d_h}_{c_h} × 𝕊^{d_s}_{c_s}上的混合曲率残差，由特征感知Ollivier-Ricci曲率代理正则化；(iii) 双曲空间切丛上的连续向量场实现跨拓扑间隙的非局部结构对齐。分支通过学习门控融合，采用与检索式评估对齐的N-pair排序损失训练。

Result: 在涵盖稀疏、稠密、无特征和有属性五个基准测试中，Concorde建立了最先进的早期检索精度。在稀疏设置下，P@10%指标比领先基线提升近一倍。

Conclusion: Concorde通过谱锚点保证拓扑稳定性、混合曲率流形建模几何结构、切丛向量场捕捉非局部对齐，有效解决了稀疏图链接预测中的连通性缺失和谱不稳定性问题，为图表示学习提供了新的理论与实践范式。

Abstract: Link prediction remains difficult in sparse graphs, where diffusion heuristics lack connectivity and geometric methods suffer spectral instability when coupling learned representations with diffusion operators. We introduce Concorde, an energy-based framework that resolves this by decomposing link likelihood into three structurally decoupled branches: (i) a parameter-free spectral anchor that establishes permutation-invariant topological stability; (ii) a mixed-curvature residual on a learnable $\mathbb{H}^{d_h}_{c_h} \times \mathbb{S}^{d_s}_{c_s}$ product manifold, regularized by a feature-aware Ollivier-Ricci curvature proxy; and (iii) a continuous vector field on the tangent bundle of hyperbolic space for non-local structural alignment across topological gaps. The branches are fused via learned gating and trained with an N-pair ranking loss aligned with retrieval-style evaluation. Across five benchmarks spanning sparse, dense, featureless, and attributed regimes, Concorde establishes state-of-the-art early retrieval precision, nearly doubling P@10% over leading baselines in sparse settings.

</details>


### [86] [From Generation to Scoring: Scaling LLM-Based Semantic Labeling at LinkedIn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36383)
*Jian Sheng,Sriram Vasudevan,Sundara Raman Ramachandran,Yang Hu,Junyu Zhou,Xiaojing Ma,Haolan Qin,Tiffany Zhou,Vedant Vipul Jhaveri,Ashwini Bhingare,Chinmay Kini,Qing Lan,Xiaoqing Wang,Suleyman Cetintas,Fedor Borisyuk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端的语义评分方法，将工业级LLM标注器从打包生成YES/NO列表改为单候选预填评分，并结合多项评分（MIS）实现百级候选并行打分，显著提升吞吐量且保持决策一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业界LLM标注器常将多个候选标签打包进单一提示并生成YES/NO列表，但这种方式将本应独立的决策耦合在一起，增加了解码开销，且依赖列表格式的良构性，存在效率与鲁棒性瓶颈。

Method: 1. 将标注任务重构为语义评分：在单候选提示上微调模型，使其通过仅预填（prefill-only）前向传播直接输出单个答案token的得分。2. 引入多项评分（MIS），通过注意力掩码实现数百候选在单序列中并行打分，消除候选间干扰。3. 离线批量推理实现为模型无关的Ray Data API；模型服务层集成至SGLang。

Result: 在LinkedIn画像资质标注任务中：语义评分在候选重排序时决策变更率≤0.01%，仅用打包生成约1/4.7的训练数据即可达到相同参考标签一致性；MIS栈离线吞吐提升1.24倍，在线服务（p99延迟1秒目标）提升1.9倍。

Conclusion: 语义评分+MIS范式有效解耦独立决策，大幅降低训练数据需求与推理延迟，且已落地生产环境并贡献至开源生态（Ray Data、SGLang），具备工业级通用性与可复用性。

Abstract: For efficiency, industrial large language model (LLM) labelers often pack many candidate labels into one prompt and generate a YES/NO answer for each, even when each decision depends only on the entity and one candidate. Generating these independent decisions as one answer list couples them, spends decoding on the list, and relies on the list being well-formed. In this paper, we present an end-to-end recipe that reformulates the labeler as semantic scoring, fine-tuning it on single-candidate prompts to return each decision as one answer-token score from a prefill-only pass. We also adapt Multiple-Item Scoring (MIS) to score hundreds of candidates in one sequence with attention masked between them. Deployed for profile qualification labeling at LinkedIn, semantic scoring changes at most 0.01% of decisions when candidates are reordered and matches the agreement with reference labels that packed generation reaches only with about 4.7x as many training pairs. The MIS-based stack raises throughput over the deployed stack by 1.24x offline and 1.9x in model serving under a one-second p99 latency target. For offline batch inference, we implement MIS-based scoring as a reusable, model-agnostic Ray Data API proposed for upstream adoption. Model serving uses MIS support contributed to SGLang.

</details>


### [87] [Three Worlds for the Price of a Logarithm: Baseline-Protected Best-of-Both-Worlds Combinatorial Semi-Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36245)
*Ting Hu,Luanda Cai,Emmanouil-Vasileios Vlatakis-Gkaragkounis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DUET（基于经验测试的加倍策略），一种无需预知时长的分阶段混合方案，用于组合半强盗问题。该方案通过两个可观测测试决定何时加倍学习权重和经验遗憾预算并重启，结合熵FTRL（混合阶段）和混合FTRL（纯阶段）两种基础学习器，实现了基线保护、对抗鲁棒性和随机适应性三大保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进基线策略需要向其之外探索，但这正是可能损失性能的地方。现有方法难以同时实现：对基线策略的对数级遗憾保护、对抗环境下的√T级遗憾、以及独立同分布环境下的多项式对数级遗憾，且无需预知时间范围T、最优动作间隙gap或环境类型。

Method: DUET采用分阶段混合方案：两个可观测测试分别控制学习权重加倍和经验遗憾预算加倍并重启。混合阶段使用熵正则化FTRL（entropy-FTRL），纯阶段使用混合FTRL（hybrid-FTRL）。假设基线策略对每个臂选择概率为正。

Result: 期望基线遗憾为对数级O(log T)；对抗非预期损失下，对任何固定动作的期望遗憾为Õ(√T)；独立同分布损失且存在唯一最优动作时，期望遗憾为gap依赖的多项式对数级。所有保证在每个确定性时刻均成立，算法无需知晓T、gap或环境类型。

Conclusion: 通过单一框架和两个基础学习器，仅以对数因子为代价，同时实现了基线保护、对抗鲁棒性和随机适应性三大目标，解决了组合半强盗问题中的核心权衡。

Abstract: Improving on a baseline requires exploring away from it, precisely where its performance may be lost. We propose DUET (Doubling Under Empirical Tests), a horizon-free phased mixing scheme for combinatorial semi-bandits with arbitrary finite action sets. Two observable tests decide when to double the learning weight and when to double the empirical-regret budget and restart. We instantiate the scheme with entropy-FTRL in mixed phases and hybrid-FTRL in pure phases. Assuming the baseline selects every arm with positive probability, this instantiation keeps expected baseline regret logarithmic in $T$, while its expected regret to every fixed action is $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$ under non-anticipating adversarial losses and polylogarithmic, gap-dependent, under i.i.d. losses with a unique optimal action. All three guarantees hold at every deterministic horizon, and the algorithm knows neither the horizon, nor the gap, nor the environment type. Baseline protection, adversarial robustness, and stochastic adaptivity thus come from one scheme and two base learners, at the price of a logarithm.

</details>


### [88] [Freeze, Don't Clone: Fine-Tuning with Expressive Policies Without Retaining Offline Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36247)
*Sami Nur Islam,Johan Obando-Ceron,Doina Precup,Aaron Courville,Pablo Samuel Castro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现离线到在线强化学习中无保留微调的核心失败模式在于Actor端：离线数据消失后继续在线行为克隆会迅速抹除预训练先验，导致表示崩溃和动作多样性丧失，使Critic缺乏有效探索。冻结预训练行为先验可打破这一反馈循环，在IFQL、FQL、QC三种算法和七个OGBench任务上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线到在线RL方法大多假设离线数据在微调阶段仍可用，但实际场景中往往无法保留离线数据（无保留设置）。以往研究主要归因于Critic在分布偏移下的重新校准问题，但作者发现Actor端存在互补的失败模式：离线数据消失后继续在线行为克隆会破坏预训练的行为分布。

Method: 提出冻结预训练的表达性行为先验（停止在线行为拟合），而非继续在有限且非平稳的在线经验上进行行为克隆。该方法应用于隐式流Q学习(IFQL)、流Q学习(FQL)和动作分块RL(QC)三种算法，在七个OGBench领域进行实验验证。

Result: 冻结预训练先验在所有三种算法和七个OGBench任务上均显著提升无保留微调性能。实验追踪表明：继续在线行为克隆会迅速抹除预训练先验，引发表示崩溃和动作多样性丧失，使Critic缺乏有用探索；冻结先验打破了这一行为反馈循环，保持离线覆盖的同时允许价值学习驱动持续改进。

Conclusion: 在无保留离线到在线RL设置中，Actor端的行为反馈循环是核心瓶颈而非仅Critic重新校准。冻结预训练行为先验是一种简单有效的解决方案，能保持离线学到的行为覆盖，同时让价值学习在线改进策略，为该设置下的算法设计提供了新视角。

Abstract: Offline-to-online reinforcement learning uses offline pretraining to accelerate subsequent online learning. While many methods assume the offline dataset remains available throughout fine-tuning, there are many settings where this is not the case. Prior work attributes failures in this setting primarily to critic recalibration under distribution shift. We identify a complementary actor-side failure: once the offline data disappears, continued behavioral cloning (BC) no longer preserves the offline behavior distribution and instead refits the behavioral model to the agent's limited, and nonstationary, online experience. We demonstrate that stopping online behavioral fitting while preserving the pretrained expressive prior substantially improves no-retention fine-tuning across implicit flow Q-learning (IFQL), flow Q-learning (FQL), and action-chunked RL (QC) on seven OGBench domains. We trace this failure to a behavioral feedback loop: continuing online behavioral cloning rapidly erases the pretrained prior, inducing representational collapse and action diversity loss that starves the critic of useful exploration. Freezing the prior breaks this cycle, preserving offline coverage while allowing value learning to drive continuous improvement.

</details>


### [89] [What Should an Information Bottleneck Encoder Learn? Separating Representation Learning from Rate Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36255)
*Hassan Tavakoli,Bella Bose*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究信息瓶颈（IB）中表示与速率控制的分离性质，证明在联合高斯数据下IB解可分解为与β无关的方向W和标量增益D(β)。进一步提出无预算表示F(X)及充分性亏损ε_F，给出分离间隙的上下界，并扩展到新目标分布和多数据域池聚场景。实验表明，冻结CNN表示配合每预算一个压缩器-解码器对，相比端到端VIB显著降低训练计算量（MACs降低7.7倍，耗时减半），在强压缩下准确率优势明显，弱压缩下VIB表现略好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统IB方法需为每个工作点（β值）重新训练编码器，计算开销大。高斯情况下IB解具有表示与速率控制分离的性质，但该性质在非高斯模型中是否成立未知。作者旨在探索这种分离是否普适，并利用该性质设计高效的多预算表示学习框架，减少重复训练成本。

Method: 1. 理论分析：定义无预算表示F(X)，引入充分性亏损ε_F = I(X;Y|F(X))刻画分离间隙，证明有限输入下最坏情况间隙至少为ε_F，有限标签字母表下间隙由ε_F统一界定。推广至新目标分布和多数据域池聚，引入条件对齐项。2. 实验设计：在MNIST、SVHN、CIFAR-10、STL-10上使用冻结CNN作为共享表示F(X)，为每个预算训练独立的压缩器-解码器对，共8个预设预算，10个随机种子。对比端到端VIB的训练MACs、壁钟时间、预算平均准确率、交叉熵、ECE及代理拉格朗日量。

Result: 冻结表示法将估计训练MACs降低7.7倍，壁钟时间减少约2倍。单域预算平均准确率65.6%对57.3%，池聚多域下67.1%对57.1%。优势集中在强压缩区（VIB崩溃处）。弱压缩（λ≤0.036）下，VIB交叉熵和ECE更低；代理拉格朗日量单域下VIB更高，宏平均与池聚VIB无显著差异，池聚测试集上冻结法更低。

Conclusion: 表示与速率控制的分离在非高斯模型中近似成立，通过充分性亏损可量化分离间隙。基于冻结共享表示的多预算压缩框架在计算效率和强压缩性能上显著优于端到端VIB，适合资源受限场景下的多速率部署。弱压缩下端到端联合优化仍有优势，提示混合策略可能更优。

Abstract: The information bottleneck (IB) trades compression of an input $X$ against prediction of a target $Y$ through a multiplier $β=1/λ$, yet each operating point is usually served by training, or fully conditioning, a new encoder. For jointly Gaussian data, we show that the IB solution factorizes as $A(β)=D(β)W$: the information-bearing directions $W$ do not depend on $β$, and $β$ acts only through scalar gains. We ask whether this separation of \emph{representation} from \emph{rate control} survives beyond Gaussian models, treating the budget as a given operating parameter rather than a quantity to be optimized. For a budget-free representation \(F(X)\), the \emph{separation gap} is controlled by the \emph{sufficiency deficit} $ε_F=I(X;Y\mid F(X))$: We show there is at least $ε_F$ in the worst case for finite inputs, and it is bounded uniformly in $λ$ by a function of $ε_F$ for finite label alphabets. The same certificate extends to a new target distribution and to pooled multi-domain data, where a conditional-alignment term appears. On MNIST, SVHN, CIFAR-10 and STL-10 (10 seeds, eight prespecified budgets), a frozen CNN representation with one compressor--decoder pair per budget reduces estimated training multiply--accumulate operations (MACs) by $7.7\times$ and wall-clock time by about $2\times$ relative to end-to-end VIBs, and attains higher budget-averaged accuracy ($65.6\%$ versus $57.3\%$ per domain; $67.1\%$ versus $57.1\%$ pooled), with the advantage concentrated at strong compression, where the VIBs collapse. At weak compression ($λ\le0.036$), VIB has higher cross-entropy and ECE; its surrogate Lagrangian is higher per domain, indistinguishable from the pooled VIB in the macro average, and lower on the pooled test set.

</details>


### [90] [Recall or Relearn? A Mixture of In-Context Experts for Recurring Concept Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36585)
*Zhangkai Wu,Xu Zhang,Zhihong Cui,Yiqi Wang,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出MICE（Mixture of In-Context Experts），一种无需训练的表格基础模型（TFM）流式学习方法，通过在检测到概念漂移时切割数据流、合并历史片段到存储上下文，并用双层加权机制（快层按对数损失加权存储上下文与FIFO窗口，慢层混合快层预测与最新FIFO窗口）实现对重现概念的高效回忆，理论上给出相对于重新学习的准确率增益界，并在合成与真实流上超越重置基线及其他池化策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型（TFM）在网络入侵检测等Web数据流场景中通过更新上下文标签行适应新概念，但当旧概念在离开上下文后重现时，TFM面临“回忆存储行”还是“从新标签重学”的抉择；现有重置策略会丢弃历史知识，而全量存储每个片段又受限于上下文容量，因此需要一种在容量约束下自动决定合并与回忆、并能理论保证性能的方法。

Method: MICE包含三个核心组件：1) 分段与合并：在检测到变化点处切割流，若TFM在该片段上的估计额外误差在容忍度内且未超容量，则将片段合并为一个存储上下文（专家）。2) 快层加权：对所有存储上下文与FIFO窗口，按最近标签批次上的对数损失计算权重，输出加权预测；理论证明在纯存储上下文与边界条件下，其期望准确率增益等于存储上下文准确率与TFM学习曲线之间归一化正面积（减去首批次项）。3) 慢层混合：将快层预测与最新标签行组成的FIFO窗口预测混合，并对折扣半Brier遗憾给出逐时刻上界。

Result: 在带随机边界相位的网格流上，MICE显著优于检测到漂移即清空上下文的重置策略；合并机制使专家数量少于存储所有片段的池化方法，且在固定分段下时间匹配的快照池仅在平移网格上与MICE持平。在真实数据流上，MICE在所有对比策略中达到最高平均准确率。学习曲线预测的回忆增益顺序与观测一致。

Conclusion: MICE通过理论指导的分段合并与双层加权，实现了表格基础模型在流式环境下对重现概念的高效回忆，兼顾容量约束、适应速度与长期性能，为TFM在非平稳流上的部署提供了免训练、有保证的解决方案。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) are being applied to Web data streams such as network intrusion detection, where they adapt by updating the labelled rows of their context without retraining. When a past concept returns after leaving the context, a TFM faces a recall-or-relearn decision between restoring stored rows of the concept and relearning it from new labels. We propose MICE (Mixture of In-Context Experts), a training-free learner that cuts the stream into segments at detected changes. It merges a closed segment into a stored context when the TFM's estimated additional error on the segment is within a tolerance, under a capacity limit on stored contexts. A fast level weights the stored contexts and first-in-first-out (FIFO) windows by the log-loss of their predictions on the last labelled batch. We show that, with pure stored contexts and a margin condition, this fast level gains over relearning, in expected accuracy, the normalised positive area between the stored context's accuracy and the TFM's learning curve, up to a first-batch term. A slow level mixes the fast level's prediction with that of the FIFO window of the last labelled rows and bounds the discounted half-Brier regret of MICE against this window at every time. Learning curves measured beforehand predict the ordering of the observed recall gains. MICE outperforms reset, which empties the context at detected drifts, on grid streams with random boundary phases. Merging keeps fewer experts than pools that store every segment, and with fixed segmentation a time-matched snapshot pool matches MICE on the shifted grid. On real streams, MICE has the highest mean accuracy of all policies we compare.

</details>


### [91] [EVA-Flow: Learning Shared Chemistry for Unified Environment-Aware Molecular Conformation Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36280)
*Bing Yan,Anuroop Sriram,Zachary Ward Ulissi,Kyunghyun Cho,Ricky T. Q. Chen,Benjamin Kurt Miller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了EVA-Flow，一个统一的环境感知分子构象生成框架，通过预训练和跨环境微调，在真空、蛋白-配体对接、溶剂化和晶体堆积四种环境中实现了分子构象的准确生成，尤其在数据稀缺场景下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子三维结构是热力学、动力学、结晶和成核模拟的基础，但分子几何构型会随环境剧烈变化。现有模型要么忽略环境效应，要么为每种环境单独训练模型，缺乏跨环境迁移能力，且在配体结合和结晶等数据稀缺场景下表现不佳。

Method: EVA-Flow采用统一框架学习共享化学结构，通过环境条件生成建模适应不同环境。具体包括：预训练学习通用化学表征，跨环境微调适应特定环境，利用环境条件生成模型实现同一分子在不同环境下的不同构象生成。

Result: 在四种环境（真空、蛋白-配体对接、溶剂化、晶体堆积）中，预训练和跨环境微调在三个数据稀缺设置中增益最大。评估显示EVA-Flow能覆盖广泛的溶剂化构象，且对跨环境共享分子能生成环境特异性构象。

Conclusion: EVA-Flow验证了跨环境迁移学习在构象生成中的可行性，统一模型优于单独训练模型，尤其适用于数据有限的药物发现和材料设计场景，为环境感知分子建模提供了新范式。

Abstract: Molecular 3D structures underpin simulations of thermodynamics, kinetics, crystallization, and nucleation, making their accurate prediction central to drug discovery and materials design. However, molecular geometry can change dramatically across environments. Existing models either ignore environmental effects or train separate models for each environment. We hypothesize that transfer learning across contexts is possible for conformer generation and that a general model will perform better when training data are limited, as in ligand binding and crystallization. We introduce EVA-Flow, a unified framework for environment-aware molecular conformation generation that learns shared chemical structure and adapts it through environment-conditioned generative modeling. Across vacuum, protein-ligand docking, solvation, and crystal packing, pretraining and cross-environment finetuning provide their largest gains in the three data-scarce settings. Evaluation against multiple reference conformations shows that EVA-Flow covers a broad range of solvation conformations. Analyses of molecules shared across environments support its ability to generate different conformations for the same molecule in different environments.

</details>


### [92] [Bridging Exploration and Loss of Plasticity in On-Policy Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36309)
*Andrea Vasco Grieco,Evelyn D'Elia,Edoardo Caldarelli,Raffaello Camoriano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文深入分析了近端策略优化（PPO）中各向同性高斯策略的标准差σ对可塑性丧失（LoP）的影响，提供了理论与实验双重论证，并在标准基准及7自由度机械臂实机部署中验证了分析结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习算法在长期训练中会丧失从新数据自适应学习的能力，即可塑性丧失（LoP）。尽管已有多个指标与LoP相关，但其因果机制及几何诱因尚不明确。本文聚焦于连续动作空间最先进的PPO高斯策略形式，旨在揭示策略标准差σ如何影响LoP，并为广泛采用的缓解技术提供理论解释。

Method: 采用理论分析与实验验证相结合的方法。在PPO各向同性高斯策略框架下，系统考察σ对多个LoP特征指标的影响；提出σ调节下的几何解释，阐述现有LoP缓解技术的有效性机制；在LoP标准基准上与主流基线对比，并部署至7自由度机械臂进行实机策略评估。

Result: 实验与理论均表明σ对LoP程度具有显著影响：σ过大或过小均会加剧可塑性丧失，存在最优σ区间可维持长期适应性。基准测试显示针对σ的调控优于标准基线；7自由度机械臂实机实验进一步确证了分析结论的实用性。

Conclusion: 策略标准差σ是导致可塑性丧失的关键几何因子之一，精准调控σ可有效缓解LoP。该工作为连续控制任务中长期可塑性保持提供了新的理论视角与实践指导。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms have been observed to lose the ability to adaptively learn from new data, a phenomenon termed Loss of Plasticity (LoP). While many metrics have been shown to correlate with it, the causal mechanisms and the geometric conditions from which it can arise have not been clearly identified. We analyze LoP in Proximal Policy Optimization with isotropic Gaussian policies, the state-of-the-art formulation for many continuous action spaces in RL. We report multiple metrics which characterize the effect that the policy standard deviation $σ$ has on LoP, and we also suggest related explanations for the effectiveness of widely adopted LoP mitigation techniques. We observe a significant effect of $σ$ on the extent of LoP, and accompany this with both theoretical and experimental supporting arguments. Our experimental validation consists of LoP benchmark evaluations against standard baselines as well as real-world policy deployment on a 7-DoF robotic arm.

</details>


### [93] [Confidence Is Not a Promise: A Reference Audit of Typed Decision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36311)
*Pedro Carvalho Brom*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究类型决策模型返回的概率分布被误视为保证的问题，通过实验对比开放权重模型 Laya 与仅通过 API 可用的 Jev 1.13，揭示概率仅许可群体层面的陈述而非单一决策，提出 Learn-then-Test 与 Beta 下界规则等方法以在分布偏移、噪声、多语言等场景下保持校准承诺，并发布冻结答案、数据表与代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软件系统往往将模型输出的概率视为对单次决策的可靠保证，但概率本质上仅许可对决策群体的统计陈述。缺乏在分布偏移、噪声、多语言、标注稀缺等真实条件下对校准承诺的系统性度量与保障机制。

Method: 在 190 个可交换评估中，对比点估计阈值、Learn-then-Test、Beta 下界规则三种决策门控策略；跨 51 个语言环境测试 Jev 的校准迁移；引入合成打字噪声评估鲁棒性；在四个领域微调 Laya 并与 Jev 对比标签效率；设计贝叶斯重训练循环应对漂移，并在 M3 月度序列上评估方向预测；利用 AUROC 衡量置信度区分能力，并通过精确联合博弈分析 API 舍入后的解释忠实度。

Result: 点估计阈值在 147/190 评估中破坏承诺，Learn-then-Test 全部保持，Beta 下界规则在 128 个评估中决策更多且从不更少但无保证。Jev 仅在 8/51 语言环境保持 95% 承诺。噪声破坏干净文本校准，带噪训练提升噪声准确率且不损干净性能。Laya 需 250–8000 标签才能非劣于 Jev，LIAR2 上未达标。漂移下贝叶斯重训练循环未支持预注册声称，但能避免不必要的重训练，重新校准阈值能更频繁地保持承诺。M3 上两模型均不优于常数预测。置信度 AUROC 0.80–0.96 但不能筛选忠实解释，API 舍入到两位小数仍允许通过联合博弈提取单决策解释信息。

Conclusion: 概率输出不应被视为单决策保证，需采用群体层面的校准承诺机制（如 Learn-then-Test 或下界规则）。跨语言、噪声、漂移等真实条件下，模型校准会显著退化，需针对性缓解。小模型经微调可在有限标签下逼近大模型，但并非普适。置信度虽能区分对错，但不足以保证解释忠实度，API 精度限制仍可通过博弈论方法部分缓解。开放冻结输出、数据表与代码促进可复现的责任 AI 评估。

Abstract: Typed decision models return a probability distribution over options supplied by the caller, and software tends to act on that number as if it were a guarantee. We measure what it licenses, comparing the open-weight Laya, in five checkpoints, with Jev 1.13, available only through an API. A probability licenses a statement about groups of decisions, not about one decision, and a gate that promises an accuracy among the cases it decides alone keeps that promise only with a lower bound checked on data like its traffic. Across 190 exchangeable evaluations, a threshold chosen by point estimate breaks the promise in 147, Learn then Test keeps it in all of them, and a Beta lower-bound rule decides more items on average than Learn then Test in 128 and fewer in none, without its guarantee. Calibrated in English, Jev keeps a promise of 95% in 8 of 51 locales. Ordinary typing noise breaks a promise calibrated on clean text, and, in exploratory runs, training with noise raises the noisy accuracy with no loss on clean text. In four further domains, a fine-tuned Laya needs from 250 to 8,000 labels to be non-inferior to Jev, and does not get there on LIAR2. Under drift, the preregistered claim for a Bayesian retraining loop is not supported: the loop avoids needless retraining where the change does not degrade the model, at a cost equivalent to recalibration alone, and re-qualifying the threshold in every window keeps the promise more often where it does. On the monthly series of M3, neither decider predicts the direction of the next value better than a constant answer. Confidence separates right from wrong answers with an AUROC from 0.80 to 0.96, but it does not select faithful explanations, and exact coalition games of single decisions show what an explanation can still say through an API that rounds to two decimals. We release the frozen answers with a datasheet, a promise check and the code.

</details>


### [94] [Compote: Collapse-Resistant Multiple Hypothesis Learning For Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37005)
*Louis Castres Saint Martin,Clement Calauzenes,Alain Rakotomamonjy,Patrick Gallinari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出COMPOTE，一种多假设Transformer模型，通过退火目标从均匀监督过渡到赢家通吃分配，使假设专业化到不同目录区域，解决序列推荐中下一项预测的固有歧义问题，并在三个Amazon Reviews 2023基准上超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准序列推荐器将多个合理的下一项延续压缩为单一预测，混淆了用户意图；现有多头架构常出现头坍缩问题，无法有效捕捉多样化用户意图。

Method: 引入COllapse-resistant Multiple hyPOThesis lEarning (COMPOTE)框架：采用多假设Transformer，配合退火目标函数从均匀监督逐渐过渡到赢家通吃分配，强制假设专业化到不同目录区域；推理时使用高效、无归一化的最大相似度规则。

Result: 理论上证明：在总体情况下，最优解按最大熵量化原则划分条件下一项分布，池化得分保持贝叶斯最优排序；泛化界随假设数量对数增长。实验上在三个Amazon Reviews 2023基准上，仅用Item ID即超越强ID基、生成式及多兴趣基线；同容量消融实验确认性能提升源于退火目标防止头坍缩，而非额外容量、集成或路由。

Conclusion: COMPOTE通过退火多假设学习有效解决下一项预测的歧义性，实现了假设的专业化分区，保持最优排序性质，且泛化能力强，为序列推荐提供了新范式。

Abstract: Next-item prediction is inherently ambiguous: a single interaction history often admits multiple plausible continuations across distinct catalogue regions. Standard sequential recommenders compress these alternatives into a single prediction, conflating user intents. We introduce COllapse-resistant Multiple hyPOThesis lEarning (COMPOTE), a multi-hypothesis transformer trained with an annealed objective that transitions from uniform supervision to a winner-takes-all assignment. This mechanism forces hypotheses to specialize into distinct catalogue regions, while inference relies on an efficient, normalizer-free max-similarity rule. Theoretically, we show that in the population case, the optimal solutions partition the conditional next-item distribution according to a max-entropy quantization principle, while pooled scores preserve the Bayes-optimal ranking. We also prove a generalization bound scaling logarithmically with the number of hypotheses. Across three Amazon Reviews 2023 benchmarks, COMPOTE outperforms strong ID-based, generative, and multi-interest baselines using item IDs alone. Matched-capacity ablations confirm that performance gains stem directly from the annealed objective, preventing the head collapse observed in conventional multi-head architectures, rather than added capacity, ensembling, or routing.

</details>


### [95] [AHEAD: Adaptive Neural Acceleration for Large-Scale Linear Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36321)
*Sihao Liu,Zhichao Hou,Huayue Gu,Raiyan Rahman,Liang Wu,Ruozhou Yu,Xiaorui Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出AHEAD，一种用于大规模线性规划的学习加速器，通过预测首阶段步长并结合学习切换策略，在保持求解器更新规则不变的前提下实现了6.5-11.6倍的端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模线性规划（百万级变量/约束）对时间和内存极具挑战。首阶段方法（如原偶混合梯度）虽避免矩阵分解且可扩展，但需要大量迭代。现有学习加速器虽能减少迭代，却因推理开销大、每层处理全量变量、预测迭代仍需求解器修正、且所有实例运行相同层数，导致端到端加速效果差。核心问题是：如何设计神经加速器在不同规模、精度下实现真正的端到端加速？

Method: AHEAD保留首阶段求解器的更新规则，仅预测步长。其权重共享预测器读取紧凑的求解器统计量（如残差、目标值等），输出原始和对偶步长的缩放因子，使每次热启动迭代保持低开销。引入学习切换策略，根据实例特征和目标精度自适应决定热启动迭代次数，之后切换回原始求解器按自身终止准则完成求解。

Result: 在包含高达10^7变量的打包线性规划上，AHEAD相比Gurobi首阶段求解器冷启动实现6.5-11.6倍端到端加速，且在未见规模/分布实例、PageRank线性规划、不同难度级别和目标精度下均保持增益。

Conclusion: AHEAD通过轻量级步长预测和自适应切换策略，有效解决了学习推理开销与求解器迭代减少之间的权衡，在大规模线性规划上实现了稳健的端到端加速，且泛化至不同问题类型、规模和精度要求。

Abstract: Large-scale linear programs with millions of variables and constraints strain time and memory budgets. First-order methods such as the primal-dual hybrid gradient scale to this regime because they avoid matrix factorizations, but they require many iterations. Learned accelerators can reduce these iterations, yet fewer iterations do not guarantee a shorter solve. Learning inference also takes time, and running the accelerator longer trades solver time for inference time. Existing unrolled accelerators handle this trade-off poorly: each layer processes every variable and constraint, the predicted iterates still need solver refinement, and every instance runs the same layers. We therefore ask: What neural accelerator design achieves end-to-end speedups on large-scale linear programs across sizes and target accuracies? We introduce AHEAD, an accelerator that predicts step sizes for first-order linear programming solvers while keeping their update rules. Its weight-shared predictor reads compact solver statistics and rescales the primal and dual step sizes, so each warm-start iteration stays inexpensive and a short warm-start phase hands the solver a warm start. A learned handoff policy sets the number of warm-start iterations for each instance and target accuracy, after which the solver finishes under its own termination criterion. On packing linear programs with up to $10^7$ variables, AHEAD achieves $6.5$--$11.6\times$ end-to-end speedups over Gurobi's first-order solver from a cold start, including on instances of unseen sizes and distributions. The gains extend to PageRank linear programs and hold across difficulty levels and target accuracies.

</details>


### [96] [LCM-Promo: An LLM-Based Uplift Model for Promotion Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37570)
*Nesta Midavaine,Floris Fok,Paulo Mendes,Joao Silva,Önder Akaçık,Dimas Lima,Zülküf Genç*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LCM-Promo，一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的提升模型，用于促销分配。通过在交易商业数据上微调LLM，学习单调的促销-响应曲线，在流失用户召回的A/B测试中，相比生产基线Segment-EMA，转化率提升1%，促销成本降低6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有促销分配方法难以同时捕捉用户意图、促销响应的非线性关系及会话上下文（如日期、配送时段），且缺乏对单调促销-响应曲线的显式建模，导致促销资源分配次优。

Method: 构建任务专用大商业模型（LCM）：在交易数据上微调LLM，将单token输出解释为转化阈值概率，从生产日志中学习单调促销-响应曲线，并以会话上下文（日期、配送时段）为条件进行预测。

Result: 在流失用户召回的生产A/B测试中，LCM-Promo相对Segment-EMA基线实现转化率提升1%，促销成本降低6%。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的提升模型能有效整合商业交易数据、上下文信息及单调约束，显著提升促销分配效率，验证了大商业模型在实际业务场景中的实用价值。

Abstract: We present LCM-Promo, an LLM-based uplift model for promotion allocation. LCM-Promo is a task-specific Large Commerce Model (LCM): a finetuned LLM trained on transactional commerce data to predict user intent and promotion response. It learns monotonic promotion-response curves from production logs by interpreting single-token outputs as conversion-threshold probabilities, while conditioning on session context such as day and delivery shift. In a production A/B test on churn reactivation, LCM-Promo increased conversion by 1% and reduced promotion cost by 6% relative to the Segment-EMA production baseline.

</details>


### [97] [Reliability-Aware Short-Term Glycemic Risk Prediction Using an Anchor-Based Comparative Framework of Sequence, Multimodal, and State-Updating Digital-Twin Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36363)
*Pir Bakhsh Khokhar,Carmine Gravino,Fabio Palomba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出基于锚点的基准测试框架，利用T1DiabetesGranada数据集构建550万标准化预测锚点，对比三种神经网络架构在短期低/高血糖预测中的表现，发现模型间预测性能无显著差异，但可靠性特征互补，强调数字孪生模型临床部署前需进行可靠性评估与外部验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习方法在短期血糖风险预测中常采用不一致的评估协议，且过分强调预测性能而忽视可靠性，亟需统一的基准测试框架全面评估模型的校准、解释可靠性、鲁棒性等多维指标。

Method: 基于736名1型糖尿病患者的纵向血糖序列与多模态患者信息，构建5,503,441个标准化预测锚点，在30/60/120分钟预测时域下，采用相同患者级验证协议对比三种架构：混合LSTM-Transformer、多模态数字孪生、具备间隙感知纵向状态演进的状态更新数字孪生。评估维度涵盖预测性能、校准度、分类阈值、解释忠实性、跨模型分歧及受控扰动鲁棒性。

Result: 经Holm校正后，三模型预测性能无统计学显著差异。多模态数字孪生实现最低平均Brier分数；状态更新数字孪生达最低平均期望校准误差（ECE-10）；混合LSTM-Transformer在时序归因协议下展现最强解释忠实性与稳定性。模型间呈现互补的可靠性特征。

Conclusion: 研究揭示数字孪生模型评估必须超越预测准确性，纳入校准、解释可靠性、鲁棒性等多维指标。临床部署前需进行系统的可靠性评估与外部验证，以确保模型在真实场景中的安全有效应用。

Abstract: Anticipating short-term hypoglycemic and hyperglycemic risk is essential for proactive diabetes management. However, existing machine learning approaches often use inconsistent evaluation protocols and emphasize predictive performance over reliability. This study introduces an anchor-based benchmarking framework integrating longitudinal glucose sequences and multimodal patient information. Using the T1DiabetesGranada dataset, 5,503,441 standardized prediction anchors were constructed from 736 individuals with type 1 diabetes to evaluate hypoglycemia and hyperglycemia prediction at 30-, 60-, and 120-minute horizons. Three neural architectures, a Hybrid LSTM-Transformer, a Multimodal Digital Twin, and a State-Updating Digital Twin with gap-aware longitudinal state progression, were compared under identical patient-level validation protocols. Beyond predictive performance, the evaluation examined calibration, classification thresholds, explanation reliability, cross-model disagreement, and robustness to controlled perturbations. No statistically significant predictive differences were observed after Holm correction, although the models exhibited complementary reliability characteristics. The Multimodal Digital Twin achieved the lowest mean Brier score, while the State-Updating Digital Twin demonstrated the lowest mean expected calibration error (ECE-10). The Hybrid LSTM-Transformer showed strong explanation faithfulness and stability under its temporal attribution protocol. These findings highlight the importance of evaluating digital-twin models beyond predictive accuracy and emphasize the need for reliability assessment and external validation before clinical deployment.

</details>


### [98] [SpecLST: Spectral-Enhanced Long-Short Term Spatio-Temporal Multimodal Modeling for Traffic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36393)
*Kenan Kang,Matthew M. Y. Kuo,Weihua Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SpecLST，一种基于频域增强的长短期时空多模态框架，利用快速傅里叶变换高效编码多周历史依赖，结合注意力融合异构上下文、稀疏专家混合骨干网络及图增强，并通过频域增强残差头捕捉高频瞬态异常，在四个数据集上显著超越八个基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时空预测框架在长序列建模中面临内存开销与序列长度的权衡，且常忽略路面事故、天气等外生因素的瞬时影响，难以同时实现长程历史建模与异常敏感性。

Method: SpecLST 包含三大核心模块：1) 基于快速傅里叶变换的频域表示模块，将长序列映射到频域压缩历史依赖，规避注意力机制的内存瓶颈；2) 基于注意力的异构模态融合，结合稀疏专家混合（MoE）骨干网络与图增强，学习时空表征；3) 频域增强残差头，分离高频分量以增强对突发交通异常的敏感度。

Result: 在 60 分钟预测时域上，SpecLST 在四个数据集、八个神经网络基线上实现平均相对误差降低：MAE 14.10%、RMSE 10.35%、MAPE 15.78%；不同数据集与指标组合下的平均降低幅度为 5.63%~22.21%。

Conclusion: SpecLST 通过频域压缩长程历史、多模态注意力融合、稀疏 MoE 与图增强的时空建模，以及高频残差头捕捉瞬态异常，有效解决了长序列内存瓶颈与外生因素瞬时效应建模难题，显著提升了城市路网交通预测精度。

Abstract: Modeling multi-scale dynamics in urban road networks is essential for intelligent transportation systems but is often hindered by the computational cost of long-range historical context and the challenge of fusing heterogeneous modalities. Existing spatio-temporal frameworks face a trade-off between sequence length and memory overhead, while frequently neglecting the transient effects of exogenous factors such as road incidents and weather. To address these limitations, we propose SpecLST, a frequency-enhanced long-short-term spatio-temporal multimodal framework. SpecLST leverages a fast Fourier transform-based frequency-domain representation module to efficiently encode multi-week historical dependencies, bypassing the memory bottlenecks of standard attention mechanisms over raw long sequences. Heterogeneous context is integrated through attention-based fusion, followed by a sparse Mixture-of-Experts backbone with graph augmentation for spatio-temporal representation learning. Additionally, a frequency-enhanced residual head isolates high-frequency transients, improving sensitivity to abrupt traffic anomalies. At the 60-minute horizon, SpecLST achieves mean relative error reductions of 14.10% in MAE, 10.35% in RMSE, and 15.78% in MAPE across eight neural forecasting baselines and four datasets, with the dataset- and metric-specific average reductions ranging from 5.63% to 22.21%.

</details>


### [99] [When Can a Learned Design Surrogate Be Trusted in a Self-Driving Microfluidics Laboratory? An Offline Closed-Loop Framework Built and Tested on Six Public Tables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36395)
*Zhiyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个框架，用于量化自驱动实验室在新目标域中的四个关键问题：可信度、所需目标测量数、首选测量选择、何时停止适应/切换模型/升级。通过在六个公开微流控/脂质体数据集上的离线实验（107个leave-one-group层级）和封闭预注册程序验证，发现：暖启动残差提升能在20个目标样本内将代理模型恢复至开发参考的1.25倍（化学层级94%、实验室层级90%、设备层级40%）；基于偏移类型预算规划在新表上不差；闭环中仅“一次性提出并每次测量暖启动”策略在漂移和标签打乱对照中存活，稀疏采样层级获益最大：达成10%目标需2.80次测量（参考3.31），3%容忍度下301个困难单元需5.72次（随机11.35）。无标签自由采集规则通过打乱标签对照，无区间感知提议或其它模型族更优，升级定价后无阈值规则优于始终继续。未声称湿实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自驱动实验室（SDL）在部署到新目标域时，需解决四个核心问题：何时可信任代理模型、需要多少目标域测量、优先测量哪些、何时停止适应或切换模型/升级。现有方法缺乏可量化、可预注册的框架来同时回答这四个问题，且常依赖标签自由统计或随机分割排名，导致泛化性差。本文旨在建立一套可测量的指标体系（胜任力比率、预算k*(ε)、时间到目标、定价决策），并在无湿实验条件下，通过严格的离线基准和预注册闭环协议验证其有效性。

Method: 1. 框架定义：将四个问题操作化为可测量指标——胜任力比率（可信度）、预算k*(ε)（所需目标测量数）、时间到目标（首选测量顺序）、定价决策（停止/切换/升级时机）。2. 离线基准：使用六个公开微流控/脂质体数据集，构建107个leave-one-group层级（行标签隐藏），模拟化学、实验室、设备三类域偏移。3. 预注册程序：两个密封负向验证（漂移对照、标签打乱对照）和一个预注册测试（三个新表），七个冻结闭环协议各运行两次。4. 算法：暖启动残差提升（warm-start residual boosting）作为代理模型适应策略；一次性批量提出并每次测量后重新暖启动（propose-at-once + warm-start per measurement）作为采集策略。5. 对照：随机提议、区间感知提议、其他模型族、各类标签自由采集规则、升级阈值规则。

Result: 1. 偏移成本按偏移类型分层；无标签自由统计能认证单个候选；随机分割模型族排名无法预测密封或闭环排名。2. 暖启动残差提升在20目标行内将代理恢复至开发参考1.25倍：化学层级94%（严格噪声底读数下27%）、实验室层级90%、设备层级40%。3. 基于他表偏移类型规划的预算在新表上无显著劣化。4. 闭环中仅“一次性提出+每次测量暖启动”策略在漂移和标签打乱对照中全层级存活，优势来自稀疏采样层级：达成10%目标需2.80次测量（参考3.31）；3%容忍度下301困难单元需5.72次（随机11.35）。5. 无标签自由采集规则通过打乱标签对照；无区间感知提议或其它模型族更优；升级定价后无阈值规则优于始终继续。

Conclusion: 本文建立的可量化框架和预注册验证流程，证明了暖启动残差提升结合一次性批量提议策略能在极少目标域数据（≤20样本）下可靠适应新域，且在稀疏采样场景下显著优于基线和随机策略。关键发现包括：偏移类型可指导预算规划、标签自由诊断不可靠、升级定价后应始终继续而非设阈值。结果仅基于离线公开数据和模拟闭环，未进行湿实验验证，实际部署仍需实验室确证。

Abstract: Self-driving laboratories couple a learned design surrogate to an instrument, and on every new target domain it must answer four questions: when it can be trusted, how many target measurements it needs, which to make first, and when to stop adapting, switch models or escalate. We state a framework making each measurable (a competence ratio, a budget $k^*(\varepsilon)$, a time-to-target and a priced decision) and instantiate it without wet experiments on six public droplet-microfluidic and liposome tables as offline laboratories: 107 leave-one-group tiers whose rows are hidden labels. A sealed, pre-registered programme (two sealed negative validations and a pre-registered test on three more tables) and seven frozen closed-loop protocols, each run twice, yield 23 findings. Shift cost is stratified by shift type; no label-free statistic certifies individual candidates; random-split model-family rankings predicted neither the sealed nor the loop rankings. Warm-start residual boosting returns a surrogate to $1.25\times$ its development reference within 20 target rows in 94% of chemistry tiers (27% under a strict noise-floor reading), 90% of laboratory and 40% of device tiers, and a budget planned from the shift type on other tables is not measurably worse on a new one. In the loop, one gain survives drift and shuffled-label controls on all tiers, carried by the sparsely sampled ones: proposing at once and warm-starting on every measurement reaches a 10% target in 2.80 measurements against 3.31 for the reference, and in 5.72 against 11.35 for random proposals on 301 hard units at a 3% tolerance. No label-free acquisition rule survives the shuffled-label control, no interval-aware proposal or other model family does better, and no threshold rule on the tested diagnostics beats always continuing once escalation is priced. No wet-lab validation is claimed.

</details>


### [100] [Persistence, Exposure, and Credit Routing in Linear Temporal Predictive Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36412)
*Akira Tanase,Julian Gould,Jeffrey Seely*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文利用胞腔层理论分析线性时间预测编码网络，揭示时间信用分配中空间位移与时间误差的拉锯战，由持久性算子P和暴露算子Q调节，发现持久性-误差权衡：长延迟保持影响力的模式难以强暴露为时间误差，但可通过持久与暴露模式间的路由规避。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解时间预测编码网络中时间信用分配的机制，特别是空间推断与时间误差传播的相互作用，以及持久性与暴露之间的根本权衡。

Method: 应用胞腔层理论工具分析线性tPC网络，推导有效循环转移算子F=PA，未来信用由伴随算子P*过滤，从数学上刻画空间模式下的持久性-误差权衡。

Result: 证明在非放大不变循环下，能长延迟保持影响力的空间模式（高持久性）同时无法强暴露为时间误差（低暴露性）；循环动力学可通过在持久模式与暴露模式间路由来规避这一权衡。

Conclusion: 时间信用分配本质上是由持久性与暴露算子调制的空间-时间拉锯战，循环网络通过模式间路由实现长期记忆保持与误差信号有效传播的平衡。

Abstract: Temporal Predictive Coding (tPC) networks offer a layer-local alternative to backpropagation. For linear tPC, we show, by applying tools from cellular sheaf theory, that temporal credit assignment is a tug-of-war between spatial displacement and temporal errors, whose ratios are modulated by persistence and exposure operators $P$ and $Q$. Spatial inference therefore gives us the effective recurrent transition $F=PA$, whilst future credit is filtered by $P^\ast$. This creates a persistence--error tradeoff in spatial modes: under non-amplifying invariant recurrence, modes that preserve influence over long delays simultaneously fail to expose that influence strongly as temporal error. Recurrence can circumvent this via routing between persistent and exposed modes.

</details>


### [101] [Repulsive Population Policy Gradients for Behaviorally Diverse Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36425)
*Saeed Hedayatian,Harsh Shah,Celine Mneimneh,Stefanos Nikolaidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RePPO（排斥种群策略优化），将PPO改造为联合优化紧凑、多样且高质量策略种群的方法，通过种群依赖的动态塑形奖励实现质量-多样性权衡，并在连续控制、像素输入及大语言模型微调任务上展示了可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QD-RL方法主要依赖进化算子（黑盒优化、变异、交叉）进行探索和诱导多样性，随着策略规模和行为空间增大，计算成本急剧上升，难以扩展到高维策略（如像素输入）或大模型微调等复杂场景。

Method: 基于Soft QD视角，将PPO扩展为RePPO：引入种群依赖的动态奖励塑形机制，使种群内各策略在优化自身回报的同时，通过排斥项相互远离行为空间，从而联合优化紧凑种群的整体质量与多样性，无需进化算子。

Result: 在标准连续控制任务上，RePPO达到最先进的质量-多样性权衡；在像素输入任务中，策略规模大幅增加时，RePPO保持与标准PPO相当的质量并显著提升多样性；首次在大语言模型微调上成功应用QD-RL，验证了其跨域可扩展性。

Conclusion: 直接使用RePPO优化紧凑策略种群，提供了一种可扩展的QD-RL范式，能有效应对日益复杂的决策领域（包括高维观测和大规模模型），为质量-多样性优化开辟了新路径。

Abstract: The goal of Quality-Diversity Reinforcement Learning (QD-RL) is to find a population of high-performing policies that solve a decision-making problem in diverse ways. Despite recent progress, QD-RL methods predominantly rely on evolutionary operators--including black-box optimizers, mutations, and crossovers--to facilitate exploration and induce diversity. Scaling these methods becomes increasingly costly as policies and behavior spaces grow larger. Building on the Soft QD view of quality-diversity optimization, we introduce \textbf{Re}pulsive \textbf{P}opulation \textbf{P}olicy \textbf{O}ptimization (\textbf{RePPO}), which adapts PPO to jointly optimize a compact population of diverse, high-quality policies using a population-dependent, dynamically shaped reward. On standard continuous-control tasks, RePPO achieves a strong quality-diversity tradeoff compared with state-of-the-art QD-RL methods. Ablations further characterize this tradeoff by examining how each component affects individual quality and collective diversity. To assess RePPO's scalability, we extend our evaluation to learning from pixels and fine-tuning large language models. In pixel-based tasks, where large policies make most traditional QD-RL methods infeasible, RePPO maintains quality on par with standard PPO while achieving substantially higher diversity. Its successful application to language model fine-tuning further demonstrates, for the first time, that QD-RL methods can scale to this domain. Together, these results show that directly optimizing compact populations with RePPO provides a scalable approach to QD-RL across increasingly complex domains.

</details>


### [102] [Property-Based Testing for Robust Softmax and Cross-Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36427)
*Muhammad Maaz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过Hypothesis属性测试发现并修复了标准机器学习库中softmax和cross-entropy函数的数值问题，并提出了更鲁棒的实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Barron等人(2026)指出了主流ML库(PyTorch、JAX)中softmax和cross-entropy的两个数值问题，作者旨在通过属性测试验证这些问题并发现潜在的额外边界情况。

Method: 使用Hypothesis进行属性测试，针对softmax和cross-entropy函数设计数学性质测试用例，识别数值不稳定性，并据此提出改进的softmax实现。

Result: 属性测试成功复现了已知问题，并发现了一个额外的边界情况导致原实现失效；新提出的softmax实现通过所有测试，且支持因果注意力掩码场景。

Conclusion: 属性测试是发现和修复ML库数值问题的有力工具，相关实现和测试已开源至GitHub。

Abstract: A recent paper of Barron et. al. (2026) identified two numerical issues with the softmax and cross-entropy functions in virtually all standard machine learning libraries (e.g., PyTorch and JAX). We show that these issues can be recovered through simple property-based tests in Hypothesis. However, our tests identify an additional edge case where their implementation fails. We then propose an even more robust softmax implementation which passes these tests and also supports use in causal attention masking. We compare our implementation with theirs and discuss some of the differences. Overall, this work shows that property-based testing can be a powerful tool for discovering and fixing numerical issues in machine learning libraries. Our implementations and property-based tests can be found at https://github.com/mmaaz-git/softmax-fix.

</details>


### [103] [Simulated petrological labels enable probabilistic mineralogy prediction from whole-rock geochemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36429)
*Matthew J. Liu,Aaron D. Kaplan,Michael L. Whittaker,Kristin A. Persson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种三部分机器学习模型，利用近百万模拟岩石学标签预测岩石矿物组成，在零样本条件下能识别大多数矿物类别并排序丰度，通过仅25个标注样本的重校准即可恢复76%的似然提升，展示了如何利用不完美模拟器并减轻其系统偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模态矿物学（岩石中矿物身份及丰度）对地质研究和资源开采至关重要，但测量耗时；全岩化学测量通量更高。然而从元素数据预测矿物学属于一对多逆问题，且标注矿物学数据集太稀疏无法大规模训练。

Method: 构建三部分模型：1）在约788,000个全岩成分上自监督预训练的Transformer编码器；2）利用近百万模拟岩石学标签（表达每种成分的多种形成条件）训练的预测头，输出矿物组合分布；3）重校准层，将预测与实测矿物学对齐。

Result: 在640块火山岩零样本测试中，模型能辨别大多数矿物类别的存在并预测相对丰度排名，但预测丰度系统性偏离实测值。仅用25个标注岩石重校准即恢复76%的似然提升（完整368样本集为基准），丰度误差从零样本11.0%降至完整集9.5%，下降较慢。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了地球化学科学中机器学习模型如何同时利用不完美模拟器并缓解其系统偏差，通过大规模模拟监督与少量实测数据重校准相结合，实现了从元素组成到矿物学的有效预测。

Abstract: Modal mineralogy, the mineral identities and abundances in rocks, guides geological studies and industrial resource extraction. While measuring modal mineralogy is time-intensive, whole-rock chemical measurements furnish elemental concentrations at higher throughput. Predicting mineralogy from elemental data, however, poses a one-to-many inverse problem, and labeled mineralogical datasets are too sparse to train on at scale. We address both challenges by simulating petrological labels that express multiple formation conditions per composition and at a scale unattainable by measurements. Nearly one million simulated labels supervise the middle component of a three-part model: a transformer encoder self-supervised on roughly 788,000 whole-rock compositions, the simulation-trained heads that predict distributions over mineral assemblages, and a recalibration layer that aligns the predictions with measured mineralogy. Zero-shot on 640 volcanic rocks, the model discriminates the presence of most mineral categories and predicts which rocks are richer in each mineral. However, the predicted abundances are systematically offset from measured values. Recalibration fit on as few as 25 labeled rocks recovers 76 percent of the likelihood improvement from fitting on the full 368-rock fitting set, while abundance error responds more slowly (11.0% zero-shot and 9.5% with the full fitting set). This work demonstrates how a machine learning model in the geochemical sciences can both leverage an imperfect simulator and mitigate its systematic biases.

</details>


### [104] [Learning atomic interactions for the thermodynamics of liquid mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36440)
*Marco Hoffmann,Sebastian Schmitt,Hans Hasse,Fabian Jirasek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: COGNAC是一种基于图神经网络的方法，在UNIFAC框架下学习原子级几何和相互作用参数，实现了比UNIFAC和HANNA更高的准确性，同时保持计算成本与UNIFAC相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于机器学习的模型（如HANNA）虽然准确性更高，但难以集成到过程模拟中；而传统的团体贡献法（如UNIFAC）计算廉价且广泛应用，但准确性有限。需要一种既能保持热力学一致性、计算高效，又能达到机器学习级别准确性的模型。

Method: 提出COGNAC，一种图神经网络，学习依赖于分子邻域的原子级几何和相互作用参数，并在UNIFAC框架内评估这些参数。在约82万个实验数据点上训练。

Result: COGNAC在二元和多元混合物上显著优于UNIFAC和HANNA，相对HANNA误差降低13-63%，可靠预测多相平衡和共沸点，产生可解释的参数，严格满足热力学一致性，且计算成本与UNIFAC相当。

Conclusion: COGNAC成功结合了机器学习的高精度与团体贡献法的计算效率和热力学一致性，为过程模拟解锁了机器学习模型的潜力。

Abstract: Predictive models for activity coefficients, which describe the non-ideality of liquid mixtures, are foundational for process design and optimization. Group-contribution methods, such as UNIFAC, are computationally cheap and well established in process simulators. Modern machine learning models, such as HANNA, offer higher accuracy and broader applicability, but their integration into process simulation is expensive or even infeasible. In this work, we introduce COGNAC, a graph neural network that learns atom-level geometry and interaction parameters that depend on the molecular neighborhood and evaluates them in the UNIFAC framework. Trained on about 820,000 experimental data points, COGNAC achieves significantly higher accuracy than UNIFAC and HANNA for binary and multi-component mixtures, reduces error relative to HANNA by 13-63%, reliably predicts multi-phase equilibria and identifies azeotropes, yields interpretable parameters, and is strictly thermodynamically consistent. COGNAC evaluations run at a cost comparable to UNIFAC, thereby unlocking the potential of machine-learning models for process simulations.

</details>


### [105] [High-Fidelity Aerostructural Dataset of Transonic Wings in Cruise Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36442)
*Pavankumar Koratikere,Abhijnan Dikshit,Leifur Leifsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发布了一个高保真多学科数据集，用于跨音速机翼的静气动结构分析，包含2471个样本，涵盖气动外形参数、飞行条件和结构载荷因子，并演示了最先进的机器学习模型在预测气动量和翼盒失效分布上的高精度表现（气动量NRMSE ≤ 0.0309，失效分布NRMSE ≤ 0.1189），为机翼多物理场机器学习建模提供了基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公开机器学习机翼设计数据集主要关注气动性能，缺乏实际飞机设计中至关重要的气动-结构耦合多学科交互数据，无法支持同时考虑气动性能和结构完整性的高效设计。

Method: 构建高保真静气动结构分析数据集：机翼平面几何由展长、后掠角、上反角、翼尖弦长、翼尖扭转角参数化；翼型截面采用类形变换（CST）方法参数化；分析条件包括马赫数、攻角、高度和结构载荷因子。共生成2471个样本及3个分布外样本。训练最先进机器学习模型预测气动面量、综合气动性能量及翼盒失效分布。
