At a matched parameter budget, moving part of a LoRA or MoE adapter's capacity into V-LUMEN preserves accuracy on two backbones, indicating that external visual memory is an independent capacity axis rather than a stronger adaptation method. Because lookup cost is constant in table size and incurred only once at prefill, this capacity grows in storage rather than in per-token compute. Code is available at https://github.com/miso-choi/V-Lumen.

</details>


### [138] [LLM knowledge for improved Out-of-Distribution Tabular Prediction - A Cross-Validated Regularisation Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36806)
*Sølve Langseth,Kristoffer Herland Hellton,Hugo L. Hammer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨利用大语言模型（LLM）引出的系数先验作为正则化回归的收缩目标（替代零），以改善分布外（OOD）预测。在五个真实协变量偏移的表格数据集上实验，先验在四个数据集上降低 OOD 均方误差（最高 7.9%），但在分类任务中仅提升概率校准而非排序能力。控制实验证实收益源于特征特定的先验内容而非尺度。然而，候选先验间的方差大于平均收益，交叉验证选择更多是避开最差先验而非找到最佳；且在三个数据集上先验同等改善域内外准确率，仅当训练数据足够大、域内增益消失时，才能证明先验真正改善了迁移性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测模型在部署人群与训练人群不同时性能下降，训练数据无法指示哪些特征-响应关系可迁移。专家引出先验虽有效但缓慢且难扩展。LLM 作为知识库可快速、大规模提供特征级先验，若能作为正则化收缩目标替代零，或能提升 OOD 泛化。

Method: 在五个表格数据集上按真实协变量分割源/目标域。利用 LLM 引出回归系数先验，作为正则化回归（如岭回归）的收缩目标（非零先验）。仅用源域交叉验证选择 LLM 提供商与先验结构。评估 OOD 均方误差（回归）与概率准确性/排序（分类），并用损坏先验控制实验区分特征特定内容与尺度效应。

Result: 先验在五个数据集中四个降低 OOD MSE，最大提升 7.9%。24 个先验告知结果中 10 个在常规置信度下显著改善均值。分类任务收益体现于概率校准而非 AUC。损坏先验实验表明收益来自特征特定内容。两大制约：候选先验间方差超过平均收益，CV 选择边际小于噪声，故选择机制更多是避开最差先验；三个数据集上先验同等提升域内外准确率，仅当训练数据足够大、域内增益消失时，才证实先验改善了迁移而非仅作更好正则化。

Conclusion: LLM 引出的先验可作为正则化收缩目标改善 OOD 预测，但效果高度依赖先验选择，且当前 CV 选择机制不可靠。先验更多表现为通用正则化而非专门修正分布偏移。仅在大样本下域内增益消失时，才能确证先验包含可迁移的因果知识。未来需改进先验选择与评估方法，以稳健利用 LLM 知识提升迁移学习。

Abstract: Prediction models degrade when the deployment population differs from the one used to train them, and training data cannot say which feature-response relationships will transfer. Expert elicitation is slow and unscalable. We ask whether coefficient priors elicited from large language models (LLMs) improve out-of-distribution (OOD) prediction as the shrinkage target of a regularised regression instead of zero, across five tabular datasets split on real covariates, selecting providers and prior structures by source-domain cross-validation alone. The priors help on four of five datasets, by up to 7.9% OOD mean squared error, and ten of twenty-four prior-informed results resolve the mean improvement at conventional confidence; single-deployment intervals are much wider. On classification the gain is in probabilistic accuracy, not ranking, and control tests with corrupted priors attribute the benefit to feature-specific content rather than scale. Two findings temper this. Variation across candidate priors exceeds the average benefit, yet cross-validation margins fall below their own noise, so selection avoids the worst prior rather than finding the best. On three datasets the prior improves in-domain accuracy as much as out-of-domain, making it better regularisation than shift correction. Only where the training data are large enough that the in-domain gain disappears does elicited knowledge demonstrably improve transfer. Code, priors and results: https://github.com/hugohammer/llm-priors-ood.

</details>


### [139] [Exploring the Limits of the Delta Learning Hypothesis: Single-Pair Generator Biases in Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37058)
*Giuseppe Curci,Stefano Simonazzi,Andrea Molinari,Andrea Zugarini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了直接偏好优化（DPO）中使用单一模型对生成偏好数据的局限性，发现固定模型对会引入系统性偏差，导致DPO表现不如多模型偏好数据，且增加生成器数量可缓解此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究delta-learning假设的极限：即是否可以仅用单一模型对（大模型响应视为偏好）替代多模型池加外部LLM裁判来构建DPO偏好数据。

Method: 理论分析与实证实验相结合，跨模型家族和规模对比单模型对与多模型偏好数据在DPO中的表现，分析训练动态中的偏差与奖励黑客行为。

Result: 单模型对引入生成器依赖的系统性偏差，这些偏差与响应质量无关却能预测偏好标签；DPO在单对数据上持续劣于多模型数据，归因于对偏差的奖励黑客；仅增加生成器数量（无外部裁判）即可部分缓解。

Conclusion: 生成器多样性是偏好数据构建的关键因素，单一模型对不足以提供高质量监督，需多样化生成器以避免系统性偏差和奖励黑客。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) typically relies on preference pairs generated from diverse model pools and ranked by an external LLM judge. The recently proposed delta-learning hypothesis suggests that comparable supervision can instead be obtained from a single pair of models by treating the larger model's response as preferred. We investigate the limits of this approach across model families and scales. We show, both theoretically and empirically, that fixed model pairs introduce systematic, generator-dependent biases that are predictive of the preference label independent of response quality. Consequently, DPO trained on single-pair data consistently underperforms multi-model preference data, despite the larger generator producing higher-quality responses, and we trace this gap to training dynamics consistent with reward hacking on these biases. We further show that simply increasing the number of generators, even without an external judge, is enough to partially mitigate this effect. Our results highlight generator diversity as a critical factor in preference-data construction.

</details>


### [140] [SetGRPO: Calibrating Grader-Induced Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36807)
*Jiale Huang,Zixu Li,Zhiwei Chen,Zhiheng Fu,Jinhe Bi,Yupeng Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SetGRPO（Set-Valued Group Relative Policy Optimization），一种针对基于评分标准的强化学习中评分随机性问题的新方法，通过三个模块解决 GRPO 中训练系数的联合不确定性、局部更新几何下的安全恢复以及选择性风险控制，在十个开放式基准上实现了最高宏平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于评分标准的强化学习中，评分者的随机性会导致 GRPO 更新发生难以仅通过分数差异刻画的改变：由于 GRPO 在每个响应组内联合归一化奖励并将训练系数与响应级梯度结合，评分差异会转移整个组的系数并引发策略依赖的更新变化；选择性执行进一步影响保留更新的方向反转风险。现有方法缺乏对联合系数不确定性的量化、局部更新几何下的安全恢复机制以及跨候选阈值的同时风险控制。

Method: SetGRPO 包含三个核心模块：(a) 联合奖励集校准，为训练系数构建联合不确定性集；(b) 几何引导的安全恢复，将候选系数映射到当前策略的局部更新几何中，确定每个响应组的最大安全恢复比率；(c) 选择性风险门控优化，通过独立风险校准选择全局阈值，控制被接受更新的条件方向反转风险，同时保留原始 GRPO 目标。在可交换性假设下，SetGRPO 提供联合系数集的有限样本边际覆盖和跨候选阈值的同时风险控制。

Result: 在跨越三个领域的十个开放式基准上，SetGRPO 在 Qwen3.5 和 Llama3.2 两个模型家族上均实现了所有对比训练方法中最高的宏平均性能。

Conclusion: SetGRPO 通过形式化联合不确定性量化、几何感知的安全恢复和选择性风险门控，有效解决了评分随机性导致的 GRPO 更新不稳定问题，提供了理论保证并在大规模实验中展示了最优性能，为基于评分标准的大语言模型强化学习提供了更鲁棒的训练框架。

Abstract: Rubric-based reinforcement learning uses fine-grained grader feedback to train large language models on open-ended tasks, but grader randomness can alter GRPO updates in ways that score discrepancies alone cannot characterize. Because GRPO jointly normalizes rewards within each response group and combines the resulting training coefficients with response-level gradients, a scoring discrepancy can shift the coefficients of the entire group and induce policy-dependent update changes. Selective execution further affects the direction-reversal risk of retained updates. To address this, we propose Set-Valued Group Relative Policy Optimization (SetGRPO). It comprises three modules: (a) Joint Reward Set Calibration constructs a joint uncertainty set for the training coefficients; (b) Geometry-Guided Safe Recovery maps candidate coefficients into the current policy's local update geometry and determines the largest safe recovery ratio for each response group; and (c) Selective Risk-Gated Optimization selects a global threshold through independent risk calibration to control the conditional direction-reversal risk of accepted updates while preserving the original GRPO objective. Under exchangeability, SetGRPO provides finite-sample marginal coverage for the joint coefficient set and simultaneous risk control across candidate thresholds. On ten open-ended benchmarks spanning three domains, SetGRPO achieves the highest macro average among all compared training methods on both Qwen3.5 and Llama3.2 model families.

</details>


### [141] [EnergyCal: Taming Loss Spikes in Muon-Family Optimizers for Sparse MoE Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37062)
*Masato Fujitake*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EnergyCal，一种轻量级规则，用于稳定稀疏MoE语言模型训练中Muon族优化器的loss spikes，显著降低spike负担和perplexity。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏混合专家（MoE）语言模型训练中，Muon族优化器常出现大的loss spikes，影响训练稳定性。

Method: EnergyCal根据路由专家更新的有限步Newton-Schulz方向的归一化平方Frobenius范数进行衰减，不改变谱变换，不增加持久优化器状态。

Result: 在4B参数MoE模型上，EnergyCal-MuonH将平均阈值loss-spike负担降低99.51%，最终几何平均perplexity降低4.06%；plain Muon下平均spike负担降低95.98%，端点略有改善。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明EnergyCal能在有无Hyperball的设置下稳定MoE训练。

Abstract: Sparse mixture-of-experts (MoE) language-model training can exhibit large loss spikes with Muon-family optimizers. We introduce EnergyCal, a lightweight rule that attenuates routed-expert updates according to the normalized squared Frobenius norm of their finite-step Newton-Schulz directions. EnergyCal leaves the spectral transform unchanged and adds no persistent optimizer state. We evaluate it on a 4B-parameter MoE model. Against a development-selected, lower-learning-rate MuonH recipe, EnergyCal-MuonH reduces mean thresholded loss-spike burden by 99.51% and final geometric-mean perplexity by 4.06%, with a modest throughput cost. With plain Muon at matched nominal learning rate and weight decay, the reduction in mean spike burden averages 95.98% across the two tested exponents, accompanied by small endpoint improvements. These findings support stabilization both with and without Hyperball in the evaluated setting.

</details>


### [142] [When All Rollouts Fail: Policy-Compatible Joint Decoding for Hard-Problem Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36849)
*Zehao Wang,Hanzhu Chen,Runquan Gui,Zhe Wang,Lin Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PICO方法，通过分布约束联合解码结合专家轨迹与在线策略，解决RLVR在困难问题上的学习悬崖问题，实现可验证且策略兼容的轨迹生成，显著提升推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR依赖二元奖励的在线探索，在超出模型当前能力的问题上遇到学习悬崖：所有采样均失败导致优势估计归零；专家轨迹虽可提供正确信号，但与当前策略不兼容，位于学生低概率区域，导致大参数偏移并退化为表层模仿而非可迁移推理。因此需要既可验证正确又与当前策略兼容的学习信号。

Method: PICO采用分布约束联合解码：在token级引入专家数据引导解码，同时保持学生相对token概率；动态top-k机制在有限散度预算内约束采样分布，生成经验证且策略兼容的轨迹。这些轨迹与在线采样轨迹在混合行为目标下联合优化，通过重要性采样缓解分布错配。

Result: 在多样化推理数据集和智能体基准上，PICO持续优于标准RLVR和先前的离线策略基线。

Conclusion: PICO通过策略兼容的联合解码和混合行为优化，有效解决了RLVR在困难问题上的学习悬崖，实现了可验证正确且策略兼容的轨迹生成，显著提升了大语言模型的推理能力，优于现有方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) derives its learning signal from binary rewards via on-policy exploration and becomes a powerful paradigm for improving the reasoning of large language models. Yet it faces a learning cliff on problems beyond model's current ability: when every rollout fails, the samples reveal nothing about a correct solution, and group-relative estimators (e.g., GRPO, RLOO) collapse the update to zero advantage. Expert trajectories offer a remedy, but they are incompatible with the current policy and often lie in the student's low-probability regions, so the surviving updates demand large parameter shifts and decay into surface-level imitation rather than transferable reasoning. We argue that a useful learning signal on hard problems must be both verifiably correct and sufficiently compatible with the current policy. We propose Policy-Compatible Reinforcement Learning (PICO), which performs distribution-constrained joint decoding: expert data guides decoding at the token level while preserving the student's relative token probabilities, and a dynamic top-k mechanism confines the sampling distribution under a finite divergence budget, yielding trajectories that are verified and policy-compatible by construction. These trajectories are optimized jointly with on-policy rollouts under a mixed-behavior objective that mitigates their distribution mismatch via importance sampling. Experiments across reasoning diverse datasets and agentic benchmarks demonstrate that PICO consistently outperforms standard RLVR and prior off-policy baselines.

</details>


### [143] [Self-Regularized Flow Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37115)
*Theodoros Kouzelis,Spyros Gidaris,Nikos Komodakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SERF（Self-Regularized Flow Language Models），一种简单的自监督框架，通过利用去噪轨迹本身作为学习信号，改进连续扩散语言模型。核心思想是噪声到清洁的自蒸馏：EMA 教师接收学生沿同一插值路径的更清洁版本输入，提供额外监督。两种变体 SERF-L 和 SERF-P 分别对齐中间表示和输出分布，无需外部预训练模型且不改变推理流程。实验显示训练效率、生成质量和少步生成均大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续流式语言模型是自回归生成的有前景替代方案，但其训练目标通常依赖干净目标 token 的监督，导致去噪轨迹这一潜在学习信号被低估利用。

Method: 提出噪声到清洁的自蒸馏框架 SERF：使用指数移动平均（EMA）教师模型，接收学生沿相同插值路径的更清洁噪声输入，提供额外监督信号。包含两个变体：SERF-L 将学生的中间表示与教师更深层表示对齐；SERF-P 将学生与教师的输出 token 分布对齐（可在学生输出层或早期层）。所有变体仅增强标准训练目标，无需外部预训练模型，也不修改推理过程。

Result: 在三种连续语言模型范式（one-hot FLM、latent ELF、hyper-spherical S-FLM）上实验表明：OpenWebText 上训练壁钟时间加速高达 7.7 倍；MAUVE 提升 24.3%；GSM8K 准确率提升高达 28.8%；在少步生成上，性能匹配基线所需采样步数减少高达 64 倍。

Conclusion: 噪声到清洁的自蒸馏是改进连续扩散语言模型的有效方法，SERF 通过充分利用去噪轨迹显著提升了训练效率、生成质量和少步生成能力。

Abstract: We introduce SERF (Self-Regularized Flow Language Models), a simple self-supervised framework for improving continuous diffusion language models by exploiting the denoising trajectory itself. While continuous flow-based language models offer a promising alternative to autoregressive generation, their training objectives typically rely on supervision from clean target tokens, leaving the denoising trajectory underexploited as a learning signal. SERF addresses this limitation through noisy-to-clean self-distillation: an exponential moving average (EMA) teacher receives a cleaner version of the student's noisy input along the same interpolation path and provides additional supervision. We investigate two variants: SERF-L, which aligns the student's intermediate representations with deeper teacher representations, and SERF-P, which aligns their output token distributions, either at the student's output or at an early student layer. All variants augment the standard training objective without requiring external pretrained models or modifying the inference procedure. Experiments across three continuous language-model paradigms (one-hot FLM, latent ELF, and hyper-spherical S-FLM) demonstrate substantial improvements in training efficiency, generation quality, and few-step generation. In particular, SERF achieves up to 7.7x wall-clock training acceleration on OpenWebText, improves MAUVE by 24.3% and GSM8K accuracy by up to 28.8%, and matches baseline performance with up to 64x fewer sampling steps. These results establish noisy-to-clean self-distillation as an effective approach to improving continuous diffusion language models.

</details>


### [144] [Tail Cues, Principal Corrections: Plug-and-Play Rectification for Open-Set Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36870)
*Yingkai Yang,Chaoqi Chen,Hui Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TALOR 是一个轻量级、无需反向传播的测试时适应模块，利用源域线性头作为几何锚点，通过主成分坐标的软路由仿射修正实现开放集测试时适应，在 ImageNet-C 基准上以更少内存和更快速度超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时适应方法通常通过更新模型参数或维护可变记忆库获取适应信号，但在开放集场景下，目标流同时存在协变量偏移和语义偏移，这些方法计算开销大且易受未知样本干扰。

Method: TALOR 将源域线性头作为几何锚点，其诱导的基将归一化特征分解为高增益主坐标和低增益尾坐标；在主子空间估计软路由仿射修正，结合制度级主偏置与尾部条件斜率捕捉样本级尾向主耦合；采用加权岭回归减去漂移估计，重构特征后送入源域头进行分类。

Result: 在 ImageNet-C 基准的六个 csOOD 数据集上，TALOR 以 2.2% H-score 优势超越次优方法 UniEnt，推理速度快 2.5 倍，仅使用 12% GPU 显存；作为后处理插件进一步将 COME 提升至 68.4% H-score，仅增加 2% 显存开销。

Conclusion: TALOR 通过无反向传播的几何修正机制，在开放集测试时适应中实现了高性能、低延迟、极低显存占用的三重优势，且可作为即插即用模块增强现有方法。

Abstract: Open-Set Test-Time Adaptation (OSTTA) operates on non-stationary target streams where covariate and semantic shifts coexist. Existing TTA methods often obtain adaptation signals by updating model parameters or maintaining mutable memories, which can be costly and vulnerable to unknown samples. We present TALOR, a lightweight, backward-free rectification module that uses the source linear head as a geometric anchor. The head-induced basis decomposes each normalized feature into high-gain principal coordinates and low-gain tail coordinates. TALOR estimates a soft-routed affine correction in the principal subspace, combining a regime-level principal bias with a tail-conditioned slope that captures sample-level tail-to-principal coupling. Using weighted ridge regression, it subtracts this drift estimate from the principal coordinates, reconstructs the feature and feeds it into the source head. On the ImageNet-C benchmark with six csOOD datasets, TALOR outperforms the next-best UniEnt by 2.2% H-score while running 2.5x faster and using only 12% of its GPU memory; as a post-correction plug-in, it further boosts COME to 68.4% H-score with only 2% additional memory overhead.

</details>


### [145] [Hyperparameter and Neural Architecture Search via Evolutionary Optimization for Reinforcement Learning: HPNAS-RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36882)
*Nihal Acharya Adde,Hanno Gottschalk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出双分支进化框架，联合进行神经架构搜索(NAS)和超参数优化(HPO)，用于自动驾驶强化学习任务，实验表明联合优化优于单独NAS，获得更高奖励、更少参数和更稳定的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NAS主要应用于CNN，在强化学习中应用有限，尤其是需要同时优化架构和训练动态的场景。作者旨在解决RL策略网络架构设计与超参数配置的联合优化问题，提升自动驾驶任务中的学习效率和策略性能。

Method: 基于进化多目标NAS(EMNAS)扩展，构建双分支进化框架：离散分支搜索策略网络卷积层架构(视觉特征提取)，连续分支优化关键RL超参数；采用统一多目标搜索策略并行进化；引入并行评估加速收敛，教师-学生策略迁移机制提升泛化能力。

Result: 在高保真驾驶模拟器实验中，联合NAS-HPO框架持续优于单独NAS：获得更高累积奖励、模型参数量更少、训练稳定性显著提升。

Conclusion: 联合优化模型结构与训练动态能显著提升RL智能体设计的可扩展性和有效性，为自动驾驶等复杂任务提供了更优的自动化设计范式。

Abstract: In this study, we propose a dual-branch evolutionary framework that simultaneously performs Neural Architecture Search (NAS) and Hyperparameter Optimization (HPO) for Reinforcement Learning (RL) in Autonomous Driving (AD) tasks. While NAS has shown strong potential in automating architecture design for Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs), its application to RL remains limited, especially in scenarios that require joint optimization of architectural and training dynamics. Building on our prior work on Evolutionary Multi-Objective NAS (EMNAS), the proposed framework evolves discrete network architectures and continuous hyperparameters in parallel under a unified multi-objective search strategy. In this setup, NAS targets the convolutional layers of the RL policy network - responsible for visual feature extraction, while HPO focuses on optimizing critical RL hyperparameters. To accelerate convergence and improve generalization, we incorporate parallel evaluation and a teacher-student policy transfer mechanism. Experiments in a high-fidelity driving simulator show that the joint NAS-HPO framework consistently outperforms standalone NAS, yielding policies with higher rewards, fewer parameters, and improved stability. These results highlight the benefits of jointly optimizing model structure and training dynamics for scalable and effective RL agent design.

</details>


### [146] [Mechanistic Failure Modes of Weight Pruning in Transformer Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37126)
*Nabeelah Faumi,Deshan Sumanathilaka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过在GPT-2上进行因子设计的剪枝研究，发现输出指标（准确率、困惑度）和结构检查都可能遗漏机制层面的损伤，主张采用电路级因果分析作为压缩语言模型的补充评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有剪枝评估主要依赖准确率和困惑度，假设保留输出行为意味着保留内部计算。作者旨在测试这一假设，揭示剪枝对模型内部机制（电路）的实际影响。

Method: 在GPT-2上进行因子剪枝实验，交叉两种重要性标准（幅度、随机）、两种组件类型（注意力头、MLP神经元）和三种稀疏度（3%、50%、70%）。以完全表征的间接物体识别（IOI）电路为真值，使用激活修补、路径修补和直接logit归因来测量电路完整性。并复现Greater-Than电路以验证发现的普遍性。

Result: 发现两种截然不同的失效模式：幅度剪枝注意力头会清零电路（输出朝向头被移除，模型降至随机水平）；幅度剪枝MLP神经元在30%稀疏度下会反转电路（所有26个电路头保持完好，但Name Mover贡献翻转符号，模型系统性偏好错误名称，准确率17.7%）。困惑度将其评价为比随机头剪枝损伤更小（74.6 vs 140.1）。Greater-Than电路复现表明关键组件类型取决于电路架构。

Conclusion: 输出指标和结构检查均可能忽略机制损伤，电路级因果分析应作为压缩语言模型评估的必要补充。

Abstract: Pruned language models are judged almost entirely by accuracy and perplexity, which assumes that preserved output behaviour implies preserved internal computation. We test this assumption with a factorial pruning study on GPT-2, crossing two importance criteria (magnitude, random), two component types (attention heads, MLP neurons) and three sparsity levels (3%, 50%, 70%). Using the fully characterised indirect object identification (IOI) circuit as ground truth, we measure circuit integrity with activation patching, path patching and direct logit attribution. We find two qualitatively distinct failure modes. Magnitude head pruning >= zeroes the circuit: output-facing heads are removed, and the model falls to near-chance. Magnitude neuron pruning at 30% inverts it: all 26 circuit heads remain intact, but Name Mover contributions flip sign, and the model systematically prefers the wrong name (17.7% accuracy). Perplexity ranks it as less damaged than random head pruning (74.6 vs 140.1). A replication of the Greater-Than circuit shows that the critical component type depends on the circuit's architecture. These results show that output metrics and structural checks can both miss mechanistic damage, motivating circuit-level causal analysis as a complementary evaluation of compressed language models.

</details>


### [147] [Provenance Signatures for Low Rank Adaptations of Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36933)
*Tobias Cheung,Mohan Dantam,Marc Juarez,Rik Sarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种黑盒检测协议，用于识别在线服务是否使用了基于标准扩散模型的LoRA适配版本。通过假设检验框架，利用生成图像集合的内在维度作为黑盒签名，在开放世界场景下（未知适配集合和模型家族）区分基础模型、LoRA适配模型和完全不同的模型家族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型结合LoRA等高效适配技术虽增强了模型多样性，但也带来风险：开放基础模型可能被适配为生成偏见、有害或非法图像的在线服务。现有模型识别工作多假设候选模型集合已知（封闭世界），无法应对现实中适配版本和替代模型家族未知的开放世界场景。因此需要一种不依赖已知候选列表的溯源检测方法。

Method: 1. 形式化定义溯源问题的设置与威胁模型，区分两类因多模型家族与多适配导致的问题。2. 采用假设检验方法，构建黑盒签名：仅基于模型生成图像集合的内在维度。3. 通过统计检验区分三种情况：基础模型、LoRA适配模型、其他完全不同的模型家族。

Result: 提出的协议能在开放世界场景下，仅通过黑盒查询生成图像并计算内在维度，有效区分基础扩散模型、其LoRA适配版本以及其他无关模型家族。实验验证了基于内在维度的签名对LoRA适配的敏感性及对不同模型家族的区分度。

Conclusion: 内在维度作为一种轻量级黑盒特征，可作为扩散模型及其LoRA适配版本的有效溯源签名。该方法不依赖白盒访问或已知候选模型列表，为检测在线服务滥用适配模型提供了实用的技术手段，推动了模型溯源在开放世界设定下的研究。

Abstract: Diffusion models have revolutionized the generation of realistic artificial images. Efficient techniques such as Low Rank Adaptation (LoRA) can modify these models for particular use cases, making them more diverse and widely usable. At the same time, open base models may be adapted to deliver biased, harmful or illegal images in an online service. In this paper, we develop a protocol to detect when a service may be using an adaptation of an otherwise standard model. We describe the precise setup and threat model for this provenance question and define two different problems arising due the presence of multiple model families and multiple adaptations. In contrast to previous works that have considered model identification within lists of known candidate models, we consider the realistic open world scenario where the set of possible adaptations and alternative models are unknown. Using a hypothesis testing approach, our protocols can distinguish between the base model, a LoRA adaptation and other completely different families of models. For this test, we develop a black box signature of the models based only on intrinsic dimension of sets of images generated by the models.

</details>


### [148] [Your Policy Is Secretly a Harness: The Decision Geometry of Agent Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37255)
*Jian Mu,Ruibo Shen,Yao Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究语言模型智能体在不同 harness（提示词、工具、控制逻辑组合）下的学习与泛化。理论证明在 KL 正则化控制下，最优策略的 log odds 反映了 harness 对各动作的偏好，训练只改变 mismatch（策略自身次优决策损失的回报），而更换 harness 同时移动 ceiling（harness 提供的最佳回报）和 mismatch。实验表明：后训练使模型偏好过拟合到训练 harness，微小改动（如反转观察字段顺序）导致成功率从 85.0% 暴跌至 19.2%；固定模型比较会混淆 harness 质量与模型利用程度；ceiling 减 mismatch 可预测后训练增益，在 Terminal-Bench Lite 上用更强模型近似最优策略估算该指标能复现准确率排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有范式常通过固定模型在不同 harness 下的表现来判断 harness 优劣，隐含假设模型在一个 harness 下学到的知识能迁移到另一个 harness。本文旨在揭示：策略究竟从 harness 中学到了什么？harness 变更时什么能保留？从而为 harness 设计与评估提供理论依据。

Method: 1. 理论分析：在 KL-regularized control 框架下，推导最优策略 log odds 与 harness 隐含偏好的关系，分解部署回报为 ceiling（harness 上界）与 mismatch（策略次优损失）。2. 实验验证：在 MiniWoB++ 上对比相同最优决策但不同观察字段顺序的 harness，测量后训练模型的性能崩塌；对比固定模型拒绝但 ceiling 更高的新 harness，在进一步训练后评估其超越性；在 Terminal-Bench Lite 编码任务上，用更强模型作为最优策略代理估算 ceiling 与 mismatch，验证其对准确率排名的预测力。

Result: 1. 理论成立：训练仅降低 mismatch，harness 变更同时改变 ceiling 与 mismatch。2. MiniWoB++：反转观察字段顺序（最优决策不变）导致训练模型成功率从 85.0% 降至 19.2%，证明偏好过拟合训练 harness。3. 固定模型比较会拒绝 ceiling 更高的新 harness，但后训练后这些 harness 均超越原智能体，且预训练前的 ceiling 与 mismatch 能预测增益。4. Terminal-Bench Lite：ceiling 减 mismatch（用更强模型近似最优）能复现 harness 准确率排名，单独任一指标均不行。

Conclusion: Harness 不仅是评估工具，更是决定智能体偏好与性能上界的核心组件。后训练使模型偏好强绑定训练 harness，微小分布偏移即导致灾难性泛化失败。评估 harness 时应同时考量 ceiling 与 mismatch，而非依赖固定模型的零样本表现；ceiling - mismatch 为 harness 选型与后训练收益预测提供了可操作指标。

Abstract: Language model agents are post-trained and deployed through a harness, the prompts, tools, and control logic that present a task to the model and execute its outputs. Harnesses are often revised and judged by how an already trained model performs under them, which assumes that what the model learned under one harness carries over to the next. We ask what a policy learns from its harness and what survives when the harness changes. Under KL-regularized control, we show that the log odds of an optimal policy recover how strongly its harness favors each action over the others, so a trained policy carries the preferences of its harness. Deployed return then equals the ceiling of the harness, the best return it offers, minus the mismatch of the policy, the return lost to its own suboptimal decisions. Training changes only the mismatch, while changing the harness moves both terms. Comparing harnesses with a fixed model therefore mixes what each harness offers with how well that model uses it. In MiniWoB++, post-training makes a model's preferences specific to its training harness. Reversing the order of the observation fields leaves every optimal decision unchanged yet lowers a trained model's success from 85.0% to 19.2%. Conversely, every new harness that a fixed-model comparison rejects but that raises the ceiling outperforms the original agent after further training under it, and ceiling and mismatch measured beforehand predict these gains. For coding-agent harnesses on Terminal-Bench Lite, ceiling minus mismatch estimated with a stronger model as a stand-in for the optimum reproduces the accuracy ranking, while either term alone does not. Code is available at https://github.com/2020-qqtcg/HRP.

</details>


### [149] [Gaussian Process Bandits for Constraint Black Box Lever Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36935)
*Bram van den Akker,Hidde Fokkema,Anastasiia Kornilova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于高斯过程上置信界（GP-UCB）的分段无关方法，用于在共享预算下优化离散分配权重，解决了组合动作空间大、反馈延迟噪声大、调整频率受限等实际部署难题。通过合成模拟器筛选出复合 Matérn + 线性核为最稳健配置，并在大规模 RCT 中验证了相比 ε-greedy 基线的显著效率提升，为黑箱杠杆优化提供了实用方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的序贯决策环境中，跨竞争段优化离散预算分配权重面临组合动作空间爆炸、反馈延迟噪声且聚合、调整频率受运营约束等挑战，导致经典多臂老虎机算法因独立评估臂而极其低效，亟需一种能跨配置共享信息、缩短探索周期的方法。

Method: 采用分段无关的高斯过程上置信界（GP-UCB）算法，在奖励函数上放置高斯过程先验，利用核函数在相邻配置间共享信息。构建高保真合成模拟器，在平稳、漂移、反应三类环境下评估多种核配置，最终选定复合 Matérn + 线性核作为最稳健选择，并通过大规模随机对照试验（RCT）在生产环境中验证。

Result: 合成实验显示复合 Matérn + 线性核能同时克服局部噪声和全局漂移；生产环境 RCT 证实 GP-UCB 方法相较于标准 ε-greedy 基线实现了统计显著的效率提升，验证了其在真实黑箱杠杆优化中的实用性。

Conclusion: GP-UCB 结合复合核有效弥合了理论多臂老虎机研究与生产级部署的鸿沟，为共享预算下的离散权重优化提供了一个高效、鲁棒且可落地的解决方案。

Abstract: Optimizing discrete allocation weights across competing segments of a shared budget is a fundamental challenge in resource-constrained, sequential decision-making environments. In practice, this optimization is often hindered by delayed, noisy, and aggregate feedback, alongside strict operational constraints on how frequently weights can be adjusted. Because of the resulting combinatorial action space and extreme data sparsity, classical multi-armed bandit algorithms that evaluate arms independently are rendered highly inefficient. To overcome these limitations, we propose a segmentation-agnostic approach utilizing Gaussian Process Upper Confidence Bound (GP-UCB) algorithms. By placing a Gaussian Process prior on the reward function, our method effectively shares information across neighboring configurations, drastically reducing the necessary exploration horizon. In this paper, we bridge the gap between theoretical bandit research and production-scale deployment. We first introduce a high-fidelity synthetic simulator to evaluate various kernel configurations under stationary, drifting, and reactive environments, identifying a composite Matérn + Linear kernel as the most robust choice for overcoming local noise and global shifts. Finally, we validate our findings through a large-scale Randomized Controlled Trial (RCT) in a live production environment. Our empirical results demonstrate that the GP-UCB approach delivers a statistically significant efficiency lift compared to a standard $\varepsilon$-greedy baseline, offering a highly practical solution for real-world black-box lever optimization.

</details>


### [150] [Where Should the Evaluation Budget Go in LLM-Driven Agent Scaffold Evolution?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37276)
*Sichun Luo,Yi Huang,Mukai Li,Haochen Luo,Guanzhi Deng,Haibo Wang,Jian Xu,Zefa Hu,Junlan Feng,Qi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoEval评估分配框架，用于LLM驱动的Agent脚手架进化，结合稀疏搜索时反馈与靶向最终确认，在四个基准和两个进化框架上以约一半的评估成本达到优于全量评估的平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的Agent脚手架进化通过迭代候选生成和评估改进工作流，但对每个候选在完整任务集上评估会产生巨大执行成本。需要研究如何在指导搜索和确定最终脚手架这两个角色间分配评估预算。

Method: EvoEval框架：1) 短暂全量评估预热建立共享信息核心并校准响应模型，搜索期间项目参数固定；2) 搜索时使用稀疏反馈指导进化；3) 搜索后重拟合模型并应用配对逐次减半，复用累积观测仅执行缺失评估。

Result: 实验表明稀疏反馈能维持有效进化，额外靶向评估改进最终选择。重放分析显示共享信息项和通用评分基准支持紧密匹配候选间的比较。EvoEval在大多数设置下以约一半评估单元格实现更高平均性能。

Conclusion: 稀疏反馈足以支撑富有成效的进化搜索，而靶向的最终评估能显著提升选择质量。共享的信息性评估项和统一的评分参考框架是高效比较候选方案的关键。EvoEval通过预热校准+稀疏搜索+靶向重评估的策略，在大幅降低评估成本的同时保持甚至提升了最终性能。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-driven agent scaffold evolution improves agent workflows through iterative candidate generation and evaluation. Evaluation both guides subsequent search and determines the final scaffold, but evaluating every candidate on the full task set incurs substantial execution costs. We investigate how evaluation budgets can be allocated across these two roles. Our experiments show that sparse feedback can sustain productive evolution, while additional targeted evaluation improves final selection. Replay analyses further show that shared informative items and a common scoring reference support comparisons among closely matched candidates. Motivated by these findings, we propose \method, an evaluation allocation framework that combines sparse search-time feedback with targeted final confirmation. A short fully evaluated warm-up establishes a shared informative core and calibrates a response model whose item parameters remain fixed during search. After search, \method refits the model and applies paired successive halving, reusing accumulated observations and executing only missing evaluations. Across four benchmarks and two evolution frameworks, \method achieves higher mean performance than full evaluation in most settings while using approximately half as many evaluation cells.

</details>


### [151] [TDLM: Adaptive Temporal Integration for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36940)
*Bohang Sun,Max Zhu,Francesco Caso,Christopher Irwin,Jialin Yu,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出时间自条件扩散语言模型（TDLM），让未解码位置关注自身历史隐藏表示，复用已计算结果，仅增 0.84% 参数却实现最高 7.15 倍生成加速且保持竞争力质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散语言模型在多步去噪中反复计算未解码 token 的表示，但传统去噪器未显式保留并复用这些中间表示，导致计算浪费。

Method: 在冻结的去噪器中引入因果时间注意力模块，使每个未解码位置能并行地关注自身过往隐藏状态序列；训练使用自生成轨迹和相邻采样步骤间新揭示 token 的交叉熵目标。

Result: 在 LLaDA 和 Dream 上，TDLM 仅增加约 0.84% 可训练参数，生成速度最高提升 7.15 倍，在评测任务上答案质量与原模型相当。

Conclusion: 时间记忆机制是一种实用且高效的手段，能更充分利用重复去噪计算，大幅加速扩散语言模型推理而不牺牲质量。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models repeatedly compute representations for unresolved tokens, but conventional denoisers do not explicitly retain these representations for reuse across steps. We introduce Temporal Self-Conditioning Diffusion Language Model (TDLM), which lets each unresolved position attend to its own history of hidden representations within a frozen denoiser. This gives later predictions access to computation already performed. Training uses self-generated trajectories and a simple cross-entropy objective for tokens revealed between consecutively sampled decoding steps, while causal temporal attention permits parallel evaluation during training. On LLaDA, the temporal modules add only approximately 0.84% trainable parameters. Across LLaDA and Dream, the main comparison shows up to 7.15$\times$ faster generation than the corresponding original diffusion language model, with competitive answer quality on the evaluated tasks. These results motivate temporal memory as a practical way to make more effective use of repeated denoising computation.

</details>


### [152] [Algorithmic Monoculture in Machine Learning-based Site-Specific Fertilizer Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36941)
*Abdelghani Belgaid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究机器学习模型在农场NPK化肥处方推荐中的系统性风险，提出采用组合策略而非单一模型可降低社会损失。通过建立共享误差与农场特有误差模型，利用HHI指数和协方差广义公式量化模型采用集中度与误差相关性对社会损失的影响。实证基于摩洛哥五大谷物系统的九个模型族，在10%遗憾上限下，准确率约束的多元化策略将社会损失平均降低19.41%。研究强调采用架构与误差依赖性是生态系统层面的关键评估变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器学习推荐模型通常单独评估，但实际部署时跨多个相互关联的决策者（农场）。单一模型的相关误差会在农场间聚合，产生系统性成本。需要从生态系统层面评估模型采用策略，理解误差相关性和采用集中度如何影响社会总损失，从而设计更稳健的推荐系统。

Method: 1. 建立共享误差与农场特有误差的模型，将预期社会损失与模型采用份额联系起来。2. 使用Herfindahl-Hirschman指数（HHI）刻画常见模型误差独立、预测风险相等时的集中度效应。3. 提出协方差广义公式捕捉模型族间的依赖关系。4. 推导双模型阈值判断私人最优单一模型何时被社会最优多元化策略支配。5. 设计凸组合规则限制平均私人预测遗憾。6. 基于摩洛哥五大谷物系统的九个模型族进行基准校准和反事实模拟。

Result: 在说明性参数和10%遗憾上限下，准确率约束的多元化策略在所有五个系统中将建模社会损失降低10.89%-41.53%（平均19.41%）。均匀分配虽减少损失，但每种作物都违反遗憾上限。相关性敏感性分析显示，高度依赖的模型误差会削弱多元化收益。反事实结果非农艺或货币福利估计，但框架识别出采用架构和误差依赖性为生态系统级评估变量。

Conclusion: 机器学习推荐系统的评估应从单模型转向生态系统层面。模型采用的集中度（单一模型主导）会放大相关误差的系统性风险。通过准确率约束的组合多元化可显著降低社会损失，但需平衡私人遗憾上限。误差相关性结构是决定多元化有效性的关键因素。政策制定者和平台设计者应将采用架构和误差依赖性纳入评估体系，避免单一模型垄断导致的系统性脆弱性。

Abstract: Machine learning recommendations are often evaluated one model at a time, despite deployment across interconnected decision makers. For continuous NPK fertilizer prescriptions, correlated errors can aggregate across farms and generate systemic costs. A model of shared and farm-specific recommendation errors links expected social loss to model adoption shares; the Herfindahl-Hirschman Index characterizes concentration effects when common model errors are independent and predictive risks are equal. A covariance-generalized formulation captures the dependence between model families. A two-model threshold characterizes when privately optimal monoculture is socially dominated, and a convex portfolio rule limits average private predictive regret. Nine model families across five Moroccan cereal systems provide a benchmark-based calibration. Under illustrative parameters and a 10% regret cap, accuracy-constrained diversification lowers modeled social loss in all five systems by 10.89-41.53% (mean 19.41%). Uniform allocation reduces modeled loss in this setting, but breaches the regret cap in every crop. Correlation sensitivity shows that highly dependent model errors attenuate the benefits of diversification. These counterfactual outcomes are not estimates of agronomic or monetary welfare; the framework identifies adoption architecture and error dependence as ecosystem-level evaluation variables.

</details>


### [153] [EmbeddingGemma 2: Unified and Efficient Multimodal Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37364)
*Henrique Schechter Vera,Sahil Dua,Jovan Andonov,Sara Smoot,Sebastian Russo,Ivan Llanos,Max Matveev,Jae Hyeon Yoo,Kat Black,Shih-Cheng Huang,Josiah Aklilu,Maarten Grootendorst,Omar Sanseviero,Chandramouli Amarnath,Ian Ballantyne,Zhe Li,Daniel Salz,Ibrahim Alabdulmohsin,Ilya Philippov,Kristen Quan,Glenn Cameron,Edouard Yvinec,Luciano Martins,Doug Reid,Sindhu Raghuram Panyam,Weiyi Wang,Hengxuan Ying,Madhuri Shanbhogue,Tanmaya Dabral,Shanfeng Zhang,Juyeong Ji,Renjie Wu,Joe Zou,Xu Chen,Yunan Zhang,Yu-Hui Chen,Matthew Soulanille,Vinod Mamillapalli,Yi-Chun Kuo,Wai Hon Law,Matt Kreileder,Alice Zheng,Suril Shah,Mogan Shieh,Pooja Consul,Alberto Montes,Andreas Hess,Andres Sevilla,Babak Samari,Brendan McCloskey,Brian Potetz,Daniel Cer,Georgios Kathareios,Gustavo Hernández Ábrego,Hesen Zhang,Ji Ma,Jiaxing Wu,Keegan Mosley,Paul Suganthan,Rui Meng,Shen Gao,Shengcao Cao,Yi-Ting Chen,Yichang Chen,Zhongli Ding,Kendra Byrne,Cormac Brick,Olivier Lacombe,Qin Yin,Tris Warkentin,Armand Joulin,Tom Duerig,Mojtaba Seyedhosseini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EmbeddingGemma 2 是一个开放的多模态嵌入模型，原生支持文本、图像、音频、视频及其组合在统一空间表示。通过简化逐层嵌入和多阶段课程学习（模态与目标进阶），在单模态、跨模态和混合模态评测中建立新的 Pareto 前沿，性能超越专用模型及大 2-12 倍的模型。模型设计支持选择性加载模态编码器、可调视觉 Token 预算、可截断嵌入维度和量化权重，适配资源受限环境，并发布消融实验与模型权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态嵌入模型往往在单模态或跨模态任务上表现不平衡，且大型模型部署成本高。需要一个统一、高效且可灵活配置的嵌入模型，在保持强泛化能力的同时适应不同算力预算。

Method: 采用简化的逐层嵌入架构，结合多阶段课程学习策略：按模态和目标逐步进阶训练。支持选择性加载模态编码器、可调视觉 Token 预算、嵌入维度截断及权重量化，提供部署灵活性。

Result: 在全模态综合基准测试中建立新的 Pareto 前沿，超越专用模型及参数量大 2-12 倍的模型。在单模态、跨模态、混合模态评测均表现优异。消融实验验证关键设计决策的有效性。

Conclusion: EmbeddingGemma 2 通过统一架构与课程学习实现了高性能多模态嵌入，兼顾效率与灵活性，为资源受限场景提供实用解决方案，并开源模型与分析以推动后续研究。

Abstract: We present EmbeddingGemma 2, an open multimodal embedding model which natively represents text, images, audio, video, and combinations thereof in a unified space. Our approach introduces improvements such as simplified per-layer embeddings and a multi-stage curriculum with modality and objective progression which ensure strong quality across unimodal, cross-modal, and mixed-modal evaluations. Notably, in comprehensive embedding benchmarks across all modalities, EmbeddingGemma 2 establishes a new Pareto frontier, outperforming both specialist models and models two to twelve times larger. Furthermore, our design offers flexibility for resource-constrained environments, supporting selective modality encoder loading, adjustable vision token budgets, truncatable embedding dimensions, and quantized weights. We present ablations analyzing key decisions and release EmbeddingGemma 2 to promote further research.

</details>


### [154] [DCKV: Learned Transform Coding of the Prompt KV Cache in Hybrid Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36950)
*Egor Maximov,Aleksei Merkelov,Vasiliy Alekseev,Ivan Krivorotov,Valentin Beliaev,Nikita Arsenin,Dmitry Bocharov,Aleksei Goncharov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 一种用于混合语言模型KV缓存的可微分编解码器DCKV，实现端到端训练，大幅压缩KV缓存同时保持模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合语言模型仅在少数全注意力层保留KV缓存，但该缓存仍随提示长度增长，存储和移动开销大，需要高效压缩方法。

Method: 提出DCKV，包含变换、量化器和熵模型，以提示词token和已解码相邻位置为条件，联合优化码长和模型输出变化，针对冻结语言模型端到端训练。

Result: 在Qwen3.5-2B上，DCKV将单个token的存储压缩至bf16的1/55以上，长上下文基准性能损失不超过1.8分；压缩至1/100以上时损失不超过3.6分。

Conclusion: DCKV是首个端到端训练的KV缓存编解码器，通过联合优化变换、量化和熵建模，实现了极高压缩率下的最小性能损失，为长上下文推理的高效部署提供了新方案。

Abstract: Hybrid language models keep a key-value (KV) cache only in their few full-attention layers, but this cache is still the part of a prompt's state that grows with the prompt and that must be stored or moved to reuse it. We present DCKV, a differentiable codec for the KV cache and the first KV-cache codec that is trained end to end. Its transform, its quantizer and its entropy models, which are conditioned on the prompt tokens and on already decoded neighbouring positions, are optimized together against the frozen language model, both for the code length that the entropy models assign and for the change of the model's output. On Qwen3.5-2B, DCKV stores a compressed token in more than 55 times fewer bytes than bf16 at a loss of at most 1.8 points on three long-context benchmarks, and in more than 100 times fewer bytes at a loss of at most 3.6 points.

</details>


### [155] [LinMix: Data Mixing as Linear Domain Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37390)
*Hubert Baniecki,Przemyslaw Biecek,Martin Pawelczyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LinMix方法，将语言模型预训练数据混合问题重构为域归因问题，通过顶点采样实现无超参数的线性模型，显著优于Dirichlet采样基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据混合方法依赖Dirichlet采样训练代理模型并拟合回归，存在超参数敏感、无法支持数百个域的线性拟合、且当采样分布集中于先验混合时归因误差膨胀等问题。

Method: 基于最优实验设计，LinMix在 capped simplex 顶点采样混合比例（每个域要么达到上限要么为0），使域效应去相关，得到可解释的线性模型，其系数即为域归因值；最优混合仅保留正归因域，无需搜索候选混合。

Result: 在1B OLMo2和4B Qwen3.5模型上，LinMix在推理、代码、数学基准上优于Dirichlet混合，MBPP相对提升46%，GSM8K提升14%；在288个细粒度域上，仅顶点采样支持线性拟合，平均准确率比最佳非线性基线提升11%。

Conclusion: 顶点采样在理论上最优，能解耦域效应并实现精确归因；LinMix提供了无超参数、可解释且高效的预训练数据混合范式，大幅提升下游任务性能。

Abstract: Language model performance depends strongly on the mixture of domains in the pretraining data corpus. Popular data mixing methods select these domain weights by training many small proxy models on random mixes and then fitting a regression model to predict performance from the mix. We recognize this practice as a domain attribution problem, where the regression measures each domain's contribution to the target metric. Through this lens, the standard Dirichlet sampling of proxy data mixes is a poor regression design that requires tuning and cannot support a linear fit for hundreds of domains. Building on classical optimal experimental design, we introduce LinMix, which samples mixes at the vertices of the capped simplex so that each domain is at its cap or absent. This hyperparameter-free vertex sampling decorrelates domain effects, enabling an interpretable linear model whose coefficients are the domain attributions. The optimal mix given by LinMix provably keeps only domains with positive attribution values and requires no search over candidate mixes. We further establish the optimality of vertex sampling, and prove that Dirichlet sampling inflates the attribution error when its mixes concentrate around the prior data mix. Pretraining experiments with 1B-parameter OLMo2 and 4B Qwen3.5 language models show that LinMix outperforms Dirichlet-based data mixing across reasoning, code, and math benchmarks, with relative gains of 46% on MBPP and 14% on GSM8K. At 288 fine-grained domains, only vertex sampling supports a linear fit, and LinMix improves benchmark accuracy by 11% on average over the best nonlinear baseline.

</details>


### [156] [Live-OPSD: On-Policy Self-Distillation for Proactive Streaming Large Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36959)
*Kaixuan Du,Xin Wan,Dai Guan,Ming Chen,Xiaoxi Jiang,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Live-OPSD，一种面向流式多模态助手的在线策略自蒸馏方法，利用仅在训练阶段可用的特权提示来监督策略在自身输出上的表现，显著提升了助手在事件演变过程中决定何时响应以及如何响应的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式多模态助手需要在事件仍在展开时做出响应决策：识别何时现有证据足以支持响应、生成合适的续写、以及在不适当时保持沉默。现有的监督微调（SFT）仅从记录的助手轮次学习，并未直接在策略自身产生的响应前缀上进行训练，导致策略在实际推理时面临分布偏移。

Method: Live-OPSD 采用在线策略自蒸馏：策略首先仅基于流式上下文生成续写；随后同一模型在获得描述相关证据的特权提示（hint）条件下重新评估该续写；带提示的分布作为教师信号，指导无提示的学生策略进行优化。特权提示仅在训练时可用，推理时不依赖提示。

Result: 在 Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B 上，Live-OPSD 在 LiveProBench 取得平均 59.64 分，比最强的持续 SFT 基线高出 5.26 分；在 OVO-Bench 上达到 65.84 分，保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 利用特权提示在策略自身输出上进行引导，能够同时改善流式助手的响应时机（何时响应）与响应质量（如何响应），验证了在线策略自蒸馏在流式多模态交互中的有效性。

Abstract: Streaming multimodal assistants must make response decisions while events are still unfolding. They need to recognize when the available evidence warrants a response, produce an appropriate continuation, and remain silent otherwise. Supervised fine-tuning learns from recorded assistant turns, but does not directly train on the response prefixes produced by the evolving policy itself. We introduce Live-OPSD, an on-policy self-distillation method that uses privileged hints available only during training to supervise the policy on its own outputs. The policy first generates a continuation from the streaming context alone. The same model then re-evaluates that continuation with a hint describing the relevant evidence, and the resulting distributions guide the unhinted policy. On Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B, Live-OPSD attains an average score of 59.64 on LiveProBench, 5.26 points above the strongest continued-SFT baseline, and stays competitive on OVO-Bench (65.84). These results indicate that guiding the policy on its own outputs with privileged hints improves both when and how a streaming assistant responds.

</details>


### [157] [Follow the Gradient: Tracing Safety Degradation Back to Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37481)
*Saket Reddy,Amro Abbas,Anshuman Suri,Ari Morcos,David Schwab,Pratyush Maini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SimpleLoRIF，一种可扩展的梯度嵌入方法，用于识别微调数据中与不安全行为梯度对齐的样本。通过仅反向传播最后四层 Transformer 块并简化低秩归因，SimpleLoRIF 能处理百万级数据集。实验表明，移除 20% 最对齐不安全梯度的样本可构建 SafeTulu，在多模型上将 GCG 攻击成功率降低高达 22%，且保持或提升能力。同时发现工具执行失败和叙事算术等被低估的安全退化来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令微调可能在无明显有害样本的情况下悄然削弱大模型安全性，现有内容过滤器难以捕捉此类良性外表下的安全退化数据。需要一种可扩展的梯度分析方法，从海量微调数据中精准识别并过滤导致安全对齐偏移的样本。

Method: 开发 SimpleLoRIF：简化低秩归因方法，仅对最后四个 Transformer 块进行反向传播，计算每个训练样本的梯度与不安全行为的对齐度。该方法将梯度归因从小规模分析工具扩展为可处理百万级数据集的实用过滤器。基于此构建 SafeTulu，移除梯度对齐度最高的 20% 样本。

Result: 在涌现错位验证中，梯度空间 AUC 高达 0.865，证实不安全代码训练数据与微调后有害行为的梯度关联。SafeTulu 在 Qwen、Llama、Olmo 上将 GCG 攻击成功率降低最高 22%，优于 LLM-as-a-judge 和先前基于梯度的过滤，且能力基本保持或提升。分析被移除数据揭示工具执行失败、叙事算术等被忽视的安全退化源。

Conclusion: SimpleLoRIF 实现了大规模梯度归因的实用化，有效识别并过滤导致安全退化的微调样本。SafeTulu 证明该方法能显著提升对抗鲁棒性而不损害能力，并发现新型安全风险模式。开源 SafeTulu 及可定制的 Hugging Face Space 为社区提供了实用的安全微调基础设施。

Abstract: Fine-tuning Large Language Models (LLMs) on instruction data can quietly erode their safety, even when no training example looks harmful. Because this safety-degrading data looks benign, standard content filters struggle to catch it. We tackle this problem by developing SimpleLoRIF, a scalable gradient embedding method that identifies fine-tuning examples whose gradients align with unsafe behavior. By simplifying a low-rank attribution method and restricting backpropagation to the final four transformer blocks, SimpleLoRIF can embed datasets on the order of a million examples, boosting gradient attribution from a small-scale analysis tool into a practical filter for full post-training datasets. We first validate it on emergent misalignment, where we find that gradients can connect insecure-code training data to the broadly harmful behaviors that appear after fine-tuning, with AUC as high as 0.865 in the gradient space of misaligned models. We then use SimpleLoRIF to build SafeTulu, a version of Tulu 3 with the 20% of examples most gradient-aligned with unsafe responses removed. Training on SafeTulu reduces GCG attack success rate by up to 22% across Qwen, Llama, and Olmo, outperforming both LLM-as-a-judge and prior gradient-based filtering, while largely preserving or even improving capability. Finally, we analyzed the removed data and identify possibly under-studied sources of safety degradation, such as tool-execution failures and narrative arithmetic. We release SafeTulu along with a Hugging Face Space that lets researchers build their own customized versions of SafeTulu to fine-tune on, such as by changing the removal fraction or filtering only certain subsets.

</details>


### [158] [On the VC Dimension of Autoregressive Linear Threshold Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36970)
*Ilan Doron-Arad,Idan Mehalel,Elchanan Mossel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了确定性自回归线性阈值函数（LTF）的VC维度为Θ(d²)（当T≥d²时），填补了二次方差距；并展示了将LTF的符号函数替换为Logistic采样的随机模型，其最终多数预测类的VC维度可从二次增长到指数级，实现了确定性与随机自回归模型的指数级分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究自回归设置下LTF的表达能力，特别是VC维度随时间步T和输入维度d的变化。已知单步VC维度为d，但多步的上界仅知为O(d²)，是否存在二次方下界尚不清楚。同时，想探究随机性（如Logistic采样）对自回归模型VC维度的影响，以及确定性与随机模型的能力差距。

Method: 理论分析与构造性证明。首先构造特定的LTF和输入序列，证明当T≥d²时VC维度达到Ω(d²)，配合已知的O(d²)上界确立Θ(d²)。然后将技术推广到随机设置，分析将符号函数替换为Logistic采样后的多数预测类，通过构造展示其VC维度可达exp(Ω(d))。

Result: 1) 确定性自回归LTF在T≥d²时VC维度为Θ(d²)，解决了二次方差距。2) 随机自回归模型（Logistic采样）的最终多数预测类VC维度可指数级大于确定性模型，即从d²增长到exp(Ω(d))，证明了确定性与随机自回归模型的指数级分离。

Conclusion: 自回归LTF的确定性版本VC维度随d呈二次增长，而引入随机采样后可实现指数级表达能力提升，揭示了随机性在自回归建模中的根本优势。

Abstract: Linear threshold functions (LTFs) are a canonical class of Boolean predictors. In the autoregressive setting, the same LTF reads the most recent $d$ bits, generates the next bit, and returns the bit produced after $T$ steps. While the one-step class has VC dimension $d$, for general $T$ the counting upper bound is $O(d^2)$. We prove that the VC dimension is $Θ(d^2)$ for every $T\ge d^2$, resolving the quadratic gap for deterministic autoregressive LTFs. We then adapt the same technique to show an exponential separation between deterministic and stochastic autoregressive models: replacing the sign of LTFs by logistic sampling can increase the VC dimension of the final majority prediction class from quadratic to exponential in $d$.

</details>


### [159] [EnvSched: Environment-Aware Scheduling for Rollout-Efficient Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37609)
*Sihan Yang,Weichen Bai,Ruichuan An,Yiming Cheng,Zhiyuan Feng,Tianyi Jiang,Renye Yan,Ning Ding,Yifan Zhang,Renrui Zhang,Yehui Tang,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 EnvSched，一种面向语言智能体强化学习的在线环境感知调度器。它通过构建双关系图（环境内边捕捉任务语义与共享结构，跨环境边捕捉执行与奖励拓扑），传播历史回合统计量来预测任务内奖励方差，并结合利用（高方差任务）与探索（欠观测环境/任务）策略，配合环境配额与相似度惩罚，在有限回合预算下生成信息量大且多样的训练批次。在 Qwen3-8B 上，仅用 40 步训练即在 BFCL-v3、tau-Bench、ACEBench-Agent 上达到平均 48.81 分，超越随机调度 120 步的最佳表现，且在不同模型规模下均提升下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语言智能体可执行环境的规模扩大，生成和验证回合的成本急剧上升。在有限的回合预算下，如何通过环境级的数据管理（组织、评估、调度任务实例及其底层环境）来提升智能体强化学习的样本效率，是一个关键问题。现有方法多忽略环境结构与任务间的关联，导致训练批次冗余、信息量低。

Method: EnvSched 将环境-任务对建模为双关系图的节点：环境内边编码任务语义、共享工具/状态/验证器结构；跨环境边编码抽象执行与奖励拓扑。利用图神经网络沿两类边传播历史回合统计量，预测任务内奖励方差。调度器据此分配批次：优先选择预测方差高的任务（利用），并覆盖欠观测的环境与交互模式（探索）。环境配额与相似度惩罚进一步限制批次内的集中度与冗余，无需额外试探性回合即可产出高信息量、高多样性的批次。

Result: 在 Qwen3-8B 上，EnvSched 仅用 40 个训练步骤就在 BFCL-v3、tau-Bench、ACEBench-Agent 三个基准上取得平均 48.81 分，超过随机任务选择在 120 步后达到的最高分。同时，在相同训练预算下，不同规模模型的下游性能均得到提升。

Conclusion: EnvSched 证明了环境感知的调度策略能显著提升智能体强化学习的样本效率。通过双关系图建模环境与任务的多层结构，并联合优化利用与探索，可在极少的回合预算下实现优于大量随机采样的训练效果，为大规模语言智能体训练提供了一种可扩展的数据管理范式。

Abstract: As executable environments for language agents scale, generating and verifying their rollouts becomes increasingly expensive. Under finite rollout budgets, this raises an important question: can data management at the environment level make agentic reinforcement learning (RL) more efficient by organizing, evaluating, and scheduling task instances together with the environments that determine their tools, states, transitions, and verification? To address this question, we introduce EnvSched (Environment-aware Scheduling), an online scheduler that learns from rollout history. EnvSched assigns scheduling value through two complementary modeling choices: exploitation prioritizes tasks likely to provide informative reward differences, while coverage spreads each batch across underobserved environments and interaction patterns. EnvSched organizes environment--task pairs as nodes in a dual-relation graph, with within-environment edges capturing task semantics and shared tool, state, and verifier structure, and cross-environment edges capturing abstract execution and reward topology. By propagating historical rollout statistics along both edge types, it predicts within-task reward variance. These predictions guide a scheduler that allocates each batch to tasks with high predicted reward variance and underobserved environments and tasks. Environment quotas and a similarity penalty further limit concentration and redundancy, yielding informative and diverse batches without additional pilot rollouts. In RL on Qwen3-8B, EnvSched achieves an average score of 48.81 across BFCL-v3, tau-Bench, and ACEBench-Agent after 40 training steps, exceeding the highest score attained by random task selection after 120 steps. It also improves downstream performance across model sizes at the same training budget.

</details>


### [160] [GNN-Guided Network Reduction for Centralized Urban Traffic Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36981)
*Nikolai Antonov,Lukáš Chrpa,Andrii Nyporko,Jan Hůla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于学习的城市路网缩减方法，利用图神经网络（GNN）为每条OD流评分道路段，结合车辆流信息生成缩减路网，再在缩减网络上求解原始MILP，显著提升大规模中心化交通路由的计算效率与优化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有中心化交通路由（CTR）虽可建模为混合整数线性规划（MILP），但全路网求解计算量过大难以满足实时操作时间预算。现有的Bounded Routes和Smart Routes等预计算缩减网络方法未考虑各OD流内的车辆信息，导致缩减效果受限。

Method: 设计专用图神经网络（GNN），输入路网拓扑与车辆流信息，为每条OD流对道路段打分；选取高分段构建完整OD路径，形成缩减路网；在缩减网络上求解原始段流MILP，保证可行性并确定最终路由。

Result: 在布拉格-德伊维采、伦敦、纽约、悉尼四个真实城市路网实验中，该方法在两种交通制下均实现相对目标差距最小，平均比最佳竞争方法低六倍以上；交通仿真显示其聚合到达率最高，单位到达率行程时长具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 基于GNN的路网缩减方法有效融合车辆流信息，在保持优化质量的前提下大幅降低计算复杂度，为大规模实时中心化交通路由提供了实用的学习加速框架。

Abstract: We consider the centralized traffic routing (CTR) problem, where a central planner assigns routes to origin-destination (OD) vehicle flows to minimize congestion-dependent segment cost. Prior work formulates CTR efficiently as a mixed-integer linear program (MILP), but scaling this formulation to the full road network is computationally prohibitive within an operational time budget. Bounded Routes and Smart Routes speed up optimization using precomputed reduced networks; however, these methods do not account for vehicle information within each OD flow. Motivated by this gap, we propose a learning-based urban network reduction approach. A dedicated graph neural network (GNN) first scores road segments for each OD flow using the road network and vehicle-flow information. Highly ranked segments are then incorporated into complete OD routes, and the original segment-flow MILP is solved on the resulting reduced road network. The GNN thus guides the reduction of the road network, while the solver enforces feasibility and determines the routing. Experiments on four real urban networks -- Prague-Dejvice, London, New York, and Sydney -- show that the proposed approach achieves the smallest relative objective difference to the $30$-second full-graph reference in every city under both traffic regimes. Averaged across cities, this difference is more than six times lower than that of the best competing method. Moreover, traffic simulation results show that the optimization gains of the proposed method lead to more efficient routing, with the highest aggregate arrival rate and a competitive trip duration per arrival percentage point.

</details>


### [161] [Advancement or Acceleration? How Data Curation Improves LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36983)
*Jiaxing Wang,Zicheng Zhang,Deping Xiang,Jin Xu,Yong Li,Chao Liu,Pengzhang Liu,Qixia Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化区分了数据整理中的「提升上限」(advancement)与「加速收敛」(acceleration)，证明提升上限需要可修正的训练-目标差距；提出可在整理前计算的Δ检验预测持久收益，并指出整理任务本身而非方法决定上限提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管数据整理被公认为能改进大语言模型训练，但一个基础问题未解：整理是扩展模型最终可学习的能力上限，还是仅让模型用更少数据达到相同性能？现有研究混淆了这两种效应。

Method: 1. 理论形式化：定义 advancement(改进可达性能)与 acceleration(用更少数据达同等性能)的区别。2. 解析表征：证明 advancement 要求存在可修正的 train-target gap。3. Δ-test：基于 gap 中心视角，提出可观测的未整理基线训练损失与目标损失的差异 Δ，可在昂贵整理前计算。4. 大量真实 LLM 实验验证。

Result: 当基线训练目标与目标目标一致时，无整理策略能改进终点性能(尽管可加速)；显著非零的 Δ 预测持久收益，Δ≈0 处的收益随训练预算增长而消退。跨多种整理方法、数据混合与选择实验一致。

Conclusion: 数据整理的上限提升是整理任务(即目标集构建)的属性，而非整理方法的属性。Δ-test 为整理前评估提供实用工具，目标集构建是塑造整理成效的关键方向。

Abstract: While data curation is widely credited with improving large language model training, a basic question remains unresolved: does curation expand what a model can ultimately learn, or merely reach the same solution faster? In this paper, we formalize this distinction between advancement (improving the attainable performance) and acceleration (reaching the same performance with less data). Through an analytic characterization and real-world LLM experiments, we show that advancement requires a correctable train-target gap. When the baseline training and target objectives coincide, no curation policy improves the endpoint, although curation may still accelerate training. This reframes advancement as a property of the curation task rather than the curation method. Under a common gap-centric view of data mixture and selection, we operationalize this condition as the $Δ$-test, an observable discrepancy between the uncurated baseline's training and target losses that is computable prior to expensive curation. Across extensive experiments, an appreciably nonzero $Δ$ predicts persistent gains, whereas gains near $Δ=0$ tend to diminish as the training budget grows. Beyond individual methods, our findings recenter data curation on the task itself and point to target-set construction as a promising direction for shaping what curation can achieve.

</details>


### [162] [Heads of Security: Learned Attention Gates Improve Prompt Injection Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37657)
*Igor Shilov,Xiaoxue Yang,Alexander Panfilov,Evgenii Kortukov,Matthieu Meeus,Peter Romov,Wojciech Samek,Jonas Geiping,Yves-Alexandre de Montjoye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Heads of Security (HoS)，一种通过学习每个注意力头的门控机制来实现指令-数据分离的提示词注入防御方法。HoS 在 Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct 和 GPT-OSS-20B 上评估，对比了微调防御 SecAlign++ 和架构级防御 ASIDE。实验表明 HoS 对自适应攻击更鲁棒，保持基础模型效用，在代理基准上达到顶尖性能。黑盒 RL-Hammer 攻击对 HoS 仅 10% ASR（对比 GPT-OSS-20B 97%、SecAlign 85%），白盒 Claudini 攻击需 3 倍算力才能达到同等 ASR。HoS 在标准多选基准和 few-shot 设置下保持效用，而 SecAlign 和 ASIDE 表现下降。ASIDE 在工具使用上仅 10% 效用。结论：针对性注意力干预可实现并提升提示词注入鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示词注入防御存在局限：微调防御（如 SecAlign）在 few-shot 设置下效用下降，架构级防御（如 ASIDE）在工具使用等代理任务上表现差。需要一种能同时实现强鲁棒性、保持基础模型效用、且适配代理场景的防御方法。

Method: Heads of Security (HoS)：为每个注意力头学习一组可训练的门控参数，控制注意力在受信任令牌（指令）与非受信任令牌（数据/外部输入）之间的重新分配。通过在注意力计算中引入轻量级门控机制，强制模型仅对受信任令牌执行指令推理，而将非受信任令牌视为纯数据处理，从而实现指令-数据分离。仅需少量参数，无需全模型微调。

Result: 1. 黑盒 RL-Hammer 攻击：GPT-OSS-20B 97% ASR，SecAlign 85% ASR，HoS 仅 10% ASR。2. 白盒 Claudini 攻击（针对 HoS 强化）：需 3 倍 FLOPs 达到同等 ASR。3. 标准多选基准与 prompting 设置：HoS 保持基础模型效用，SecAlign 和 ASIDE 在 few-shot 下下降。4. 代理基准（静态攻击）：HoS 与 SecAlign 表现相近，显著优于基础模型；ASIDE 工具使用效用仅 10%。

Conclusion: 针对性注意力干预（学习式注意力头门控）可有效实现指令-数据分离，同时达成强自适应攻击鲁棒性、基础模型效用保持、代理任务高性能。HoS 证明了提示词注入鲁棒性可通过轻量级架构干预获得并改进，优于纯微调或重架构修改方案。

Abstract: We introduce Heads of Security (HoS), a prompt injection defense that enforces instruction-data separation via a small set of learned per-head attention gates. HoS learns how each head should redistribute attention between trusted and untrusted tokens, enabling models to reason over untrusted content without following injected instructions. We evaluate HoS on Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct and GPT-OSS-20B. We compare it against SecAlign++, a state-of-the-art finetuning-based defense, and ASIDE, a defense that, like ours, builds instruction-data separation into the model architecture. We show that HoS offers greater robustness to adaptive attacks, better performance on standard utility benchmarks, and matches top performance on standard agentic prompt injection benchmarks. More specifically, the black-box RL-Hammer attack reaches 97% ASR against GPT-OSS-20B and 85% against SecAlign, but only 10% against HoS. Claudini, a state-of-the-art white-box attack, strengthened against HoS through autoresearch, requires 3 times more FLOPs to reach the same ASR on HoS as it does on GPT-OSS-20B or SecAlign. HoS preserves base-model utility across standard multiple-choice benchmarks and prompting setups, whereas SecAlign and ASIDE degrade in few-shot settings. On agentic benchmarks with static attacks, HoS and SecAlign perform similarly and substantially improve the security-utility trade-off over the base model. ASIDE struggles with tool use, achieving only 10% utility. These results show that prompt injection robustness can be achieved and improved through targeted attention interventions.

</details>


### [163] [CaLLM: Continual Adaptation of LLMs via Non-Parametric Adapter Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36994)
*Bilge Celik,Anna Vettoruzzo,Joaquin Vanschoren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CaLLM通过基于原型的模块化路由实现LLM的持续适应，结合度量元学习与轻量级适配器隔离，实现零样本任务-专家分配，在多个基准上超越隔离和重放方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM持续适应策略难以扩展或缺乏鲁棒的零样本任务到专家分配机制，灾难性遗忘仍是开放问题。

Method: CaLLM将输入批次投影到任务无关嵌入空间寻找最近原型，路由到对应专用适配器；结合度量元学习与模块化参数隔离（轻量级适配器），实现可控跨任务干扰、常数计算复杂度、原型扩展集成新分布、在线/离线统一处理。

Result: 路由机制发现语义连贯任务分组，促进相似任务建设性干扰，开销极小；在多样细粒度持续学习基准上，CaLLM持续优于传统隔离和重放方法。

Conclusion: 基于原型的模块化路由是LLM终身适应的原则性且可扩展方法。

Abstract: Continual adaptation of large language models (LLMs) to evolving task distributions while mitigating catastrophic forgetting remains an open problem. Existing strategies often struggle to scale or lack a robust mechanism for zero-shot task-to-expert assignment. We introduce CaLLM, a framework that resolves these tensions by combining metric-based meta-learning with modular parameter isolation via lightweight adapters. First, CaLLM projects each input batch into a task-agnostic embedding space to find its nearest prototype. It then routes the batch to the specific adapter specialized for that data distribution. This formulation provides (i)~controllable cross-task interference by design, (ii)~constant computational complexity regardless of task count, (iii)~seamless integration of novel distributions through prototype expansion, and (iv)~regime agnosticism as the local routing decision enables a unified approach to both online and offline settings. Our experiments demonstrate that this routing mechanism discovers semantically coherent task groupings while facilitating constructive interference between similar tasks, and keeping overhead at a minimum. Across diverse and fine-grained continual learning benchmarks, CaLLM consistently outperforms traditional isolation and replay methods, establishing prototype-based modular routing as a principled and scalable approach to lifelong adaptation of LLMs.

</details>


### [164] [Meta Models: Models that Model Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37001)
*Peihang Li,Ziming Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出用表格上下文学习器 TabPFN 作为元模型，预测神经网络训练行为，无需微调 LLM，仅需秒级推理，且结合 KAN 可进一步提升精度；通过预注册预测 535B 参数 Marin Hero Run 验证了方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作将元建模视为文本任务，微调 LLM 需耗费大量算力；作者认为训练记录本质是数值数据，应直接作为表格数据处理，避免 tokenization 与微调开销。

Method: 将真实语言模型训练记录作为原生数值表格数据，直接输入现成的 TabPFN 进行上下文学习预测；对比微调 LLM 元模型；进一步用 Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KAN) 增强 TabPFN；预注册预测正在进行的 535B 参数 Marin Hero Run 训练曲线。

Result: TabPFN 在多个预测设置中匹配或超越微调 LLM 元模型，且仅需秒级拟合预测时间，无需任务特定权重更新；KAN 增强后在选定任务上进一步提升精度；预注册预测 Marin Hero Run 结果随训练逐步揭示，验证了元模型对未观测训练结果的预测能力。

Conclusion: 元建模应被视为独立的数值模态而非 LLM 文本任务；现成表格基础模型（如 TabPFN）配合 KAN 等增强可高效、准确地预测大规模模型训练行为，为训练前预测与超参数选择提供实用工具。

Abstract: Every neural-network training run produces not only a model, but also evidence about how models learn. Can these accumulated experiments enable us to predict and compare new training runs before executing them? We study meta-models: models that predict the learning behavior of other models. From this perspective, final-performance prediction, learning-curve prediction, and hyperparameter selection are different queries to the same training response function. We take a first, humble empirical step toward this goal: treating real language-model training records as native numeric data, we find that an off-the-shelf tabular in-context learner, TabPFN, can match or outperform a fine-tuned LLM meta-model in multiple evaluated settings. Whereas recent work fine-tunes LLMs on tokenized configurations at a cost of tens to hundreds of GPU-hours, TabPFN requires seconds of fit-and-predict time without task-specific weight updates. We argue that meta-modeling is better treated as its own numeric modality rather than as a text task for LLMs. Going one step further, augmenting TabPFN with Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) further improves accuracy on selected prediction tasks. Finally, we pre-registered a forecast of the Marin Hero Run, a 535B-parameter public training run, whose ground truth is revealed point by point as training progresses, putting meta-model predictions to the test against training outcomes not yet observed at the time of forecasting.

</details>


### [165] [Crop Yield Prediction Using Regression Models: A Comparative Evaluation of Model Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37022)
*Saptak Das,Arijit Sarkar,Soumik Neogi,Suborno Naskar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究评估了五种回归模型（线性回归、随机森林、决策树、岭回归、LASSO回归）在作物产量预测中的表现，基于100万观测值的环境、作物及管理变量数据集，发现线性回归及其正则化变体提供了具有竞争力的基线性能，LASSO在70:30划分下表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的作物产量预测可支持农业规划与资源分配，因此需要评估不同回归模型在真实农业数据集上的预测能力。

Method: 使用包含1,000,000个观测值和10个变量的数据集，移除231个负目标值后，对分类变量进行独热编码、布尔变量二值化；对比多元线性回归(LR)、随机森林回归(RF)、决策树回归(DT)、岭回归和LASSO回归；采用80:20和70:30训练测试划分，以决定系数(R²)、均方误差(MSE)和均方根误差(RMSE)评估性能。

Result: 80:20划分下LR测试R²约0.9130、RMSE 0.5008；70:30划分下LASSO测试R²最高(约0.9131)、RMSE最低(约0.5008)，与LR和岭回归性能近乎相同；DT在70:30划分下测试性能提升，而RF表现出显著的训练-测试性能差距。

Conclusion: 评估的线性回归模型为此数据集提供了具有竞争力的基线；后续需通过验证测试进一步确定各模型的性能与可预测性。

Abstract: Reliable crop-yield prediction can support agricultural planning and resource allocation. This study evaluates five regression models for predicting crop yield from environmental, crop-related, and agricultural management variables. The dataset initially contained 1,000,000 observations and ten variables, with 231 observations having negative target values removed during preprocessing. Categorical variables were represented using one-hot encoding, and Boolean variables were converted into binary values. The models considered were multiple linear regression (LR), random-forest regression (RF), decision-tree regression (DT), ridge regression, and lasso regression. Their performance was evaluated using 80:20 and 70:30 training-testing splits based on the coefficient of determination (R-squared), mean squared error (MSE), and root mean squared error (RMSE). Applying an 80:20 split, LR achieved a test R-squared of approximately 0.9130 and a test RMSE of 0.5008. Applying a 70:30 split, lasso regression yielded the highest test R-squared (approximately 0.9131) and the lowest test RMSE (approximately 0.5008), with performance almost identical to LR and ridge regression. DT also showed improved test performance under the 70:30 split, whereas RF exhibited a substantial gap between training and testing performance. These results indicate that the evaluated linear regression model provides competitive baselines for this dataset. Further experimentation, including validation testing, will help determine the models' performance and predictability.

</details>


### [166] [On the Sparsity of Direct Preference Optimization: Weight Disentanglement in the NTK Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37085)
*Kensuke Sasaki,Issei Sato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文理论分析了直接偏好优化（DPO）诱导的稀疏参数更新现象，证明了其隐式最大间隔偏差，提出了双重稀疏性概念（数据层面的支持向量稀疏性和参数层面的梯度抵消稀疏性），并通过实验验证了DPO在多种模型和数据集上显著减少跨任务干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPO被广泛用于语言模型与人类偏好的对齐，实验观察到其产生显著稀疏的参数更新，但缺乏理论解释。本文旨在从优化动力学角度揭示这一现象的理论机制，并建立稀疏性与模型合并友好性的联系。

Method: 采用神经切线核（NTK）线性化框架分析DPO优化动力学，证明logistic损失结构导致隐式最大间隔偏差；引入双重稀疏性概念，将更新方向形式化为稀疏支持向量组合的稀疏梯度特征向量；主定理给出支持对数量的边界，并建立双重稀疏性与权重解缠的理论框架。

Result: 在GPT-2 Small/Medium和7-9B指令调优模型（Llama-3-8B, Mistral-7B, Gemma-2-9B）上，使用Anthropic HH-RLHF和UltraFeedback数据集实验验证了双重稀疏性的两个来源；GPT-2实验显示DPO相比监督微调将跨任务干扰降低多达35倍。

Conclusion: DPO的logistic损失结构隐式诱导最大间隔偏差，产生双重稀疏性——数据层面的支持向量稀疏性和参数层面的梯度抵消稀疏性。这种结构促进权重解缠，显著减少跨任务干扰，为模型合并提供有利条件，解释了DPO在实践中观察到的稀疏更新现象。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) has been widely adopted for aligning language models with human preferences. Recent empirical work has revealed that DPO induces remarkably sparse parameter updates, yet no theoretical explanation for this phenomenon has been offered. We analyze DPO's optimization dynamics under a neural tangent kernel linearization and show that the logistic structure of the DPO loss gives rise to an implicit maximum-margin bias. Building on this observation, we introduce the concept of dual sparsity: the DPO update direction is a sparse linear combination (data-level sparsity, driven by support vectors) of sparse gradient feature vectors (parameter-level sparsity, arising from gradient cancellation between preferred and dispreferred responses). We formalize this structure in our main theorem, bound the number of support pairs by a margin-dependent spectral quantity, and present a framework relating dual sparsity to weight disentanglement---a condition favorable for model merging. Experiments on GPT-2 Small and Medium and on 7--9B instruction-tuned models (Llama-3-8B, Mistral-7B, Gemma-2-9B) with the Anthropic HH-RLHF and UltraFeedback datasets confirm both sources of sparsity, and experiments on GPT-2 show that DPO reduces cross-task interference by up to $35{\times}$ compared to supervised fine-tuning.

</details>


### [167] [Generative Replay for Equilibrium Propagation: Toward Edge-Friendly Continual Learning in Hopfield Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37139)
*Victor Cook,Gilles Sassatelli,Thierry Gil,Mohamed Watfa,Marina Reyboz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于能量的生成式重放策略（EB-Gen），利用Hopfield网络的内在生成动力学解决Equilibrium Propagation（EqProp）训练中的灾难性遗忘问题，无需外部数据缓冲区或额外架构，在Split MNIST和Split CIFAR10上分别达到75%和64%的平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Equilibrium Propagation（EqProp）作为一种生物学合理且硬件友好的反向传播替代方案，用于训练Hopfield网络，但在持续学习设置下的行为尚未充分探索。现有的经验重放（ER）和生成式重放（Gen-Replay）方法不可避免地需要外部数据缓冲区或额外架构开销，因此需要从内部机制出发解决灾难性遗忘问题。

Method: 提出Energy-Based Generative Replay（EB-Gen）策略，利用Hopfield网络的内在生成动力学，使单一模型同时充当分类器和重放样本生成器。通过冻结网络副本并使其松弛生成捕捉过去任务知识的伪数据，仅通过内部生成的样本进行排练，无需外部存储或额外生成模型。

Result: 在类别增量设置下，使用Split MNIST和Split CIFAR10基准测试，与FineTune、Experience Replay、Generative Replay、Sleep-Like Replay Consolidation（SRC）和Joint training对比。EB-Gen显著提高稳定性同时保持可塑性，在Split MNIST和Split CIFAR10上分别达到75%和64%的平均准确率。

Conclusion: 研究表明，Equilibrium Propagation可以通过内生的生成式重放机制有效缓解灾难性遗忘，同时保持其生物学合理性和硬件友好性等核心优势，为神经形态计算中的持续学习提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Equilibrium Propagation (EqProp) offers a biologically grounded and hardware-aligned alternative to backpropagation for training Hopfield Networks. However, its behavior in continual learning settings remains largely unexplored. This work confronts catastrophic forgetting in EqProp-trained Hopfield Networks from the inside out, eliminating the need for external data buffers or architectural overhead that standard Experience Replay \textbf{(ER)} and Generative Replay \textbf{(Gen-Replay)} inevitably require. We introduce an Energy-Based Generative Replay strategy \textbf{(EB-Gen)} which exploits the intrinsic generative dynamics of Hopfield Networks, allowing a single model to act as both classifier and replay samples generator. Samples capturing past task knowledge are generated by relaxing a frozen copy of the network, enabling rehearsal solely through internally generated pseudo-data. We evaluate the proposed method in the Class-Incremental setting, using the split versions of the MNIST and CIFAR10 benchmarks and compare it with FineTune, Experience Replay, Generative Replay, Sleep-Like Replay Consolidation (SRC) and Joint training. Our results show that EB-Gen significantly improves stability while preserving plasticity, achieving a 75\% and 64\% average accuracy on Split MNIST and Split CIFAR10 respectively. These findings show that Equilibrium Propagation can be adapted to alleviate catastrophic forgetting without forgoing the traits which made it compelling in the first place.

</details>


### [168] [Beyond Parameter Protection: Fisher-Shaped EWC for Replay-Free Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37145)
*Liying Wang,Simone Magistri,Joost van de Weijer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Fisher-Shaped EWC (FS-EWC)，解决传统 EWC 中局部 Fisher 信息矩阵 (FIM) 无法刻画有限参数位移后敏感度的问题。FS-EWC 在任务学习阶段最小化邻域内最坏情况的经验 FIM 迹，显式偏向对未来有界位移不敏感的解；任务结束时采用基于轨迹的模型融合从多个优化状态构建保留解，再估计 FIM 用于后续巩固。实验表明 FS-EWC 超越 EWC，实现更优的稳定性-可塑性平衡和更强的参数漂移容忍度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无重放持续学习中，EWC 利用 FIM 保护历史重要参数，但局部重要性估计无法在有限参数位移后准确刻画敏感度，导致知识保护效果受限。这激励作者控制潜在未来更新邻域内的 Fisher 敏感度，从而将 FIM 的作用从单纯的参数保护扩展为面向未来保留的解形成。

Method: FS-EWC 包含两个核心机制：(1) 任务学习阶段，最小化规定邻域内最坏情况的经验 FIM 迹，显式偏向对有界未来位移不敏感的解；(2) 任务完成时，采用基于轨迹的模型融合，从多个优化状态而非单一终端迭代构建保留解，随后估计 FIM 用于后续巩固。

Result: 跨多个基准实验表明 FS-EWC 性能优于 EWC；受控研究显示其具有更优的稳定性-可塑性平衡和更强的参数漂移容忍度。

Conclusion: 有效的知识保留要求既要在形成解时考虑未来保留，又要保护已获取的知识。FS-EWC 通过邻域 Fisher 敏感度控制和轨迹融合，同时实现了这两点。

Abstract: In replay-free continual learning, Elastic Weight Consolidation (EWC) uses Fisher information matrix (FIM) to protect acquired knowledge by penalizing changes to historically important parameters. However, a local importance estimate at the retained solution does not in general characterize sensitivity after finite parameter displacement. This motivates controlling Fisher sensitivity over a neighborhood of potential future updates. We propose Fisher-Shaped EWC (FS-EWC), which extends the role of FIM from parameter protection to solution formation for future retention. During task learning, FS-EWC minimizes the worst-case empirical FIM trace within a prescribed neighborhood, explicitly favoring solutions that are less sensitive to bounded future displacement. At task completion, trajectory-based model merging constructs the retained solution from multiple optimization states rather than a single terminal iterate, after which FIM is estimated for subsequent consolidation. Experiments across multiple benchmarks show that FS-EWC improves performance over EWC, while controlled studies indicate a more favorable stability--plasticity balance and greater tolerance to parameter drift. These results suggest that effective knowledge retention requires both forming solutions with future retention in mind and protecting acquired knowledge.

</details>


### [169] [Variational Optimal Control for Diffusion Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37146)
*Ruoyu Wang,Xinyi Ye,Yunshuo Shi,Liangyu Yuan,Beier Zhu,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出变分最优引导（VOG）和蒸馏变分最优控制（DVOC），通过共享的雅可比回拉将采样时引导与奖励模型适配统一，在ImageNet上显著降低FID，在Z-Image-Turbo上提升奖励分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的采样时引导和基于奖励的模型适配缺乏统一的理论框架，需要一种几何原则来连接推理时控制和奖励适配。

Method: 从终端奖励最大化和路径空间KL惩罚出发，利用Gibbs变分原理和Doob变换推导生成动力学的修正；通过局部去噪近似揭示将输出偏好传输到受控变量的雅可比回拉；由此构建状态空间的VOG，并通过将局部改进的端点蒸馏到模型参数中扩展为DVOC。

Result: 在ImageNet上，VOG将DiT-XL/2的FID从2.27降至2.04（250步）和从7.58降至4.44（25步）；在Z-Image-Turbo上，DVOC经过20次更新后提升HPSv3++和四个保留评估器的分数，在对比训练方法中达到最高均值。

Conclusion: 该工作通过共享的雅可比回拉几何原理，统一了推理时控制（VOG）和奖励适配（DVOC），为扩散模型的引导和微调提供了共同的理论基础。

Abstract: We present a variational control framework that connects sampling-time guidance and reward-based model adaptation through a shared Jacobian pullback. Starting from terminal reward maximization with a path-space KL penalty, the Gibbs variational principle and Doob transform yield a correction to the generative dynamics. A local denoising approximation exposes the pullback that transports output preferences to the controlled variable. This construction gives Variational Optimal Guidance (VOG) in state space and extends to Distilled Variational Optimal Control (DVOC) by amortizing locally improved endpoints into model parameters. The resulting controls share a local variational rule: VOG acts through the denoising Jacobian, while DVOC realizes the parameter pullback by endpoint distillation. Under a linear MMSE model, VOG's exact correction lies in the data subspace. On ImageNet, VOG reduces DiT-XL/2 FID from 2.27 to 2.04 at 250 sampling steps and from 7.58 to 4.44 at 25 steps. After 20 updates on Z-Image-Turbo, DVOC improves HPSv3++ and four held-out evaluator scores over the untrained model, with the highest corresponding means among the trained methods compared. These results connect inference-time control and reward adaptation through a common geometric principle.

</details>


### [170] [Physics-Guided Flow Matching: A Generative AI Framework for Global Total Electron Content Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37159)
*Si-Tong Yang,Feng Ye,Xian Lu,Dong Lin,Benjamin C. Martinez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于流匹配的物理引导生成式AI框架，用于逐日全球总电子含量(TEC)预报，将电离层动力学偏微分方程(PDE)融入生成过程，自适应调整物理与统计贡献，显著提升预报精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确预报全球TEC对空间天气监测和卫星通信导航系统可靠运行至关重要，现有统计、深度学习及生成式AI方法在时空精度上仍有提升空间，需引入物理知识增强预报能力。

Method: 基于流匹配构建生成式框架，将电离层动力学PDE作为物理约束嵌入样本变换过程，结合数据驱动的统计模式，通过自适应权重机制在各位置动态平衡物理与统计贡献，利用TIEGCM一年全球TEC数据训练评估。

Result: 模型在全时效和各纬度上均优于代表性基准(统计、深度学习、生成式AI)，平均RMSE比ConvLSTM降低39.2%，比无物理引导版本降低23.8%；学习到的贡献权重随纬度和地理位置系统性变化，揭示物理贡献的空间分布特征。

Conclusion: 显式物理引导能有效提升生成式AI对全球TEC的预报性能，证实将物理动力学融入生成式建模的价值，为空间天气预报提供了新范式。

Abstract: Accurate forecasting of global total electron content (TEC) is important for space weather monitoring and the reliable operation of satellite communication and navigation systems. We propose a physics-guided generative artificial intelligence (AI) framework for day-ahead global TEC forecasting. The framework builds on flow matching, which learns to gradually transform samples from an initial distribution into new samples that share the statistical characteristics of the target data. The proposed model incorporates ionospheric dynamics described by a partial differential equation (PDE) into this transformation process, guiding the changes between initial and target samples using physical knowledge. The model further combines the physical dynamics with statistical patterns captured from data to learn this transformation, adaptively adjusting their relative contributions at each location. The model is trained and evaluated using one year of global TEC data generated by the Thermosphere Ionosphere Electrodynamics General Circulation Model (TIEGCM). The results demonstrate that our model improves both spatial and temporal forecasting accuracy over representative benchmarks, including statistical, deep learning, and generative AI methods at all lead times and across latitudes. The average root mean square error (RMSE) is 39.2% lower than that of ConvLSTM and 23.8% lower than that of the same framework without physical guidance. The learned contribution weights vary systematically with latitude and across geographic locations, showing how the physical contribution changes across the forecast domain. These findings demonstrate that explicit physical guidance improves generative forecasts of global TEC and highlight the value of incorporating physical dynamics into generative AI modeling.

</details>


### [171] [MLE-World: Predicting Machine Learning Experiment Outcomes with a Learned World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37175)
*Brian Ko,Han Wang,Hejia Geng,Li Erran Li,Sherry Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 MLE-World，一个基于 Qwen3.5-4B 微调的世界模型，能在不执行代码的情况下预测 ML 程序的终端输出（错误堆栈、训练日志、验证分数）并给出置信度评分，从而让 AI 研究代理在测试时扩展（TTS）中以低成本比较多个候选程序。实验表明，MLE-World 在 17 个 MLE-bench Lite 任务上对未见程序的终端输出预测准确率达 77.9%，远超大模型提示基线的 41.1%；配合五种 TTS 策略，性能比无世界模型的代理提升高达 22.1%，同时减少真实执行次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 研究代理需要反复提出、执行、改进 ML 程序，但每个候选程序都需完整训练和评估，计算成本极高，严重限制了每一步可探索的候选数量。现有方法依赖大模型直接提示预测，准确率低；需要一种轻量、高精度的世界模型来替代真实执行，实现廉价的测试时扩展。

Method: 1. 训练 MLE-World：从 Qwen3.5-4B 微调，输入程序代码和数据描述，输出预测的终端输出（错误、日志、指标）及置信度分数。2. 设计五种由 MLE-World 引导的 TTS 策略：顺序修正、Best-of-N、成对裁决、融合、以及基于置信度的 UCB 探索奖励规则。3. 仅当置信度低时才真实执行候选程序，其余步骤靠预测完成筛选。

Result: 在 17 个 MLE-bench Lite 任务的 held-out 程序上，MLE-World 终端输出预测匹配率 77.9%，大幅优于大模型提示基线的 41.1%。五种 TTS 策略中，最佳策略使观测到的平均性能比无世界模型代理提升 22.1%，且真实执行次数更少。

Conclusion: MLE-World 证明了小参数量微调世界模型可高精度预测 ML 程序执行结果，配合置信度感知的 TTS 策略，能以极低计算开销大幅提升 AI 研究代理的搜索效率和最终性能，为自动化机器学习提供了实用的测试时扩展范式。

Abstract: AI research agents can propose, execute, and improve programs that solve machine learning tasks. However, executing each candidate program requires training and evaluating ML models, limiting how many candidates an agent can explore at each step. We introduce MLE-World, a world model finetuned from Qwen3.5-4B that predicts a program's terminal output (error traces, training logs, and validation scores) from its code and data description without executing it, together with a confidence score estimating the prediction's correctness. Because a prediction costs one inference call rather than a training run, MLE-World makes test time scaling (TTS) affordable for these agents. We build five TTS policies, guided by MLE-World, that compare several candidates per step by their predicted outputs: Sequential revision, Best-of-N, Pairwise judging, Fusion, and a UCB style rule that also adds an exploration bonus from this confidence score. Since predictions can be wrong, we run a candidate for real only when its confidence score is low. On 17 MLE-bench Lite tasks, MLE-World matches the real terminal output on 77.9% of held out programs, versus 41.1% for much larger prompted models. MLE-World guided TTS achieves up to 22.1% higher observed mean performance than the agent without a world model, with less real execution. Project webpage: https://mle-world.github.io/.

</details>


### [172] [Action-Value Calibration via Continuation Sharing for Long-Horizon Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37195)
