Flat node scores also obscure cross-document evidence trails, limiting continued exploration. We propose Contrastive Evidence Paths (CEP), which scores candidates by their strongest individual paths on a query-conditioned document graph and calibrates these scores against the same graph's background visitation distribution to mitigate structural bias. CEP ranks candidates by the calibrated scores and retains the corresponding source paths. Building on CEP, we develop Structured Corpus Interaction (SCI), which organizes candidates and their connecting paths into a structured Map, enabling agents to verify source documents and pursue missing information through expansion from anchor documents. Experiments on BrowseComp-Plus and TREC-COVID show that SCI achieves state-of-the-art answer accuracy and nDCG@10 with the lowest generation cost. Compared with the strongest baselines by task performance, maximum reductions in tool calls and total generation costs exceed 50% and 40%, respectively. Further analysis confirms that SCI with CEP suppresses ranking inflation induced by structural connections and improves gold recall and exploration efficiency.

</details>


### [207] [Is the education gap in generative AI use smaller than the internet's was at a similar level of use? Evidence from 16 European countries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36181)
*Keisuke Watanabe*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文比较了2025年生成式AI使用的教育差距与互联网在相似普及水平时的教育差距，发现生成式AI的教育差距在大多数国家显著更小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究新技术采用早期的教育差距模式，检验生成式AI是否比互联网早期更少受教育程度影响。

Method: 使用Eurostat ICT使用调查数据（16-74岁），将各国2025年生成式AI使用率与互联网使用率最接近的年份匹配（误差≤5个百分点），以对数比率和百分点差异衡量高学历（ISCED 5-8）与低学历（ISCED 0-2）人群的使用差距，共匹配16个国家。

Result: 在匹配的16国中，生成式AI的教育差距在13国的对数比率上更小（中位数变化-0.625，95% bootstrap区间-0.969至-0.291，p=0.021），在15国的百分点差距上更小（中位数变化-27.6点，区间-32.2至-16.8，p=0.0005）。百分点结果在敏感性分析中稳健，对数比率结果较脆弱。

Conclusion: 生成式AI早期采用的教育差距小于互联网早期，但结果仅描述非随机国家样本中的未调整教育对比，不代表教育因果效应，不能推广至整个欧盟。

Abstract: New technologies are usually adopted first by people with more formal education. This paper asks whether the education gap in generative AI use in 2025 is smaller or larger than the internet's gap in the same country in the year when overall internet use was at a similar level. It uses public Eurostat survey data on ICT usage by individuals aged 16 to 74. Each country's 2025 rate of generative AI use in the last three months is matched to the internet year whose overall last-three-months use was closest, within 5 percentage points, and the gap between people with high (ISCED 5 to 8) and low (ISCED 0 to 2) completed education is measured as a log ratio and as a percentage-point difference. Sixteen of 35 countries could be matched; most of the others were early internet adopters. In the matched countries, the generative AI gap was smaller in 13 of 16 countries on the log ratio (median change -0.625, 95% bootstrap interval -0.969 to -0.291, two-sided sign test p = 0.021) and in 15 of 16 on the percentage-point gap (median change -27.6 points, interval -32.2 to -16.8, p = 0.0005). The point-gap result keeps its sign in every generative-AI-versus-internet sensitivity analysis with 8 or more countries, including comparisons restricted on both sides to ages 25 to 54 and 55 to 74, but its size is sensitive to matching that takes education composition into account (about -10 to -11 points in 7 and 8 countries). The ratio result is fragile: it fails the decision rule when countries matched at their first internet observation are removed (9 of 11, p = 0.065). The analysis follows an unpublished plan whose timing cannot be verified, so it is not presented as pre-registered. The results describe unadjusted education contrasts in a non-random set of countries, not an education effect, and do not generalise to the EU as a whole.

</details>


### [208] [Counterfactual Profile Perturbations for Recommendation Explanations: A Reproducibility Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36208)
*Meysam Varasteh,Elizabeth McKinnie,Robin Burke*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文复现并扩展了 LXR 反事实解释技术，验证其在非最高推荐项目上的有效性，对比流行度与潜在因子相似度基线，发现流行度影响解释质量，并通过暴力搜索估计最优扰动下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在复现并扩展 LXR 研究，探索其在更广泛推荐场景下的解释能力，评估替代基线的表现，量化项目流行度对解释质量的影响，并建立最优扰动大小的理论下界以供比较。

Method: 在原始 LXR 框架基础上，扩展解释目标至非最高推荐项目；引入基于项目流行度和潜在因子相似度的基线方法；分析不同流行度项目的解释扰动长度；对部分扰动空间进行暴力搜索以估计最优扰动下界。

Result: LXR 对非最高推荐项目也有效；流行度基线和潜在因子相似度基线在某些任务中表现出人意料地好；解释不太流行的项目时 LXR 能产生更短的扰动（更高质量）；暴力搜索提供了最优扰动大小的下界估计。

Conclusion: LXR 技术具有较强的泛化能力，但简单基线在特定条件下具备竞争力；项目流行度是影响反事实解释质量的关键因素；最优扰动下界为未来方法评估提供了基准。

Abstract: This reproducibility paper explores the counterfactual explanation technique known as Learning to eXplain Recommendations (LXR). Prior work has shown that LXR can reliably identify perturbations that, if applied to a user's profile, cause the user's top ranked item to no longer be recommended, thereby explaining the item's recommendation as a function of the user's rating choices. In this paper, we extend this work in multiple ways. We expand the scope of the original LXR study to look at explanations for items other than the most highly recommended item, finding that LXR is also effective at this version of the task. We consider alternate baselines for LXR, including item popularity and latent factor similarity, and we see that for some tasks these alternatives work surprisingly well. We also examine the impact of item popularity, finding that LXR offers better explanation quality (shorter perturbations) when less popular items are being explained. Finally, we conduct a brute-force exploration of a subset of the perturbation space in order to estimate a lower bound for optimal perturbation size against which LXR and other such methods can be compared.

</details>


### [209] [Preserving rare-item recall under a storage budget: quantization, distillation, and the limits of teacher fidelity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36267)
*Han Chen,Yingrui Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 在固定嵌入存储预算下，高秩低精度因子分解（4-bit，秩300）仅用教师模型13%的存储，却能保留95-103%的尾部召回率和93-97%的NDCG，显著优于低秩全精度模型和知识蒸馏学生模型；教师命中保真度不能可靠反映尾部召回表现，压缩推荐器应以总尾部召回而非教师保真度为评判标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在嵌入存储预算固定的情况下，推荐系统面临选择小规模全精度模型、蒸馏学生模型或高秩低精度模型的权衡。现有工作缺乏系统性对比，且常以教师模型保真度为代理指标，但未验证其是否真实反映稀有物品召回能力。本文旨在实证研究哪种压缩策略最能保留尾部召回，以及教师保真度是否为可靠代理指标。

Method: 在七个数据集-骨干网络组合（六个数据集上的矩阵分解，一个数据集上的LightGCN）上，设定每用户/物品行168字节存储预算，对比七种候选模型：40维BPR、两个40维无偏知识蒸馏学生模型、以及300维教师模型得分在秩40、80、155、300下的因子分解（分别用32、16、8、4位存储）。固定候选模型后在测试集上单次评估，进行确证性分析。

Result: 秩300的4-bit因子分解仅用教师13%存储，在所有七个单元中保留95-103%的尾部召回@20和93-97%的NDCG@20。秩40因子分解尾部召回最低；4-bit因子分解尾部召回是其1.2-12.7倍，NDCG为0.99-1.46倍。UnKD学生保留教师尾部召回的56-102%。在十个预设定对比中，4-bit因子分解尾部召回均显著更高。教师命中保真度在147个候选对中有23个与尾部召回排序不一致，在3/7个单元中指出了错误的尾部召回领先者。

Conclusion: 在固定存储预算下，分配字节给秩而非精度能以7.5倍的算术运算量保留稀有物品召回。压缩推荐器的评估应直接使用总尾部召回，而非仅依赖教师保真度指标。

Abstract: Under a fixed embedding-storage budget, recommenders can use a small full-precision model, a distilled student, or a higher-rank low-precision model. We asked which choice preserves rare-item recall and whether teacher fidelity reflects it in seven dataset-backbone cells: matrix factorization on Gowalla, Last.fm, Amazon Video Games, Steam, Goodbooks-10k and MovieLens-20M, and LightGCN on Amazon Video Games. Seven candidates fit in 168 bytes per user or item row: 40-dimensional Bayesian personalized ranking (BPR), two 40-dimensional unbiased knowledge distillation (UnKD) students, and factorizations of a 300-dimensional teacher's scores at ranks 40, 80, 155 and 300, stored in 32, 16, 8 and 4 bits. Each test set was evaluated once after fixing candidates and confirmatory analyses. The rank-300 4-bit factorization used 13% of teacher storage and retained 95-103% of its tail recall@20 and 93-97% of its NDCG@20 in every cell. The rank-40 factorization had the lowest tail recall in every cell; the 4-bit factorization had 1.2-12.7 times its tail recall and 0.99-1.46 times its NDCG. UnKD students retained 56-102% of teacher tail recall. In all ten prespecified comparisons across five prospective-expansion cells, the 4-bit factorization had higher tail recall, with simultaneous 95% intervals excluding zero. Teacher-hit retention, the share of the teacher's relevant tail hits also found by a model, ordered 23 of 147 candidate pairs differently from tail recall and identified the wrong tail-recall leader in 3 of 7 cells: models can find relevant tail items the teacher misses. In these cells, spending bytes on rank rather than precision preserved rare-item recall at 7.5 times the arithmetic per score. Compressed recommenders should be judged by total tail recall, not teacher fidelity alone.

</details>


### [210] [Cross-Day Compatibility of Daily Item Embeddings: An Empirical Study of Alignment, Scale Management, and CTR Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36408)
*Yicheng Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究每日物品共现嵌入与次日行为的配对，避免未来行为泄露，对比Word2Vec和基于物品图的XSimGCL变体，在初始化、继承、后处理、训练约束和下游增强等维度评估48种配置，发现上游干预显著提升兼容性和CTR，热启动虽强但积累尺度漂移，全局重缩放可抑制漂移并提升GAUC，下游降维编码器能进一步适应残留漂移，工业结果支持上游稳定化、继承尺度管理和下游任务学习的互补作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决每日物品嵌入与次日行为配对时的版本不一致问题：多日CTR训练混合了同一物品不同版本的嵌入，其方向和范数可能差异大，导致上游嵌入与下游CTR模型的兼容性下降，需要系统性探索上游稳定化、继承尺度控制及下游适应策略。

Method: 采用工业级大规模实验设计：对比Word2Vec与物品图XSimGCL适配，涵盖初始化（ID-init）、继承（正交对齐）、后处理（全局重缩放）、训练约束及下游增强（降维编码器）等48种配置，其中32种为通用协议CTR评估；指标以Word2Vec基线为0%报告相对百分比变化，原始数值和数据集规模未公开。

Result: 三大发现：1) 上游干预显著提升兼容性与CTR：ID-init和正交对齐分别使余弦相似度提升51.2%和93.4%，GAUC提升2.2%和2.6%；检索与几何指标不足以作为下游替代指标。2) 热启动虽强但累积尺度漂移：8天后日匹配范数增幅达102.5%，每日全局重缩放将其限制在8.2%并获得最高GAUC增益4.0%。3) 下游降维编码器适应残留漂移：对比随机投影、SVD和未训练网络，训练编码器在6种上游方法中有4种达到最高跨日余弦相似度，其余两种分列第3和第2。

Conclusion: 工业实验证实上游稳定化（初始化/对齐）、继承尺度管理（全局重缩放）和下游任务学习（降维编码器）三者互补，共同解决每日嵌入版本不一致导致的CTR性能下降问题。

Abstract: Daily item co-occurrence embeddings are paired with next-day behavior without future-behavior leakage. Multi-day click-through rate (CTR) training therefore mixes versions whose directions and norms may differ for the same item. We compare Word2Vec and an item-graph XSimGCL adaptation across initialization, inheritance, post-processing, training constraints, and downstream augmentation. The inventory contains 48 configurations, including 32 common-protocol CTR evaluations. Observed metrics are reported as relative percentage changes from the corresponding Word2Vec baseline (0%), with raw values and exact dataset sizes withheld. Three findings emerge. First, multiple upstream interventions improve compatibility and CTR. ID-init and orthogonal alignment increase cosine by 51.2% and 93.4%, and GAUC by 2.2% and 2.6%, relative to ordinary Word2Vec. Retrieval and geometry remain insufficient downstream surrogates. Second, warm starts perform strongly but accumulate scale. The day-matched norm increase over Word2Vec reaches 102.5% after eight days; daily global rescaling limits it to 8.2% and attains the highest observed GAUC increase, 4.0%. Third, CTR learning through a dimensionality-reducing encoder further adapts to residual drift. Against dimension-matched random-projection, SVD, and untrained-network controls, trained encoders attain the highest cross-day cosine for four of six upstream methods. The other two rank third and second in train-to-test cosine. These industrial results support complementary roles for upstream stabilization, inherited-scale management, and downstream task learning.

</details>


### [211] [ACOR: incremental evidence accumulation towards real-time DNA storage reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36500)
*Linxuan Han,Huaming Wu,Shi Jia,Guanjin Qu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ACOR 是一种用于 DNA 存储的流式共识重建方法，能在测序过程中实时处理读段，利用持久化账本记录 k-mer 转移和边界支持，通过长度约束束搜索和基于优先级的选择在聚类闭合时解码，实现了秒级重建千万级读段且恢复率极高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNA 存储需要从含噪副本中重建序列。传统离线方法在读段收集完成后才重建，将重建与持续读段收集分离。流式重建可将证据处理与测序重叠，减少下游解码前的计算量。

Method: ACOR 在持久化账本中记录 k-mer 转移和边界支持，聚类闭合时采用长度约束束搜索和基于优先级的选择进行解码。采用稀疏存储降低高上下文内存需求，并行执行加速重建计算。

Result: 在真实和模拟 DNA 存储数据集上表现具有竞争力。在短读长 NGS 湿实验文库上实现了近乎完整的恢复，并在秒级内重建了超过一千万个读段；较小的公开基准在毫秒级完成。并行 ACOR 相较串行基线配置实现了数十倍到千倍以上的加速。

Conclusion: ACOR 专为流式读出管线设计，能在测序持续进行时处理已分配的读段，支持实时重建。

Abstract: DNA offers high-density, durable storage by encoding digital information into synthetic strands and retrieving it through sequencing. Sequence reconstruction combines noisy copies of each strand before downstream decoding. Conventional offline methods typically reconstruct completed read clusters, separating reconstruction from ongoing read collection. Streaming reconstruction offers a way to overlap evidence processing with sequencing and reduce the computation remaining before downstream decoding. We present ACOR, a streaming consensus reconstruction method that records $k$-mer transitions and boundary support in a persistent ledger, then decodes through length-constrained beam search and priority-based selection at cluster closure. Evaluation on real and simulated DNA-storage datasets showed competitive recovery. On our own short-read next-generation sequencing (NGS) wet-lab library, ACOR combined near-complete recovery with second-scale reconstruction of more than ten million reads. A smaller public benchmark was completed in a fraction of a second. Parallel ACOR achieved speedups of tens to over a thousand-fold relative to the tested serial baseline configurations. Sparse storage reduced high-context memory requirements, while parallel execution accelerated reconstruction computation. ACOR is designed for streaming readout pipelines that process assigned reads while sequencing continues.

</details>


### [212] [QESChunker: A Single Objective Unifies Overlapping and Non-Overlapping Chunking for RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36557)
*Yifan Zhao,Qianyang Zhao,Xinyu Guan,kai wei,Yuming Deng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出QESChunker，首个基于问答证据监督构建静态分块索引的方法，将分块重新定义为有监督的、预算感知的组合优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分块是RAG的基础预处理步骤，但现有分块器主要依赖启发式规则，缺乏监督信号指导，难以针对特定文档和检索任务进行优化。

Method: QESChunker利用人工标注或LLM生成的问题-证据对，通过统一的问题-证据效用最大化目标构建文档特定的分块索引。非重叠约束下目标可分解，动态规划可在多项式时间内精确求解；允许重叠时目标为单调子模块函数，NP-hard，贪心算法达到(1-1/√e)近似比。

Result: 在两个自建和三个公开数据集上，两种变体在相同重叠制度和固定混合检索器下，几乎所有评估设置中均以完全证据召回率超越现有分块方法。

Conclusion: 实验结果将分块重新定位为面向证据检索的有监督、预算感知的索引构建任务，为RAG系统提供了理论依据更强的分块范式。

Abstract: Chunking is a fundamental preprocessing step in retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), yet most existing chunkers rely primarily on heuristics. We introduce the Question--Evidence-Supervised Chunker (QESChunker), to our knowledge the first method for constructing static chunk indices from question--evidence supervision. QESChunker constructs document-specific chunk indices by optimizing over human-annotated or LLM-generated question--evidence pairs, without access to held-out test queries. We formulate non-overlapping and overlapping index construction through a unified question--evidence utility-maximization objective, turning chunking into a supervised combinatorial optimization problem. Under the non-overlap constraint, the objective decomposes over chunks and can be optimized exactly in polynomial time by dynamic programming. When overlap is allowed, the objective becomes monotone submodular; exact optimization is NP-hard, but our greedy achieves a $(1-1/\sqrt{e})$-approximation. Across two self-constructed and three public datasets, both variants outperform existing chunking methods in complete-evidence recall in nearly all evaluated settings, under the same overlap regime and a fixed hybrid retriever. These results recast chunking as supervised, budget-aware index construction for evidence retrieval.

</details>


### [213] [CCP-DP: Cross-cluster context-guided phase dynamic programming for DNA storage sequence reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36559)
*Zixin Zhang,Guanjin Qu,Huaming Wu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出CCP-DP（跨簇上下文引导的相位动态规划）方法，利用数据集级别的跨簇信息补充簇内局部证据，选择性地修正模糊的临时共识序列，在低测序覆盖度和强IDS噪声条件下显著提升DNA数据存储的序列重建准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNA数据存储具有高信息密度和长期稳定性优势，但在测序覆盖度有限和强插入、缺失、替换（IDS）噪声下，准确的序列重建仍具挑战性。现有重建方法主要依赖单个读段簇内的局部证据，当测序覆盖度低或由indel引起的相位偏移变得明显时，这种局部证据不足以区分紧密竞争的候选序列。

Method: 提出跨簇上下文引导的相位动态规划（CCP-DP），利用数据集级别的跨簇共享信息作为互补证据来源，补充簇内局部证据，并选择性地修正模糊的临时共识序列。该方法通过动态规划框架整合跨簇上下文信息来指导相位对齐和共识推断。

Result: 在主要基准设置下，CCP-DP实现了95.36%-99.98%的精确重建率，在多个数据集上优于现有方法。在5×覆盖度下，CCP-DP在测试的IDS条件下，相较于BBS和CPL中较强者，将精确重建率提升了6.2-17.5个百分点，同时保持了低重建误差和计算开销。

Conclusion: CCP-DP引入跨簇共享信息作为互补证据来源，为在具有挑战性条件下的DNA序列重建提供了有效解决方案，显著提升了低覆盖度和强噪声场景下的重建性能。

Abstract: Motivation: DNA data storage offers high information density and long-term stability, but accurate sequence reconstruction remains challenging under limited sequencing coverage and strong insertion, deletion, and substitution (IDS) noise. Existing reconstruction methods mainly rely on local evidence within individual read clusters. When sequencing coverage is low or indel-induced phase shifts become pronounced, such local evidence may be insufficient to distinguish closely competing candidate sequences.
  Results: We present Cross-Cluster Context-Guided Phase Dynamic Programming (CCP-DP), which uses dataset-level cross-cluster information to complement cluster-local evidence and selectively refines ambiguous provisional consensuses. Under the main benchmark settings, CCP-DP achieved exact reconstruction rates of 95.36%-99.98% and outperformed existing methods on multiple datasets. At $5\times$ coverage, CCP-DP improved exact reconstruction by 6.2-17.5 percentage points over the stronger of BBS and CPL across the tested IDS conditions, while maintaining low reconstruction error and computational overhead. Overall, CCP-DP introduces cross-cluster shared information as a complementary source of evidence for DNA sequence reconstruction under challenging conditions.
  Availability: Source code is available at https://github.com/ZixinZ3899/CCP-DP.

</details>


### [214] [The Vocabulary Is Already Here: Adapting Pretrained Interpretable Features for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36763)
*Ziluowen Luo,Ruochen Liu,Jun Yin,Hao Yan,Zhengxuan Hu,Xinze Shi,Chaozhuo Li,Chengqi Zhang,Senzhang Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出API框架，利用预训练稀疏自编码器的可解释特征索引作为可复用的语义ID词汇，通过语义预测适配器（SPA）联合预测特征索引和激活强度，实现无需额外词汇学习的有效且可解释的生成式推荐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐方法需将语义ID词汇拟合到预训练语言模型的物品描述表示上，带来额外训练成本且词汇覆盖依赖拟合领域。预训练稀疏自编码器暴露了带有人类可解释语义标注的PLM特征，特征索引可作为可复用的语义ID词汇，但复用该词汇面临在紧凑token预算下保持物品区分度、学习偏好目标物品而非共享主导特征的候选物品、将特征可解释性从物品内容扩展到检索决策等挑战。

Method: 提出API框架，包含预训练可解释ID（PIDs）和语义预测适配器（SPA）。PIDs保留选定的特征索引及相对激活强度用于物品区分。SPA以PIDs为监督，从交互历史中通过联合特征索引和强度预测学习用户偏好。SPA结合两者预测对候选物品评分，物品级监督鼓励观测的下一物品超越替代项。API通过特征贡献及其预训练语义标注解释候选得分差异。

Result: 大量实验表明，API在不同骨干网络和解码方案下均达到竞争性性能，同时在紧凑token预算下保持物品区分度，并实现可解释检索。

Conclusion: API通过复用预训练稀疏自编码器的可解释特征索引作为语义ID词汇，避免了额外词汇学习，结合联合特征索引与强度预测的适配器，实现了有效且可解释的生成式推荐，在保持物品区分度和可解释性方面表现优异。

Abstract: Generative recommendation (GR) predicts the next item through its semantic ID (SID). Existing methods commonly fit SID vocabularies to pretrained language model (PLM) representations of item descriptions, incurring additional training costs and making vocabulary coverage dependent on the fitting domains. We observe that pretrained sparse autoencoders expose PLM features with human-interpretable semantic annotations, allowing feature indices to serve as a reusable SID vocabulary. However, reusing this vocabulary requires preserving item distinctions under compact token budgets and learning to favor target items over candidates with shared dominant features. It also requires extending feature interpretability from item content to retrieval decisions. To address these challenges, we propose API, a framework that Adapts the Pretrained Interpretable vocabulary for effective and explainable GR without additional vocabulary learning. Within API, Pretrained Interpretable IDs (PIDs) retain selected feature indices and relative activation strengths for item discrimination. With PIDs as supervision, the Semantic Prediction Adapter (SPA) learns user preferences from interaction histories through joint feature-index and strength prediction. SPA combines both predictions to score candidate items, with item-level supervision encouraging the observed next item to outrank its alternatives. API further explains candidate score differences through feature contributions paired with their pretrained semantic annotations. Extensive experiments show that API achieves competitive performance across backbones and decoding schemes, while preserving item distinctions under compact token budgets and enabling explainable retrieval.

</details>


### [215] [DuTri: Dual-Branch Modeling with Triadic Factorization for Multimodal Multi-Scenario Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36836)
*Jintao Huang,Chaohua Yang,Kailiang Hao,Dugang Liu,Xing Tang,Zhong Ming*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 DuTri 框架，通过双分支建模（协作分支与多模态分支）和三元分解（CP分解+残差量化）解决多模态多场景推荐中协作信息与多模态信息建模需求不匹配的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多场景推荐（MSR）方法在引入多模态信息时，通常将文本和视觉表示作为通用特征直接输入原有参数共享架构，但这种扩展未能充分适应协作信息与多模态信息的不同建模需求，控制实验表明直接采用这些架构并非最优建模范式。

Method: DuTri 包含协作分支和多模态分支：协作分支利用 CANDECOMP/PARAFAC (CP) 分解用户-场景-物品交互并结合残差量化，学习结构化协作偏好表示，捕捉跨场景共性与场景特性；多模态分支利用学习到的用户和场景偏好指导自适应模态融合，并通过物品级对比学习对齐协作物品偏好表示与多模态表示，促进双分支有效协作。

Result: 在 AntM$^2$C 和 Amazon 两个真实数据集上的大量实验表明，DuTri 在整体推荐性能上优于现有多模态和多场景推荐基线。

Conclusion: 实验结果验证了 DuTri 框架的有效性，证明双分支建模结合三元分解能更好地建模跨场景用户偏好与多模态语义。

Abstract: As recommendation scenarios become increasingly diverse and multimodal information grows more prevalent, effectively modeling cross-scenario user preferences and multimodal semantics has become a critical challenge in recommender systems. When incorporating multimodal information, existing multi-scenario recommendation (MSR) methods typically treat textual and visual representations as generic input features and directly feed them into their original parameter-sharing architectures. However, this extension may not fully accommodate the different modeling requirements of collaborative and multimodal information and may not be the best modeling paradigm. Our controlled experiments show that directly adopting these architectures is not the optimal modeling approach. To this end, we propose Dual-Branch Modeling with Triadic Factorization (DuTri), a framework for multimodal multi-scenario recommendation comprising a collaborative branch and a multimodal branch. Specifically, the collaborative branch learns structured collaborative preference representations through CANDECOMP/PARAFAC (CP) decomposition of user-scenario-item interactions and residual quantization, thereby capturing cross-scenario commonalities and scenario-specific differences. The multimodal branch leverages the learned user and scenario preferences to guide adaptive modality fusion; additionally, item-level contrastive learning aligns collaborative item preference representations with multimodal representations to facilitate effective collaboration between the two branches. Extensive experiments on two real-world datasets, AntM$^2$C and Amazon, demonstrate that DuTri outperforms existing multimodal and multi-scenario recommendation baselines in overall recommendation performance, validating the effectiveness of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [216] [Latent Residual Reasoning for Ranking in Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36890)
*Haihan Shi,Ruidong Han,Wenjie Wang,Shangyu Chen,Sunhao Dai,Yongqi Li,Yangyang Li,Fei Jiang,Wei Lin,Fuli Feng,Xiangnan He*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Refiner，一种带有参数高效微调的潜在残差推理框架，用于生成式排序。通过残差推理机制、历史查询和LoRA微调，逐步细化排序分数，在公开基准、工业离线数据集和在线A/B测试中均显著优于直接预测基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐模型在排序任务中通常采用快速思考的直接预测范式，但排序需要对多个候选物品进行细粒度评分，现有模型主要依赖单次预测。受大语言模型Chain-of-Thought启发，近期工作尝试引入推理，但基于LLM的显式文本推理不适用于广泛部署的非文本ID型推荐模型。潜在推理虽已扩展到工业排序，但连续推理步骤如何改进细粒度候选评分仍缺乏探索。

Method: Refiner框架包含三个关键组件：(1) 残差推理机制，通过累积加性修正逐步细化排序分数；(2) 历史查询，提供浓缩的用户行为表示以增强针对候选物品的推理；(3) 参数高效微调策略，利用LoRA模块将预训练模型适配至潜在推理任务。

Result: 在两个公开基准、一个工业离线数据集和在线A/B测试上的大量实验表明，Refiner持续优于直接预测方法，验证了其有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: Refiner成功将潜在残差推理引入生成式排序，通过参数高效微调实现了排序质量的提升，并在工业场景中展示了实际应用价值。

Abstract: Generative recommendation (GR) models for ranking typically adopt a fast-thinking direct prediction paradigm with strong practical efficiency. However, ranking requires fine-grained scoring across multiple candidate items, while existing GR models still mainly rely on one-shot prediction. This raises the question whether additional inference-time computation can further improve ranking quality. Recently, inspired by Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning in large language models (LLMs), some work has begun to introduce reasoning into recommendation systems. Some of these methods rely on LLM reasoning frameworks to perform explicit reasoning in textual space, but such designs are not suitable for more widely deployed non-textual recommendation models. A more relevant direction is to introduce latent reasoning into ID-based recommendation models. Although recent work has extended latent reasoning to industrial ranking, how successive reasoning steps should improve fine-grained candidate scores remains less explored. To address this problem, we propose Refiner, a latent residual reasoning framework with parameter-efficient fine-tuning for generative ranking. Our approach introduces three key components: (1) a residual reasoning mechanism that progressively refines ranking scores through cumulative additive corrections, (2) history queries that provide condensed user behavior representations to enhance candidate-specific reasoning, and (3) a parameter-efficient fine-tuning strategy that leverages LoRA modules to adapt pre-trained models for latent reasoning. Extensive experiments on two public benchmarks, an offline industrial dataset, and an online A/B test demonstrate that Refiner consistently improves over direct prediction, validating its effectiveness and practical utility.

</details>


### [217] [When Diffusion Recommenders Stop Sampling: Preserving Generative Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36954)
*Louis Castres Saint Martin,Alain Rakotomamonjy,Patrick Gallinari*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出 DARTS，一种三阶段潜在扩散框架，解决序列推荐中扩散模型的采样坍缩问题，通过解耦表示学习、生成建模和离散排序，显著提升采样多样性并超越基线 7-12%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统序列推荐器输出单一确定性排序，而用户历史可能对应多个合理的下一交互。扩散模型虽能采样多样化延续，但现有目标物品扩散推荐器常遭受严重采样坍缩，导致独立抽样产生近乎相同的输出。

Method: DARTS 采用三阶段潜在扩散框架：1) 预先学习并冻结潜在空间，解耦表示学习；2) 在冻结潜在空间上训练去噪器，进行生成建模；3) 将排序监督推迟到最终解码阶段，避免轨迹内离散损失导致的坍缩。同时引入采样多样性的互补度量（解码前后的变异）及后验预测聚合策略利用推理时的多样性。

Result: 在 Amazon Reviews 2023 三个类别上，DARTS 保持显著更大的采样多样性，且在标准排序指标上比强序列、生成检索和扩散基线高出 7-12%。聚合多个样本可在固定候选集大小下进一步提升排序准确性。

Conclusion: DARTS 通过显式解耦表示学习、生成建模和离散排序，有效缓解了扩散推荐器的采样坍缩，实现了多样性与准确性的双重提升，为序列推荐中的多样化生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: A user history can admit multiple plausible next interactions, yet conventional sequential recommenders produce a single deterministic ranking. While diffusion models offer a natural way to sample alternative continuations, we show that existing target-item diffusion recommenders often suffer from severe sampling collapse, producing nearly identical outputs across independent draws. We identify two ubiquitous design choices that promote this collapse: joint learning of the latent space and denoiser, and in-trajectory supervision via discrete losses. To address these failure modes, we introduce DARTS (Diverse and Accurate Recommendation Through Sampling), a three-stage latent diffusion framework that explicitly decouples representation learning, generative modeling, and discrete ranking by learning and freezing the latent space before diffusion training and postponing ranking supervision to the final decoding stage. We further characterize sampling diversity using complementary measures of variation before and after decoding, and use posterior-predictive aggregation to exploit this diversity at inference. Across three Amazon Reviews 2023 categories, DARTS preserves substantially greater sampling diversity while outperforming strong sequential, generative-retrieval, and diffusion baselines by 7-12% on standard ranking metrics. Aggregating multiple samples further improves ranking accuracy at a fixed slate size.

</details>


### [218] [PailitaoMMSearch2.0 Technical Report: Scaling Generative Retrieval to Unified End-to-End Multimodal Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36958)
*Xiaohan Ye,Zihan Gong,Jingqian Zhao,Lianyu Du,Yunmeng Shu,Xu Chen,Zesheng Zhou,Renchun You,Bocheng Chen,Jinsong Lan,Haihong Tang,Bo Zheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 PailitaoMMSearch，一个统一的端到端多模态搜索系统，直接从原始查询图像和自由指令生成商品语义 ID，通过数据、意图、场景三维扩展实现开放式图文查询、异构购物意图及不同资源约束下的检索。在淘宝拍立淘部署的在线 A/B 测试中，商品页面浏览 +17.90%、交易量 +27.04%、GMV +8.94%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工业界生成式检索模型通常绑定固定在线场景，依赖场景特定交互数据，仅支持狭窄的查询形式和检索目标。需要解耦这些限制，构建统一系统以支持开放式图文查询、异构购物意图以及不同资源约束下的在线场景。

Method: PailitaoMMSearch 采用三维扩展策略：1) 数据扩展：可复用工具集组合成意图和生命周期特定工作流，支持冷启动合成、质量控制、实时流量细化和持续数据生产。2) 意图扩展：通过持续预训练 (CPT) 注入电商商品知识，混合预算监督微调 (SFT) 学习多种检索关系，统一强化学习 (RL) 结合意图平衡优化和 Sample-Beam rollout 以适配多候选服务，实现可调推理预算。3) 场景扩展：在模型容量和推理预算维度暴露质量-成本操作点。

Result: 在淘宝拍立淘多模态搜索应用中部署，受控在线 A/B 测试显示统计学显著提升：商品页面浏览量 +17.90%，交易量 +27.04%，商品交易总额 (GMV) +8.94%。

Conclusion: PailitaoMMSearch 通过数据、意图、场景三维协同扩展，成功构建了统一的端到端多模态生成式检索系统，有效解决了工业界生成式检索模型场景绑定、查询形式受限、资源约束僵化等问题，在实际电商搜索业务中取得了显著的业务指标提升，验证了统一多模态生成式检索范式的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Generative retrieval models have been increasingly adopted in search and recommendation systems. However, industrial generative retrieval models are commonly specialized to a fixed online scenario: they learn from scenario-specific interactions, support a narrow set of query forms and retrieval objectives. We study how to remove these couplings and build a unified end-to-end multimodal search system for open-ended image--text queries, heterogeneous shopping intents, and online scenarios with different resource constraints. We present \textbf{PailitaoMMSearch}, which directly generates product Semantic IDs from a raw query image and free-form instruction and scales along three dimensions. \textbf{Data scaling} uses a reusable toolset that can be composed into intent- and lifecycle-specific workflows for cold-start synthesis, quality control, real-traffic refinement, and continuous data production. \textbf{Intent scaling} progressively equips one model with e-commerce product knowledge, multiple retrieval relations, and adjustable reasoning budgets through continual pre-training (CPT), mixed-budget supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and unified reinforcement learning (RL). The RL stage combines intent-balanced optimization and Sample--Beam rollouts aligned with multi-candidate serving. \textbf{Scenario scaling} exposes quality--cost operating points across model capacity and reasoning budget. The system is deployed in multimodal search applications on Taobao Pailitao. A controlled online A/B test reports statistically significant improvements, including item page views $\textbf{+17.90\%}$, transactions $\textbf{+27.04\%}$, and gross merchandise value $\textbf{+8.94\%}$.

</details>


### [219] [KL-MOPD: Discrepancy-Aware Multi-Objective On-Policy Distillation for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36973)
*Weiqin Yang,Yandong Wang,Yuan Gao,Hao Deng,Jinxin Hu,Jing Cao,Jiawei Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出KL-MOPD（KL加权多目标在线策略蒸馏）解决生成式推荐中多目标知识蒸馏的token级不平衡与跨token不一致问题，通过按初始student-teacher token-wise KL散度加权教师，理论证明优于vanilla MOPD，实验在公开基准、工业数据集及线上A/B测试中显著提升单目标与联合推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐需协调点击、转化等多目标，但传统item-wise方法缺乏token-wise监督；引入多目标在线策略蒸馏（MOPD）将单目标教师蒸馏到学生，却发现vanilla MOPD存在教师在各token蒸馏难度不均（token级不平衡）及较难蒸馏教师随token变化（跨token不一致），导致各目标在某些解码步骤学习不足，阻碍正样本检索。

Method: KL-MOPD：根据初始student-teacher token-wise KL散度分配token级教师权重，动态加强对较难蒸馏教师的监督；即插即用，仅需一次预训练权重校准，计算开销极小。

Result: 在三个公开基准、十亿级工业数据集及电商广告平台线上A/B测试中，KL-MOPD显著提升单目标（点击、转化）及联合推荐指标。

Conclusion: KL-MOPD是一种简单有效的多目标蒸馏方法，理论保证在温和条件下严格优于vanilla MOPD，实验验证其在生成式推荐多目标协调中的优越性，适合大规模工业部署。

Abstract: Coordinating multiple recommendation objectives (e.g., click and conversion) is critical for real-world generative recommendation (GR). However, since a positive item in GR can be retrieved only when its tokens are successively selected at every decoding step, traditional item-wise methods lack fine-grained token-wise supervision to guide intermediate steps. Motivated by its success in foundation models, we consider introducing multi-objective on-policy distillation (MOPD) into GR to distill two single-objective teachers into a student at each token. Yet, we find that in vanilla MOPD, (i) teachers exhibit unequal distillation difficulty at each token (i.e., token-level imbalance), and (ii) the harder-to-distill teacher varies across tokens (i.e., cross-token inconsistency), leaving each objective poorly learned at certain steps and hindering positive item retrieval for both objectives, leading to suboptimal performance.
  To address this challenge, we propose KL-MOPD (KL-Weighted Multi-Objective On-Policy Distillation), a simple yet effective distillation method that allocates token-wise teacher weights proportional to the initial student-teacher token-wise KL divergences, thereby strengthening supervision for the harder-to-distill teacher at every decoding step. Despite its simplicity, we theoretically prove that KL-MOPD strictly outperforms vanilla MOPD in the additive joint recommendation objective under mild conditions. Moreover, KL-MOPD is plug-and-play and computationally efficient, requiring only a one-time pre-training weight calibration with negligible training overhead. Extensive experiments on three public benchmarks, a billion-scale industrial dataset, and online A/B tests on our e-commerce advertising platform demonstrate that KL-MOPD significantly improves both individual and joint recommendation performance. The anonymous code is provided for peer review.

</details>


### [220] [Tessera: Structure-Completed Evidence Units for Web Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37080)
*Peichun Hua,He Sun,Zirui Zhuang,Qi Qi,Yiming Qiu,Shinan Liu,Yunming Xiao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文指出现有 Web RAG 管线（文本分块或截图切片）将索引、检索、阅读单元耦合，导致结构化证据（表格、信息框）丢失。提出免训练的证据分发层 Tessera：从源 HTML 重建细粒度证据单元（含标题、章节路径、表头），按查询筛选并在不增加 token 预算下送入阅读器，解耦检索与阅读，适用于文本、截图及智能体管线。实验显示在三个问答基准上准确率提升 5-6 个百分点，且优于强基线，增益迁移至多步智能体搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG 管线将固定大小的文本块或截图瓦片同时作为索引、排序和阅读单元，这种耦合导致源页面中的证据（尤其是表格、信息框等结构化内容）在分块过程中与表头、上下文分离，无法完整到达阅读器。更强的抽取、上下文扩展和更大的阅读预算均无法根本解决该损失。

Method: Tessera 是一个免训练的证据分发层：给定检索器返回的页面列表，它将原始 HTML 重建为细粒度证据单元，每个单元补全页面标题、章节路径和表格表头；随后根据查询选择相关单元，在保持 token 预算不变的前提下打包送入阅读器。索引器、检索器和阅读器保持不变，可无缝接入文本、截图及智能体管线。

Result: 在三个问答基准上，Tessera 使文本和截图管线的答案准确率提升 5-6 个百分点，增益在不同阅读器、检索器和阅读预算下一致；在相同预算下优于强 HTML 感知和结构感知 RAG 基线；性能提升延伸至多步智能体搜索场景。

Conclusion: 通过解耦检索单元与阅读单元，并利用 HTML 结构信息重建语义完整的证据单元，Tessera 在不修改现有管线核心组件的前提下显著提升了 Web RAG 的证据利用率和答案准确性，展示了结构感知证据分发的普适价值。

Abstract: Web retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) splits a page into fixed-size text chunks or, in recent visual pipelines, screenshot tiles, and the retrieved item serves at once as the unit of indexing, ranking, and reading. We trace answer evidence from source HTML to reader input over the full English Wikipedia and find that this coupling loses evidence in text and screenshot pipelines alike: evidence present in the source page often fails to reach the reader intact, particularly structured content such as tables and infoboxes, which chunking separates from the headers and context that give it meaning. Stronger extraction, context expansion, and larger reader budgets leave the loss in place. We present Tessera, a training-free evidence delivery layer that decouples what a pipeline retrieves from what its reader reads. Given the pages behind any ranked list, Tessera rebuilds their HTML into fine-grained evidence units, each completed with its page title, section path, and table headers, selects the units relevant to the query, and packs them into the reader context at an unchanged token budget. The index, the retriever, and the reader stay as they are, so Tessera applies to text, screenshot, and agentic pipelines. Across three question answering benchmarks, Tessera improves answer accuracy by 5 to 6 points on text and screenshot pipelines, consistently across readers, retrievers, and reader budgets, and outperforms strong HTML-aware and structure-aware RAG baselines under matched budgets. The gains carry over to multi-step agentic search. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/sarendis56/Tessera.

</details>


### [221] [Rethinking Positional Encoding and Interaction Granularity for CTR Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37140)
*Xinxun Zhang,Yuxiang Wang,Xiaokai Zhou,Jiayue Li,Yi Li,Yue Liu,Jun Zhang,Xiaoling Zhou,Tieyun Qian,Xiao Yan,Jiawei Jiang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 TRIP 模型，结合目标感知的秩-时间位置编码与时序分组交互建模，解决 CTR 预测中位置编码无效及交互粒度不当的问题，在四个公开基准上 AUC 最高提升 4.45%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Transformer 基 CTR 模型采用索引或时间位置编码及 token 级因果交互，但实验发现多数位置编码不优于无编码，且在目标相对时序组内放宽因果约束反而有增益，揭示了当前位置编码与交互粒度与 CTR 任务不匹配，亟需明确 CTR 真正需要的位置表示与交互粒度。

Method: TRIP 包含两大核心组件：1) 目标感知秩-时间位置编码：因子化秩编码用低秩分解表示行为在历史序列中的序数位置；目标相对时间编码按粗到细尺度刻画行为到目标项的时间距离，联合建模序列上下文与目标时序关系。2) 时序分组因果交互：组内双向交互与掩码重构增强组内表示，组间保持因果顺序；最后将行为压缩为紧凑组摘要，供后续层进行高效组级建模。

Result: 在四个公开 CTR 基准数据集上，TRIP 相对最强基线 AUC 最高提升 4.45%，显著优于现有 Transformer 及非 Transformer 方法，验证了所提位置编码与分组交互设计的有效性。

Conclusion: CTR 预测不需要传统的绝对位置编码，而需目标相对的秩-时间联合编码；交互粒度应在时序组内双向、组间因果，并以组摘要形式高效传播。TRIP 为 CTR 预测提供了更符合任务本质的位置表示与交互范式。

Abstract: Click-through rate (CTR) prediction relies on historical behaviors and contextual features to estimate clicks on a target item. Existing Transformer-based CTR models often adopt index-based or temporal positional encodings and token-level causal interaction. However, we observe that most existing positional encodings do not outperform NoPE, while relaxing causal constraints within target-relative temporal groups also yields gains. These observations motivate a fundamental question: what positional encoding and interaction granularity does CTR prediction actually require? To address this question, we propose \textbf{TRIP}, which combines target-aware rank--time positional encoding with temporal-group interaction modeling. Specifically, Factorized Rank Encoding represents each behavior's ordinal position within the historical sequence through a low-rank factorization, while Target-Relative Time Encoding characterizes its temporal distance to the target on a coarse-to-fine scale. This joint design grounds each behavior's positional representation in its sequence context and temporal relation to the current target. Furthermore, Temporal Group-Causal Interaction enriches intra-group representations through bidirectional interaction and masked reconstruction while preserving inter-group causal order. Finally, behavior representations are condensed into compact group summaries for efficient group-level modeling in subsequent layers. Experiments on four public benchmarks show that TRIP improves AUC by up to 4.45\% relative to the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [222] [Coverage Under Contradiction: A Benchmark for Negation-Aware Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37162)
*Pasha Abrishamchian,Eugene Yang,Andrew Yates,Nazli Goharian,Ophir Frieder*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 CoverageBench-Negate 基准，通过在覆盖率评估基准中引入受控矛盾，研究检索器在参数知识与语料库证据冲突时的行为。实验显示 BM25 优于密集检索器（Qwen3、Jina）区分原始与编辑后的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有覆盖率评估基准多基于已知事实，无法测试检索器在语料库证据与预训练知识冲突时的表现，亟需研究检索器如何处理此类矛盾。

Method: 基于 CoverageBench，为每个信息块创建否定版本并编辑支持文档以支持否定内容，保留上下文不变，构建 CoverageBench-Negate，包含六个数据集约 63,000 对原始-编辑证据对，附带构建元数据、LLM 判断和人工审计。

Result: 基线实验中，BM25 能显著区分原始与编辑后的近孪生证据，而密集检索器（Qwen3、Jina）表现接近随机猜测。

Conclusion: 检索器在面对参数知识与语料库冲突时，稀疏检索（BM25）比密集检索更鲁棒；发布的基准可推动后续研究。

Abstract: Coverage-evaluation benchmarks quantify whether retrieved evidence covers the different pieces of information needed to satisfy an information need. Existing benchmarks mostly consider factual claims that may already be known to pretrained retrieval components, leaving open whether retrievers find corpus evidence when it contradicts those priors. We study how retrievers behave when their parametric knowledge and the information in a corpus disagree. To do so, we develop a process to augment a nugget-based coverage benchmark with controlled contradictions. For each nugget, we create a negated counterpart and edit its supporting documents to support that negation while preserving the context. Applying this process to CoverageBench, a benchmark measuring information coverage, we create CoverageBench-Negate, spanning six datasets and roughly 63,000 original-edited evidence pairs (one per nugget-document link). We attach rich construction metadata to every pair and additionally provide out-of-family LLM judgments over all pairs and a human audit of a sample. In baseline experiments, BM25 separates original from edited near-twin evidence substantially better than dense retrievers (Qwen3 and Jina), which stay near chance. We release the benchmark on Hugging Face Datasets (nuggets, documents, pairs) together with the construction materials, including the prompts used to create it.

</details>


### [223] [Lossless Compression of Proximity Graph Indexes for Efficient Approximate Nearest Neighbors Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37176)
*Francesco Benocci,Leonard Dahlmann,Francesco Gargiulo,Tobias Heuer,Silvio Martinico,Franco Maria Nardini,Cosimo Rulli,Rossano Venturini*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种针对HNSW实时路由的压缩技术，通过离线图排列诱导结构局部性，启用SIMD加速的整数压缩器（如Stream VByte）实现快速即时解压。在多样化真实工作负载评估中，压缩率高达52.7%，延迟损失可忽略；已在eBay生产环境部署，边存储减少44.3%且查询延迟无下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于图的ANN索引（如HNSW）是现代向量搜索的核心，但巨大的内存占用造成严重基础设施瓶颈。向量量化虽减少数据向量存储，但图的边结构逐渐主导总内存使用。在线搜索时压缩邻接表面临关键挑战：解压发生在查询处理期间（即图导航时），内存节省不能以查询延迟为代价。

Method: 应用离线图排列人为诱导结构局部性，从而启用SIMD加速的整数压缩器（如Stream VByte）进行快速即时解压，专门针对实时HNSW路由设计。

Result: 在多样化真实工作负载评估中，显著超越现有最佳方法的压缩率和查询延迟。实现高达52.7%的压缩率，相比未压缩基线延迟惩罚可忽略。已在eBay生产环境有效部署，边存储减少高达44.3%，查询延迟无退化。

Conclusion: 提出的压缩技术通过离线排列诱导的结构局部性和SIMD加速压缩，有效解决了HNSW图边的内存瓶颈，在不牺牲查询性能前提下实现大幅内存节省，经基准测试和eBay生产部署双重验证。

Abstract: Graph-based Approximate Nearest Neighbor (ANN) indexes, such as HNSW, are the backbone of modern vector search. However, their massive memory footprint creates a severe infrastructure bottleneck. While vector quantization is widely adopted to reduce the memory required to store data vectors, the graph's edge structure increasingly dominates total memory usage. Compressing these adjacency lists for online search presents a critical system challenge: because decompression occurs during query processing, i.e., while navigating the graph, memory savings cannot come at the expense of query latency. In this paper, we present a compression technique explicitly tailored for live HNSW routing. By applying offline graph permutations to artificially induce structural locality, we enable the use of SIMD-accelerated integer compressors, such as Stream VByte, for rapid on-the-fly decompression. Evaluated across diverse real-world workloads, we significantly outperform the current state of the art in both compression ratio and query latency. Our experimental results show that we achieve compression ratios up to 52.7% with negligible penalties in latency compared to the uncompressed baseline. Furthermore, our technique has been effectively deployed in production at eBay, achieving up to 44.3% reduction in edge storage with no degradation in query latency.

</details>


### [224] [AutoRSE: Evaluating LLM Agents as Recommendation Engineers for Self-Evolving Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37228)
*Rui Tian,Yi Fang,Yejin Hua,Du Wang,Yuzhe Wang,Minxuan Shan,Zirui Ren,Xinyu Lin,Chongming Gao,Wenjie Wang,Fuli Feng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出 AutoRSE 评估框架，对三种前沿 LLM 在三种 Agent 框架上跨六个工业级推荐任务进行大规模实证研究。结果显示 RSE-Agents 能在五个任务上平均提升 16.1% 性能，但轨迹分析揭示其主要充当工程优化器（超参数调优、训练策略、模型集成），而非算法创新者。注入创新想法实验表明，想法生成是当前 RSE-Agents 的核心瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Recommender Systems Engineer Agents (RSE-Agents) 作为利用 LLM 自主改进推荐算法的新范式正在兴起，但因缺乏统一标准的评估框架和系统性的轨迹级分析，其能力与局限尚不清晰。

Method: 构建 AutoRSE 评估框架，涵盖六个代表性工业级推荐任务；选取三个前沿 LLM 与三个主流 Agent 框架组合，开展大规模实证研究；进行轨迹级分析以区分工程优化与算法创新；设计想法注入实验，对比自发生成想法与来自前沿研究/人类专家的创新想法的效果。

Result: 1) RSE-Agents 在六个任务中五个显著提升骨干模型性能，平均相对提升 16.1%。2) 轨迹分析显示增益主要源于超参数调优、训练策略调整和模型集成等工程优化，真正的算法创新极少。3) 注入前沿研究或专家创新想法的 Agent 取得的改进远大于依赖自发生成想法的 Agent，证实想法生成是核心瓶颈。

Conclusion: 当前 RSE-Agents 已能自主提升推荐性能，但本质上是工程优化器而非算法创新者；想法生成能力不足是其根本局限，未来研究应聚焦于增强 Agent 的算法创新与想法生成能力。

Abstract: Recommender Systems Engineer Agents (RSE-Agents), which use LLM-based agents to autonomously improve recommendation algorithms, are emerging as a promising paradigm. However, their capabilities and limitations remain poorly understood due to the lack of a unified, standardized evaluation framework and systematic trajectory-level analysis. To address this gap, we introduce AutoRSE, an evaluation framework spanning six representative industrial-grade recommendation tasks, and conduct a large-scale empirical study of three frontier LLMs across three widely used agent harnesses. Our results show that RSE-Agents consistently enhance the performance of backbones on five of the six tasks, with an average relative improvement of 16.1%, indicating that they can already autonomously improve recommendation performance. However, trajectory-level analysis also reveals that current RSE-Agents function primarily as engineering optimizers rather than algorithmic improvers: their gains are largely driven by hyperparameter tuning, training strategies, and model ensembling, while genuine algorithmic innovations are rare. Since the largest gains in recommender systems often stem from algorithmic advances, this behavior underscores a fundamental limitation of current RSE-Agents. To probe this limitation, we perform idea-injection experiments and show that agents equipped with innovative ideas from frontier research or human experts achieve substantially larger improvements than those relying on self-generated ideas. These results suggest that idea generation is a central bottleneck of current RSE-Agents, highlights a key direction for the future recommendation system research. Our implementation is publicly available on GitHub: https://github.com/Ray-Tiann/AutoRSE.

</details>


### [225] [TaskBridge: Technical Report for SIGIR 2026 eCommerce Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37231)
*Zhiheng Fu,Yupeng Hu,Zixu Li,Zhiwei Chen,Qinlei Huang,Shaokun Wang,Han Liu,Fan Liu,Weili Guan,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TaskBridge 在 SIGIR 2026 eCommerce Challenge 查询重写任务中获得第一名。该框架通过目录语义锚定、统一五候选重写和协议感知选择，有效弥合任务导向查询与商品目录描述之间的词汇鸿沟，并在自动与交互两种协议下均显著提升检索效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务导向查询常通过活动级语义隐含商品需求，与目录描述存在词汇鸿沟。系统需在保持任务意图的前提下，根据自动协议（单查询）和交互协议（多查询）自适应生成重写。

Method: 提出 TaskBridge 框架，包含三大模块：1) 目录语义锚定，将查询映射到目录词汇空间；2) 统一五候选重写，生成五个互补的重写候选；3) 协议感知选择，利用 BM25 分数、查询-商品重排序和加权 Jaccard 重叠度，为自动协议选择 1 个查询，为交互协议选择最多 4 个查询。选定查询独立经 BM25 检索并输出标准六列格式结果。

Result: 实验表明 TaskBridge 显著提升检索效果；交互协议下多互补查询带来额外增益；最终在 SIGIR 2026 eCommerce Challenge 中夺得冠军。

Conclusion: TaskBridge 通过目录锚定和协议感知的多候选选择策略，成功解决电商场景下任务查询与商品目录的语义鸿沟问题，证明了多查询互补在交互式检索中的优势。

Abstract: The SIGIR 2026 eCommerce Challenge Query Reformulation Task evaluates the reformulation of task-oriented queries into expressions suitable for product retrieval, followed by BM25-based retrieval. These queries often imply product needs through activity-level semantics, creating a lexical gap with catalog descriptions. Systems must preserve task intent while adapting the number of reformulations to the Automatic and Interactive protocols. We propose TaskBridge, a catalog-anchored framework comprising Catalog Semantic Anchoring, Unified Five-Candidate Rewriting, and Protocol-Aware Selection. It grounds queries in catalog vocabulary, generates five complementary reformulations, and selects one query for Automatic retrieval or up to four for Interactive retrieval using BM25 scores, query-product reranking, and weighted Jaccard overlap. The selected queries are independently executed using BM25 to produce results in the required six-column format. Experiments demonstrate improved retrieval effectiveness, with multiple complementary queries providing additional gains in the Interactive setting. TaskBridge achieved first place in the SIGIR 2026 eCommerce Challenge.

</details>


### [226] [Modeling Preference Dependencies with Directed Acyclic Graphs for Narrative-Driven Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37280)
*Mingzhe Lu,Jian Qin,Ruize Qin,Jinhao Liu,Yunpeng Li,Hang Su*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出基于偏好依赖图的叙事驱动推荐框架 PDG-F，通过将用户自由文本解析为有向无环偏好依赖图（PDG），利用有向路径检索获取候选证据，并以拓扑路径排序生成推荐，显著优于现有基线、开放域检索代理及前沿大语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有叙事驱动推荐方法将用户叙事视为扁平关键词组合或无约束生成查询，无法捕捉复杂偏好间的层级细化与语境制约关系，导致推荐质量受限。

Method: PDG-F 包含 PDG-Agent：先将非结构化请求解析为有向无环偏好依赖图（PDG），显式建模偏好间的相互限定与层级依赖；再基于图结构执行有向路径检索从开放网络获取候选证据；最后通过拓扑路径排序确定最终推荐。

Result: 在跨越英、中语境的三个多样化领域实验中，PDG-F 持续超越竞争基线、开放域检索代理及前沿大语言模型；诊断分析表明依赖引导检索显著提升了候选池上限，验证了建模偏好依赖的根本价值。

Conclusion: 显式建模用户偏好间的依赖关系并以图结构指导检索与排序，能从根本上提升叙事驱动推荐的性能上限，为复杂偏好理解提供了可泛化的框架。

Abstract: Narrative-driven recommendation allows users to express multifaceted needs through free-form text where stated preferences naturally qualify and condition one another. Existing methods typically treat narratives as flat keyword combinations or unconstrained generative queries, failing to capture how complex preferences hierarchically refine and contextualize each other. We propose the Preference Dependency Graph Framework (PDG-F), a narrative-driven recommendation framework centered on explicit preference dependencies. Specifically, PDG-A (PDG-Agent) first parses unstructured requests to organize user preferences and their mutual qualifications into a directed acyclic Preference Dependency Graph (PDG). Through this graph, candidate evidence from the open Web is acquired via directed path retrieval, and final recommendations are determined by topological path ranking. Comprehensive experiments across three diverse domains spanning English and Chinese contexts confirm that PDG-F consistently achieves superior recommendation performance over competitive baselines, open-domain retrieval agents, and frontier language models. Further diagnostic analyses show that our dependency-guided retrieval markedly raises the attainable candidate-pool ceiling, demonstrating the fundamental value of modeling preference dependencies in narrative-driven recommendation.

</details>


### [227] [LESSRec: Level-Specific History Modeling for Semantic ID-Based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37286)
*Shangxin Yang,Zongwei Wang,Jing Long,Junliang Yu,Min Gao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: LESSRec针对语义ID推荐中自回归预测需attend所有语义层历史token的问题，提出分级编码历史token并限制注意力至目标token语义层，引入item级语义序列补全上下文，解码器双分支融合；LESSRec+进一步采用分离编码器降低编码计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义ID推荐范式自回归生成下一item的token时需attend所有语义层级的历史token，但推理时分析发现目标token同语义层级的历史token最具信息量，其他层级反而引入干扰。

Method: LESSRec分别编码各语义层级的历史token序列，预测时仅对目标token所在层级的序列进行注意力计算；显式构建item级语义序列按时间顺序表示每个历史item整体语义；解码器通过两个独立cross-attention分支分别访问层级对齐token序列与item级语义序列并融合上下文。LESSRec+为不同语义层级历史配备独立编码器且总参数量不变，降低编码计算开销。

Result: 在三个公开数据集、三种tokenizer上的大量实验验证了LESSRec与LESSRec+的推荐效果优势，效率分析显示LESSRec+带来显著计算收益。

Conclusion: 通过层级解耦注意力机制消除跨层干扰、显式item级语义序列补全整体语义、分离编码器提升编码效率，LESSRec系列为语义ID推荐提供了更有效且高效的新范式。

Abstract: Semantic ID-based recommendation represents each item as a sequence of semantic tokens obtained through residual quantization. This paradigm generates the next item's tokens autoregressively from coarse to fine, entailing each prediction attending to historical tokens at all semantic levels. However, our inference-time analysis reveals that historical tokens at the target-token's semantic level tend to be more informative, whereas other levels can introduce interference. Motivated by these findings, we propose LESSRec that separately encodes the historical token sequence at each semantic level and restricts attention over historical tokens to the sequence at the target token's semantic level during prediction. To complement these level-specific histories, LESSRec explicitly provides an item-level semantic sequence that represents each historical item's overall semantics in chronological order. The decoder accesses the level-aligned token sequence and the item-level semantic sequence through two separate cross-attention branches and fuses their contexts. We further introduce LESSRec+, which uses separate encoders for different semantic-level histories with the same total parameter count to reduce encoder computation. Extensive experiments across three public datasets and three tokenizers demonstrate the recommendation effectiveness of LESSRec and LESSRec+, with efficiency analyses highlighting the computational benefits of LESSRec+.

</details>


### [228] [When Is Additional Retrieval Worthwhile? A Cost-Aware Study of Evidence Gain and Answer Reliability in RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37358)
*Zhouting Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种成本感知的双动作检索策略，在密集检索+重排序基线与混合词法-密集检索器之间动态路由，实验表明该策略仅在24.5%的问题上触发混合检索，即可达到与全量混合检索相当的证据召回率（Hit@10 78.70%），但并未带来生成答案正确性或忠实度的显著提升，揭示了高效证据检索与答案可靠性提升之间的本质差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统中，更复杂的检索虽能提升证据召回，但往往伴随额外计算开销，且证据召回的提升不一定转化为更忠实或正确的答案。需要一种能在检索前预估问题级增量收益、实现成本效益权衡的自适应检索策略。

Method: 基于路由决策前可获得的特征，训练正则化线性模型预估混合检索相对于基线的证据召回增量收益；采用文档分组交叉验证选择决策阈值并冻结模型；在QASPER训练集划分的60篇未见文章（188问题，169可评估证据）上进行保留集评估；对比三种检索策略（基线、全量混合、自适应策略）下的证据Hit@10及4-bit Qwen3-4B生成答案的正确性与忠实度（模型辅助评估）。

Result: 自适应策略仅在46个问题（24.5%）触发混合检索，证据Hit@10达到78.70%，与全量混合检索的78.70%持平，显著优于基线的76.92%；配对论文级bootstrap置信区间未显示可靠的效用优势；生成答案层面的模型辅助评估显示，自适应策略在正确性和忠实度上均无明显提升，且缺乏独立人工验证。

Conclusion: 高效检索相关证据与提升生成答案可靠性是两个不同目标；证据召回指标的提升不自动保证答案质量改善；本工作为可复现的实证研究，不宣称在答案质量上具有统计学显著的优越性。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) can benefit from more sophisticated retrieval, but extra retrieval may be unnecessary for many questions and gains in evidence retrieval may not translate into faithful or correct answers. We study a cost-aware, two-action retrieval policy that chooses between a dense-retrieval-plus-reranking baseline and a hybrid lexical--dense retriever. Rather than classifying baseline retrieval as simply successful or unsuccessful, we estimate the question-specific incremental evidence-retrieval benefit of hybrid retrieval using features available before the routing decision. A regularized linear model and decision threshold are selected using document-grouped cross-validation and frozen prior to held-out evaluation. On 60 previously unseen QASPER training-split articles (188 questions, 169 evidence-evaluable), the policy triggers hybrid retrieval on 46 questions (24.5\%) and obtains the same evidence Hit@10 as always-hybrid retrieval (78.70\%, versus 76.92\% for always-keep). Paired, paper-cluster bootstrap intervals do not establish a reliable utility advantage. We further compare generated answers from a 4-bit Qwen3-4B model under the three retrieval policies. Provisional answer-level assessments show no clear correctness or faithfulness improvement after partial review, despite the evidence-retrieval result. The generation assessments remain model-assisted and lack independent human validation. These results expose an important distinction between retrieving relevant evidence efficiently and improving the reliability of the generated answer. The work is presented as a reproducible empirical study, not a claim of statistically established answer-quality superiority.

</details>


### [229] [Can Complementary Signals Bridge Similarity Islands? Manifold-Augmented Graph Embedding for Multimodal Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37409)
*Zhenye Yang,Jinpeng Chen,Wenbo Fu,Jun Ma,Huan Li,Qimin Zhou,Tao Wang,Li Sun,Senzhang Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 MAGE (Manifold-Augmented Graph Embedding)，一种用于个性化多模态排序的框架，通过解耦互补性建模为离线几何变形和在线拓扑传播，解决了基于相似度的图构建偏向替代品而非互补品的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态推荐系统依赖相似度驱动的图构建与传播，倾向于语义相似的替代品而非功能相关的互补品。当行为关联连接语义遥远的商品时，互补信号在几何上被孤立，形成碎片化的“相似度岛屿”，导致标准传播出现语义漂移。

Method: MAGE 包含两个阶段：离线阶段利用 Soft Conceptor 机制提取互补感知的残差方向，并在局部间隙保持约束下注入增强项表示，融合共购证据并控制几何扰动幅度；在线阶段采用非对称双流 GNN，将视觉特征作为稳定锚点、增强文本特征为柔性探针，通过自校准跨模态融合机制协调共享语义与模态特异性残差线索。

Result: 在四个 Amazon 数据集上的大量实验表明，MAGE 在强多模态推荐基线上取得一致性提升。附录中的支持分析进一步验证了几何感知增强能缓解互补推荐场景中的语义错配。

Conclusion: MAGE 通过离线几何变形与在线拓扑传播的解耦设计，有效建模互补关系，解决了相似度驱动方法忽视互补品的问题，为多模态推荐中的语义错配提供了几何感知的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal Recommender Systems (MMRS) leverage rich item features to mitigate data sparsity, yet similarity-driven graph construction and propagation can favor semantically similar substitutes over functionally related complements. When behavioral associations connect semantically distant items, complementary signals can remain geometrically isolated, yielding fragmented "Similarity Islands" and semantic drift when standard propagation is applied. To overcome these limitations, we propose MAGE (Manifold-Augmented Graph Embedding), a framework for personalized multimodal ranking that decouples complementarity modeling into offline geometric warping and online topological propagation. The offline phase enhances item representations by extracting complement-aware residual directions via a Soft Conceptor mechanism and injecting them under a local gap-preserving constraint, incorporating co-purchase evidence while controlling the magnitude of geometric perturbation. Synergistically, the online phase employs an asymmetric dual-stream GNN that treats visual features as stable anchors and augmented textual features as flexible probes, fused through a self-calibrated cross-modal mechanism to reconcile shared semantics and modality-specific residual cues. Extensive experiments on four Amazon datasets show that MAGE yields consistent improvements over strong multimodal recommendation baselines. Together with the supporting analyses in the appendix, these results suggest that geometry-aware augmentation can alleviate the semantic mismatch that arises in complementary recommendation settings. Our code is available at https://github.com/Alex-bupt/mage.

</details>


### [230] [REGER: Modeling inter-item Relations with Semantic IDs for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37584)
*Yuxi Huang,Silin Zhou,Chenhao Wang,Zihan Li,Gangming Guo,Pei Ke,Shen Gao,Shuo Shang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: REGER是一个统一框架，通过显式引入项间语义和协作关系，结合生成式语义检索预训练、全局-局部注意力机制和基于SID前缀树的偏好蒸馏，提升生成式推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐（GR）模型随机初始化SID token嵌入且从头训练，无法直接继承项间固有语义关系，协作关系仅通过token级注意力和next-token监督隐式学习，导致语义与协作建模不足。

Method: 1) 生成式语义检索预训练，让模型通过生成语义邻居的SID继承语义相似性；2) 结合全局注意力与对齐SID边界的局部注意力，捕捉历史上下文及相邻项间的有向转移；3) 提出基于SID前缀树的层次偏好分解理论框架，证明协作偏好分布的精确因式分解，将项级KL散度加权分解为前缀条件KL散度；4) 基于此蒸馏判别式教师模型在真值前缀上的全条件分布，联合优化蒸馏目标与交叉熵。

Result: 在真实数据集上的大量实验表明，REGER相较于现有GR基线持续提升性能，验证了所提偏好蒸馏的有效性。

Conclusion: REGER通过显式建模项间语义与协作关系，并结合理论指导的偏好蒸馏，有效解决了生成式推荐中语义继承不足和协作建模隐式的问题，为生成式推荐提供了新范式。

Abstract: Personalized recommendation depends on learning semantic and collaborative relations among items from online content and user interactions. Generative recommendation (GR) offers a promising paradigm by autoregressively generating discrete semantic IDs (SIDs) of items. However, randomly initialized SID token embeddings and GR models trained from scratch do not directly inherit the semantic relations inherent in items, while inter-item collaborative relations are learned largely implicitly through token-level attention and next-token supervision. To address this limitation, we propose REGER, a unified framework that explicitly incorporates inter-item relations into semantic pretraining, history encoding, and SID generation. We pretrain the GR model through generative semantic retrieval, enabling it to inherit inter-item semantic similarity by learning to generate semantic neighbors' SIDs. We combine global attention with local attention aligned with SID boundaries to capture historical context and directed transitions between adjacent items. We further develop a theoretical framework for hierarchical preference decomposition over the SID prefix tree, proving an exact factorization of collaborative preference distributions and a weighted decomposition of item-level KL divergence into prefix-conditional KL divergences. Building on this analysis, we distill a discriminative teacher's full conditional distributions over all legal next tokens at ground-truth prefixes along the target SID path, jointly optimizing this objective with cross-entropy. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate consistent improvements over existing GR baselines and support the effectiveness of the proposed preference distillation.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [231] [Counting the Bandages: On measuring the time that AI saves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35942)
*Solomon Shalom Lijo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文对比了AI辅助节省时间的两种视角：秒表视角（仅计时）与账本视角（考虑错误成本的反事实核算），并主张后者更合理。通过理论建模、实证复现和预注册实验，证明过度信任AI会以更大的总时间成本换取表面上的时钟节省，且人类检查行为存在非线性阈值效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI效率评估多采用简单的计时指标，忽略了错误逃逸成本与检查成本的权衡。作者旨在建立一个不依赖模型假设的决策框架，量化AI辅助的真实净收益，并揭示人类在AI辅助下的检查行为规律。

Method: 1. 理论建模：定义无模型假设的时间节省、超额逃逸缺陷、盈亏平衡缺陷成本三个核心量，构建基线检查模型。2. 实证复现：利用公开数据复现METR开发者研究，重新估计效果量并给出区间而非点估计。3. 预注册实验：在SEC文件摘要任务上，测试AI审阅者对AI草稿的检查行为，操纵告知的错误率观察阈值切换与过度信任后果。

Result: 1. 理论层面：校准工人的逃逸缺陷率在模型未达'免检阈值'前不随模型质量变化；检查时间随模型错误率对数增长；过度信任在时钟上节省分钟但以更大总分钟数为代价。2. 复现层面：同246个任务支持19%或24%的减速（未调整失衡时34%），AI帮助比例在3%-82%区间。GDPval工作流中审查时间将GPT-5加速上限锁定3.6倍。3. 实验层面：告知真实错误率时，审阅者在盈亏平衡点全或无切换；告知错误率低10倍时，检查减少7/9个图表，22.5%任务放行错误图表——完全因努力减少导致更快更差。

Conclusion: AI辅助的真实效率必须采用账本视角核算：需将检查成本、逃逸缺陷成本与盈亏平衡点纳入决策。人类检查行为表现为阈值型策略而非平滑调整，过度信任会导致灾难性质量下降。政策与实践应要求报告区间估计而非点估计，并在关键任务中强制校准检查强度。

Abstract: I consider what it means for AI assistance to save time. I contrast a stopwatch view, on which time saved is whatever the clock records, with a ledger view, on which it is a counterfactual that has to be read next to the errors the work lets through, and I argue for the latter. The argument has four parts. I define a model-free pair of quantities, time saved and excess escaped defects, and one decision number built from them: the break-even defect cost. I give a baseline model of checking in which, until the model is good enough not to check, a calibrated worker's escaped-defect rate does not depend on how good the model is; checking time grows with the logarithm of the model's error rate; and over-trust buys minutes on the clock at a larger cost in total minutes. I show what randomized studies can and cannot identify, and I replicate METR's developer study from its public data: the same 246 issues support a slowdown of 19% or 24% depending on the estimand (34% without adjusting for a chance imbalance), and AI helped on somewhere between 3% and 82% of them. Finally, I restate the published record, with stated bounds in place of imported point assumptions. In GDPval's own review-every-output workflow, review time alone caps GPT-5's speedup at 3.6x; the consultants outside the frontier were faster, and more often wrong. Last, I test two of the model's predictions in a small pre-specified experiment on AI reviewers checking AI drafts of the same kind of brief, built from SEC filings made after the models' training data were collected. Told the drafter's true error rate, the reviewer checked more as it rose, but all-or-nothing, switching near where its prompt's costs break even. Told that the drafter erred ten times less often than it did, it checked 7 fewer of 9 figures and let a wrong figure through in 22.5% of briefs instead of none: faster and worse, entirely through reduced effort.

</details>


### [232] [Enhancing Deep Learning Model Performance through Data Generation Strategies: Classification of Olive Tree Diseases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35984)
*Hicham El Akhal,Abderrahim Waga,Aissa Ben Yahya,Abdelbaki El Belrhiti El Alaoui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种综合数据生成策略，结合传统增强、CutMix、MixUp 和 DCGAN，通过三种数据分布场景（S1 类别平衡、S2 增加样本数、S3 扩展且保持平衡）改善植物病害分类数据集，实验基于 EfficientNetV2B0 显示显著提升分类准确率，强调数据多样性与类别平衡的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在植物病害诊断中表现优异，但常受限于数据不足和类别不平衡，导致模型泛化能力下降，亟需有效的数据生成与分布优化方法。

Method: 采用传统数据增强、CutMix、MixUp 及 DCGAN 生成合成图像，构建三种数据分布场景：S1 平衡原始数据集类别分布，S2 增加各类别样本数量，S3 在保持类别平衡前提下扩充数据集；使用预训练并微调的 EfficientNetV2B0 评估不同场景对分类性能的影响。

Result: 实验表明，三种场景均显著提升分类准确率，其中 S3 效果最佳，验证了数据多样性与类别平衡对模型性能的关键贡献。

Conclusion: 所提数据生成与分布策略能有效提升数据集质量，适用于植物病害检测及精准农业等领域，具有良好的推广应用前景。

Abstract: Deep learning models are highly effective in a wide range of domains, particu larly in plant disease diagnosis. However, their performance is often limited by challenges such as insufficient data and class imbalance. In our work, we propose a comprehensive data generation strategy to overcome these limitations. First, we apply various data generation techniques, including traditional augmentation methods, CutMix, MixUp, and synthetic image generation using Deep Convolu tional Generative Adversarial Networks (DCGANs), to create new data samples. Second, we exploit this generated data in three scenarios: (S1) balancing class distribution in the original dataset, (S2) increasing the number of samples per class, and (S3) expanding the dataset while maintaining balanced classes. Third, we evaluate the impact of these data distributions (S1, S2, S3) on model perfor mance using the EfficientNetV2B0 architecture with pre-trained and fine-tuned weights. The experimental results show a substantial improvement in classifica tion accuracy, emphasizing the critical role of data diversity and class balance. These findings highlight the effectiveness and adaptability of the proposed strat egy of data generation and distribution for enhancing dataset quality, offering promising applications in plant disease detection and precision agriculture.

</details>


### [233] [The Trinity:Capability-Driven Recursive Self-Improvement for Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36077)
*Weidong Tang,Ruiheng Wang,Jingxuan Zhang,Hexin Wang,Haoyuan Chen,Zhengfeiyang Zhang,Yixing Ma,Zichen Jiang,Ziyu Guo,Haodong Li,Zhengbo Jiao,Zhiqing Cui,Xinxiang Yin,Shuo Liang,Zhenglin Wan,Hongzhan Lin,Wangbo Zhao Kaipeng Zhang,Pengfei Zhou,Odin Zhang,Yang You*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 The Trinity 框架，通过 Creator、Explorer、Evolver 三角色协同，实现多模态智能的递归自我改进，将任务组织从表层类别转向共享能力需求，构建分层能力树与 Harness 树，并引入能力感知路由器，显著提升基准测试性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态智能的扩展面临两大瓶颈：构建异构、可验证环境成本高昂；人工 Harness 迭代无法跟上任务多样性与复杂度增长。核心洞见是不同模态和表层形式的任务可共享相同底层能力需求。

Method: 1. The Trinity 框架：Creator 抽象同一能力节点下异构任务的共同需求为简化可验证仿真世界（Real2Sim2Real），Explorer 通过选择性继承在能力树上渐进进化可复用能力模块，Evolver 基于验证执行轨迹将任务树重组为层级能力树。2. Harness 树：固定拓扑内累积验证更新，形成专用于不同能力的 Harness。3. Capability-Aware Harness Router：利用公开任务信息从树中选择兼容模块配置。

Result: 进化后的 Harness 在 28 个训练基准上平均性能提升 34 分，在 18 个未见基准（含两个真实具身任务）上平均提升 26 分。

Conclusion: 通过能力驱动的递归自我改进，The Trinity 有效解决了环境构建成本与人工迭代滞后的问题，实现了跨模态任务的能力共享与迁移，为多模态智能的规模化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Multimodal intelligence can be viewed as human-guided co-evolution between worlds and agents through interaction. However, scaling this process faces two bottlenecks: constructing heterogeneous, verifiable environments is costly, and manual Harness iteration cannot keep pace with growing task diversity and complexity. A key insight is that tasks with different modalities and surface forms can share the same underlying capability requirements. We therefore introduce The Trinity, a recursive self-improvement framework coupling world generation with Harness evolution through three roles: Creator, Explorer, and Evolver. Drawing on verified execution trajectories, Evolver shifts the basis of task organization from surface categories to shared requirements, reorganizing the original task tree into a hierarchical capability tree. We introduce Capability-Driven Real2Sim2Real: for heterogeneous tasks at the same node, Creator abstracts their common requirements into simplified, verifiable simulated worlds for capability learning, while evaluation on original tasks measures transfer. Along this capability tree, Explorer progressively evolves reusable capability modules through selective inheritance, forming a Harness tree whose validated updates accumulate across iterations within a fixed topology. The resulting Harnesses specialize in different capabilities, motivating a Capability-Aware Harness Router that uses public task information to select compatible module configurations from the tree. Our evolved Harnesses improve mean performance by 34 points across 28 training benchmarks and 26 points across 18 unseen benchmarks, including two real-world embodied tasks.

</details>


### [234] [CohortFlow: A Steerable Clinical Cohort Curation Agent with Localized Semantic Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36100)
*Neil Chen,Adrian Serapio,Hanxue Gu,Evan Lee,Kang Wang,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 CohortFlow，一种可导向的智能体，用于临床队列构建。它将自然语言修正表示为对结构化队列规范的局部更新，并与持久执行图协调，利用显式语义依赖选择性失效受影响计算，保留有效先前结果。实验显示：在 100 个临床试验入选标准衍生场景（126 轮修正）上，局部导向达到 77.0% 需求级 micro-F1，与全量重生成 76.4% 无显著差异（p=0.493），但平均修正延迟降低 64.8%（完成后）和 44.3%（执行中），模型调用减少 38.1%。MIMIC 概念验证中 13 个队列状态均成功执行，修正轨迹展示了受影响节点的选择性重算与无关计算的保留。结论：局部语义导向能大幅降低修正延迟和模型调用，同时保持强需求级准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床队列构建中的语言模型智能体需要适应不断演变的入选标准，避免对未变更的需求重复处理，以提高效率并减少计算开销。

Method: CohortFlow 将自然语言修正转化为对结构化队列规范的局部更新，并与持久执行图协调。通过显式记录语义依赖，实现受影响计算的选择性失效，同时保留有效的先前结果。

Result: 在 100 个临床试验入选标准衍生场景（126 轮修正）上，局部导向达到 77.0% 需求级 micro-F1，全量重生成为 76.4%（p=0.493，差异不显著）。平均修正延迟分别降低 64.8%（完成后）和 44.3%（执行中），模型调用减少 38.1%。MIMIC 概念验证中，所有 13 个队列状态成功执行，修正轨迹显示选择性保留无关计算并重算变更或依赖节点。

Conclusion: 局部语义导向能在保持强需求级准确性的同时，大幅降低修正延迟和模型调用次数，为临床队列构建中的动态标准演变提供高效解决方案。

Abstract: Language model agents for clinical cohort curation should accommodate evolving eligibility criteria without repeatedly processing unchanged requirements. We introduce CohortFlow, a steerable agent that represents natural language corrections as localized updates to a structured cohort specification and reconciles them with a persistent execution graph. Explicitly recorded semantic dependencies enable affected computations to be selectively invalidated while preserving valid prior results. On 100 scenarios derived from clinical-trial eligibility criteria, comprising 126 correction turns, localized steering achieves 77.0% requirement micro-F1 versus 76.4% for full regeneration using the same 8B model, a small numerical advantage that is not statistically significant ($p=0.493$). It reduces mean correction latency by 64.8% after completion and 44.3% during execution, with 38.1% fewer model calls. In a separate proof-of-concept evaluation over MIMIC, all 13 cohort states execute successfully, while correction trajectories demonstrate selective preservation of unaffected computations and recomputation of changed or dependent nodes. Together, these results show that localized semantic steering can substantially reduce correction latency and model calls while maintaining strong requirement-level accuracy.

</details>


### [235] [Why and How to Do Science When AI Is Better at It: A Framework Grounded in Human Understanding and Direction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36116)
*Hoda Heidari,Aydin Mohseni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个以人类理解和方向为核心的框架，用于判断何时可将科研任务委托给AI。核心观点是：科学的根本目的是服务于人类目的和价值的持久、集体的人类理解。由此衍生出双重测试：1) 人类理解——科学共同体必须能解释、辩护、争辩结果并保持相关技能；2) 人类方向——关于哪些问题重要及发现意义的判断必须由可问责的人类保留。将此测试应用于CRediT分类学的各个科研阶段，得出可按领域定制的建议。该框架区别于现有指南：基于科学目的而非诚信规范；即使AI超越人类研究者依然适用；按对人类理解和方向的影响而非工具类型评判AI使用，覆盖同行评审和新研究者培养等所有阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI在核心科学任务上达到或超越人类研究者，迫切需要回答两个问题：人类为何仍应从事科学，以及如何在从事科学时使用AI。作者认为第二个问题的回答取决于第一个问题的回答，因此需要建立一个基于科学根本目的的原则性框架来指导AI在科研中的使用。

Method: 1) 提出科学的不可或缺目标：服务于人类目的和价值的持久、集体的人类理解。2) 从此目标推导出双重委托测试：人类理解测试（共同体能解释、辩护、争辩主张并保持技能）和人类方向测试（重要问题判断和发现意义解释权归可问责人类）。3) 将测试应用于CRediT分类学定义的各科研阶段，生成可定制的领域建议。4) 与现有指南对比，阐述三大差异。5) 提出维持科学指向人类理解和方向所需的改革。

Result: 形成了一套适用于科研全流程（包括同行评审、研究者培养）的AI使用评判框架。该框架不依赖AI当前的局限性，即使AI超越人类仍有效；不以工具类型为判断标准，而是以对人类理解和方向的影响为准；不基于诚信规范，而基于科学目的。可为不同领域提供定制化的委托建议。

Conclusion: 为保持科学指向人类理解和方向，需要实施改革：建立机制确保共同体保持解释和争辩能力；将研究议程设定和意义解释权保留给可问责的人类；重新设计同行评审、培训和激励制度以强化人类理解和方向；制定政策防止理解和方向的不可逆丧失。

Abstract: As AI begins to match or exceed human researchers at core scientific tasks, two questions become pressing: \emph{why} should humans keep doing science, and \emph{how} should they use AI when they do? This paper argues that the second can be answered only by answering the first. It holds that one indispensable aim of science is \emph{durable, collective human understanding of the world, pursued in service of human ends and values}. From this principle it derives a two-part test for when a research task can be delegated to AI. The first part, \emph{human understanding}, requires that the scientific community remain able to explain, defend and contest the resulting claims, and retain the skills to do so. The second, \emph{human direction}, requires that judgments about which questions matter and what findings mean stay with accountable humans. Applying the test to each phase of research in the Contributor Roles Taxonomy (CRediT) yields recommendations that fields can tailor to their questions and methods. The framework differs from existing guidance in three ways. It rests on an account of what science is for rather than on integrity norms. It holds even if AI outperforms human researchers, rather than depending on AI's current limitations. And it judges each use of AI by its effects on human understanding and direction rather than by the type of tool, across every phase of research, including peer review and the training of new researchers. It closes with the reforms needed to keep science aimed at human understanding and direction.

</details>


### [236] [From Structural Feedback to Prompt Policies: Learning Faithful Text-to-Image Prompt Editors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36146)
*Wentao Ye,Yali Ye,Zhiqing Xiao,Ru Peng,Xinpeng Ti,Liyao Li,Zhanming Shen,Peng Lu,Jie Zhang,Haobo Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: StruPO 是一种结构感知的提示词编辑器，利用校准场景图作为共享表示，通过图条件策略和图像 grounding 监督实现单次通过的提示词优化，在保持场景结构的前提下提升生成图像的忠实度与美学质量，尤其在关系保真度上取得一致增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示词优化方法在风格重写时往往削弱物体属性和关系，尽管全局文本-图像相似度保持较高。需要一种能够明确保留场景结构、同时改进提示词的方法，避免迭代式的评判-重写循环。

Method: 提出 StruPO：1) 使用校准场景图作为策略条件和图像 grounding 监督的共享表示，图显式暴露应保留的结构；2) 通过删除、重排、插入等 token 对齐的局部编辑操作修改提示词；3) 将外部评判的结构反馈转化为可复用编辑策略的训练信号；4) 采用平滑奖励塑形保留 grounding 置信度，并对每次编辑生成多张图像取平均计算群体相对优势，处理 grounding 不确定性和图像生成随机性。

Result: 在三个不相交的 2K 提示词测试集和四个冻结生成主干上实验，StruPO 改善忠实度-美学权衡，在关系保真度上获得一致提升。自动评估器和 15 人标注研究提供互补证据，人类偏好优势随关系复杂度增加而扩大。消融实验证实增益源于图条件、局部编辑和图像 grounding 学习。

Conclusion: StruPO 实现了单次通过即可生成可复用的优化文本，无需迭代评判-重写循环，通过结构感知的图表示和图像 grounding 监督，有效平衡了场景忠实度与生成质量，为提示词优化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Prompt optimization should improve the image a user receives without changing the scene the user requested. Yet stylistic rewriting can weaken object attributes and relations even when global text--image similarity remains high. We propose StruPO, a structure-aware prompt editor that uses a calibrated scene graph as a shared representation for policy conditioning and image-grounded supervision. The graph exposes what should be preserved, while deletion, reordering, and insertion provide a token-aligned way to modify the prompt. This turns structural feedback from an external critique into a training signal for a reusable editing policy. Learning from that signal requires handling both uncertain grounding and stochastic image generation. StruPO retains grounding confidence through smooth reward shaping and averages multiple images for each edit before computing group-relative advantages. Experiments span three disjoint 2K-prompt test sets and four frozen generation backbones, with comparisons to learned prompt optimizers, iterative structural feedback, diffusion-model fine-tuning, and inference-time attention control. StruPO improves the faithfulness--aesthetics trade-off, with consistent gains in relational fidelity. Independent automatic evaluators and a 15-annotator study provide complementary evidence, and human preference margins increase with relation complexity. Controlled ablations connect these gains to graph conditioning, local editing, and image-grounded learning. The resulting policy produces reusable optimized text in one pass after graph calibration, without an iterative critique--rewrite loop.

</details>


### [237] [Don't Pick a Winner: Knowledge Conflicts Need a Prior, Not a Rule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36169)
*Minh Pham Van,Cong Tran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于决策理论的检索增强语言模型冲突解决方法，通过显式建模检索上下文与模型内部知识两个信息源的独立可靠性证据及先验概率，在GRACE和ClashEval基准上显著优于所有固定规则基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RALM在检索上下文与模型参数知识冲突时，仅采用设计时固定的启发式规则（如遵循上下文、按比例放大上下文、信任模型记忆）。决策理论分析表明：任意固定规则的得分随可靠上下文比例线性变化，而最优可达得分为凸函数，故固定规则在非最优点均被击败；且两源间的对称散度不携带哪个源正确的信息。因此必须引入各源独立的证据和上下文正确率先验才能最优解决冲突。

Method: 构建四模块系统：1) 知识笔记生成：模型将内部知识显式化为结构化笔记；2) 校准策略网络：轻量策略为每条笔记输出校准后的置信度；3) 双声道读取器：分别基于检索上下文与知识笔记生成两个候选答案；4) 先验感知选择器：在真实正确上下文比例π的精确先验偏移下，从两个候选答案中选择提交或弃权。

Result: GRACE基准上，系统在所有上下文混合比例下均超越最强基线达26分；总分Qwen3.5-35B-A3B为45.3、Llama-3.1-8B为35.0，最强基线分别仅为29.4和27.5。预注册ClashEval上比同骨干CK-PLUG和TCR高出12和26分。当模型缺乏相关知识时增益消失，与理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 显式建模各信息源的独立证据并结合先验进行动态决策，从根本上优于任何固定启发式规则；理论与实验双重验证了决策框架的有效性，且方法在模型无知识时优雅退化为基线，确保了鲁棒性。

Abstract: A retrieval-augmented language model answers from two sources, the retrieved context and its own knowledge, and the two disagree whenever the context is incomplete or wrong. Existing methods settle the disagreement with a rule fixed at design time: follow the context, amplify it in proportion to how far it moves the model, or trust the model's memory. Each rule is confidently wrong in some deployment. In a decision-theoretic model of two sources, the score of any fixed rule is linear in the share of reliable contexts while the best attainable score is convex, so every fixed rule is beaten wherever it is not exactly optimal; and a symmetric divergence between the two sources, the signal adaptive decoding uses, carries no information about which one is right. Resolving a conflict therefore needs two inputs that no existing method keeps: evidence about each source separately, and a prior on how often the context is right. We test these claims on GRACE, a benchmark that intervenes on the knowledge-graph context of a fixed question, so that what the model knows, what the context says and what is true are measured separately. There, every baseline from four families is strong on right contexts or on wrong ones, never on both. We then build the two inputs: the model states its knowledge as notes, a small policy voices each note with a calibrated confidence, a reader answers in two voices, and a selector commits or abstains under a prior $π$ applied as an exact prior shift. With $π$ set to the true share of right contexts, the system is above the best baseline at every mixture of GRACE contexts, by up to 26 points. Its overall score is 45.3 and 35.0 (Qwen3.5-35B-A3B, Llama-3.1-8B), against 29.4 and 27.5 for the best baseline. On preregistered ClashEval it beats same-backbone CK-PLUG and TCR by 12 and 26 points. Where the model lacks the knowledge, the gains vanish, as the theory predicts.

</details>


### [238] [Learning Human Sports Motion Feasibility via Quantile Regression on Tracking Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36183)
*Marc Gutiérrez-Pérez,Antonio Agudo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于条件分位数回归的方法来预测球员到达任意位置的最短时间（TTA），使用轻量级MLP模型在FIFA World Cup 2022追踪数据上训练，并在多项指标上超越六种物理基准模型，还验证了模型在篮球和NFL数据上的跨运动迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的空间控制建模主要依赖位置密度估计或封闭形式的物理模型，难以准确捕捉球员在当前运动学状态下到达任意位置的最短合理时间。需要一种数据驱动的方法直接学习运动时间的下包络，避免物理模型的简化假设带来的偏差。

Method: 将TTA建模为条件分位数回归问题，估计运动时间的低分位数（下包络）。使用轻量级多层感知机（MLP）作为预测器，输入包含球员当前运动学状态和目标位置，输出条件分位数。在PFF FC FIFA World Cup 2022全部64场比赛的光学追踪数据上训练，与六种文献中的球员运动模型（重新表达为TTA预测器）和可达凸包基线进行对比评估。
