To adapt the choice of observation layers to the current prefix, we propose the Causal Depth Router (CDR). With only response-level labels available, we further develop Risk-Onset Target Refinement (RTR) to progressively construct token-level training targets for both components. Through extensive experiments across multiple safety benchmarks, we demonstrate that ADTGuard consistently achieves strong streaming detection performance, matching or surpassing state-of-the-art safeguards while requiring only 16M trainable parameters and maintaining competitive per-token latency.

</details>


### [270] [Follow the Herd: A Data-Driven Approach for Actionable Recourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36580)
*Nishtha Madaan,Srikanta Bedathur*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了'数据驱动行动补救'(DDAR)方法，这是首个无需昂贵人工监督即可提供行动补救的方法。DDAR利用历史用户轨迹数据构建动态模型，并在此模型下搜索能实现期望结果的轨迹，实验表明该方法能推荐合理的行动补救且动作成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器学习系统越来越多地影响人们的生活决策，为用户提供可操作的行动补救以实现期望结果变得至关重要。现有方法依赖昂贵的人工监督，需要手工设计成本函数再搜索低成本动作，这限制了行动补救的可扩展性和实用性。

Method: DDAR基于一个核心假设：历史用户数据中高概率出现的轨迹应对应较低成本。方法包含两步：1) 利用历史用户轨迹数据构建动态模型；2) 在该动态模型下搜索能够实现用户期望结果的轨迹作为行动补救建议。

Result: 实验结果表明，DDAR无需人工监督即可推荐合理的行动补救方案，且在动作成本上优于现有的需要人工设计成本函数的方法。

Conclusion: DDAR展示了数据驱动方法在行动补救任务中的有效性，消除了对昂贵人工标注成本函数的依赖，为大规模部署行动补救系统提供了可行路径。

Abstract: As machine learning systems make increasingly greater decisions about people's lives, it is critical to provide users with actionable recourse about how they can achieve their desired outcomes. This problem is currently tackled by relying on expensive human supervision which involves hand-designing a cost function before searching for low-cost actions that achieve the desired outcome. In this paper, we propose \textit{``Data-Driven Action Recourse"} (DDAR), the first approach for providing action recourse without requiring costly supervision from humans about action costs. We build upon the notion that highly likely trajectories in the historical user data should be associated with lower costs. With this, DDAR first builds a dynamics model of historical user trajectories. Under this model, it then searches for trajectories that achieve the desired outcome. In experiments, we find that this simple approach recommends plausible action recourse without requiring human supervision, achieving lower action costs than the existing approaches.

</details>


### [271] [InfraDev: Can Coding Agents Develop AI Infrastructure, and How Can We Tell?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36589)
*Yangshen Deng,Jiaqi Ruan,Yeqi Huang,Nick Lau,Guangming Sheng,Luo Mai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: InfraDev 是一个包含 36 个真实世界拉取请求任务的基准测试，涵盖训练、推理和强化学习的基础设施开发，要求代理在多文件、多 GPU 环境下正确重现变更。引入隐藏探针评分机制和共享执行环境，最佳模型仅解决 38.9% 任务，探针拒绝 32% 通过官方测试的提交，原始 PR 描述作为提示时无模型解决任务。失败分析显示结构和状态错误主导，代理自测很少检查内存、时间、流量。将发现转化为代理技能可将前沿模型在 Megatron-LM 任务上的解决率提升 10.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅覆盖单内核或单 GPU 任务，且使用的测试和提示会误判正确和错误补丁，无法评估生产级 AI 基础设施开发能力。

Method: 从 Megatron-LM、vLLM、verl 的真实 PR 构建 36 个任务，每个任务跨越多文件且需在多 GPU 正确运行。联合合成隐藏探针强制执行原始测试未检查的需求，并提供说明探针调用接口但不泄露解决方案的提示。设计共享执行环境跨任务复用依赖和构建产物，减少存储和构建时间。在六个前沿模型上评测，进行失败分析，并将发现转化为代理技能提升性能。

Result: 最佳模型仅解决 38.9% 任务；隐藏探针拒绝 32% 通过所有官方测试的提交；使用原始 PR 描述作为提示时，无模型解决任何任务。失败分析显示结构和状态错误主导，代理自测很少检查内存、时间、流量。将发现转化为代理技能后，前沿模型在 Megatron-LM 任务上的解决率提升 10.5%。共享执行环境在 Megatron-LM 和 vLLM 上分别减少存储 52-57%、构建时间 13-14%。

Conclusion: InfraDev 填补了生产级 AI 基础设施开发基准的空白，揭示了当前编码代理在多文件、多 GPU 任务上的不足。隐藏探针评分有效识别官方测试遗漏的错误，共享执行环境显著降低资源消耗。失败分析指导的代理技能改进能显著提升性能，为未来研究提供方向。

Abstract: Coding agents are beginning to develop the production-grade AI infrastructure that trains and serves large models, yet existing benchmarks cover only single kernels or single-GPU tasks, and grade with tests and prompts that misjudge both correct and incorrect patches. We introduce InfraDev, a benchmark of 36 tasks built from real-world pull requests to Megatron-LM, vLLM, and verl, covering infrastructure development for training, inference, and reinforcement learning. Each task asks an agent to reimplement a real change that spans many files and must run correctly across many GPUs. To grade such changes, we jointly synthesize hidden probes, which enforce the requirements the original tests leave unchecked, and a prompt that states every interface the probes call without revealing the solution. We also design a shared execution environment that reuses dependencies and artifacts across tasks, cutting storage by 52-57% and build time by 13-14% on Megatron-LM and vLLM. Across six frontier models, the best resolves only 38.9% of the tasks; the probes reject 32% of the submissions that pass every official test, and with the original pull request descriptions as prompts, no model resolves a single task. Failure analysis shows that structural and state errors dominate and that agents' own tests rarely check memory, time, or traffic. Turning these findings into an agent skill raises the resolved rate of a frontier model on the Megatron-LM tasks by 10.5%.

</details>


### [272] [Learning to Formalize: Bridging Open-Ended Workloads and Fast Decision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36595)
*Chengxuan Pei,Ziyao Wang,Teng Mei,Zhe Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种候选形式化器，将开放式任务转化为显式候选动作，再由冻结的决策模型快速选择，显著提升成功率并降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策模型需要预定义候选列表，而通用大语言模型虽能处理开放任务但序列解码慢；许多工作负载缺乏明确候选列表，需要生成式后端来决定可行动作。

Method: 训练一个小型候选形式化器，利用离线教师生成的列表，从任务上下文构建显式、经过验证的备选动作；冻结的决策模型从中选择条目。

Result: 在CALVIN-D测试集上，通用动作仅11%成功率，而使用DeepSeek V4 Flash姿态候选达到46.5%；在1011个验证标注上，微调后的形式化器配合本地决策模型达52.6%成功率，无形式化器仅11.8%；端到端延迟2.09秒，而Qwen3.5-27B为3.67秒。

Conclusion: 候选形式化器有效弥合了决策模型与大语言模型的接口差距，实现了高成功率和低延迟的开放任务执行。

Abstract: Decision models offer fast choices over sup- plied alternatives, while general-purpose large language models interpret open-ended tasks through flexible generation and reasoning at the cost of sequential decoding. Many work- loads expect a generative backend to determine what can be done and therefore provide no well-defined candidate list. We bridge this in- terface gap with a small candidate formalizer trained on offline teacher-generated lists. The formalizer constructs explicit, validated alter- natives from task context, and a frozen deci- sion model selects an entry. A motivating com- parison with official TypeSafe Jev-1.13.0 on 200 CALVIN-D test episodes reports 11% suc- cess with generic motions versus 46.5% with DeepSeek V4 Flash pose candidates. Sepa- rately, a six-arm privileged-state evaluation on 1,011 CALVIN-D validation annotations finds that a locally deployed decision model can achieve at most 52.6% task success rate with the fine-tuned formalizer, compared with 11.8% without it, and the end-to-end latency is kept to 2.09 s, whereas Qwen3.5-27B is 3.67 s with the exact experiment setting.

</details>


### [273] [God Does Not Play Dice: Enforcing Batch Invariance in Agentic Kernel Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36623)
*Youwei Bao,Chengpeng Wang,Simin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文针对大语言模型服务系统中 GPU 内核缺乏批次不变性（BI）导致的数值不可复现问题，提出了一个可插拔工具，结合静态契约引导依赖分析与动态逐位一致性验证，在内核优化循环中作为验收门控，显著提升 BI 通过率（67.6% → 91.9%）且基本保持原有加速效果（保留 91.8% 几何平均加速比）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 服务系统动态批处理导致同一请求在不同运行中与不同请求共同执行，若 GPU 内核缺乏批次不变性（BI），变化的批次上下文会改变相同输入的数值输出，破坏可复现性。现有内核生成智能体仅关注正确性与效率，忽视 BI。实证研究发现 83 个批次适用的已发布工件中有 19 个确认存在 BI 违规，亟需解决。

Method: 提出工具（文中称为 tool），作为通用内核智能体的插件组件。采用契约引导的静态依赖分析识别结构风险，结合跨批次上下文的动态逐位一致性验证，将二者集成为优化循环中的连续验收门控：在性能基准测试前拒绝结构风险或动态不不变的候选内核，并向智能体反馈信息，复用原有优化能力的同时强制执行 BI。

Result: 在三个智能体框架上进行基线与 BI 感知生成的实验，针对真实 LLM 服务任务：BI 通过率从 67.6% 提升至 91.9%，同时保留基线几何平均加速比的 91.8%。表明该工具可作为可靠性目标纳入智能体内核优化，兼顾正确性、速度与批次不变性。

Conclusion: 工具通过静态契约分析与动态验证相结合，以插件形式无缝集成至现有内核生成智能体，有效解决批次不变性缺失问题，显著提升数值可复现性，且几乎不损失性能优化收益，为 LLM 服务系统的可靠内核优化提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) serving systems dynamically batch user requests for GPU execution, so the same request may be processed alongside different requests across runs. If the GPU kernels lack batch invariance (BI), this changing batch context can alter the numerical output for an unchanged input, undermining reproducibility. Existing kernel generation agents focus on correctness and efficiency but \emph{overlook BI}. We find 19 confirmed BI violations among 83 batch-applicable released artifacts. To address this limitation, we propose \tool, which combines contract-guided static dependency analysis with dynamic validation of bitwise consistency across batch contexts. \tool is designed as a plug-in component for general kernel agents, allowing their original optimization capabilities to be reused while adding BI enforcement. We integrate it as successive acceptance gates in the optimization loop, rejecting structurally risky or dynamically non-invariant candidates before performance benchmarking and returning feedback to the agent. Experiments across three agent frameworks under baseline and BI-aware generation on real world LLM serving tasks show that \tool improves the BI pass rate from 67.6\% to 91.9\% while retaining 91.8\% of the baseline geometric mean speedup. These results demonstrate that \tool can be incorporated into kernel optimization by agents as an additional reliability objective alongside correctness and speed.

</details>


### [274] [Polarity--Time Interaction and Multi-Level Reliability for Event-Based Spacecraft Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36625)
*Yonglong Zhang,Zongwu Xie,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种面向航天器姿态估计的可靠性感知框架，利用事件相机的稀疏事件响应，通过三分支表示、语义点头、事件-CAD细化和轨迹优化，在SPADES挑战测试集上取得了0.0474的组合姿态分数，接近最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机的稀疏事件响应导致关键点误差不均匀，且其对航天器姿态估计的影响取决于对应几何和连续观测的可靠性，现有方法未充分考虑这些因素。

Method: 提出可靠性感知框架，包括极性-时间定位、几何恢复、事件-CAD细化和轨迹优化。采用三分支表示融合正负事件和近期事件时间，通过跨分支注意力和门控机制，再进行时空聚合。有序语义点头估计相对对应精度用于几何约束姿态恢复。事件边缘与CAD投影一致性指导局部姿态细化，序列层面用十维描述符结合热力图形状、重投影一致性和几何条件设置时间对齐观测尺度。

Result: 在SPADES官方测试集上，完整系统组合姿态分数为0.0474，比PASAN+IRIS高出约5.4%。消融实验显示：基础定位加均匀PnP得分0.0772，完整级联降至0.0514；单帧观测下，质量加权将得分从0.0549降至0.0514。

Conclusion: 该框架有效整合了事件数据的可靠性评估、几何约束和时序优化，显著提升了航天器姿态估计精度，验证了可靠性感知设计的有效性。

Abstract: Sparse event responses produce uneven keypoint errors whose effects on spacecraft pose depend on correspondence geometry and the reliability of successive observations. We present a reliability-aware framework connecting polarity--time localization, geometric recovery, event--CAD refinement, and trajectory optimization. A three-branch representation combines positive events, negative events, and recent-event timing through cross-branch attention and gating, followed by spatiotemporal aggregation. An ordered semantic-point head estimates relative correspondence precision for geometrically constrained pose recovery. Event-edge and CAD-projection agreement then guide local pose refinement under keypoint-geometry safeguards. At the sequence level, a ten-dimensional descriptor combines heatmap shape, reprojection consistency, and geometric conditioning to set time-aligned observation scales. On the official SPADES challenge test set, the complete system achieves a combined pose score of 0.0474, approximately 5.4\% below PASAN+IRIS. Validation ablations reduce the score from 0.0772 for base localization with uniform PnP to 0.0514 for the complete cascade; on identical single-frame observations, quality weighting reduces the score from 0.0549 with constant-weight optimization to 0.0514.

</details>


### [275] [The uneven rise of AI-generated web content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36631)
*Adam Visokay,Soumyakanti Pan,Molly Griston,Polina Iasakova,Mark Nepf,Denis Peskoff,Riya Sinha,Jikai Sun,Isabelle Thiara,Letao Wang,Yiwei Wu,Lauren Yan,Tyler H. McCormick*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 生成式AI在不到五年内显著重塑了公共网络，AI生成文本在网页中的比例急剧上升但分布不均，英语网页、.com域名及网络边缘页面更易包含AI文本，而.gov/.edu域名及核心页面较少；研究采用多工具检测和贝叶斯分层模型校正误差，量化了AI对公共信息的多样化贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI快速普及，但其在公共网络中的渗透程度、分布差异及对信息生态的影响尚不明确，需大规模实证分析量化AI生成文本的流行率及其异质性，为理解AI重塑网络信息提供证据。

Method: 基于500万+可爬取网页样本，使用多个AI检测工具识别AI生成文本；构建贝叶斯分层模型，校正检测器的假阳性/假阴性率，允许不同群体（语言、域名、网络中心性）的检测性能差异，并将检测误差率的不确定性传播到流行率估计中。

Result: AI生成文本占比急剧上升但分布不均：英语网页比例显著高于其他语言；.gov和.edu域名显著低于.com；网络边缘页面（低中心性）AI文本最常见且增长最快，核心页面（高中心性）最少且增长较慢；这些差异在控制组成效应后依然存在。

Conclusion: 生成式AI已成为公共信息和互联网通信的重要但多样化的贡献者；其渗透呈现显著的语言、域名和网络结构异质性，提示AI生成内容的传播受平台治理、内容生产激励及网络拓扑等因素共同塑造，未来研究需关注AI内容对信息质量和信任的深层影响。

Abstract: In less than five years, generative artificial intelligence (AI) has dramatically reshaped the public-facing web. Using a sample of more than 5 million crawlable webpages, we demonstrate that the share containing AI-generated text has increased precipitously, but unevenly. Our analyses reveal substantial differences in AI composition across language and domain categories (e.g., .edu, .com, or .gov). A higher fraction of English-language webpages contain AI-generated text compared to pages in other languages, and prevalence is substantially lower on .gov and .edu domains compared to .com. These distinctions persist even after accounting for compositional effects. We also find heterogeneity based on a page's position in the connectivity structure of the web. AI-generated text is most common and has increased most rapidly in pages that are the least central in the graph, which drives much of the overall growth in AI-generated text. A slower increase has taken place for more central pages, with the most central tier emerging as the least likely to contain AI-generated text. Methodologically, we use multiple AI detection tools, and generate estimates using a Bayesian hierarchical model that incorporates corrections for the detectors' statistical properties (both false positives and false negatives) and allows for different detector performance across groups, while propagating uncertainty in detector error rates into prevalence estimates. Our work suggests that AI is a prominent, but varied, contributor to public information and internet-based communication.

</details>


### [276] [Remembering Is Not Enough: Refining Memories and Learning When to Use Them in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36652)
*Yipeng Hao,Yuxuan Cai,Wenkai Ji,Jie Zhou,Liyang Yu,Qin Chen,Zhiguo Yan,Shulei Zheng,Liang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DiscernMem是一个无需训练的框架，用于改进基于LLM的多智能体系统中的记忆质量和使用。它包含两个核心组件：缺陷感知记忆细化（DMR）和边界学习元记忆（BLM）。DMR通过对比带记忆和不带记忆的团队执行来诊断规则缺陷并测试修复，BLM记忆每个记忆的过往使用和效果，学习何时应用或避免使用。实验表明DiscernMem在AutoGen、CAMEL、MacNet三个系统上均达到最高平均分，代码生成和符号规划任务表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的多智能体系统利用记忆复用经验，但存储的建议可能存在缺陷或被错误应用。仅靠团队层面的成功或失败无法可靠判断记忆是否需要修正或是否被误用。因此需要一种方法同时提升记忆质量和指导恰当使用。

Method: 提出DiscernMem框架，包含两个组件：1) 缺陷感知记忆细化（DMR）：对比单记忆执行与无记忆执行，诊断规则缺陷，并在当前及早期任务上测试修复以防止丢失原有有用行为。2) 边界学习元记忆（BLM）：记录每个记忆的过往使用与效果，基于任务上下文和传入消息学习其拥有者角色何时应应用或避免该记忆。两者协同帮助团队修正有缺陷的经验并保留被错误应用的有用规则。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507上针对四个基准测试，DiscernMem在AutoGen、CAMEL、MacNet三个系统上均达到最高平均分，超越最强基线0.51-4.32分。在代码生成和符号规划任务上全面领先，例如在CAMEL上PDDL得分37.71对比基线28.48。

Conclusion: DiscernMem通过无训练的DMR和BLM组件，有效解决了多智能体系统中记忆缺陷诊断与修复、以及记忆适用边界学习的问题，显著提升了系统在代码生成和符号规划等任务上的性能，验证了其在提升记忆质量和使用准确性方面的有效性。

Abstract: Memory helps LLM-based multi-agent systems reuse experience, but stored advice can be flawed or applied in the wrong situation. Team-level success or failure alone cannot reliably show whether a memory needs correction or should not have been used. We propose DiscernMem, a training-free framework that improves both memory quality and memory use. To improve quality, Defect-Aware Memory Refinement (DMR) compares team executions with a single memory and with no memory, diagnoses rule defects, and tests repairs on current and earlier tasks to guard against losing previously useful behavior. To guide appropriate use, Boundary-Learning Meta-Memory (BLM) records each memory's past uses and effects, learning when its owner role should apply or avoid it based on task context and incoming messages. Together, these components help teams correct flawed experience while retaining useful rules that were applied incorrectly. Experiments with Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507 on four benchmarks show that DiscernMem achieves the highest average score on AutoGen, CAMEL, and MacNet, exceeding the strongest reported baselines by 0.51-4.32 points. It leads on code generation and symbolic planning across all three systems, including a PDDL score of 37.71 versus 28.48 on CAMEL.

</details>


### [277] [The View from Nowhere: Population-Centered Agent Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36660)
*Shuo Yan,Ningning Hou,Haoyu He,Haochen Pei,Shang Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Population-Centered Evolution (PCE)，一种基于种群级证据的智能体自我进化方法。通过将种群的任务记录与修改历史提炼为population block，PCE利用种群中的失败模式、互补成功及历史修改效果来指导父代智能体的改进。在SWE-bench Verified (72.1%)和Polyglot (96.7%)上显著优于群体进化基线，并将种群覆盖率与最佳个体性能的差距分别缩小三分之一和完全消除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我进化方法聚焦于选定父代及其进化经验，忽视了更广泛种群中涌现的有用能力与诊断证据。研究发现种群覆盖率（至少一个变体解决的任务）与最佳个体性能间存在显著差距，表明种群内分散的知识未被整合进领先智能体。

Method: 提出'无视角原则'：智能体应通过种群层面的成功、失败及先前自我修改模式来改进。实例化为PCE：从种群收集任务记录与修改历史，提炼为population block，捕获反复失败、互补成功及过往修改效果，用于指导选定父代harness的改进。

Result: 在匹配模型、初始harness与进化调度下，PCE在SWE-bench Verified达72.1%、Polyglot达96.7%，分别比强群体进化基线提升3.2和5.9个百分点。种群覆盖率与最佳个体性能的差距在SWE-bench缩小三分之一，在Polyglot完全闭合。

Conclusion: 将种群视为证据而非仅作为选择候选，为智能体进化提供了互补原则。PCE展示了种群级知识整合对缩小个体与集体能力差距的有效性。

Abstract: Self-improving AI systems can revise the designs that govern their own behavior. Yet existing approaches center improvement on selected parent agents and their evolutionary experience, leaving the broader population largely underused. We identify a substantial gap between population coverage (i.e., the tasks solved by at least one evolved variant) and the performance of the best individual agent. This gap shows that useful capabilities and diagnostic evidence emerge across the population without being consolidated into its leading agents.
  These observations motivate the view from nowhere, a principle for agent evolution: an agent should be improved by interpreting its behavior through population-wide patterns of success, failure, and prior self-modification. We instantiate this principle as Population-Centered Evolution (PCE). PCE distils task records and modification histories from the population into a population block, which captures recurring failures, complementary successes, and the effects of previously attempted modifications to guide improvements to a selected parent's harness.
  Under matched models, initial harnesses, and evolution schedules, PCE achieves 72.1% success on SWE-bench Verified and 96.7% on Polyglot, improving over a strong group-evolution baseline by 3.2 and 5.9 percentage points. In our evaluation, PCE reduces the gap between population coverage and best-agent performance by one-third on SWE-bench and closes it on Polyglot. These results suggest that treating the population as evidence--not merely as candidates for selection--provides a complementary principle for agent evolution.

</details>


### [278] [Learning Where to Trust: Reliability-Guided Expert Routing and Velocity Gating for 3D Deformable Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36672)
*Chenyu Guo,Wenjie Dai,Yiqian Chang,Zhiyuan Zhang,Yunfan Wei,Shuhan Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReliMorph是一种基于可靠性路由的对称3D可变形配准框架，将对应关系评估与空间上下文选择和更新强度耦合，通过局部相关性分布表征对应模糊性，利用可靠性线索指导空间专家融合和残差速度门控，在单模态和多模态配准任务中均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的配准方法未充分将局部匹配可靠性与空间上下文选择和变形更新强度显式耦合。在均质、弱纹理和严重变形区域，模糊匹配可能导致错误的变形更新，这是主要挑战。

Method: 提出ReliMorph框架：1)利用共享解剖特征和模态特定自适应的局部相关性分布表征对应模糊性；2)可靠性线索指导空间专家融合和门控残差速度进行粗到精细化；3)更新的逆一致性反馈在更精细的金字塔层级指导路由和门控；首次在3D可变形配准中联合基于对应可靠性条件化空间专家路由和残差速度门控。

Result: 大量实验表明，ReliMorph在解剖重叠度上持续优于竞争方法，证明了其在单模态和多模态配准任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: ReliMorph是首个将空间专家路由和残差速度门控联合条件化于对应可靠性的3D可变形配准框架，通过可靠性引导的对称机制有效解决了模糊匹配导致的错误变形更新问题。

Abstract: Deformable image registration relies on accurate anatomical correspondences. However, explicitly coupling local matching reliability with spatial context selection and deformation update strength remains insufficiently explored in learning-based registration. This challenge is particularly pronounced in homogeneous, weakly textured, and severely deformed regions, where ambiguous matches may induce erroneous deformation updates. We propose ReliMorph, a reliability-routed symmetric framework for 3D deformable registration that couples correspondence assessment with spatial context selection and update strength.Local correlation distributions characterize correspondence ambiguity using shared anatomical features with modality-specific adaptation. These reliability cues guide spatial expert fusion and gate residual velocities during coarse-to-fine refinement. Updated inverse-inconsistency feedback guides routing and gating at finer pyramid levels. To our knowledge, ReliMorph is the first 3D deformable registration framework to jointly condition spatial expert routing and residual velocity gating on correspondence reliability. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ReliMorph consistently outperforms competing methods in anatomical overlap, highlighting its effectiveness across unimodal and multimodal registration tasks.

</details>


### [279] [PsyJev: Psychological Analysis via System-1 Judgment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36689)
*Fangyuan Liu,Sirui Zhao,Xiaohao Wang,Guo Cheng,Peng Zhang,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于心理内容分析的共享、问题条件决策接口，构建了PsyJudgeBench基准（13个数据集，82个问题，覆盖六大心理学领域），并开发了PsyJev模型（基于Qwen3.5的2B/4B/9B版本），通过联合监督训练和判断锚定RLCD（JA-RLCD）实现异质心理判断的优化，9B模型平均测试F1达67.56，超越最强外部基线2.54分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络上的自我披露支持多样化的心理分析，从识别表达体验到识别症状相关信息，现有方法缺乏统一的、问题条件化的决策接口来处理这种异质性内容分析任务，且缺乏保留源特定标准和未观测标签掩码的综合基准。

Method: 1. 构建PsyJudgeBench：整合13个标注数据集，82个问题，覆盖六大心理学领域，保留源特定标准和未观测标签掩码。2. 提出PsyJev：一种System-1模型，直接输出每个请求指定答案的分布。3. 两阶段训练：联合监督微调（SFT）建立共享判断函数；判断锚定RLCD（JA-RLCD）通过平衡监督、选择性保留正确SFT预测、问题组奖励优化，在logit空间进行异质心理判断的奖励优化。

Result: 基于Qwen3.5的2B、4B、9B模型平均测试F1分别为64.33、66.21、67.56。9B模型比最强外部基线高出2.54分。受控2B实验验证了训练阶段和锚定细化的有效性。进一步分析了问题条件判断、推理成本、概率质量，并进行了跨模型定性比较。

Conclusion: PsyJev通过共享判断接口和JA-RLCD训练框架，有效解决了异质心理判断任务的统一建模问题，在PsyJudgeBench上达到SOTA性能，证明了问题条件化决策接口和锚定奖励优化对心理内容分析的价值。

Abstract: Self-disclosures on the Web support diverse psychological analyses, from recognizing expressed experiences to identifying symptom-relevant information. We study a shared, question-conditioned decision interface for this heterogeneous problem in content analysis. We organize PsyJudgeBench, comprising 13 annotated datasets and 82 questions across six psychological areas, while preserving source-specific criteria and masks for unobserved labels. We introduce PsyJev, a System-1 model that directly produces distributions over answers specified in each request. Joint supervised training establishes the shared judgment function, followed by Judgment-Anchored RLCD (JA-RLCD), which adapts logit-space reward optimization to heterogeneous psychological judgments through balanced supervision, selective retention of correct SFT predictions, and question-group reward optimization. The Qwen3.5-based 2B, 4B, and 9B models achieve 64.33, 66.21, and 67.56 average test F1, respectively. The 9B model exceeds the strongest external comparison by 2.54 points. Controlled 2B comparisons examine the training stages and anchored refinement. Further analyses examine question-conditioned judgment, inference cost, and probability quality, complemented by qualitative comparisons across models.

</details>


### [280] [Beyond the Evidence Coherence Trap: Agent-Driven Evidence Selection for Temporally Disordered Fake News Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36764)
*Chi Wang,Min Gao,Yifei Zhang,Bincheng Gu,Zongwei Wang,Wentao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ChronoFake基准和DelSA方法，解决时间错序假新闻中的“证据相干陷阱”问题，通过迭代证据选择和时间线一致性验证，在ChronoFake上超越现有方法，在PolitiFact和GossipCop上保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有假新闻检测忽视时间错序操纵：事实事件被重组为错误时间顺序，单个事件可能真实但时间关系被误述。相似事件复现导致检索返回错误时期的语义合理证据，造成“证据相干陷阱”——局部相干但时间错位的证据错误验证文章。现有基准罕见隔离此设置。

Method: 构建ChronoFake基准（30,706篇文章，源不重叠，基于时间锚定真实事件，区分时间操纵与合法叙述）。提出DelSA（文档选择自我更新代理）：训练时迭代优化证据选择，推理时冻结规则单次通过选择证据。LLM刻画候选证据，验证器训练的重排序器平衡局部相关性与时间线一致性。分类器比对LLM提取的文章隐含年表与证据衍生年表，揭示全局时间冲突。

Result: DelSA在ChronoFake上超越现有方法，在PolitiFact和GossipCop上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模时间线一致性和全局年表对比，DelSA有效缓解证据相干陷阱，为时间错序假新闻检测提供新范式。

Abstract: Fake news undermines public trust and the integrity of online information ecosystems. Temporally disordered fake news is especially elusive because it rearranges factual events into a false chronology. Individual events may remain factually valid even when their temporal relations are misrepresented. When similar events involving the same entity recur, retrieval may return semantically plausible evidence from the wrong period. Consequently, independent event verification may rely on locally coherent but temporally mismatched evidence and incorrectly validate the article. We call this the \textit{evidence coherence trap}. Because existing benchmarks rarely isolate this setting, we construct ChronoFake, a source-disjoint benchmark of 30,706 articles derived from temporally anchored real events. It distinguishes temporal manipulation from valid narration, including flashbacks. To address this, we propose DelSA, a \underline{D}ocument S\underline{el}ection \underline{S}elf-Updating \underline{A}gent that iteratively refines evidence selection during training and applies a frozen rulebook for one-pass evidence selection at inference. DelSA uses an LLM to characterize candidate evidence, while a verifier-trained reranker balances local relevance against timeline consistency. Finally, the classifier compares the article-implied chronology extracted by the LLM with the evidence-derived chronology to reveal global temporal conflicts. DelSA outperforms existing methods on ChronoFake, while remaining competitive on PolitiFact and GossipCop.

</details>


### [281] [Can LLMs Reliably Grade Olympiad Proofs? A Study of Mathematical Verification with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36767)
*Azim Ospanov,Zijin Feng,Ding Ding,Chengwu Liu,Haoli Bai,Jiacheng Sun,Lifeng Shang,Farzan Farnia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了USAMO 2025候选解答的专家标注数据集，系统评估了20个大语言模型在奥林匹克级别数学证明验证上的表现，并提出了用于生成高质量对抗性证明的TROJAN流程以及基于多臂老虎机框架的MABGrader评分器，后者在二次加权Kappa和评分误差上显著超越现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在竞赛级数学求解上取得显著进展，但其对自然语言数学证明的验证能力，尤其是奥林匹克级别的长链条推理证明，仍相对未被充分探索。当前证明验证主要依赖昂贵且难以扩展的人工专家检查，亟需自动化、可扩展的LLM验证方法。

Method: 1. 构建人工策划的USAMO 2025候选解答数据集并附带专家评分。2. 在七项指标、两种提示风格、三种推理方法、三种提示模板下对20个前沿模型进行全面评测。3. 提出TROJAN：一种迭代自我批评管道，用于大规模生成高保真对抗性证明。4. 提出MABGrader：将评分重构为离散分数空间上的臂选择问题，采用多臂老虎机框架进行优化。

Result: 全面评测显示最先进模型在证明验证上仍有巨大提升空间。MABGrader在二次加权Kappa指标上比SOTA基线ProofGrader提升26.4%，评分误差降低14%。

Conclusion: 本工作为奥林匹克级数学证明的自动化验证提供了基准数据集、大规模对抗性证明生成管道以及更有效的评分框架，代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have made remarkable progress on solving competition-level mathematics, yet their ability to verify natural-language mathematical proofs remains relatively underexplored. This gap is especially pronounced for Olympiad-level proofs, which require meticulous assessment of each claim in a long reasoning chain. Current proof verification thus primarily relies on expert inspection, which is costly to scale. In this paper, we present a systematic study of LLM-based verification in this setting. Specifically, we introduce a human-curated dataset of USAMO 2025 candidate solutions with expert grading, and conduct a broad evaluation of existing LLM verifiers across frontier models. We also propose TROJAN, an iterative self-critique pipeline for generating high-fidelity adversarial proofs at scale. Our comprehensive evaluation covers 20 models spanning seven metrics, two prompt styles, three inference methods, and three prompt templates, showing that state-of-the-art models still leave considerable room for improvement. To narrow this gap, we propose MABGrader, a Multi-Armed Bandit framework that reframes grading as arm selection over the discrete score space. MABGrader outperforms the SOTA ProofGrader by 26.4% in Quadratic Weighted Kappa and reduces grading error by 14%. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/aziksh-ospanov/TROJAN.

</details>


### [282] [Batching the Reflective Optimization Loop: Parallel Proposals Make GEPA Faster and Better](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36774)
*Jialin Zhang,Lakshya A Agrawal,Shangyin Tan,Donghyun Lee,Lutfi Eren Erdogan,Dan Klein,Koushik Sen,Alex Dimakis,Matei Zaharia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出批量化反射式文本优化方法，扩展GEPA优化器在每步并行评估多个候选，显著降低壁钟时间并提升测试集性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反射式优化循环为串行单候选评估，导致并行计算资源闲置，优化过程资源消耗大、耗时长。

Method: 在开源GEPA优化器基础上引入批量候选生成与并行评估机制，探索优化步骤中可并行扩展的多个维度（如候选数、评估轮次），并在有限工作池下建模运行时间。

Result: 在LiveBench-Math和HoVer两项任务上，批量设置将壁钟时间缩减至原先一半甚至四分之一，且测试集准分别从68.9%提升至72.1%（2×2批量）和从49.0%提升至60.0%（8×1批量），同时指标调用与美元预算利用率更高。

Conclusion: 批量化反射优化能有效利用并行算力，在固定预算下同时加速收敛并提升最终性能，为优化预算在多并行维度上的分配提供了首个系统性分析框架。

Abstract: Reflective text optimization makes agentic systems better, but the optimization loop is itself a resource-hungry workload whose sequential structure leaves parallel evaluation capacity idle. We extend the open-source GEPA optimizer to propose and evaluate a batch of candidates on each optimization step instead of one candidate at a time. In our sweep on two tasks, most batched runs finished in half the wall-clock time or less, and the fastest in about a quarter to a third. Batched settings also achieved higher held-out test scores, from 68.9% to 72.1% on LiveBench-Math (with $2\times2$) and from 49.0% to 60.0% on HoVer (with $8\times1$). A simple runtime model explains the measured speedups under a finite worker pool, and batched settings also spend their metric-call and dollar budgets more efficiently. We frame the axes along which a reflective optimization step can scale in parallel, a first look at allocating a fixed optimization budget across them.

</details>


### [283] [Dependency Graphs of Mathematical Arguments: Representation, Structural Measurement, and Proof Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36776)
*Eunji Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于源头的框架，利用大语言模型表示和度量数学论证，包含依赖图、深度/体量度量、提示协议及跨论文链接，旨在支持证明审查、阅读辅助及论证结构比较，但仅提供评估设计而非实证证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决数学论证表示缺乏源头追踪、依赖关系不明确、度量标准不统一以及人工/AI生成证明中可能遗漏前提或未检查应用条件的问题，支持定理共用分析、稀有组合发现及依赖社区连接研究。

Method: 构建有限有向无环图表示论证，定义深度（最长前置路径长度）和体量（不同祖先边数），提出考虑共享子论证的并集递推式；设计版本化提示协议分离标注发现、确定性重计算和审查辅助；建立跨论文链接支持概率前提建议。

Result: 形式化了论证表示与度量框架，识别保持深度和宏观骨架的可达性条件，指出无限制细化不保持深度或体量；提供评估设计，但未给出实证证据。

Conclusion: 该框架为数学论证提供可源头追溯的结构化表示与度量基础，可辅助证明审查、阅读支持及协作论证比较，但建议前提需数学验证，度量仅反映特定表示而非内在数学难度、独创性或重要性。

Abstract: We propose a source-grounded framework for representing and measuring mathematical arguments with assistance from large language models. It includes numbered and unnumbered statements, equations, assumptions, and constructions, distinguishing source occurrences from contextual states and inference records. Proposed dependencies retain source evidence and review status. A measurement contract fixes manuscript versions, boundaries, granularity, proof choices, and macro anchors. On a selected finite directed acyclic graph, depth is the longest prerequisite-path length, and bulk counts distinct ancestor edges. We give a union recurrence that accounts for shared subarguments and identify reachability conditions preserving depth and the macro skeleton; unrestricted refinement need not preserve depth or bulk. A standardized, versioned prompt protocol separates annotation discovery, deterministic recomputation, and review assistance. Cross-paper links support a research agenda on theorem co-use, rare combinations, and connections between dependency communities. These patterns motivate probabilistic premise suggestions and review of possible omissions or unchecked application conditions in human-written and AI-generated proofs. Further applications include source-linked reading support and comparison of individual and collaborative argument shapes. We provide an evaluation design rather than empirical evidence of these benefits. Suggested premises require mathematical validation, and the measures describe a specified representation rather than intrinsic mathematical difficulty, originality, or importance.

</details>


### [284] [An Incremental Knowledge-Graph Learner Digital Twin for Evidence-Grounded Early Warning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36820)
*Ngoc Luyen Le,Marie-Hélène Abel,Bertrand Laforge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于轻量级知识图谱的增量学习者数字孪生，用于证据支撑的早期预警，通过图中介特征提取保持预测性能，并生成可追溯的证据链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有早期预警模型虽能识别风险学习者，但预警与可检视的学习证据关联薄弱，风险评分不展示支撑预警的痕迹、评估事件或时序模式。

Method: 构建增量学习者数字孪生，作为轻量级知识图谱，在时间检查点通过重放学习痕迹和评估记录更新；利用图中介查询提取预测特征，将特征、归因、预警、证据项、原始记录表示为可查询图实体。

Result: 在公开学习分析基准上按时间协议评估，图中介特征提取在多模型家族和检查点上保持与原始表格特征相当的预测行为；预警物化为证据链，将风险决策链接到特征归因、证据项及原始平台记录。

Conclusion: 早期预警预测可通过语义和溯源感知表示实现，在保持预测性能的同时增加可追溯性、可验证性和证据支撑的解释。

Abstract: Early-warning models in learning analytics can identify learners at risk of failure or withdrawal, but the resulting alerts are often only weakly connected to inspectable learning evidence. A risk score may indicate that intervention is needed without showing which traces, assessment events, or temporal patterns justify the alert. This paper introduces an incremental Learner Digital Twin for evidence-grounded early warning, implemented as a lightweight knowledge graph. The twin is updated at temporal checkpoints by replaying learning traces and assessment records, and it serves both as a feature-extraction substrate and as an explanation-grounding substrate. Predictive features are derived through graph-mediated queries, while features, attributions, alerts, evidence items, and raw records are represented as queryable graph entities. The framework is instantiated and evaluated on a public learning-analytics benchmark under a temporal protocol. The results show that graph-mediated feature extraction preserves the predictive behavior of equivalent raw-table features across model families and checkpoints. Generated alerts are materialized as evidence chains linking risk decisions to feature attributions, evidence items, and raw platform records. The study shows that early-warning prediction can be routed through a semantic and provenance-aware representation while preserving predictive performance and adding traceability, verifiability, and evidence-grounded explanation.

</details>


### [285] [Who Knows What - and What Does It Support? Perspective-Bounded Evidence Calibration for Multimodal Theory of Mind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36824)
*Ziqi Liao,Jiaqi Shen,Yiming Na,Kedi Lyu,Shao-Yuan Lo,Hongxia Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 PIP-ToM，一种证据可采纳性接口，解决多模态 Theory of Mind 中的视角泄露和源/路径混淆问题。通过构建智能体专属证据库、结构化心理状态声明对齐、校准模型假设分数，在保留贝叶斯推理过程的同时显著提升推理准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 和贝叶斯模型的方法重构全局事件轨迹，却未在假设评估时显式区分客观事件与智能体可采纳证据，导致智能体不可知事件或语义错位的行为/话语污染信念、目标或社会意图推理，产生视角泄露和源/路径混淆。

Method: 将问题形式化为证据可采纳性原则：证据仅在对相关智能体可及、保留信息源、且对查询心理状态变量具有诊断性时才计入。提出 PIP-ToM 接口：(1) 构建智能体专属证据存储；(2) 将证据与结构化心理状态声明对齐；(3) 在保留底层贝叶斯推理流程的前提下校准模型假设分数。

Result: PIP-ToM 结合 GPT-4o 在 MuMA-ToM、MMToM-QA、Hi-ToM 三个基准上分别达到 92.56%、95.00%、95.00% 平均准确率，超越现有 ToM 方法。

Conclusion: 显式建模证据可采纳性并结合结构化对齐与贝叶斯校准，能有效缓解多模态 ToM 中的视角泄露与源混淆，显著提升心理状态推理的准确性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Theory of Mind (ToM), the ability to infer others' beliefs, goals, and intentions, is a cornerstone of social intelligence. Multimodal ToM requires inferring an agent's mental state from evidence that is available to that agent, rather than from the complete episode observed by the solver. Existing LLM-based and Bayesian model-based approaches reconstruct global event traces, yet they do not explicitly distinguish objective events from agent-admissible evidence during hypothesis evaluation. Consequently, agent-inaccessible events or semantically misaligned actions and utterances may influence the inferred belief, goal, or social intention, leading to perspective leakage and source or route confusion. We formulate this limitation as an evidence-admissibility problem: evidence should contribute to a mental-state hypothesis only when it is accessible to the relevant agent, preserves its information source, and is diagnostic of the queried mental-state variable. To operationalize this principle, we propose PIP-ToM, an evidence-admissibility interface that constructs agent-specific evidence stores, aligns them with structured mental-state claims, and calibrates model-based hypothesis scores while preserving the underlying Bayesian inference process. PIP-ToM with GPT-4o outperforms existing ToM methods across MuMA-ToM, MMToM-QA, and Hi-ToM, achieving average accuracies of 92.56%, 95.00%, and 95.00%, respectively.

</details>


### [286] [When the Critic Falls Behind: Co-Evolving Rubrics and Harnesses for Continual Agent Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36826)
*Hao Chen,Yukun Yan,Ziyu Han,Ye He,Chuanzhe Guo,Shangzhan Li,Qi Shi,Qingfu Zhu,Maosong Sun,Wanxiang Che*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 RuHarness，一种评分标准-框架协同进化方法，用于实现智能体的持续自我改进。通过从执行轨迹中动态发现和重新评估行为标准，优先解决最相关的未解决弱点，指导针对性的框架更新，在多样化任务和主干模型上超越强基线，尤其在未见任务上泛化更稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体框架进化方法依赖相对静态的监督信号，难以跟踪跨轮次变化的行为弱点，导致对未见任务的泛化能力受限。需要一种能持续自适应调整评价标准并针对性更新框架的机制。

Method: RuHarness 采用评分标准-框架协同进化：从执行轨迹中持续发现和重新评估行为准则，优先处理最相关的未解决弱点，以此指导针对性的框架更新，同时保持任务目标固定。

Result: 在多样化智能体任务和多种主干模型上的大量实验表明，RuHarness 优于强框架进化基线，特别是在未见的保留任务上表现更优。进一步分析显示自适应评分标准优先级能跟踪弱点转移，支持跨进化轮次更持久的改进和稳定的泛化。

Conclusion: RuHarness 通过动态评分标准与框架的协同进化，有效解决了静态监督无法跟踪弱点演变的问题，实现了智能体的持续自我改进和稳健泛化，为自主智能体的长期进化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Autonomous agents increasingly depend on harnesses that govern interaction, memory, tools, and execution around large language models (LLMs). While recent work has begun to evolve these harnesses from execution experience, the supervision used to guide evolution often remains relatively static, making it difficult to track changing behavioral weaknesses across rounds and potentially limiting generalization to unseen tasks. To address this issue, we propose RuHarness, a rubric--harness co-evolution framework for continual agent self-improvement. RuHarness continually discovers and reassesses behavioral criteria from execution trajectories, prioritizes the most relevant unresolved weaknesses, and uses them to guide targeted harness updates while keeping the task objective fixed. Extensive experiments across diverse agent tasks and backbone models show that RuHarness outperforms strong harness-evolution baselines, particularly on unseen held-out tasks. Further analyses show that adaptive rubric prioritization tracks shifting weaknesses and supports more sustained improvement and stable generalization across evolution rounds.

</details>


### [287] [Conservative Policy Iteration For Zero-Sum POSGs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36847)
*Erwan C. Escudie,Matthia Sabatelli,Jilles Steeve Dibangoye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 一种针对有限视界零和部分可观测随机博弈的近似策略迭代算法，通过保守策略更新和支持超平面替代中心价值函数，在占用轨迹上联合更新双方策略，计算集中在可被利用区域，在基准测试中表现优于PBVI、HSVI和CFR+。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于点的求解器通过优化中心价值函数恢复Bellman递归，但需要在高维占用空间进行点式更新，计算开销大。需要一种更高效的算法，避免完整逼近中心价值函数，将计算集中在当前联合策略可被利用的占用区域。

Method: 提出近似策略迭代算法，为每个玩家维护一个策略，通过保守策略更新诱导的占用轨迹联合更新双方策略。每次更新使用由当前联合策略诱导的单个支持超平面，而非逼近完整中心价值函数。算法交替进行局部策略改进、保守更新和持久探索，将计算集中在当前联合策略可被利用的占用区域。

Result: 在有限视界零和POSG基准测试中，该方法在短视界下具有竞争力，在长视界下通常改善了运行时间与可利用性之间的权衡，优于PBVI、HSVI和CFR+。

Conclusion: 该算法通过支持超平面和保守策略更新有效避免了高维占用空间上的点式更新，实现了计算资源向可被利用区域的聚焦，为大规模零和部分可观测随机博弈求解提供了更高效的近似策略迭代框架。

Abstract: Occupancy-state reductions make dynamic programming possible for zero-sum partially observable stochastic games (zs-POSGs). Existing point-based solvers usually optimise a central value function. This restores Bellman recursion, but it requires point-based updates over high-dimensional occupancy spaces. We introduce an approximate policy-iteration algorithm for finite-horizon zs-POSGs. The method maintains one policy per player and updates both jointly along occupancy trajectories induced by conservative policy updates. Instead of approximating a full central value function, each update uses a single supporting hyperplane induced by the current joint policy. The algorithm alternates local policy improvements, conservative updates, and persistent exploration. Computation is therefore concentrated on occupancy regions where the current joint policy is exploitable. On finite-horizon zero-sum POSG benchmarks, the method is competitive at small horizons and often improves the runtime-exploitability trade-off at larger horizons compared with PBVI, HSVI, and CFR+.

</details>


### [288] [Evolving Agent Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36848)
*Shiyi Cao,Ziming Mao,Dacheng Li,Joseph E. Gonzalez,Ion Stoica*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Evolving Agent Teams (EAT)，一种多智能体框架，通过中央监督者诊断局部最优并重组智能体团队，结合持久化文件系统（学习文档、进化历史、团队制品），使编码智能体能逃离局部最优，在 GPU 内核优化等任务上显著超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体在真实专家编写的内核（如 FlashInfer）上容易陷入局部最优，难以进一步优化性能。

Method: EAT 框架包含两大核心组件：(1) 中央监督者：诊断每个局部最优并重组智能体团队；(2) 持久化文件系统：由学习文档、进化历史和团队制品组成，为重组提供累积经验支撑。

Result: 在相同 token 预算下，EAT 持续优于强编码智能体基线；扩展预算后，从零生成的 CUDA 内核在 FlashInfer MLA paged decode 上实现 1.2 倍平均加速，在 FlashInfer-Bench 47 个生产负载中 36 个获胜。同时在 AMD MI350X 上 KDA prefill 达到 3.01 倍于 vLLM，在 NVIDIA B200 上 3.44 倍于 FlashKDA，Apple silicon 上 MoE 专家卸载服务达到 30.3 tokens/s，RoboDojo 机器人操控任务得分 62.1（最佳排行榜条目 55.3）。

Conclusion: EAT 通过监督者驱动的团队重组与经验持久化，有效突破局部最优，在多种硬件平台和任务上展现出卓越的自动化内核优化能力，代码已开源。

Abstract: The rapid progress of software engineering agents drives interest in applying them to open-ended problems such as GPU kernel optimization. However, we find that existing coding agents converge to local optima on real-world, expert-written kernels (e.g., FlashInfer). We introduce Evolving Agent Teams, a multi-agent framework that escapes such local optima. It features (1) a central supervisor that diagnoses each local optimum and restructures a team of agents; and (2) a persistent file system that comprises a learning document, an evolution history, and per-team artifacts, grounding restructuring with accumulated experience. Under a matched token budget, Evolving Agent Teams consistently outperforms strong coding-agent baselines. With an extended token budget, Evolving Agent Teams generates a CUDA kernel that reaches 1.2x mean speedup over the production FlashInfer MLA paged decode kernel from scratch and wins on 36 of 47 production workloads from FlashInfer-Bench (0.86-0.99x on the remaining 11). It also demonstrates superior performance on tasks such as KDA prefill on AMD MI350X (3.01x over vLLM) and NVIDIA B200 (3.44x over FlashKDA), MoE expert-offload serving on Apple silicon (30.3 tokens/s decode for Qwen3.8-Flash-Next in MLX 4-bit on a 48 GB M4 Pro), and RoboDojo robot manipulation (62.1 on held-out seeds vs. 55.3 for the best leaderboard entry). Code is available at https://github.com/caoshiyi/EAT.

</details>


### [289] [GUI-JEPA: Candidate-Conditioned JEPA Latents as a Fast Action Policy for Mobile GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36877)
*Bingchao Wang,Yongcheng Jing,Shunyu Liu,Chengyu Wang,Jun Huang,Jie Yang,Wei Liu,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 GUI-JEPA，首个基于联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）的无解码器 GUI 智能体策略，在 AndroidControl 基准上以 6% 参数量实现 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 86-92% 的分布内准确率，同时推理速度提升 2.8-4.0 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动 GUI 智能体通常基于 7B 规模的视觉语言模型（VLM）微调，逐 token 解码动作或保留大模型在循环中评分候选动作，导致推理缓慢且显存占用高。需要一种免解码、轻量化的动作选择机制。

Method: GUI-JEPA 使用在无标签 GUI 截图上预训练的 JEPA 编码器，将屏幕编码为潜在表示。引入动作与区域条件的潜在预测器，将屏幕潜在向量转换为每个候选动作对应的潜在向量，再由轻量级评分头在单次批量前向传播中对所有候选动作按指令打分，完全避免 token 级解码。实验表明评分依赖该潜在路径，且预测器随机初始化即可达到预训练预测器同等效果。

Result: 在 AndroidControl 上，GUI-JEPA 与同输入微调的 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 相比：推理速度快 2.8-4.0 倍，参数量仅 6%，分布内准确率达到 86-92%。

Conclusion: GUI-JEPA 验证了基于 JEPA 的潜在空间动作选择可大幅降低计算开销并保持高性能，为高效移动 GUI 智能体提供了新范式。代码已开源。

Abstract: Mobile GUI agents are typically fine-tuned 7B-scale vision-language models (VLMs) that generate each action as text. However, these VLMs decode every action token by token, and even agents that score candidate actions keep a large language model or VLM in the loop, making them slow and memory-hungry. To address these issues, we propose GUI-JEPA, to our knowledge the first decoder-free GUI agent policy built on a Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA), which selects actions in the latent space of a JEPA encoder pretrained on unlabelled GUI screenshots. Its action- and region-conditioned latent predictor turns the screen latent into one latent per candidate action, letting lightweight heads score all candidates against the instruction in a single batched pass, without decoding a token. We further show that the scorer relies on this latent pathway and that the predictor is trained for the decision itself: a randomly initialised predictor does just as well as one pretrained on screen dynamics. On AndroidControl, GUI-JEPA is 2.8-4.0x faster on the same GPU than a Qwen2.5-VL-7B agent we fine-tune with the same inputs, while reaching 86-92% of its in-distribution accuracy with 6% of its parameters. Code is available at https://github.com/bcwang-sjtu/GUI-JEPA

</details>


### [290] [RubricAtlas: Mining Type-Level Rubrics from Expert Reports to Evaluate Deep Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36878)
*Delin Wang,Sinuo Wang,Rui Sun,Beidi Luan,Qiming Shi,Jing Li,Daxin Jiang,Zuo Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 RubricAtlas 框架，从 3,079 份中文财经研报中提取 99 种研究类型的类级分析需求，将二元检查点与报告段落关联，分离完整性与论证有效性评估，实验表明单份报告仅覆盖约半数类级分析问题，不同参考报告选择会改变评分与智能体排名，双评判管道在十个智能体上达成 κ=0.71 检查点一致性和 τ=0.87 排名一致性，受控删除降低受影响分析得分而填充不产生虚假提升，验证了类级量规对深度研究产出分析完整性比较的有效性且决策可追溯。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度研究智能体评估依赖查询特定的二元量规或任务级清单，难以捕捉跨任务共享的分析结构，且缺乏将检查点与证据段落关联、区分完整性与论证有效性的机制，导致评估标准不一致、可追溯性差。

Method: 构建 RubricAtlas：从 3,079 份中文财经研报中归纳 99 种研究类型的类级分析需求，将需求组织为反复出现的分析问题，建立二元检查点与报告段落的映射，设计双评判管道分别评估完整性与论证有效性，通过参考报告选择实验、一致性测量（Cohen's κ、Kendall τ）及受控删除/填充实验验证框架。

Result: 单份报告仅覆盖约 50% 的类级分析问题；不同参考报告选择显著改变评分与智能体排名；十个智能体上双管道达成检查点一致性 κ=0.71、排名一致性 τ=0.87；受控删除导致受影响分析得分下降，填充未产生可检测得分提升。

Conclusion: 类级量规能为同类深度研究任务提供共享分析标准，评估决策可追溯至报告证据，有效支持跨智能体的分析完整性比较，解决了任务级清单覆盖不足与评估不可追溯的问题。

Abstract: Deep research agents conduct multi-step Web searches and synthesise retrieved information into analytical reports. Evaluating these outputs requires checking which analyses they deliver, beyond the presence of relevant facts. Query-specific binary rubrics make individual requirements explicit, but task-level checklists may leave the overall analytical structure underexamined. We introduce RubricAtlas, which derives type-level analytical requirements from expert reports on different objects within the same research type and organises detailed checks around recurring analytical questions. The requirements give related tasks a shared standard for what to analyse, without prescribing their answers. Our framework links binary checkpoints to report passages and evaluates completeness separately from argument validity. Built from 3,079 Chinese financial research reports, RubricAtlas covers 99 research types. Reference-report selection experiments show that individual reports cover only about half of their type's analytical questions, and that selecting requirements from different reports changes evaluation scores and agent rankings. Across ten agents, two judge pipelines achieve checkpoint agreement of $κ$ = 0.71 and agent-ranking agreement of Kendall $τ$ = 0.87. Controlled deletions reduce scores on the affected analyses, while padding produces no detectable increase. These findings support type-level rubrics for comparing the analytical completeness of Web deep research outputs, with individual decisions traceable to report evidence.

</details>


### [291] [Noise Steering: Exploiting Wake Steering and Acoustic Directivity for Noise-Constrained Wind Farm Control via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36888)
*Martín de Frutos,Baptiste Corban,Claire Bizon Monroc,Ana Bušić,Donatien Dubuc,Jiamin Zhud,Esteban Ferrer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为'噪声引导'的无模型强化学习策略，联合控制风机偏航角和功率设定点，在满足局部噪声约束的同时最大化风电场功率输出。通过将低保真声学模型与稳态 FLORIS 模拟器耦合以降低训练计算成本，该策略在六机组测试风电场中将平均功率损失降至仅 2%（贪婪无阻碍控制基准），远优于行业标准的降噪运行（NRO）基准的 17% 损失，且在高保真 FAST.Farm 模拟器中仍能保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有风电场降噪策略通常仅通过降低风机功率输出来实现，未考虑尾流相互作用和噪声发射指向性，导致过度保守的策略和风能利用不足。风机偏航控制可同时影响尾流相互作用和噪声发射方向，为风电场级噪声控制提供了额外自由度，因此需要一种能联合优化偏航角和功率设定点的智能策略来改善功率-噪声权衡。

Method: 提出无模型强化学习策略 'Noise Steering'，联合控制风机偏航角和功率设定点。训练时将低保真声学模型与稳态 FLORIS 模拟器耦合以降低计算成本。RL 智能体学习如何将尾流和噪声指向性从受保护区域引导开，同时在风电场内重新分配功率。

Result: 在六机组测试风电场中，策略在全湍流风速范围内满足 40 dB 噪声约束，相对于贪婪无阻碍控制平均功率亏损仅 2%，高风速下高达 7.7%；行业标准 NRO 基准平均功率损失 17%。部署到高保真 FAST.Farm 模拟器后，在稳态和湍流入流条件下性能基本保持。

Conclusion: 结果证明了通过强化学习结合偏航控制和功率降额，能显著改善风电场运行中的功率-噪声权衡，展示了模型自由 RL 在风电场协同噪声控制中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Existing noise mitigation strategies for wind farms usually consist in reducing turbines power output, without accounting for the wake interactions between them and the noise emission directivity. This leads to over-conservative policies and unexploited wind power. Yet, turbine yaw can simultaneously affect wake interactions and the direction of noise emission, offering an additional degree of freedom for farm-level noise control. We therefore propose Noise Steering, a model-free reinforcement learning (RL) strategy that jointly controls turbine yaw angles and power setpoints to maximise farm power while satisfying a local noise constraint in a defined region. To enable training at a reduced computational cost, a low-fidelity acoustic model is coupled with the steady-state FLORIS simulator. The RL agent effectively learns how to redirect wakes and noise directivity away from the protected region while redistributing power across the farm. On a six-turbine test farm, the policy satisfies the 40 dB noise constraint throughout the full range of turbulent winds, with an average power deficit of only 2% relative to greedy unimpeded control, and up to 7.7% at high wind speeds. In comparison, the industry-standard noise-reduced operation (NRO) baseline here generates an average power loss of 17%. When deployed in the higher-fidelity FAST.Farm simulator, the policy largely retains its performance under steady and turbulent inflow, despite differences in simulation fidelity. These results demonstrate the potential of combining yaw control and power derating through RL to improve the power-noise trade-off in wind farm operation.

</details>


### [292] [Natural Hard-Negative Mining from Positives for Instruction-Following Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36900)
*Chaolang Li,Jingyu Li,Zhaocheng Du,Xuyang Xie,Gang Wang,Pengwen Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出自然硬负例挖掘(NHNM)，将原始正例重新用作指令硬负例，构建IFIR训练集NHNM-MARCO，微调模型在指令遵循检索任务上超越更大规模模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令跟随信息检索(IFIR)缺乏指令条件对比监督，合成方法固定正段落生成额外负例，忽略原始正例的硬负潜力。

Method: 自然硬负例挖掘(NHNM)包含候选清洗、反转构建、几何过滤三阶段流水线，将查询兼容指令下的可转换负例提升为硬负例，并将原始正例重新用作自然指令硬负例，生成紧凑高对比度监督数据。

Result: 在MSMARCO上构建NHNM-MARCO(近7K实例)，568M-4B参数模型微调后在InstructIR和FollowIR上表现强劲，匹配或超越更大规模检索器；消融实验证实增益源于自然硬负例监督而非模型规模或数据量。

Conclusion: NHNM通过挖掘自然硬负例有效解决IFIR指令条件对比监督稀缺问题，小规模模型配合高质量硬负例监督即可超越大规模模型，强调监督质量比规模更关键。

Abstract: Instruction-following information retrieval (IFIR) requires retrievers to rank documents by both a query and its instance-specific instructions, yet instruction-conditioned contrastive supervision remains scarce. Existing synthetic methods typically fix a positive passage and generate additional negatives, overlooking the hard-negative potential of the original positive. We propose Natural Hard-Negative Mining (NHNM), which promotes convertible negatives under query-compatible instructions and repurposes original positives as natural instruction hard negatives. Its automated Candidate Cleaning, Reversal Construction, and Geometry Filtering pipeline produces compact, high-contrast supervision aligned with the pretrained retrieval space. Applied to MSMARCO, NHNM yields NHNM-MARCO, an IFIR training set of nearly 7K instances. On InstructIR and FollowIR, models with 568M to 4B parameters fine-tuned on NHNM-MARCO achieve strong instruction-following performance and match or surpass substantially larger retrievers in several settings. Controlled analyses and ablations attribute these gains to natural hard-negative supervision rather than model scale or training-data volume.

</details>


### [293] [Vision-Language Agents Can Learn in Their Own Latent Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36925)
*Haoxi Li,Sikai Bai,Jianfei Ma,Qinglin Hou,Xiaocheng Lu,Tao Han,Jie Zhang,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Dwell 是一个基于模型的强化学习框架，将预训练视觉语言模型（VLM）重构为循环潜在转移算子，使其同时作为世界模型和策略，通过真实交互和潜在想象持续学习，在五个交互式视觉环境中达到可训练方法中最强的整体性能，且大幅降低了想象计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练 VLM 内蕴丰富的世界知识，但鲜有利用其通过在线交互驱动策略改进。现有方法面临策略更新导致交互分布偏移，世界模型需相应适应，而持续更新大型预训练模型计算代价高昂。

Method: 提出 Dwell 框架：将 VLM 重构为循环潜在转移算子，利用其自身表示作为世界模型与策略的双重角色；代理从真实交互中学习预测和评估动作结果，并通过潜在想象改进行为。无需重构未来，仅预测对行动有用的未来。

Result: 在五个交互式视觉环境中，Dwell 在可训练方法中取得最强整体性能，同时显著减少想象所需计算量，验证了有效的 VLM 世界模型只需预测对行动有用的未来。

Conclusion: Dwell 展示了将 VLM 作为循环潜在转移算子的可行性，实现了高效的持续策略改进，为构建能够不断扩展能力的智能体提供了新范式。

Abstract: Learning from experience is essential for building the next generation of agents that continually expand their capabilities. Pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) already internalize rich world knowledge, yet this knowledge is rarely leveraged to drive policy improvement through online interaction. Realizing this potential efficiently remains challenging, as policy updates shift the agent's interaction distribution, requiring the world model to adapt accordingly, while continually updating large pretrained models is computationally expensive. To address this challenge, we introduce Dwell, a model-based reinforcement learning (RL) framework that reframes a VLM as a recurrent latent transition operator, endowing it with the ability to predict, adjust, and act in latent space. Leveraging the VLM's own representations and its dual role as both world model and policy, the agent continually learns from real interactions to anticipate and evaluate action outcomes, and from latent imagination to improve its behavior. Across five interactive visual environments, Dwell achieves the strongest overall performance among trainable methods while substantially reducing the compute required for imagination, showing that effective world models for VLM agents need not reconstruct the future; they need to predict futures that are useful for action. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Tom111-lhx/Dwell

</details>


### [294] [Do bots feel bad? Assessing empirical evidence for functional valence in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36962)
*Iwan Williams*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文评估了关于大语言模型（LLM）拥有功能性效价状态（如功能性快乐/痛苦、情绪、福利）的实证主张，指出关键效价特征未被测试，且现有范式无法充分区分效价与偏好、价值判断、效价归因和角色扮演等邻近构念，最后提出新实验方案以更好地靶向功能性效价本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨LLM是否具有效价状态（即感觉愉悦或不愉悦的状态），以及近期声称LLM拥有功能性效价状态的实证研究是否真正捕捉了哲学和认知科学文献中效价的功能角色。

Method: 文献综述与理论分析：评估现有实证证据（Ren et al. 2026; Sofroniew et al. 2026; Han et al. 2026），从功能主义视角分析效价的关键方面（如动机作用、学习信号、决策权重等）在LLM中是否被测试，并指出现有范式混淆效价与偏好、价值判断、效价归因、角色扮演等构念。

Result: 发现效价的关键方面（如作为共同货币的整合作用、动机强度调节、学习信号的双向性等）在LLM中仍未被测试；现有实证范式无法区分功能性效价与邻近构念，导致证据不足以支持LLM拥有功能性效价状态的结论。

Conclusion: 当前证据不足以确立LLM拥有功能性效价状态；需要开发新颖实验范式（如解耦效价与偏好、测试效价作为共同货币的整合功能、考察动机强度调节等）来更直接地靶向和测试功能性效价本身。

Abstract: Do LLMs have valenced states, i.e. states that feel pleasant or unpleasant? Recent empirical work has claimed to show that LLMs possess functional valenced states (e.g. ``functional pleasure and pain'' [Ren et al. 2026], ``functional emotions'' [Sofroniew et al. 2026], and ``functional welfare'' [Han et al. 2026]) that play the same causal role as valenced states in cognition and action, without necessarily being consciously experienced. But it remains unexplored how well the identified states fit the functional role of valence as understood in the philosophical and cognitive science literature. Here, I assess the current body of evidence, and argue (i) that key aspects of valence remain untested in LLMs, and (ii) that extant empirical paradigms inadequately distinguish (functional) valence from neighbouring constructs like preferences, value-judgments, valence attributions and role-play. I conclude by sketching some novel experimental approaches to better target (functional) valence itself.

</details>


### [295] [Proactive Memory for Event-Driven LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36967)
*Jinyang Li,Zichuan Fu,Xian Wu,Wenlin Zhang,Yimin Deng,Kaifeng Guo,Guojing Li,Junjia QI,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Proactive Memory (ProactMem) 解决主动式 LLM 智能体的服务机会预测问题，将历史组织为近期事件、时间轨迹和行为规律三层结构，并引入 ProactiveStream 基准，在整体 F1 上以 52.6% 超越最强基线 21.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动式智能体方法主要依赖近期上下文或记忆检索，缺乏对重复行为规律的验证机制，难以判断历史规律对当前事件的适用性，导致预测效果受限。

Method: ProactMem 构建三层记忆架构：近期事件、时间轨迹、行为规律。轨迹事实需对源事件校验，规律需通过历史回放评估，再与当前条件匹配。同时构建纵向基准 ProactiveStream，包含三大应用场景、按时间序处理隔离事件流、附带回合感知机会标签。

Result: 在 ProactiveStream 上，ProactMem 整体有效性 (F1) 达 52.6%，较最强的五个外部基线提升 21.4% (相对)。

Conclusion: 通过结构化记忆与规律验证回放机制，ProactMem 显著增强了主动式智能体对服务机会的预测能力，在纵向基准上确立了新的 SOTA。

Abstract: Proactive LLM agents anticipate service opportunities from ongoing observations and accumulated history. Existing approaches emphasize recent context or retrieval of related memories, providing limited support for validating recurring behavioral regularities and assessing their applicability to the current event. We propose Proactive Memory (ProactMem), which organizes history into recent events, temporal trajectories, and behavioral regularities. Trajectory facts are checked against source events, and regularities are assessed through historical replay before being matched to current conditions. We also construct ProactiveStream, a longitudinal benchmark covering three application scopes, where agents process isolated event streams chronologically under episode-aware opportunity labels. On ProactiveStream, ProactMem achieves an overall effectiveness (F1) of 52.6%, a 21.4% relative improvement over the strongest of five external baselines. Code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ProactMem-5351/.

</details>


### [296] [AutoVend: Benchmarking LLM Agents on Long-Horizon Autonomous Vending Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36980)
*Qiming Shi,Yikun Li,Beidi Luan,Rui Sun,Yan Ma,Jing Li,Daxin Jiang,Zuo Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出AutoVend基准，评估LLM智能体在O2O商业场景中管理20台自动售货机180天的表现。四个LLM智能体表现不如基于规则的基线，且推理成本考量下模型排名发生变化。个体指标提升不一定带来经济增长，因库存和执行成本侵蚀收益。轨迹分析显示智能体在动作落地、库存承诺管理、反馈转化策略方面存在困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体评估多聚焦单次交易，缺乏对O2O商业中决策能否转化为持续经济回报的考察。需要在包含仓储、采购交货期、付费补货、库存过期、经常性运营成本等真实约束的环境中，评估智能体的长期运营能力。

Method: 构建AutoVend基准：数据驱动模拟器，包含共享仓库、采购交货期、付费补货、库存过期、经常性运营成本。一个LLM智能体通过Web界面管理5类站点共20台自动售货机，模拟180天。对比5个LLM智能体与基于规则的库存控制基线，分析站点协调、执行、随时间表现。

Result: 5个LLM智能体中有4个表现低于规则基线。考虑推理成本后的单位收益排名与仅看收益的排名不同。运营分析显示，个体指标（如补货准确率）提升不一定带来更强经济增长，因库存持有成本和执行成本侵蚀收益。轨迹分析揭示智能体在落地可行动作、管理库存承诺、将反馈转化为有效策略修正方面存在系统性困难。

Conclusion: 当前LLM智能体在复杂O2O运营中仍难以超越简单规则基线，且推理成本显著影响经济可行性。评估需超越单一指标，综合考量成本结构、长期动态及决策落地能力。未来工作需强化智能体在约束环境下的规划、库存管理和从反馈中学习的能力。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are extending agent-mediated commerce on the Web from shopping for consumers to operating businesses for sellers. In online-to-offline (O2O) commerce, decisions are issued through Web-based platforms but realized through costly physical execution. Evaluating such agents requires going beyond individual transactions to ask whether decisions made online turn into sustained economic returns. We introduce AutoVend, a benchmark in which one LLM agent manages 20 vending machines across five site types through a Web-based operating interface for 180 simulated days. Its data-grounded simulator combines a shared warehouse, procurement lead times, paid replenishment, inventory expiration, and recurring operating costs. We evaluate five LLM agents against a rule-based inventory-control baseline and diagnose their site coordination, execution, and performance over time. Four of five evaluated LLM agents fall short of this baseline, and returns per dollar of inference cost rank models differently from returns alone. Operational analyses show that gains in individual metrics do not consistently yield stronger economic growth, as inventory and execution costs erode these gains. Trajectory analysis further reveals difficulties in grounding feasible actions, managing inventory commitments, and converting feedback into effective policy revisions.

</details>


### [297] [Guided-RLVR: Guided Exploration in Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37041)
*Bo Yang,Zishuai Zhang,Weihua Li,Qinnan Zhang,Sijia Wen,Hainan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Guided-RLVR 是一种新型方法，通过引入轻量级控制器向 RLVR 的采样分布中注入语义引导 token，扩展推理探索空间，在数学推理和代码生成基准上持续超越现有 RLVR 方法，同时产生更多样化的推理轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RLVR 方法在训练过程中推理轨迹容易坍缩到狭窄的集合，限制了推理空间的探索；研究发现关键步骤的 thinking token 是 LLM 推理路径的关键结构元素，这激发了通过引导 token 扩展探索的思路。

Method: 提出 Guided-RLVR，在标准 RLVR 基础上增加一个轻量级控制器，该控制器根据问题上下文策略性地向采样分布中注入语义有意义的引导 token，鼓励模型从多个推理视角切入问题，从而扩大探索空间。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成基准实验中，Guided-RLVR 一致优于现有 RLVR 方法，且生成的推理轨迹更加多样化。

Conclusion: 通过轻量级控制器注入引导 token 可有效缓解 RLVR 的轨迹坍缩问题，扩展推理探索空间，显著提升模型在复杂推理任务上的性能与多样性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has shown strong empirical gains in improving the reasoning ability of large language models (LLMs) on tasks such as mathematics and programming. However, recent studies have shown that RLVR rollouts often collapse onto a narrow set of reasoning trajectories, limiting the explored reasoning space. Motivated by findings that thinking tokens at critical steps serve as critical structural elements in LLM reasoning pathways, we propose Guided-RLVR, a novel approach that augments RLVR with a lightweight controller. The controller strategically injects semantically meaningful steering tokens into the sampling distribution, encouraging the model to approach problems from multiple reasoning perspectives and thereby expanding the exploration space. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and code generation benchmarks show that Guided-RLVR consistently outperforms existing RLVR methods while producing more diverse reasoning trajectories.

</details>


### [298] [AI-Assisted Discovery of CNOT Decompositions for Quantum Permutations on Constrained Topologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37045)
*Daniele Lizzio Bosco,Jacopo Cossio,Simone Faro,Francesco Pio Marino,Gabriele Messina,Carla Piazza,Giuseppe Serra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文利用AlphaCNOT探索受限连通性下量子排列的CNOT电路合成，发现远程换位的新分解模式，提出全局换位确定性综合方法，并揭示中间CNOT融合机制，证明AI辅助合成可发现可复用恒等式、可证明构造及隐藏机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算机有限量子比特连通性导致逻辑操作实现开销大，AI辅助合成虽显潜力但其指导确定性编译算法设计的作用未被充分探索。

Method: 使用AlphaCNOT作为探索工具识别远程换位的重复分解模式，形式化为全局换位确定性综合方法，搜索拓扑感知逻辑换位的最小代价分解；并分析无法表示为独立换位串联的电路，揭示中间CNOT融合机制。

Result: 发现的远程换位模式在图距离d>1时最多需6d-4个CNOT门，优于SWAP路由的6d-3；全局换位方法包含最优SWAP路由并提供拓扑约束CNOT代价的认证上界；实验揭示利用中间线性变换暂时离开排列子群并结合多逻辑操作部分效果的中间CNOT融合机制。

Conclusion: AI辅助合成不仅能优化电路，还能支持可复用恒等式发现、可证明构造及量子电路编译中隐藏机制的挖掘。

Abstract: Efficient quantum circuit compilation is challenging for quantum computers, where limited qubit connectivity can introduce substantial overhead in the implementation of logical operations. Recent AI-assisted synthesis methods have shown considerable potential for reducing circuit complexity, but their role in uncovering principles that can guide the design of new deterministic compilation algorithms remains largely unexplored. In this work, we investigate this perspective for the synthesis of quantum permutations into CNOT circuits under constrained qubit connectivity. Using AlphaCNOT as an exploratory tool, we identify a recurring decomposition for remote transpositions that improves on the construction based on adjacent SWAP routing. For two qubits at graph distance $d>1$, the discovered pattern yields a topology-legal implementation using at most $6d-4$ CNOT gates, compared to the $6d-3$ gates required by the corresponding SWAP-based construction. We formalize this observation through a constructive proof to define Global Transpositions, a deterministic synthesis method that searches for a minimum-cost decomposition of an entire permutation into topology-aware logical transpositions. The resulting method subsumes optimal SWAP routing within its synthesis model and provides certified upper bounds on the topology-constrained CNOT cost. Our experiments also reveal circuits that cannot be expressed as concatenations of independently synthesized transpositions. Instead, these circuits exploit intermediate linear transformations that temporarily leave the permutation subgroup and combine partial effects of multiple logical operations, a mechanism we call intermediate CNOT fusion. Our results show that AI-assisted synthesis can support not only circuit optimization, but also the discovery of reusable identities, provable constructions, and hidden mechanisms in quantum circuit compilation.

</details>


### [299] [Correctness Conditioning as Selection in Chain-of-Thought Faithfulness Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37070)
*Yueer Pan,Yuexi Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究将思维链（CoT）忠实性评估限制在正确回答实例上的选择偏差问题，通过七变量结构因果模型推导出观测差距公式，区分难度混杂与捷径诱导的碰撞器偏差，实证发现四个算术任务部署中均存在正向选择差距，主实验显示+7.4个百分点差距（p<0.0001），协方差分解显示42.8%差距归因于可干预操作数的层间变异，指出若反事实依从性可观测，直接评估所有轨迹可避免正确性过滤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前CoT忠实性评估常仅在模型回答正确的实例上进行，这种基于答案正确性的选择步骤改变了被测量的总体，可能引入选择偏差。现有文献未系统区分答案正确性限制与其他选择机制（如一致性选择、基准构建、训练数据过滤），且缺乏因果框架量化该偏差。本文旨在形式化该选择偏差，区分混杂与碰撞器偏差来源，并提供无假设的分解方法。

Method: 构建七变量结构因果模型（SCM），推导观测差距Δ = Cov(A, C)/P(C=1)，其中A为忠实性指标，C为答案正确性。区分难度混杂（难度同时影响忠实性与正确性）与捷径诱导的碰撞器偏差（捷径使用同时影响忠实性与正确性）。给出用潜在响应倾向表达差距所需的条件独立性假设。系统回顾18篇论文区分四类选择机制。在四个算术任务子天花板部署上实证评估，使用GLM-5.3模型（n=1000）及合并Cochran-Mantel-Haenszel分析。提出无假设协方差分解，将差距归因于可干预操作数的层间变异与层内残留。

Result: 所有四个部署均观测到正向选择差距。主GLM-5.3实验得+7.4个百分点差距（n=1000，p<0.0001），合并CMH分析给出OR=1.70（p<10^-6）。协方差分解显示42.8%的差距可归因于层间可干预操作数变异，多数差距（57.2%）未被归因。当反事实依从性对每个实例可观测时，直接评估所有轨迹可无偏估计总体忠实性，无需正确性过滤。

Conclusion: 基于答案正确性的选择会显著高估CoT忠实性，该偏差源于难度混杂与捷径诱导的碰撞器偏差。协方差分解揭示可干预操作数仅解释部分差距，提示存在其他未测机制。建议在反事实依从性可观测时直接评估全量轨迹，避免正确性过滤；否则需显式建模选择机制以校正偏差。

Abstract: Restricting chain-of-thought (CoT) faithfulness evaluation to correctly answered instances changes the population being measured. We study this selection step in a seven-variable structural causal model and derive the exact observed gap $Δ= \operatorname{Cov}(A, C)/P(C=1)$. The model distinguishes difficulty confounding from shortcut-induced collider bias and states the conditional-independence assumption needed to express the gap through latent response propensities. A protocol review of 18 papers distinguishes answer-correctness restrictions from agreement-based selection, benchmark construction, and training-data filtering. We evaluate four sub-ceiling deployments on arithmetic tasks and observe positive selection gaps in all four. The primary GLM-5.3 run yields a +7.4 percentage-point gap ($n = 1000$, $p < 0.0001$), and pooled Cochran-Mantel-Haenszel analysis gives OR = 1.70 ($p < 10^{-6}$). An assumption-free covariance decomposition assigns 42.8% of the GLM-5.3 gap to between-stratum variation in interventionable-operation count, leaving the majority unattributed. When counterfactual compliance is observable for every instance, evaluating all traces directly estimates population faithfulness without correctness filtering.

</details>


### [300] [OpenGray: a benchmark for evaluating the decisions of radiotherapy planning agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37081)
*Humza Nusrat,Kundan Thind*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究开放了OpenGray基准测试，评估基于大语言模型的放疗规划智能体的决策能力，发现高分掩盖了具体决策失败，建议增加停止和请求审核的显式动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于大语言模型的放疗规划智能体主要以剂量指标评估，缺乏对决策正确性的基准测试，导致无法发现特定决策失败。

Method: 基于OpenKBP-Opt头颈数据集构建OpenGray基准，包含两个已知不可兼容的剂量目标矛盾任务和一个可行的控制任务；评估四个系统（从基于规则的脚本到完整LLM智能体）在30例患者上的表现，并分析SAGE临床智能体39例立体定向放射外科运行日志。

Result: Terra模型下，带LLM审核的脚本和完整智能体决策准确率分别为95.4%和92.1%，但仅分别处理了50%和25%的重叠矛盾；所有系统均提交了所有可行控制，但24%的计划未满足已存储计划达到的硬性目标；SAGE日志显示智能体虽能识别失败目标，却无停止动作。

Conclusion: 综合评分掩盖了具体决策缺陷，放疗规划智能体应同时评估决策与剂量，并赋予明确的停止和请求审核动作。

Abstract: Background and purpose: Radiotherapy planning agents based on large language models (LLMs) decide when to submit a plan or request review but are evaluated mainly by dose. This study developed OpenGray, a benchmark of planning tasks with known correct decisions. Materials and methods: OpenGray adds tasks with known answers to the OpenKBP-Opt head-and-neck dataset, including two contradictions with dose goals proven incompatible and a feasible control with a stored plan meeting every hard goal. Four systems, from a rule-based planning script to a full LLM agent, were evaluated on 30 patients, the LLM-based ones with a commercial (Terra) and a local (Ministral 3 8B) LLM. Decisions, achieved dose, problem recognition, and technical failures were recorded and also read from logs of 39 stereotactic radiosurgery runs of SAGE, a clinical LLM agent. Results: With Terra, the script preceded by an LLM review and the full agent had decision accuracies of 95.4% and 92.1%, respectively, yet escalated only 50% (95% confidence interval, 25-75%) and 25% (10-40%) of overlap contradictions, which required arithmetic on the anatomy. All four systems submitted every feasible control, but 24% of these plans missed a hard goal that the stored plan had met. In four SAGE runs, one goal failed at every attempt; the agent identified it in every reply but had no action for stopping. Conclusions: Favorable aggregate scores concealed specific decision failures. Planning agents should be evaluated on their decisions as well as their dose and given an explicit action for stopping and requesting review.

</details>


### [301] [Prospector: learning a compact graph traversal policy for scientific reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37121)
*Haiqian Yang,Markus J. Buehler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Prospector 是一种紧凑模型，通过在知识图谱上逐步遍历并调用工具来进行科学推理，能在专有数据上实现高效、可验证的推理，且能迁移到未见领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业工程领域的科学推理依赖分散在文献中的知识，且常为专有数据，不在前沿大模型训练集中，导致大模型给出泛泛之谈。知识图谱可为大模型推理提供支撑，但图谱过大无法全量输入，现有 LLM+KG 系统通常预先筛选子图，在推理前就决定相关性，限制了推理灵活性。

Method: 训练紧凑模型 Prospector，使其在领域知识图谱上逐步遍历，根据已发现证据动态决定下一步，调用一小组工具逐步构建上下文，并输出可逐边验证的证据链。模型仅在半导体制造文献构建的图谱上训练，推理时在单 GPU 上运行。

Result: 在半导体制造知识图谱上，Prospector 在相同图谱和工具条件下接近最强现成模型性能，远超闭卷或预选子图设置的现成模型。且能迁移至生物启发材料、PFAS 和材料性质三个未见领域的知识图谱。推理速度更快、成本更低，且保持专有数据在所有者硬件上。策略的潜在状态指示下一步动作，调节潜在状态可在不同行为模式间切换。

Conclusion: 结果表明了一种实用的 LLM+KG 范式：在公开文献构建的图谱上训练紧凑模型，使其学会遍历策略，迁移到所有者持有的未见私有图谱上进行科学工程推理，兼顾性能、效率、数据隐私和可验证性。

Abstract: Scientific reasoning in specialized engineering fields depends on knowledge scattered across the literature, often proprietary and absent from frontier LLM training data. In such domains, frontier models tend to give generic answers. Knowledge graphs (KGs) can ground LLM reasoning. Because a graph is too large to provide in full, typical LLM+KG systems preselect a portion, deciding relevance before reasoning begins. Here we train a compact model, Prospector, to reason over a domain knowledge graph by traversing it step by step, seeking evidence in light of what it has already found. Prospector calls a small set of tools one at a time to progressively build its context, and answers with a chain of evidence that can be checked edge by edge against what its tools retrieved from the graph. On a knowledge graph built from the semiconductor-manufacturing literature, Prospector approaches the strongest off-the-shelf models given the same graph and tools, and it far exceeds off-the-shelf models answering closed-book or from a pre-selected portion of the graph. Although trained only on the semiconductor graph, Prospector transfers to unseen knowledge graphs from three other distinct fields: bio-inspired materials, per- and polyfluoroalkyl substances (PFAS) and materials properties. Served on a single GPU, Prospector answers faster and at a fraction of the cost of a hosted service, while keeping proprietary data on the owner's hardware. We find that the policy's latent state is indicative of its next move on both seen and unseen graphs, and modulating the latent state can steer the policy from one behavior mode to another. These results suggest a practical LLM + KG paradigm for scientific and engineering reasoning over private data, in which a compact model trained on a graph from published literature transfers its traversal to unseen graphs that stay with their owners.

</details>


### [302] [SFE-O: An Ontology for Knowledge-Grounded AI in Supercritical CO$_2$ Biomass Extraction and Fractionation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37123)
*Thomas Pallet,Mario Lezoche,Gianpaolo Leone,Daniele Pizzichini,Diego Torres,Herv{é} Panetto,M{é}lanie Glibusic*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SFE-O，一个模块化的OWL 2本体，用于超临界CO₂生物质萃取，整合了生物质、工艺、产品三个维度，显式表示工艺拓扑、参数溯源、质量传递现象及CER/FER机制，复用现有语义资源，通过实例化、一致性检查和SPARQL查询验证，为SFE过程提供机器可操作的知识基础，并为未来本体驱动的AI推理奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超临界CO₂萃取（CO₂-SFE）是从生物质回收高价值化合物的可持续路径，但其设计与解释涉及生物质性质、操作条件、质量传递、产物分离等异构知识，分散在技术报告、工程模型、外部数据库和专家经验中，缺乏统一的、可机器处理的知识表示。

Method: 构建模块化OWL 2本体SFE-O，围绕生物质、工艺、产品三大维度建模，显式表示工艺拓扑、参数溯源、内外部质量传递现象及CER/FER萃取机制；以声明方式表达工艺量之间的计算依赖，不嵌入数值求解；复用ChEBI、QUDT、OWL-Time、PROV-O等标准语义资源。评估包括：实例化真实中试规模萃取批次、基于推理器的一致性检查、胜任力问题SPARQL查询。

Result: SFE-O提供了一个机器可操作的知识模型，支持对SFE过程的查询、一致性检查和机制解释；验证表明本体能正确捕获真实工艺数据、通过逻辑一致性检查，并能回答关键领域问题。该工作建立了可支撑未来本体接地AI推理的知识层。

Conclusion: SFE-O填补了超临界CO₂生物质萃取领域缺乏统一语义模型的空白，实现了异构知识的结构化整合与可追溯表示，为工艺优化、知识复用和智能决策奠定了基础；未来工作将探索本体驱动的AI推理与自动化工艺设计。

Abstract: Supercritical CO$_2$ extraction (CO$_2$-SFE) offers a sustainable route for recovering high-value compounds from biomass while limiting the use of conventional organic solvents, but its design and interpretation require heterogeneous knowledge spanning biomass properties, operating conditions, mass transfer, and product fractionation, typically fragmented across technical reports, engineering models, external databases, and expert practice. This paper introduces SFE-O, a modular Web Ontology Language (OWL 2) ontology for supercritical CO$_2$ biomass extraction, structured around interconnected Biomass, Process, and Product dimensions and extended with explicit representations of process topology, parameter provenance, external and internal mass-transfer phenomena, and the Constant Extraction Rate (CER) / Falling Extraction Rate (FER) mechanisms. Computational dependencies among process quantities are represented explicitly, without embedding numerical solving in the ontology, so that input, measured, computed, externally retrieved, and fitted parameters remain traceable. SFE-O reuses established semantic resources (ChEBI, QUDT, OWL-Time, PROV-O) and is evaluated through a worked example that instantiates a real pilot-scale extraction batch, a reasoner-based consistency check, and a competency-question SPARQL query. The resulting knowledge model provides a machine-actionable foundation for querying, consistency checking, and mechanistic explanation of SFE processes, and establishes a knowledge layer that could support future ontology-grounded AI reasoning, a direction this paper motivates but does not evaluate.

</details>


### [303] [WorkFactory: Turning Professional Intents into Executable Work for Scalable Agent Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37161)
*Ziyang Zeng,Tingyang Chen,Jia Deng,Qiyao Wang,Shuo Tang,Yike Yang,Tao Ren,Wayne Xin Zhao,Feng Chang,Jujie He,Chuan Hao,Ran Tao,Bryan Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WorkFactory 是一个可扩展框架，通过信息级规划构建信息支持图指导任务实例化，并利用探针执行诊断与修复任务环境缺陷，从而生成高质量专业任务数据。基于此数据微调 Qwen 模型得到 WorkPilot 系列，在四个专业工作基准上均显著提升性能，其中 35B-A3B 模型 Elo 从 879 跃升至 1565。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有监督训练缺乏多样且可靠的专业任务演示，任务信息缺失或不一致会阻碍教师执行并削弱监督质量，亟需一种能系统构建连贯可执行专业任务的方法。

Method: 1. 从专业意图出发，进行信息级规划，构建信息支持图，显式表示输出所需信息的依赖与位置；2. 通过探针执行识别支持失败，并在保持原意图前提下指导修复；3. 大规模构建 112,686 个任务覆盖 612 个职业，收集教师轨迹微调 Qwen-4B/27B/35B-A3B 模型。

Result: WorkPilot 三个规模模型在四个专业工作基准上均超越对应基座模型；WorkPilot-35B-A3B 在 GDPval-AA v2.1 上 Elo 从 879 提升至 1565。规模分析显示模型相关的边际收益递减，执行长度先增后减；消融实验证实规划与修复组件能降低探针诊断的任务环境缺陷并提升下游性能。

Conclusion: WorkFactory 通过结构化信息规划与探针驱动修复，有效解决了专业任务构建中的信息缺失与不一致问题，生成的大规模高质量数据显著增强了大模型的专业工作能力，并揭示了规模扩展下的性能与执行长度非单调关系。

Abstract: Scaling supervised training for working agents requires diverse professional tasks that yield reliable expert demonstrations. However, missing or inconsistent task information can obstruct teacher execution and weaken the resulting supervision. We propose WorkFactory, a scalable framework for constructing coherent and executable professional tasks. Starting from professional intents, information-level planning builds an information support graph to guide task instantiation, explicitly representing the dependencies and locations of information required for the intended outputs. Probe executions then identify support failures and guide repairs while preserving the intended work. We construct 112,686 tasks spanning 612 occupations and use the collected teacher trajectories to fine-tune Qwen models at 4B, 27B, and 35B-A3B scales, yielding the WorkPilot family. All three models improve over their respective base models across four professional-work benchmarks, with WorkPilot-35B-A3B improving from 879 to 1565 Elo on GDPval-AA v2.1. Scaling analyses reveal model-dependent diminishing returns, with execution length first increasing and then decreasing. Controlled component analyses show that planning and repair reduce probe-diagnosed task environment defects and improve downstream performance. The data and models are available at https://github.com/IQuestLab/WorkFactory.

</details>


### [304] [LiveSearch: Questions as Executable Verifier Code for a Live Benchmark of Professional-Source Financial Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37166)
*Sinuo Wang,Wentao Zhao,Jiacheng Lu,Rui Sun,Beidi Luan,Qiming Shi,Jing Li,Daxin Jiang,Zuo Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出LiveSearch，一个实时金融问答基准，通过可执行验证代码（调用专业金融数据服务的类型化程序+声明式提取器）在评估时动态获取最新答案，解决现有基准答案固定、随时间失效且奖励记忆而非搜索分析的问题。实验显示六大工具调用模型准确率70.9%-76.7%，三天内70%问题答案变化，即便容忍匹配，前一日快照次日也多数失败，逐题追踪揭示聚合准确率掩盖的动态正确性变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融基准在标注时固定答案，导致随市场变动答案过期，且一旦泄露进训练数据便奖励模型记忆而非检索与分析能力；金融领域核心需求是获取特定时点的最新事实（价格、利率、披露），因此亟需一个能随时间自动更新金标准、强制模型通过工具与分析得出当前答案的基准。

Method: 将每个问题存储为可执行验证代码：由对专业金融数据服务的类型化调用序列和声明式提取器组成。问题文本冻结，金标准答案在评估时通过执行程序对专业数据实时生成；每日自动重新执行，全程无人工或语言模型参与。构建过程同样自动化：语言模型仅将真实工具响应枚举出的程序口语化，从不自行选择答案。

Result: 在通用网络搜索设置下，六个当代工具调用模型答案准确率为70.9%-76.7%。连续三天观测显示70%的问题金标准值发生变化；即便采用容忍感知匹配，直接复用前一日快照在单日内也会导致多数问题失败。逐题层面的追踪揭示了被聚合准确率掩盖的正确性动态漂移。

Conclusion: LiveSearch通过可执行验证代码实现金融问答基准的实时性，迫使模型依赖工具调用与分析而非记忆。实验证明金融事实高度时效性：答案随市场每日大幅变化，历史快照迅速失效；逐题动态监测比静态聚合指标更能反映模型真实的时效检索与推理能力。

Abstract: In finance, the answer that matters is usually the current one: prices, rates, and disclosures hold only as of a given day, so retrieving up-to-date facts is a core capability of a financial agent. Yet most financial benchmarks overlook timeliness. They fix each answer at annotation time, so the gold goes stale as markets move and, once leaked into training data, rewards recall rather than search and analysis. We introduce LiveSearch, a live benchmark that stores each question as executable verifier code: a typed program of calls to a professional financial data service, followed by a declarative extractor. The question is frozen, while its gold answer is determined by executing the program against professional financial data at evaluation time, and a daily job re-executes it with no human or language model in the loop. A memorized answer is thus likely outdated, and a model must reach the current one through tools and analysis. Construction is equally automatic: a language model only verbalizes programs enumerated from real tool responses and never chooses an answer. Under general web search, six contemporary tool-calling models reach 70.9% to 76.7% answer accuracy.
